==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\main.py ====================
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""EVA OS - Ponto de entrada principal."""
import sys
import os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import asyncio
import sys
import signal
from pathlib import Path
import threading
import time
import socket

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))

from src.kernel import Boot


def check_ollama():
    try:
        sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
        sock.settimeout(1)
        result = sock.connect_ex(('127.0.0.1', 11434))
        sock.close()
        return result == 0
    except:
        return False


class EVAOS:
    def __init__(self):
        self.boot = Boot()
        self._shutdown_requested = False
        self._llm = None
        self._brain = None
        self._web_server = None
    
    def _run_cli(self) -> None:
        try:
            import asyncio
            from src.interfaces.cli.cli import CLI
            
            core = self.boot.core
            if not core:
                print("âŒ Core nÃ£o inicializado")
                return
            
            loop = asyncio.new_event_loop()
            asyncio.set_event_loop(loop)
            
            cli = CLI(core.event_bus, core.logger, core.config)
            loop.run_until_complete(cli.initialize())
            loop.run_until_complete(cli.start())
            
            try:
                loop.run_forever()
            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                pass
            finally:
                loop.close()
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"âŒ Erro no CLI: {e}")
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()
    
    async def run(self) -> None:
        signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, self._signal_handler)
        signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, self._signal_handler)
        
        print("\nðŸ” Verificando Ollama...")
        if check_ollama():
            print("âœ… Ollama estÃ¡ rodando!")
        else:
            print("âš ï¸ Ollama nÃ£o detectado.")
        
        success = await self.boot.boot()
        
        if not success:
            print("âŒ Falha no boot. Encerrando.")
            sys.exit(1)
        
        core = self.boot.core
        registry = core.service_registry
        event_bus = core.event_bus
        logger = core.logger
        config = core.config
        
        # ============================================================
        # 1. LLM
        # ============================================================
        print("ðŸ§  Iniciando LLM...")
        try:
            from src.llm.llm_manager import LLMManager
            self._llm = LLMManager(event_bus, logger, config)
            await self._llm.initialize()
            registry.register("llm_manager", self._llm)
            print("âœ… LLM iniciado")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"âŒ Erro ao iniciar LLM: {e}")
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()
        
        # ============================================================
        # 2. BRAIN
        # ============================================================
        print("ðŸ§  Iniciando Brain...")
        try:
            from src.consciousness.brain import Brain
            self._brain = Brain(event_bus, logger, config)
            self._brain._llm_manager = self._llm
            await self._brain.initialize()
            registry.register("brain", self._brain)
            print("âœ… Brain iniciado")
            
            if event_bus.has_subscribers("user.message"):
                logger.info("âœ… Listener user.message registrado")
            else:
                logger.error("âŒ Listener user.message NÃƒO registrado!")
                
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"âŒ Erro ao iniciar brain: {e}")
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()
        
        # ============================================================
        # 3. WEB SERVER
        # ============================================================
        print("ðŸŒ Iniciando Web Server...")
        try:
            from src.interfaces.web.web_server import WebServer
            self._web_server = WebServer(event_bus, logger, config)
            await self._web_server.initialize()
            await self._web_server.start()
            registry.register("web_server", self._web_server)
            print("âœ… Web Server iniciado")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"âŒ Erro ao iniciar Web Server: {e}")
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()
        
        # ============================================================
        # 4. CLI
        # ============================================================
        print("ðŸ’¬ Iniciando CLI...")
        try:
            cli_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run_cli, daemon=True)
            cli_thread.start()
            time.sleep(1)
            print("âœ… CLI iniciada")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"âŒ Erro ao iniciar CLI: {e}")
        
        print("\n" + "=" * 60)
        print("ðŸš€ EVA OS PRONTA!")
        print("   ðŸ’¬ CLI: digite qualquer coisa para conversar")
        print("   ðŸŒ Web: http://localhost:8000")
        print("   ðŸŽ¤ Fale com a EVA usando o microfone")
        print("=" * 60 + "\n")
        print("ðŸ’¡ Digite 'sair' para encerrar\n")
        
        try:
            while not self._shutdown_requested:
                await asyncio.sleep(1)
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            pass
        finally:
            await self.shutdown()
    
    def _signal_handler(self, signum, frame) -> None:
        print(f"\nðŸ“´ Desligando...")
        self._shutdown_requested = True
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        if self._web_server:
            try:
                await self._web_server.shutdown()
                print("âœ… Web Server desligado")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"âŒ Erro ao desligar Web Server: {e}")
        
        if self._brain:
            try:
                await self._brain.shutdown()
                print("âœ… Brain desligado")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"âŒ Erro ao desligar Brain: {e}")
        
        await self.boot.shutdown()


async def main():
    eva = EVAOS()
    await eva.run()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        asyncio.run(main())
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\nðŸ“´ EVA OS interrompido.")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"\nâŒ Erro fatal: {e}")
        import traceback
        traceback.print_exc()
        sys.exit(1)

# ADR-001: Arquitetura Modular

## Data
2026-07-16

## Status
Aprovado

## Contexto
A EVA precisa evoluir por dÃ©cadas sem perder estabilidade.

## DecisÃ£o
Arquitetura baseada em mÃ³dulos independentes que se comunicam exclusivamente via EventBus.

## ConsequÃªncias
- MÃ³dulos podem ser substituÃ­dos individualmente
- Testes isolados
- Escalabilidade horizontal
- Facilidade de manutenÃ§Ã£o


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\docs\ADR\ADR-002-eventbus-como-unicos-meio-de-comunicacao.md ====================
# ADR-002: EventBus como Ãšnico Meio de ComunicaÃ§Ã£o

## Data
2026-07-16

## Status
Aprovado

## DecisÃ£o
Nenhum mÃ³dulo chama outro diretamente. Toda comunicaÃ§Ã£o passa pelo EventBus.

## ConsequÃªncias
- Desacoplamento total
- Facilidade para adicionar novos mÃ³dulos
- Auditoria de todas as interaÃ§Ãµes
- PrevenÃ§Ã£o de dependÃªncias circulares


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\docs\ADR\ADR-003-sistema-de-plugins.md ====================
# ADR-003: Sistema de Plugins

## Data
2026-07-16

## Status
Aprovado

## DecisÃ£o
ExtensÃµes externas (integraÃ§Ãµes) sÃ£o plugins instalÃ¡veis em `plugins/`.
MÃ³dulos internos (`src/`) fazem parte do nÃºcleo e nÃ£o sÃ£o plugins.

## ConsequÃªncias
- NÃºcleo protegido
- Extensibilidade controlada
- AtualizaÃ§Ãµes independentes
- Ecossistema de plugins no futuro

MemÃ³ria dividida em 3 camadas:

- Family: legado e histÃ³ria da famÃ­lia

## ConsequÃªncias
- InformaÃ§Ãµes organizadas por relevÃ¢ncia temporal
- ConsolidaÃ§Ã£o inteligente
- PreservaÃ§Ã£o do legado familiar


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\__init__.py ====================
"""EVA OS - MÃ³dulo principal."""


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\agents\agent_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de agentes da EVA."""

import asyncio
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List, Type
from datetime import datetime

from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus
from src.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent
from src.agents.agent_registry import AgentRegistry


class AgentManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de agentes da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Registrar agentes
    - Gerenciar ciclo de vida dos agentes
    - Selecionar agentes para tarefas
    - Monitorar saÃºde dos agentes
    - Distribuir tarefas
    """
    
    # Atributos de plugin
    __plugin_name__ = "agent_manager"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Gerenciador de agentes da EVA"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["core", "agents", "orchestration"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["agent_management"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        # Registro de agentes
        self.registry = AgentRegistry()
        
        # Agentes ativos
        self._agents: Dict[str, BaseAgent] = {}
        self._agent_status: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        
        # Filas de tarefas por agente
        self._task_queues: Dict[str, asyncio.Queue] = {}
        self._queue_workers: Dict[str, asyncio.Task] = {}
        
        # Estado
        self._running = False
        self._monitor_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
        self._monitor_interval = 30
        
        # Registra listeners
        self._register_listeners()
        
        self.logger.info("AgentManager inicializado")
    
    def _register_listeners(self) -> None:
        """Registra ouvintes de eventos."""
        # Comandos
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.register", self._on_register)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.select", self._on_select)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.assign_task", self._on_assign_task)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.get_status", self._on_get_status)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.list", self._on_list_agents)
        
        # Eventos automÃ¡ticos para agentes especÃ­ficos
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.general_agent", self._on_agent_event)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.knowledge_agent", self._on_agent_event)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.memory_agent", self._on_agent_event)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.planner_agent", self._on_agent_event)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.home_automation_agent", self._on_agent_event)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.information_agent", self._on_agent_event)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.learning_agent", self._on_agent_event)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.security_agent", self._on_agent_event)
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o AgentManager."""
        self.logger.info("AgentManager inicializando...")
        
        # Registra agentes base
        self._register_base_agents()
        
        self._running = True
        
        # Inicia monitoramento
        self._monitor_task = asyncio.create_task(self._monitor_agents())
        
        self.logger.info(f"AgentManager inicializado com {len(self._agents)} agentes")
    
    async def start(self) -> None:
        """Inicia o AgentManager."""
        self.logger.info("AgentManager iniciando...")
        
        # Inicia todos os agentes
        for agent in self._agents.values():
            try:
                await agent.start()
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro ao iniciar agente {agent.name}: {e}")
        
        self.logger.info("AgentManager pronto")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o AgentManager."""
        self.logger.info("AgentManager desligando...")
        
        self._running = False
        
        # Para workers
        for worker in self._queue_workers.values():
            worker.cancel()
            try:
                await worker
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
        
        # Para monitor
        if self._monitor_task:
            self._monitor_task.cancel()
            try:
                await self._monitor_task
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
        
        # Desliga agentes
        for agent in self._agents.values():
            try:
                await agent.shutdown()
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro ao desligar agente {agent.name}: {e}")
        
        self.logger.info("AgentManager desligado")
    
    # ===== REGISTRO =====
    
    def _register_base_agents(self) -> None:
        """Registra agentes base."""
        # Importa agentes
        from src.agents.general_agent import GeneralAgent
        from src.agents.knowledge_agent import KnowledgeAgent
        from src.agents.memory_agent import MemoryAgent
        from src.agents.planner_agent import PlannerAgent
        from src.agents.home_automation_agent import HomeAutomationAgent
        from src.agents.information_agent import InformationAgent
        from src.agents.learning_agent import LearningAgent
        from src.agents.security_agent import SecurityAgent
        
        # Registra
        self.register_agent(GeneralAgent)
        self.register_agent(KnowledgeAgent)
        self.register_agent(MemoryAgent)
        self.register_agent(PlannerAgent)
        self.register_agent(HomeAutomationAgent)
        self.register_agent(InformationAgent)
        self.register_agent(LearningAgent)
        self.register_agent(SecurityAgent)
    
    def register_agent(self, agent_class: Type[BaseAgent]) -> bool:
        """
        Registra um agente.
        
        Args:
            agent_class: Classe do agente (deve herdar de BaseAgent)
            
        Returns:
            True se registrado com sucesso
        """
        try:
            # Cria instÃ¢ncia
            agent = agent_class(
                event_bus=self.event_bus,
                logger=self.logger,
                config=self.config
            )
            
            # Inicializa
            asyncio.create_task(agent.initialize())
            
            # Armazena
            self._agents[agent.name] = agent
            self._agent_status[agent.name] = {
                "status": "registered",
                "capabilities": agent.capabilities,
                "task_count": 0
            }
            
            # Cria fila
            self._task_queues[agent.name] = asyncio.Queue()
            
            # Inicia worker
            self._queue_workers[agent.name] = asyncio.create_task(
                self._process_queue(agent.name)
            )
            
            self.logger.info(f"Agente registrado: {agent.name}")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao registrar agente: {e}")
            return False
    
    async def _on_register(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Registra um agente via evento."""
        payload = event.payload
        agent_class_path = payload.get("agent_class")
        
        if not agent_class_path:
            self.logger.error("Nenhuma classe de agente fornecida")
            return
        
        try:
            # Importa dinamicamente
            module_path, class_name = agent_class_path.rsplit(".", 1)
            module = __import__(module_path, fromlist=[class_name])
            agent_class = getattr(module, class_name)
            
            success = self.register_agent(agent_class)
            
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "agent.registered",
                {
                    "success": success,
                    "agent_class": agent_class_path,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                },
                source="agent_manager"
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao registrar agente via evento: {e}")
    
    # ===== SELEÃ‡ÃƒO =====
    
    async def _on_select(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Seleciona um agente para uma tarefa."""
        payload = event.payload
        intent = payload.get("intent", "general")
        task = payload.get("task", {})
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        
        selected = self.select_agent(intent, task)
        
        if selected:
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "agent.selected",
                {
                    "agent": selected,
                    "intent": intent,
                    "task": task,
                    "request_id": request_id,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                },
                source="agent_manager"
            )
        else:
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "agent.no_agent_available",
                {
                    "intent": intent,
                    "task": task,
                    "request_id": request_id,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                },
                source="agent_manager"
            )
    
    def select_agent(self, intent: str, task: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        Seleciona o melhor agente para uma tarefa.
        
        EstratÃ©gia:
        1. Agentes especializados na intenÃ§Ã£o
        2. Agentes com capacidades correspondentes
        3. Agente geral (fallback)
        """
        # Mapeamento de intenÃ§Ã£o para agente
        intent_map = {
            "conversation": "general",
            "question": "knowledge",
            "command": "information",
            "planning": "planner",
            "learning": "learning",
            "emergency": "security",
            "memory": "memory",
            "home_automation": "home_automation",
            "information": "information"
        }
        
        preferred = intent_map.get(intent)
        
        if preferred and preferred in self._agents:
            agent = self._agents[preferred]
            if agent.can_handle(intent) and self._is_available(preferred):
                return preferred
        
        # Busca agente por capacidade
        for name, agent in self._agents.items():
            if agent.can_handle(intent) and self._is_available(name):
                return name
        
        # Fallback: general agent
        if "general" in self._agents and self._is_available("general"):
            return "general"
        
        # Qualquer agente disponÃ­vel
        for name in self._agents.keys():
            if self._is_available(name):
                return name
        
        return None
    
    def _is_available(self, agent_name: str) -> bool:
        """Verifica se um agente estÃ¡ disponÃ­vel."""
        status = self._agent_status.get(agent_name, {})
        return status.get("status") != "busy"
    
    # ===== ATRIBUIÃ‡ÃƒO =====
    
    async def _on_assign_task(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Atribui uma tarefa a um agente."""
        payload = event.payload
        agent_name = payload.get("agent")
        task = payload.get("task")
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        
        if not agent_name or not task:
            await self._send_error("invalid_request", "Agente ou tarefa nÃ£o fornecidos", request_id)
            return
        
        if agent_name not in self._task_queues:
            await self._send_error("agent_not_found", f"Agente {agent_name} nÃ£o encontrado", request_id)
            return
        
        # Adiciona Ã  fila
        await self._task_queues[agent_name].put({
            "task": task,
            "request_id": request_id,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
        
        # Atualiza status
        self._agent_status[agent_name]["status"] = "queued"
        
        self.logger.debug(f"Tarefa enfileirada para {agent_name}")
    
    async def _process_queue(self, agent_name: str) -> None:
        """Processa a fila de um agente."""
        queue = self._task_queues.get(agent_name)
        agent = self._agents.get(agent_name)
        
        if not queue or not agent:
            return
        
        while self._running:
            try:
                # Pega da fila
                item = await queue.get()
                
                # Atualiza status
                self._agent_status[agent_name]["status"] = "busy"
                
                # Processa
                try:
                    await agent.handle_event(
                        Event(
                            type=f"agent.{agent_name}",
                            payload=item,
                            source="agent_manager"
                        )
                    )
                    self._agent_status[agent_name]["task_count"] = \
                        self._agent_status[agent_name].get("task_count", 0) + 1
                except Exception as e:
                    self.logger.error(f"Erro processando tarefa em {agent_name}: {e}")
                
                # Atualiza status
                self._agent_status[agent_name]["status"] = "idle"
                
                queue.task_done()
                
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro no worker de {agent_name}: {e}")
    
    # ===== EVENTOS DE AGENTES =====
    
    async def _on_agent_event(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Roteia evento para o agente apropriado."""
        # Extrai o nome do agente do tipo do evento
        agent_name = event.type.replace("agent.", "")
        
        if agent_name in self._agents:
            # Coloca na fila do agente
            if agent_name in self._task_queues:
                await self._task_queues[agent_name].put({
                    "task": event.payload,
                    "source": event.source,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                })
        else:
            self.logger.warning(f"Agente nÃ£o encontrado: {agent_name}")
    
    # ===== STATUS =====
    
    async def _on_get_status(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Retorna status dos agentes."""
        request_id = event.payload.get("request_id")
        
        status = self.get_status()
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "agent.status",
            {
                "status": status,
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="agent_manager"
        )
    
    async def _on_list_agents(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Lista agentes registrados."""
        request_id = event.payload.get("request_id")
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "agent.list_result",
            {
                "agents": [
                    {
                        "name": name,
                        "capabilities": agent.capabilities,
                        "status": self._agent_status.get(name, {}).get("status", "unknown")
                    }
                    for name, agent in self._agents.items()
                ],
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="agent_manager"
        )
    
    # ===== MONITORAMENTO =====
    
    async def _monitor_agents(self) -> None:
        """Monitora a saÃºde dos agentes."""
        while self._running:
            try:
                await asyncio.sleep(self._monitor_interval)
                
                for name, agent in self._agents.items():
                    try:
                        health = await agent.health_check()
                        self._agent_status[name]["health"] = health
                    except Exception as e:
                        self.logger.error(f"Erro no health check de {name}: {e}")
                        self._agent_status[name]["health"] = {"error": str(e)}
                
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro no monitoramento: {e}")
    
    # ===== UTILITÃRIOS =====
    
    async def _send_error(self, code: str, message: str, request_id: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        """Envia erro."""
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "agent.error",
            {
                "code": code,
                "message": message,
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="agent_manager"
        )
    
    # ===== MÃ‰TODOS PÃšBLICOS =====
    
    def get_agent(self, name: str) -> Optional[BaseAgent]:
        """ObtÃ©m um agente pelo nome."""
        return self._agents.get(name)
    
    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m status de todos os agentes."""
        return {
            "total": len(self._agents),
            "agents": {
                name: {
                    "status": status.get("status", "unknown"),
                    "capabilities": agent.capabilities,
                    "task_count": status.get("task_count", 0),
                    "health": status.get("health", {})
                }
                for name, (agent, status) in zip(self._agents.keys(), self._agent_status.values())
            },
            "running": self._running
        }
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        """Health check do AgentManager."""
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "total_agents": len(self._agents),
            "active_agents": len([s for s in self._agent_status.values() if s.get("status") == "idle"])
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\agents\agent_registry.py ====================
"""Registro central de agentes."""

from typing import Dict, Type, Optional, List, Any

from src.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent


class AgentRegistry:
    """
    Registro central de todos os agentes disponÃ­veis.
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._agents: Dict[str, Type[BaseAgent]] = {}
        self._agent_metadata: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
    def register(self, name: str, agent_class: Type[BaseAgent],
                 metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> None:
        """
        Registra um agente.
        
        Args:
            name: Nome do agente
            agent_class: Classe do agente
            metadata: Metadados adicionais
        """
        self._agents[name] = agent_class
        self._agent_metadata[name] = {
            "name": name,
            "class": agent_class.__name__,
            "module": agent_class.__module__,
            "capabilities": getattr(agent_class, "__plugin_capabilities__", ["general"]),
            "version": getattr(agent_class, "__plugin_version__", "1.0.0"),
            "description": getattr(agent_class, "__plugin_description__", ""),
            **(metadata or {})
        }
    
    def get(self, name: str) -> Optional[Type[BaseAgent]]:
        """ObtÃ©m uma classe de agente pelo nome."""
        return self._agents.get(name)
    
    def get_metadata(self, name: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m metadados de um agente."""
        return self._agent_metadata.get(name)
    
    def list(self) -> List[str]:
        """Lista todos os agentes registrados."""
        return list(self._agents.keys())
    
    def list_with_metadata(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Lista todos os agentes com metadados."""
        return list(self._agent_metadata.values())
    
    def get_by_capability(self, capability: str) -> List[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m agentes com uma capacidade especÃ­fica."""
        result = []
        for name, meta in self._agent_metadata.items():
            if capability in meta.get("capabilities", []):
                result.append(name)
        return result
    
    def unregister(self, name: str) -> bool:
        """Remove um agente do registro."""
        if name in self._agents:
            del self._agents[name]
            if name in self._agent_metadata:
                del self._agent_metadata[name]
            return True
        return False
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Limpa o registro."""
        self._agents.clear()
        self._agent_metadata.clear()


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\agents\base_agent.py ====================
"""Classe base para todos os agentes da EVA."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from datetime import datetime

from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus


class BaseAgent(ABC):
    """
    Classe base para todos os agentes.
    
    Todo agente deve herdar desta classe e implementar:
    - initialize(): InicializaÃ§Ã£o
    - start(): InÃ­cio das operaÃ§Ãµes
    - process(): Processamento de tarefas
    - shutdown(): Desligamento
    - health_check(): VerificaÃ§Ã£o de saÃºde
    """
    
    # Atributos de plugin (devem ser sobrescritos)
    __plugin_name__ = "base_agent"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Agente base da EVA"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["agent", "base"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["base"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config, name: str = None):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        # Nome do agente
        self.name = name or self.__class__.__name__.lower().replace("agent", "")
        
        # Estado
        self._running = False
        self._initialized = False
        self._task_count = 0
        self._error_count = 0
        self._last_task: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
        self._started_at: Optional[datetime] = None
        
        # Capacidades
        self.capabilities = self._get_capabilities()
    
    @abstractmethod
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o agente."""
        self._initialized = True
        self.logger.info(f"Agente {self.name} inicializado")
    
    @abstractmethod
    async def start(self) -> None:
        """Inicia o agente."""
        self._running = True
        self._started_at = datetime.now()
        self.logger.info(f"Agente {self.name} iniciado")
    
    @abstractmethod
    async def process(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Processa uma tarefa.
        
        Args:
            task: DicionÃ¡rio com a tarefa a ser processada
            
        Returns:
            Dict com o resultado do processamento
        """
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o agente."""
        self._running = False
        self.logger.info(f"Agente {self.name} desligado")
    
    @abstractmethod
    async def health_check(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Verifica a saÃºde do agente."""
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "running": self._running,
            "initialized": self._initialized,
            "task_count": self._task_count,
            "error_count": self._error_count,
            "uptime": (datetime.now() - self._started_at).total_seconds() if self._started_at else 0
        }
    
    def _get_capabilities(self) -> List[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m as capacidades do agente."""
        return getattr(self, "__plugin_capabilities__", ["general"])
    
    def can_handle(self, intent: str) -> bool:
        """Verifica se o agente pode lidar com uma intenÃ§Ã£o."""
        # Por padrÃ£o, verifica se a intenÃ§Ã£o estÃ¡ nas capacidades
        return intent in self.capabilities or "general" in self.capabilities
    
    async def handle_event(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Manipula um evento recebido."""
        if not self._running:
            self.logger.warning(f"Agente {self.name} recebeu evento mas estÃ¡ desligado")
            return
        
        try:
            # Extrai tarefa do evento
            task = event.payload.get("task", {})
            if not task:
                task = event.payload
            
            # Processa a tarefa
            result = await self.process(task)
            
            # Registra
            self._task_count += 1
            self._last_task = {
                "task": task,
                "result": result,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
            
            # Publica resultado
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "agent.task_completed",
                {
                    "agent": self.name,
                    "result": result,
                    "original_event": event.id if hasattr(event, 'id') else None,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                },
                source=self.name
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            self._error_count += 1
            self.logger.error(f"Erro no agente {self.name}: {e}")
            
            # Publica erro
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "agent.task_failed",
                {
                    "agent": self.name,
                    "error": str(e),
                    "task": event.payload,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                },
                source=self.name
            )
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m estatÃ­sticas do agente."""
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "running": self._running,
            "initialized": self._initialized,
            "task_count": self._task_count,
            "error_count": self._error_count,
            "uptime": (datetime.now() - self._started_at).total_seconds() if self._started_at else 0,
            "capabilities": self.capabilities,
            "last_task": self._last_task
        }

"""Agente Geral - ConversaÃ§Ã£o e tarefas bÃ¡sicas."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime

from src.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent
from src.kernel.events import EventBus


class GeneralAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Agente Geral da EVA.
    
    ResponsÃ¡vel por:
    - ConversaÃ§Ã£o geral
    - Tarefas bÃ¡sicas
    - Roteamento simples
    - InteraÃ§Ãµes amigÃ¡veis
    """
    
    # Atributos de plugin
    __plugin_name__ = "general_agent"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Agente geral para conversaÃ§Ã£o e tarefas bÃ¡sicas"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["agent", "core", "conversation"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["general", "conversation", "basic_tasks"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        super().__init__(event_bus, logger, config, name="general")
        self._greeting_count = 0
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o agente."""
        await super().initialize()
        self.logger.info("GeneralAgent: Pronto para conversar")
    
    async def start(self) -> None:
        """Inicia o agente."""
        await super().start()
        self.logger.info("GeneralAgent: Ativo e aguardando")
    
    async def process(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Processa uma tarefa.
        
        Tipos de tarefa:
        - conversation: Mensagem para conversar
        - greeting: SaudaÃ§Ã£o
        - help: Ajuda
        - basic_task: Tarefa bÃ¡sica
        """
        task_type = task.get("type", "conversation")
        
        if task_type == "conversation":
            return await self._handle_conversation(task)
        elif task_type == "greeting":
            return await self._handle_greeting(task)
        elif task_type == "help":
            return await self._handle_help(task)
        else:
            return await self._handle_basic_task(task)
    
    async def _handle_conversation(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula conversaÃ§Ã£o."""
        text = task.get("text", "")
        user = task.get("user", "unknown")
        
        self.logger.debug(f"ConversaÃ§Ã£o com {user}: {text[:50]}...")
        
        # TODO: Integrar com LLM
        response = f"Recebi sua mensagem: {text[:50]}..."
        
        return {
            "type": "conversation",
            "response": response,
            "user": user,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _handle_greeting(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula saudaÃ§Ã£o."""
        self._greeting_count += 1
        
        greetings = [
            "OlÃ¡! Como posso ajudar vocÃª hoje?",
            "Oi! Estou aqui para ajudar!",
            "OlÃ¡! O que vocÃª precisa?",
            "Oi! Como estÃ¡?",
            "OlÃ¡! Sempre um prazer falar com vocÃª!"
        ]
        
        response = greetings[self._greeting_count % len(greetings)]
        
        return {
            "type": "greeting",
            "response": response,
            "greeting_count": self._greeting_count,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _handle_help(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula pedido de ajuda."""
        help_text = """
        Posso ajudar com:
        - Conversas e perguntas
        - Comandos bÃ¡sicos
        - Busca de informaÃ§Ã£o
        - Lembretes
        - AutomaÃ§Ã£o da casa
        
        O que vocÃª gostaria?
        """
        
        return {
            "type": "help",
            "response": help_text,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _handle_basic_task(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula tarefa bÃ¡sica."""
        return {
            "type": "basic_task",
            "result": "Tarefa recebida e processada",
            "task": task,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o agente."""
        self.logger.info(f"GeneralAgent: {self._greeting_count} saudaÃ§Ãµes")
        await super().shutdown()
    
    async def health_check(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Verifica saÃºde do agente."""
        health = await super().health_check()
        health["greeting_count"] = self._greeting_count
        return health


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\agents\home_automation_agent.py ====================
"""Agente de AutomaÃ§Ã£o Residencial."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

from src.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent
from src.kernel.events import EventBus


class HomeAutomationAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Agente de AutomaÃ§Ã£o Residencial.
    
    ResponsÃ¡vel por:
    - Controlar luzes
    - Controlar temperatura
    - Monitorar sensores
    - Executar automaÃ§Ãµes
    - Controlar dispositivos
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "home_automation_agent"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Agente especializado em automaÃ§Ã£o residencial"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["agent", "automation", "iot"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["home_automation", "lights", "temperature", "sensors"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        super().__init__(event_bus, logger, config, name="home_automation")
        self._lights = {}
        self._temperature = 22.0
        self._sensors = {}
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o agente."""
        await super().initialize()
        self.logger.info("HomeAutomationAgent: Pronto para controlar a casa")
    
    async def process(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Processa uma tarefa de automaÃ§Ã£o.
        """
        task_type = task.get("type", "control")
        
        if task_type == "light":
            return await self._handle_light(task)
        elif task_type == "temperature":
            return await self._handle_temperature(task)
        elif task_type == "sensor":
            return await self._handle_sensor(task)
        elif task_type == "status":
            return await self._handle_status(task)
        else:
            return {"error": f"Tipo de tarefa desconhecido: {task_type}"}
    
    async def _handle_light(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula controle de luz."""
        light_id = task.get("light_id", "all")
        action = task.get("action", "toggle")  # on, off, toggle
        
        if light_id == "all":
            for lid in self._lights:
                if action == "on":
                    self._lights[lid] = True
                elif action == "off":
                    self._lights[lid] = False
                else:
                    self._lights[lid] = not self._lights.get(lid, False)
            
            status = "acesas" if all(self._lights.values()) else "apagadas"
            return {
                "type": "light",
                "action": action,
                "light_id": "all",
                "status": status,
                "success": True
            }
        else:
            if action == "on":
                self._lights[light_id] = True
            elif action == "off":
                self._lights[light_id] = False
            else:
                self._lights[light_id] = not self._lights.get(light_id, False)
            
            return {
                "type": "light",
                "action": action,
                "light_id": light_id,
                "status": "acesa" if self._lights.get(light_id) else "apagada",
                "success": True
            }
    
    async def _handle_temperature(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula controle de temperatura."""
        action = task.get("action", "get")
        value = task.get("value")
        
        if action == "set" and value is not None:
            self._temperature = float(value)
            return {
                "type": "temperature",
                "action": "set",
                "value": self._temperature,
                "success": True
            }
        else:
            return {
                "type": "temperature",
                "action": "get",
                "value": self._temperature,
                "success": True
            }
    
    async def _handle_sensor(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula sensores."""
        sensor_id = task.get("sensor_id")
        value = task.get("value")
        
        if value is not None:
            self._sensors[sensor_id] = value
            return {
                "type": "sensor",
                "action": "update",
                "sensor_id": sensor_id,
                "value": value,
                "success": True
            }
        else:
            value = self._sensors.get(sensor_id)
            return {
                "type": "sensor",
                "action": "get",
                "sensor_id": sensor_id,
                "value": value,
                "success": True
            }
    
    async def _handle_status(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula consulta de status."""
        return {
            "type": "status",
            "lights": self._lights,
            "temperature": self._temperature,
            "sensors": self._sensors,
            "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o agente."""
        self.logger.info(f"HomeAutomationAgent: Ãšltimo estado - {len(self._lights)} luzes, {len(self._sensors)} sensores")
        await super().shutdown()

"""Agente de InformaÃ§Ã£o."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
import subprocess
import platform
import psutil

from src.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent
from src.kernel.events import EventBus


class InformationAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Agente de InformaÃ§Ã£o.
    
    ResponsÃ¡vel por:
    - InformaÃ§Ãµes do sistema
    - Status de serviÃ§os
    - InformaÃ§Ãµes de rede
    - Dados de desempenho
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "information_agent"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Agente especializado em informaÃ§Ãµes do sistema"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["agent", "information", "system"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["information", "system_info", "status"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        super().__init__(event_bus, logger, config, name="information")
    
    async def process(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Processa uma tarefa de informaÃ§Ã£o.
        """
        task_type = task.get("type", "system")
        
        if task_type == "system":
            return await self._handle_system_info(task)
        elif task_type == "network":
            return await self._handle_network_info(task)
        elif task_type == "resources":
            return await self._handle_resources(task)
        elif task_type == "services":
            return await self._handle_services(task)
        else:
            return {"error": f"Tipo de tarefa desconhecido: {task_type}"}
    
    async def _handle_system_info(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula informaÃ§Ãµes do sistema."""
        return {
            "type": "system",
            "platform": platform.platform(),
            "python_version": platform.python_version(),
            "hostname": platform.node(),
            "processor": platform.processor(),
            "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _handle_network_info(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula informaÃ§Ãµes de rede."""
        # TODO: Implementar informaÃ§Ãµes de rede completas
        return {
            "type": "network",
            "hostname": platform.node(),
            "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _handle_resources(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula informaÃ§Ãµes de recursos."""
        cpu = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.1)
        memory = psutil.virtual_memory()
        disk = psutil.disk_usage('/')
        
        return {
            "type": "resources",
            "cpu": cpu,
            "memory": {
                "percent": memory.percent,
                "used_gb": memory.used / (1024**3),
                "total_gb": memory.total / (1024**3)
            },
            "disk": {
                "percent": disk.percent,
                "used_gb": disk.used / (1024**3),
                "total_gb": disk.total / (1024**3)
            },
            "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _handle_services(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula informaÃ§Ãµes de serviÃ§os."""
        # TODO: Implementar informaÃ§Ãµes de serviÃ§os
        return {
            "type": "services",
            "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o agente."""
        await super().shutdown()
    
    async def health_check(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Verifica saÃºde do agente."""
        return await super().health_check()

"""Agente de Conhecimento."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

from src.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent
from src.kernel.events import EventBus


class KnowledgeAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Agente de Conhecimento.
    
    ResponsÃ¡vel por:
    - Responder perguntas factuais
    - Buscar conhecimento
    - Relacionar conceitos
    - Validar informaÃ§Ãµes
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "knowledge_agent"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Agente especializado em conhecimento e informaÃ§Ã£o"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["agent", "knowledge", "information"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["knowledge", "facts", "information"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config", "knowledge_manager"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        super().__init__(event_bus, logger, config, name="knowledge")
        self.knowledge_manager = None
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o agente."""
        # ObtÃ©m o knowledge manager do service registry
        from src.core.service_registry import ServiceRegistry
        registry = ServiceRegistry()
        self.knowledge_manager = registry.get("knowledge_manager")
        
        await super().initialize()
        self.logger.info("KnowledgeAgent: Pronto para responder perguntas")
    
    async def process(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Processa uma tarefa de conhecimento.
        """
        task_type = task.get("type", "query")
        
        if task_type == "query":
            return await self._handle_query(task)
        elif task_type == "define":
            return await self._handle_definition(task)
        elif task_type == "relate":
            return await self._handle_relation(task)
        elif task_type == "search":
            return await self._handle_search(task)
        else:
            return {"error": f"Tipo de tarefa desconhecido: {task_type}"}
    
    async def _handle_query(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula consulta de conhecimento."""
        query = task.get("query", "")
        user = task.get("user", "unknown")
        
        if not self.knowledge_manager:
            return {"error": "Knowledge Manager nÃ£o disponÃ­vel"}
        
        # Busca conhecimento
        results = await self.knowledge_manager.query.execute(query)
        
        return {
            "type": "query",
            "query": query,
            "results": results,
            "user": user,
            "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _handle_definition(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula pedido de definiÃ§Ã£o."""
        concept = task.get("concept", "")
        
        if not self.knowledge_manager:
            return {"error": "Knowledge Manager nÃ£o disponÃ­vel"}
        
        node = self.knowledge_manager.get_concept(concept)
        
        if node:
            return {
                "type": "definition",
                "concept": concept,
                "definition": node.get("description"),
                "category": node.get("category"),
                "confidence": node.get("confidence"),
                "found": True
            }
        else:
            return {
                "type": "definition",
                "concept": concept,
                "found": False,
                "message": f"NÃ£o encontrei definiÃ§Ã£o para '{concept}'"
            }
    
    async def _handle_relation(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula pedido de relaÃ§Ã£o entre conceitos."""
        source = task.get("source", "")
        target = task.get("target", "")
        
        if not self.knowledge_manager:
            return {"error": "Knowledge Manager nÃ£o disponÃ­vel"}
        
        related = self.knowledge_manager.get_related(source)
        
        found = None
        for item in related:
            if item.get("concept") == target:
                found = item
                break
        
        if found:
            return {
                "type": "relation",
                "source": source,
                "target": target,
                "relation_type": found.get("relation_type"),
                "confidence": found.get("confidence"),
                "found": True
            }
        else:
            return {
                "type": "relation",
                "source": source,
                "target": target,
                "found": False,
                "message": f"NÃ£o encontrei relaÃ§Ã£o entre '{source}' e '{target}'"
            }
    
    async def _handle_search(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula busca de conhecimento."""
        query = task.get("query", "")
        
        if not self.knowledge_manager:
            return {"error": "Knowledge Manager nÃ£o disponÃ­vel"}
        
        results = self.knowledge_manager.index.search(query, limit=10)
        
        return {
            "type": "search",
            "query": query,
            "results": results,
            "count": len(results),
            "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
        }

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

from src.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent
from src.kernel.events import EventBus


class LearningAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Agente de Aprendizado.
    
    ResponsÃ¡vel por:
    - Estudar novos tÃ³picos
    - Extrair conhecimento de fontes
    - Atualizar conhecimento existente
    - Identificar lacunas de conhecimento
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "learning_agent"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Agente especializado em aprendizado"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["agent", "learning", "study"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["learning", "study", "knowledge_acquisition"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        super().__init__(event_bus, logger, config, name="learning")
        self.knowledge_manager = None
        self._learning_topics = []
        self._current_topic = None
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o agente."""
        try:
            from src.core.service_registry import ServiceRegistry
            registry = ServiceRegistry()
            self.knowledge_manager = registry.get("knowledge_manager")
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(f"NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel obter KnowledgeManager: {e}")
        
        await super().initialize()
        self.logger.info("LearningAgent: Pronto para aprender")
    
    async def process(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Processa uma tarefa de aprendizado.
        """
        task_type = task.get("type", "study")
        
        if task_type == "study":
            return await self._handle_study(task)
        elif task_type == "topic":
            return await self._handle_topic(task)
        elif task_type == "summary":
            return await self._handle_summary(task)
        else:
            return {"error": f"Tipo de tarefa desconhecido: {task_type}"}
    
    async def _handle_study(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula estudo de um tÃ³pico."""
        topic = task.get("topic", "")
        
        if not topic:
            return {"error": "Nenhum tÃ³pico fornecido"}
        
        self._current_topic = topic
        self._learning_topics.append(topic)
        
        # TODO: Implementar estudo real (integraÃ§Ã£o com internet, LLM, etc.)
        
        return {
            "type": "study",
            "topic": topic,
            "status": "studying",
            "message": f"Iniciando estudo de '{topic}'",
            "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _handle_topic(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula lista de tÃ³picos."""
        return {
            "type": "topic",
            "topics": self._learning_topics,
            "current": self._current_topic,
            "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _handle_summary(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula resumo do aprendizado."""
        return {
            "type": "summary",
            "topics_studied": len(self._learning_topics),
            "current_topic": self._current_topic,
            "knowledge_added": 0,  # TODO: Contar conhecimento adicionado
            "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o agente."""
        self.logger.info(f"LearningAgent: {len(self._learning_topics)} tÃ³picos estudados")
        await super().shutdown()
    
    async def health_check(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Verifica saÃºde do agente."""
        health = await super().health_check()
        health["topics_studied"] = len(self._learning_topics)
        return health

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

from src.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent

class MemoryAgent(BaseAgent):

"""Agente de Planejamento."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

from src.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent
from src.kernel.events import EventBus


class PlannerAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Agente de Planejamento.
    
    ResponsÃ¡vel por:
    - Criar planos
    - Decompor objetivos
    - Gerenciar execuÃ§Ã£o
    - Acompanhar progresso
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "planner_agent"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Agente especializado em planejamento"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["agent", "planner", "planning"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["planning", "decomposition", "scheduling"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config", "planner_manager"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        super().__init__(event_bus, logger, config, name="planner")
        self.planner_manager = None
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o agente."""
        from src.core.service_registry import ServiceRegistry
        registry = ServiceRegistry()
        self.planner_manager = registry.get("planner_manager")
        
        await super().initialize()
        self.logger.info("PlannerAgent: Pronto para planejar")
    
    async def process(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Processa uma tarefa de planejamento.
        """
        task_type = task.get("type", "plan")
        
        if task_type == "plan":
            return await self._handle_plan(task)
        elif task_type == "status":
            return await self._handle_status(task)
        elif task_type == "cancel":
            return await self._handle_cancel(task)
        else:
            return {"error": f"Tipo de tarefa desconhecido: {task_type}"}
    
    async def _handle_plan(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula criaÃ§Ã£o de plano."""
        goal = task.get("goal", {})
        
        if not self.planner_manager:
            return {"error": "Planner Manager nÃ£o disponÃ­vel"}
        
        # Cria objetivo
        goal_obj = __import__('src.planner.goal').Goal(
            title=goal.get("title", "Objetivo"),
            description=goal.get("description", ""),
            goal_type=goal.get("goal_type", "oneshot"),
            priority=goal.get("priority", 5),
            created_by=goal.get("created_by", "planner_agent"),
            context=goal.get("context", {})
        )
        
        self.planner_manager._goals[goal_obj.id] = goal_obj
        
        # Cria plano
        await self.planner_manager._on_create_plan(
            __import__('src.kernel.events').Event(
                type="planner.create_plan",
                payload={"goal_id": goal_obj.id},
                source="planner_agent"
            )
        )
        
        return {
            "type": "plan",
            "goal_id": goal_obj.id,
            "status": "created",
            "message": "Plano criado com sucesso",
            "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _handle_status(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula consulta de status."""
        plan_id = task.get("plan_id")
        
        if not self.planner_manager:
            return {"error": "Planner Manager nÃ£o disponÃ­vel"}
        
        if plan_id:
            plan = self.planner_manager.get_plan(plan_id)
            if plan:
                return {
                    "type": "status",
                    "plan": plan.to_dict(),
                    "found": True,
                    "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
                }
            else:
                return {
                    "type": "status",
                    "found": False,
                    "message": f"Plano {plan_id} nÃ£o encontrado"
                }
        else:
            status = self.planner_manager.get_status()
            return {
                "type": "status",
                "status": status,
                "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
    
    async def _handle_cancel(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula cancelamento de plano."""
        plan_id = task.get("plan_id")
        
        if not self.planner_manager:
            return {"error": "Planner Manager nÃ£o disponÃ­vel"}
        
        if plan_id:
            await self.planner_manager._on_cancel_plan(
                __import__('src.kernel.events').Event(
                    type="planner.cancel_plan",
                    payload={"plan_id": plan_id},
                    source="planner_agent"
                )
            )
            
            return {
                "type": "cancel",
                "plan_id": plan_id,
                "success": True,
                "message": f"Plano {plan_id} cancelado"
            }
        else:
            return {"error": "plan_id nÃ£o fornecido"}

"""Agente de SeguranÃ§a."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime

from src.agents.base_agent import BaseAgent
from src.kernel.events import EventBus


class SecurityAgent(BaseAgent):
    """
    Agente de SeguranÃ§a.
    
    ResponsÃ¡vel por:
    - Monitorar ameaÃ§as
    - Responder a emergÃªncias
    - Gerenciar permissÃµes
    - Auditoria
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "security_agent"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Agente especializado em seguranÃ§a"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["agent", "security", "emergency"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["security", "emergency", "monitoring"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        super().__init__(event_bus, logger, config, name="security")
        self._alerts = []
        self._incidents = []
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o agente."""
        await super().initialize()
        self.logger.info("SecurityAgent: Sistema de seguranÃ§a ativo")
    
    async def process(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Processa uma tarefa de seguranÃ§a.
        """
        task_type = task.get("type", "check")
        
        if task_type == "check":
            return await self._handle_check(task)
        elif task_type == "alert":
            return await self._handle_alert(task)
        elif task_type == "emergency":
            return await self._handle_emergency(task)
        elif task_type == "status":
            return await self._handle_status(task)
        else:
            return {"error": f"Tipo de tarefa desconhecido: {task_type}"}
    
    async def _handle_check(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula verificaÃ§Ã£o de seguranÃ§a."""
        # TODO: Implementar verificaÃ§Ãµes
        return {
            "type": "check",
            "status": "ok",
            "message": "Sistema seguro",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _handle_alert(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula alerta de seguranÃ§a."""
        alert = task.get("alert", {})
        self._alerts.append({
            "alert": alert,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
        
        return {
            "type": "alert",
            "alert": alert,
            "acknowledged": True,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _handle_emergency(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula emergÃªncia."""
        incident = task.get("incident", {})
        self._incidents.append({
            "incident": incident,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
        
        # AÃ§Ãµes de emergÃªncia
        if incident.get("type") == "fire":
            return {
                "type": "emergency",
                "action": "fire_protocol",
                "steps": ["Desligar energia", "Acionar bombeiros", "Evacuar"],
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
        elif incident.get("type") == "break_in":
            return {
                "type": "emergency",
                "action": "security_protocol",
                "steps": ["Acionar seguranÃ§a", "Bloquear portas", "Gravar cÃ¢meras"],
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
        else:
            return {
                "type": "emergency",
                "action": "emergency_protocol",
                "steps": ["Avaliar situaÃ§Ã£o", "Tomar medidas necessÃ¡rias"],
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
    
    async def _handle_status(self, task: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Manipula consulta de status."""
        return {
            "type": "status",
            "alerts": len(self._alerts),
            "incidents": len(self._incidents),
            "last_alert": self._alerts[-1] if self._alerts else None,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o agente."""
        self.logger.info(f"SecurityAgent: {len(self._alerts)} alertas, {len(self._incidents)} incidentes")
        await super().shutdown()
    
    async def health_check(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Verifica saÃºde do agente."""
        health = await super().health_check()
        health["alerts"] = len(self._alerts)
        health["incidents"] = len(self._incidents)
        return health
