# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

"""
O cÃ©rebro da EVA - Pipeline Cognitivo Completo e Funcional.
"""

import asyncio
import sys
import os
import importlib
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime

from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus
from src.core.event_types import EventType


class Brain:
    """
    CÃ©rebro da EVA - Pipeline Cognitivo Completo.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self._running = False
        self._llm_manager = None
        
        # MÃ³dulos do pipeline (serÃ£o carregados no initialize)
        self._modules = {}
        
        # Fila de percepÃ§Ã£o
        self._perception_queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue()
        self._loop_count = 0
        self._conversation_history: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
        
        # Registra listeners
        self._register_listeners()
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [BRAIN] Inicializado")
    
    def _register_listeners(self) -> None:
        """Registra listeners para o pipeline."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [BRAIN] Registrando listeners...")
        
        # Listener principal
        self.event_bus.subscribe(EventType.USER_MESSAGE, self._on_user_message)
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§  [BRAIN] âœ… Listener registrado para {EventType.USER_MESSAGE}")
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o Brain e todos os componentes."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [BRAIN] Inicializando...")
        
        # ===== CARREGA MÃ“DULOS =====
        await self._load_modules()
        
        # ===== VERIFICA LLM =====
        if self._llm_manager:
            self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [BRAIN] âœ… LLM disponÃ­vel")
        else:
            self.logger.warning("ðŸ§  [BRAIN] âš ï¸ LLM NÃƒO disponÃ­vel")
        
        self._running = True
        asyncio.create_task(self._cognitive_loop())
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [BRAIN] Inicializado com sucesso")
    
    async def _load_modules(self) -> None:
        """Carrega todos os mÃ³dulos com fallback seguro."""
        
        # ðŸ”¥ GARANTE QUE O DIRETÃ“RIO RAIZ ESTEJA NO sys.path
        root_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
        if root_dir not in sys.path:
            sys.path.insert(0, root_dir)
            self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“‚ Adicionado {root_dir} ao sys.path")
        
        modules_to_load = [
            ("context_manager", "src.context.context_manager", "ContextManager"),
            ("memory_manager", "src.memory.memory_manager", "MemoryManager"),
            ("knowledge_manager", "src.knowledge.knowledge_manager", "KnowledgeManager"),
            ("planner_manager", "src.planner.planner_manager", "PlannerManager"),
            ("reasoning_manager", "src.reasoning.reasoning_manager", "ReasoningManager"),
            ("emotion_engine", "src.emotion.emotion_engine", "EmotionEngine"),
            ("prompt_builder", "src.prompt.prompt_builder", "PromptBuilder"),
            ("user_manager", "src.users.user_manager", "UserManager"),
            ("long_term_memory", "src.memory.long_term_memory", "LongTermMemory"),
            ("reminder_manager", "src.reminders.reminder_manager", "ReminderManager"),
            ("conversation_flow", "src.context.conversation_flow", "ConversationFlow"),
            ("family_memory", "src.memory.family_memory", "FamilyMemory"),
            ("voice_manager", "src.voice.voice_manager", "VoiceManager"),
            ("time_manager", "src.time.time_manager", "TimeManager"),
            ("calendar_manager", "src.time.calendar_manager", "CalendarManager"),
            ("weather_manager", "src.time.weather_manager", "WeatherManager"),
            ("lunar_manager", "src.time.lunar_manager", "LunarManager"),
            ("news_manager", "src.news.news_manager", "NewsManager"),
        ]
        
        for name, module_path, class_name in modules_to_load:
            try:
                # ðŸ”¥ USA importlib EM VEZ DE __import__
                module = importlib.import_module(module_path)
                class_obj = getattr(module, class_name)
                
                # Instancia
                instance = class_obj(self.event_bus, self.logger, self.config)
                
                # Inicializa se tiver mÃ©todo
                if hasattr(instance, "initialize"):
                    await instance.initialize()
                
                # Guarda
                self._modules[name] = instance
                self.logger.info(f"âœ… {name} carregado")
                
                # Injeta referÃªncias especiais
                if name == "voice_manager":
                    self._voice_manager = instance
                elif name == "news_manager":
                    instance._llm_manager = self._llm_manager
                    
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"âŒ Erro ao carregar {name}: {e}")
                # Continua mesmo com erro
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“¦ {len(self._modules)} mÃ³dulos carregados")
    
    async def _cognitive_loop(self) -> None:
        """Loop principal."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [BRAIN] ðŸš€ Loop cognitivo iniciado")
        
        while self._running:
            try:
                event = await asyncio.wait_for(
                    self._perception_queue.get(),
                    timeout=1.0
                )
                
                self._loop_count += 1
                self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§  [BRAIN] ðŸ”„ Loop #{self._loop_count} - Evento: {event.type}")
                
                await self._process_event(event)
                
            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                continue
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"ðŸ§  [BRAIN] âŒ Erro no loop: {e}")
                await asyncio.sleep(1)
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [BRAIN] Loop finalizado")
    
    async def _process_event(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa um evento especÃ­fico."""
        pass
    
    # ===== HANDLERS =====
    
    async def _on_user_message(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Recebe mensagem do usuÃ¡rio."""
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§  [BRAIN] â­ _on_user_message CHAMADO!")
        
        data = event.data
        text = data.get("text", "")
        user_id = data.get("user", "unknown")
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§  [BRAIN] ðŸ“¥ Mensagem de {user_id}: '{text[:50]}'")
        
        # ===== COMANDO STOP =====
        if text.lower() in ["stop", "eva stop"]:
            voice = self._modules.get("voice_manager")
            if voice:
                await voice.stop_listening()
                await self._say("â¹ï¸ Escuta desativada. Diga 'EVA' para ativar novamente.")
            return
        
        # ===== PROCESSAMENTO =====
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            EventType.PROCESSING_START,
            {
                "message": text,
                "user": user_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="brain"
        )
        
        # Gera resposta
        response = await self._generate_response(text, user_id)
        
        # Fala resposta
        await self._say(response)
        
        # Publica resposta
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            EventType.ASSISTANT_RESPONSE,
            {
                "text": response,
                "user": user_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="brain"
        )
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            EventType.PROCESSING_END,
            {
                "message": text,
                "user": user_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="brain"
        )
    
    async def _say(self, text: str) -> None:
        """Fala uma mensagem."""
        voice = self._modules.get("voice_manager")
        if voice:
            try:
                asyncio.create_task(voice.say(text))
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro ao falar: {e}")
    
    async def _generate_response(self, text: str, user_id: str) -> str:
        """Gera uma resposta usando o pipeline completo."""
        # Tenta usar o ContextManager
        context_manager = self._modules.get("context_manager")
        memory_manager = self._modules.get("memory_manager")
        knowledge_manager = self._modules.get("knowledge_manager")
        planner_manager = self._modules.get("planner_manager")
        reasoning_manager = self._modules.get("reasoning_manager")
        emotion_engine = self._modules.get("emotion_engine")
        prompt_builder = self._modules.get("prompt_builder")
        llm = self._llm_manager
        
        # Pipeline completo
        try:
            # 1. Contexto
            context = await context_manager.process(text, user_id) if context_manager else {"message": text, "user_id": user_id}
            
            # 2. MemÃ³ria
            memory = await memory_manager.process(context) if memory_manager else {}
            
            # 3. Conhecimento
            knowledge = await knowledge_manager.process(context, memory) if knowledge_manager else {}
            
            # 4. Planejamento
            plan = await planner_manager.process(context, memory, knowledge) if planner_manager else {}
            
            # 5. RaciocÃ­nio
            reasoning = await reasoning_manager.process(context, memory, knowledge, plan) if reasoning_manager else {}
            
            # 6. EmoÃ§Ã£o
            emotion = await emotion_engine.process(context, memory, knowledge, plan, reasoning) if emotion_engine else {}
            
            # 7. Prompt
            prompt_data = await prompt_builder.process(context, memory, knowledge, plan, reasoning, emotion) if prompt_builder else None
            prompt = prompt_data.get("prompt", text) if prompt_data else text
            
            # 8. LLM
            if llm:
                response = await llm.generate(prompt)
                
                # Armazena na memÃ³ria de longo prazo
                long_term = self._modules.get("long_term_memory")
                if long_term:
                    user_profile = self._modules.get("user_manager")
                    user_name = user_profile.get_current_user().name if user_profile else user_id
                    await long_term.store_conversation(user_id, user_name, text, response)
                
                return response
            
            return f"Recebi: '{text[:50]}...' (LLM nÃ£o disponÃ­vel)"
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro no pipeline: {e}")
            
            # Fallback: LLM direto
            if self._llm_manager:
                try:
                    return await self._llm_manager.generate(text)
                except:
                    return f"Desculpe, tive um problema: {e}"
            
            return f"Recebi sua mensagem: '{text}'"
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o Brain."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [BRAIN] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        
        # Desliga todos os mÃ³dulos
        for name, module in self._modules.items():
            try:
                if hasattr(module, "shutdown"):
                    await module.shutdown()
                    self.logger.info(f"âœ… {name} desligado")
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"âŒ Erro ao desligar {name}: {e}")
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [BRAIN] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "running": self._running,
            "llm": self._llm_manager is not None,
            "modules": list(self._modules.keys()),
            "total_modules": len(self._modules),
            "loops": self._loop_count
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\consciousness\consciousness_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador central da ConsciÃªncia da EVA."""

import asyncio
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List, Union
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum

from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus
from src.consciousness.decision_engine import DecisionEngine
from src.consciousness.intent_classifier import IntentClassifier
from src.consciousness.intent_router import IntentRouter
from src.consciousness.context_analyzer import ContextAnalyzer
from src.consciousness.priority_arbiter import PriorityArbiter
from src.consciousness.reflection_engine import ReflectionEngine
from src.consciousness.state_orchestrator import StateOrchestrator


class ConsciousnessState(Enum):
    """Estados da ConsciÃªncia."""
    IDLE = "idle"
    ANALYZING = "analyzing"
    DECIDING = "deciding"
    ROUTING = "routing"
    REFLECTING = "reflecting"
    LEARNING = "learning"


class ConsciousnessManager:
    """
    Gerenciador central da ConsciÃªncia da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Receber e analisar eventos
    - Classificar intenÃ§Ãµes
    - Analisar contexto
    - Tomar decisÃµes
    - Rotear para agentes/planners
    - Refletir sobre aÃ§Ãµes passadas
    - Orquestrar estados do sistema
    """
    
    # Atributos de plugin
    __plugin_name__ = "consciousness_manager"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Centro de decisÃ£o da EVA"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["core", "consciousness", "intelligence"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["decision_making", "intent_recognition", "orchestration"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config", "context_manager", "planner_manager"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config, context_manager, planner_manager):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        self.context_manager = context_manager
        self.planner_manager = planner_manager
        
        # Inicializa componentes
        self.decision_engine = DecisionEngine(self)
        self.intent_classifier = IntentClassifier(self)
        self.intent_router = IntentRouter(self)
        self.context_analyzer = ContextAnalyzer(self)
        self.priority_arbiter = PriorityArbiter(self)
        self.reflection_engine = ReflectionEngine(self)
        self.state_orchestrator = StateOrchestrator(self)
        
        # Estado interno
        self._state = ConsciousnessState.IDLE
        self._last_decision: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
        self._decision_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._running = False
        self._reflection_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
        self._reflection_interval = 3600  # 1 hora
        
        # Registra listeners
        self._register_listeners()
        
        self.logger.info("ConsciousnessManager inicializado")
    
    def _register_listeners(self) -> None:
        """Registra ouvintes de eventos."""
        # Eventos de entrada
        self.event_bus.subscribe("user.message", self._on_user_message)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("voice.input", self._on_voice_input)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("command", self._on_command)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("system.event", self._on_system_event)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("sensor.trigger", self._on_sensor_trigger)
        
        # Eventos internos
        self.event_bus.subscribe("consciousness.decide", self._on_decide)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("consciousness.reflect", self._on_reflect)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("consciousness.get_status", self._on_get_status)
        
        # Eventos de resultado
        self.event_bus.subscribe("plan.completed", self._on_plan_completed)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("task.completed", self._on_task_completed)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.response", self._on_agent_response)
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa a ConsciÃªncia."""
        self.logger.info("ConsciousnessManager inicializando...")
        
        self._running = True
        
        # Inicia reflexÃ£o periÃ³dica
        self._reflection_task = asyncio.create_task(self._auto_reflect())
        
        self.logger.info("ConsciousnessManager inicializado")
    
    async def start(self) -> None:
        """Inicia a ConsciÃªncia."""
        self.logger.info("ConsciousnessManager iniciando...")
        
        # Publica que a consciÃªncia estÃ¡ pronta
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "CONSCIOUSNESS_READY",
            {
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "state": self._state.value
            },
            source="consciousness_manager"
        )
        
        self.logger.info("ConsciousnessManager pronto")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga a ConsciÃªncia."""
        self.logger.info("ConsciousnessManager desligando...")
        
        self._running = False
        
        if self._reflection_task:
            self._reflection_task.cancel()
            try:
                await self._reflection_task
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
        
        self.logger.info("ConsciousnessManager desligado")
    
    # ===== EVENTOS DE ENTRADA =====
    
    async def _on_user_message(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa mensagem do usuÃ¡rio."""
        self.logger.debug(f"Mensagem do usuÃ¡rio: {event.payload.get('text', '')[:50]}...")
        self._set_state(ConsciousnessState.ANALYZING)
        
        # 1. Analisa contexto
        context = await self.context_analyzer.analyze(event)
        
        # 2. Classifica intenÃ§Ã£o
        intent = await self.intent_classifier.classify(event.payload.get("text", ""), context)
        
        # 3. Toma decisÃ£o
        decision = await self.decision_engine.decide(intent, context, event)
        
        # 4. Roteia para o destino apropriado
        await self.intent_router.route(decision, context)
        
        # 5. Registra decisÃ£o
        self._record_decision(decision, context)
        
        self._set_state(ConsciousnessState.IDLE)
    
    async def _on_voice_input(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa entrada de voz."""
        # Converte para mensagem de usuÃ¡rio
        text = event.payload.get("text", "")
        await self._on_user_message(
            Event(
                type="user.message",
                payload={"text": text, "source": "voice", "user": event.payload.get("user")},
                source=event.source
            )
        )
    
    async def _on_command(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa comando direto."""
        self.logger.debug(f"Comando recebido: {event.payload.get('command', '')}")
        self._set_state(ConsciousnessState.ANALYZING)
        
        # Comandos sÃ£o de alta prioridade
        command = event.payload.get("command", "")
        params = event.payload.get("params", {})
        
        # Tenta executar diretamente se for um comando do sistema
        if command.startswith("system."):
            await self._handle_system_command(command, params)
        else:
            # Trata como mensagem de usuÃ¡rio com intenÃ§Ã£o de comando
            await self._on_user_message(
                Event(
                    type="user.message",
                    payload={
                        "text": command,
                        "params": params,
                        "user": event.payload.get("user", "system")
                    },
                    source=event.source
                )
            )
        
        self._set_state(ConsciousnessState.IDLE)
    
    async def _on_system_event(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa evento do sistema."""
        event_type = event.payload.get("event_type", "unknown")
        
        # Eventos importantes podem exigir decisÃ£o
        if event_type in ["error", "warning", "critical"]:
            await self._handle_critical_event(event)
    
    async def _on_sensor_trigger(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa trigger de sensor."""
        sensor_id = event.payload.get("sensor_id", "unknown")
        value = event.payload.get("value")
        
        self.logger.debug(f"Sensor {sensor_id}: {value}")
        
        # Verifica se requer aÃ§Ã£o
        if self._requires_action(sensor_id, value):
            await self._on_system_event(
                Event(
                    type="system.event",
                    payload={
                        "event_type": "sensor_trigger",
                        "sensor_id": sensor_id,
                        "value": value
                    },
                    source="consciousness_manager"
                )
            )
    
    # ===== EVENTOS INTERNOS =====
    
    async def _on_decide(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """SolicitaÃ§Ã£o de decisÃ£o."""
        self._set_state(ConsciousnessState.DECIDING)
        
        payload = event.payload
        intent = payload.get("intent")
        context = payload.get("context", {})
        
        decision = await self.decision_engine.decide(intent, context, event)
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "consciousness.decision_made",
            {
                "decision": decision,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="consciousness_manager"
        )
        
        self._set_state(ConsciousnessState.IDLE)
    
    async def _on_reflect(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """SolicitaÃ§Ã£o de reflexÃ£o."""
        await self.reflection_engine.reflect()
    
    async def _on_get_status(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Retorna status da ConsciÃªncia."""
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "consciousness.status",
            {
                "state": self._state.value,
                "last_decision": self._last_decision,
                "decision_count": len(self._decision_history),
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="consciousness_manager"
        )
    
    # ===== EVENTOS DE RESULTADO =====
    
    async def _on_plan_completed(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa conclusÃ£o de plano."""
        self.logger.info(f"Plano concluÃ­do: {event.payload.get('plan_id', '')[:8]}")
        
        # Registra aprendizado
        await self.reflection_engine.learn_from_plan(event.payload)
    
    async def _on_task_completed(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa conclusÃ£o de tarefa."""
        self.logger.debug(f"Tarefa concluÃ­da: {event.payload.get('task_id', '')[:8]}")
    
    async def _on_agent_response(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa resposta de agente."""
        # Pode gerar reflexÃ£o se necessÃ¡rio
        pass
    
    # ===== MÃ‰TODOS AUXILIARES =====
    
    def _set_state(self, state: ConsciousnessState) -> None:
        """Define o estado da consciÃªncia."""
        old_state = self._state
        self._state = state
        
        if old_state != state:
            self.logger.debug(f"Estado da consciÃªncia: {old_state.value} -> {state.value}")
    
    def _record_decision(self, decision: Dict[str, Any], context: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Registra uma decisÃ£o."""
        self._last_decision = decision
        self._decision_history.append({
            "decision": decision,
            "context": context,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
        
        # Limita histÃ³rico
        if len(self._decision_history) > 1000:
            self._decision_history = self._decision_history[-1000:]
    
    def _requires_action(self, sensor_id: str, value: Any) -> bool:
        """Verifica se um sensor requer aÃ§Ã£o."""
        # TODO: Implementar lÃ³gica de decisÃ£o para sensores
        return False
    
    async def _handle_system_command(self, command: str, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Manipula comandos do sistema."""
        if command == "system.shutdown":
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "system.shutdown",
                params,
                source="consciousness_manager"
            )
        elif command == "system.restart":
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "system.restart",
                params,
                source="consciousness_manager"
            )
        elif command == "system.status":
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "system.status.request",
                params,
                source="consciousness_manager"
            )
        else:
            self.logger.warning(f"Comando desconhecido: {command}")
    
    async def _handle_critical_event(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Manipula eventos crÃ­ticos."""
        # Toma decisÃ£o imediata
        decision = await self.decision_engine.decide_emergency(event)
        
        if decision:
            await self.intent_router.route(decision, {})
    
    # ===== REFLEXÃƒO AUTOMÃTICA =====
    
    async def _auto_reflect(self) -> None:
        """Executa reflexÃ£o periÃ³dica."""
        while self._running:
            try:
                await asyncio.sleep(self._reflection_interval)
                
                if self._running:
                    self._set_state(ConsciousnessState.REFLECTING)
                    await self.reflection_engine.reflect()
                    self._set_state(ConsciousnessState.IDLE)
                    
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro na reflexÃ£o automÃ¡tica: {e}")
    
    # ===== MÃ‰TODOS PÃšBLICOS =====
    
    def get_state(self) -> ConsciousnessState:
        """ObtÃ©m o estado atual."""
        return self._state
    
    def get_last_decision(self) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m a Ãºltima decisÃ£o."""
        return self._last_decision
    
    def get_decision_history(self, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m histÃ³rico de decisÃµes."""
        return self._decision_history[-limit:]
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        """Health check da ConsciÃªncia."""
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "state": self._state.value,
            "decisions_made": len(self._decision_history),
            "last_decision": self._last_decision
        }

"""Gerenciamento de contexto da ConsciÃªncia."""

from typing import Any, Dict, Optional
from datetime import datetime


class ContextManager:
    """Gerencia o contexto atual da conversa e interaÃ§Ãµes."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._context: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "current_topic": None,
            "last_interaction": None,
            "participants": [],
            "conversation_history": [],
            "active_goals": [],
            "temporary_state": {}
        }
    
    def set(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
        """Define um valor no contexto."""
        self._context[key] = value
        self._context["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def get(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        """ObtÃ©m um valor do contexto."""
        return self._context.get(key, default)
    
    def update_topic(self, topic: str) -> None:
        """Atualiza o tÃ³pico atual da conversa."""
        self._context["current_topic"] = topic
        self._context["topic_history"] = self._context.get("topic_history", [])
        self._context["topic_history"].append({
            "topic": topic,
            "timestamp": datetime.now()
        })
    
    def add_participant(self, participant: str) -> None:
        """Adiciona um participante Ã  conversa."""
        if participant not in self._context["participants"]:
            self._context["participants"].append(participant)
    
    def clear_temporary(self) -> None:
        """Limpa o estado temporÃ¡rio."""
        self._context["temporary_state"] = {}
    
    def get_context_string(self) -> str:
        """Gera uma string de contexto para IA."""
        # TODO: Gerar contexto formatado para LLM
        return f"TÃ³pico atual: {self._context['current_topic']}"


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\consciousness\context_analyzer.py ====================
"""Analisador de contexto."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional


class ContextAnalyzer:
    """
    Analisa o contexto para auxiliar decisÃµes.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, consciousness_manager):
        self.consciousness = consciousness_manager
        self.logger = consciousness_manager.logger
        self.context_manager = consciousness_manager.context_manager
    
    async def analyze(self, event: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Analisa o contexto atual.
        """
        context = {
            "user": {},
            "environment": {},
            "temporal": {},
            "conversation": {},
            "system": {},
            "event": {}
        }
        
        # 1. Contexto do usuÃ¡rio
        user_context = self.context_manager.user.get_all()
        context["user"] = {
            "current": user_context.get("current_user"),
            "active_users": user_context.get("active_users", [])
        }
        
        # 2. Contexto do ambiente
        env_context = self.context_manager.environment.get_all()
        context["environment"] = {
            "location": env_context.get("location"),
            "room": env_context.get("room"),
            "temperature": env_context.get("temperature"),
            "light_status": self._get_light_status(env_context)
        }
        
        # 3. Contexto temporal
        temporal_context = self.context_manager.temporal.get_all()
        context["temporal"] = {
            "period": temporal_context.get("period"),
            "is_weekend": temporal_context.get("is_weekend"),
            "is_school_day": temporal_context.get("is_school_day"),
            "season": temporal_context.get("season")
        }
        
        # 4. Contexto da conversa
        conv_context = self.context_manager.conversation.get_all()
        context["conversation"] = {
            "topic": conv_context.get("current_topic"),
            "message_count": conv_context.get("message_count", 0),
            "last_messages": conv_context.get("history", [])[-3:]
        }
        
        # 5. Contexto do sistema
        sys_context = self.context_manager.system.get_all()
        context["system"] = {
            "state": sys_context.get("state"),
            "active_modules": sys_context.get("active_modules", [])
        }
        
        # 6. Contexto do evento
        if hasattr(event, 'payload'):
            context["event"] = {
                "source": event.source,
                "type": event.type,
                "payload": event.payload
            }
        
        return context
    
    def _get_light_status(self, env_context: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """ObtÃ©m status das luzes."""
        lights = env_context.get("lights", {})
        if not lights:
            return "unknown"
        
        on_count = sum(1 for l in lights.values() if l.get("on", False))
        total = len(lights)
        
        if on_count == 0:
            return "all_off"
        elif on_count == total:
            return "all_on"
        else:
            return f"{on_count}/{total}_on"


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\consciousness\decision_engine.py ====================
"""Motor de decisÃ£o da ConsciÃªncia."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum


class DecisionType(Enum):
    """Tipos de decisÃ£o."""
    ROUTE_TO_AGENT = "route_to_agent"
    CREATE_PLAN = "create_plan"
    EXECUTE_TOOL = "execute_tool"
    RESPOND = "respond"
    ASK_CLARIFICATION = "ask_clarification"
    DEFER = "defer"
    EMERGENCY = "emergency"
    REFLECT = "reflect"


class Decision:
    """Uma decisÃ£o tomada pela ConsciÃªncia."""
    
    def __init__(self, decision_type: DecisionType, target: str = "",
                 confidence: float = 0.5, reason: str = "",
                 data: Dict[str, Any] = None):
        self.type = decision_type
        self.target = target
        self.confidence = confidence
        self.reason = reason
        self.data = data or {}
        self.timestamp = datetime.now()
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Converte para dicionÃ¡rio."""
        return {
            "type": self.type.value,
            "target": self.target,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "reason": self.reason,
            "data": self.data,
            "timestamp": self.timestamp.isoformat()
        }


class DecisionEngine:
    """
    Motor de decisÃ£o da ConsciÃªncia.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Receber intenÃ§Ãµes e contexto
    - Avaliar opÃ§Ãµes
    - Tomar decisÃµes
    - Justificar decisÃµes
    """
    
    def __init__(self, consciousness_manager):
        self.consciousness = consciousness_manager
        self.logger = consciousness_manager.logger
        self.event_bus = consciousness_manager.event_bus
    
    async def decide(self, intent: Dict[str, Any], context: Dict[str, Any], 
                     event: Optional[Any] = None) -> Decision:
        """
        Toma uma decisÃ£o baseada na intenÃ§Ã£o e contexto.
        """
        intent_type = intent.get("type", "unknown")
        confidence = intent.get("confidence", 0.5)
        
        self.logger.debug(f"Decidindo para intenÃ§Ã£o: {intent_type}")
        
        # 1. EmergÃªncia - prioridade mÃ¡xima
        if intent_type == "emergency":
            return await self._decide_emergency(intent, context)
        
        # 2. Comando direto
        if intent_type == "command":
            return await self._decide_command(intent, context)
        
        # 3. Pergunta
        if intent_type == "question":
            return await self._decide_question(intent, context)
        
        # 4. Planejamento
        if intent_type == "planning":
            return await self._decide_planning(intent, context)
        
        # 5. Conversa
        if intent_type == "conversation":
            return await self._decide_conversation(intent, context)
        
        # 6. Aprendizado
        if intent_type == "learning":
            return await self._decide_learning(intent, context)
        
        # 7. Desconhecido - pede clarificaÃ§Ã£o
        return Decision(
            decision_type=DecisionType.ASK_CLARIFICATION,
            reason=f"IntenÃ§Ã£o desconhecida: {intent_type}",
            confidence=0.3
        )
    
    async def _decide_emergency(self, intent: Dict[str, Any], 
                                context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Decision:
        """DecisÃ£o para emergÃªncias."""
        return Decision(
            decision_type=DecisionType.EMERGENCY,
            target="security_agent",
            confidence=1.0,
            reason="EmergÃªncia detectada",
            data=intent.get("data", {})
        )
    
    async def _decide_command(self, intent: Dict[str, Any], 
                              context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Decision:
        """DecisÃ£o para comandos."""
        command = intent.get("data", {}).get("command", "")
        
        # Comandos do sistema
        if command.startswith("system."):
            return Decision(
                decision_type=DecisionType.EXECUTE_TOOL,
                target="system_tool",
                confidence=0.9,
                reason=f"Comando do sistema: {command}",
                data={"command": command, "params": intent.get("data", {}).get("params", {})}
            )
        
        # Comandos de automaÃ§Ã£o
        if "luz" in command or "temperatura" in command or "dispositivo" in command:
            return Decision(
                decision_type=DecisionType.ROUTE_TO_AGENT,
                target="home_automation_agent",
                confidence=0.9,
                reason=f"Comando de automaÃ§Ã£o: {command}",
                data={"command": command}
            )
        
        # Comandos de informaÃ§Ã£o
        if "status" in command or "informaÃ§Ã£o" in command:
            return Decision(
                decision_type=DecisionType.ROUTE_TO_AGENT,
                target="information_agent",
                confidence=0.9,
                reason=f"Comando de informaÃ§Ã£o: {command}",
                data={"command": command}
            )
        
        # Comando genÃ©rico
        return Decision(
            decision_type=DecisionType.ROUTE_TO_AGENT,
            target="general_agent",
            confidence=0.7,
            reason=f"Comando genÃ©rico: {command}",
            data={"command": command}
        )
    
    async def _decide_question(self, intent: Dict[str, Any], 
                               context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Decision:
        """DecisÃ£o para perguntas."""
        question = intent.get("data", {}).get("question", "")
        
        # Perguntas de conhecimento
        if "o que Ã©" in question.lower() or "quem Ã©" in question.lower():
            return Decision(
                decision_type=DecisionType.ROUTE_TO_AGENT,
                target="knowledge_agent",
                confidence=0.9,
                reason=f"Pergunta de conhecimento: {question[:50]}...",
                data={"question": question}
            )
        
        # Perguntas de memÃ³ria
        if "lembra" in question.lower() or "quando" in question.lower():
            return Decision(
                decision_type=DecisionType.ROUTE_TO_AGENT,
                target="memory_agent",
                confidence=0.8,
                reason=f"Pergunta sobre memÃ³ria: {question[:50]}...",
                data={"question": question}
            )
        
        # Perguntas de planejamento
        if "como" in question.lower() or "planejar" in question.lower():
            return Decision(
                decision_type=DecisionType.CREATE_PLAN,
                target="planner",
                confidence=0.8,
                reason=f"Pergunta de planejamento: {question[:50]}...",
                data={"question": question}
            )
        
        # Pergunta genÃ©rica
        return Decision(
            decision_type=DecisionType.ROUTE_TO_AGENT,
            target="general_agent",
            confidence=0.6,
            reason=f"Pergunta genÃ©rica: {question[:50]}...",
            data={"question": question}
        )
    
    async def _decide_planning(self, intent: Dict[str, Any], 
                               context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Decision:
        """DecisÃ£o para planejamento."""
        return Decision(
            decision_type=DecisionType.CREATE_PLAN,
            target="planner",
            confidence=0.9,
            reason="IntenÃ§Ã£o de planejamento detectada",
            data=intent.get("data", {})
        )
    
    async def _decide_conversation(self, intent: Dict[str, Any], 
                                   context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Decision:
        """DecisÃ£o para conversaÃ§Ã£o."""
        return Decision(
            decision_type=DecisionType.ROUTE_TO_AGENT,
            target="general_agent",
            confidence=0.7,
            reason="ConversaÃ§Ã£o geral",
            data=intent.get("data", {})
        )
    
    async def _decide_learning(self, intent: Dict[str, Any], 
                               context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Decision:
        """DecisÃ£o para aprendizado."""
        return Decision(
            decision_type=DecisionType.ROUTE_TO_AGENT,
            target="learning_agent",
            confidence=0.9,
            reason="IntenÃ§Ã£o de aprendizado detectada",
            data=intent.get("data", {})
        )
    
    async def decide_emergency(self, event: Any) -> Optional[Decision]:
        """
        DecisÃ£o rÃ¡pida para emergÃªncias.
        """
        return Decision(
            decision_type=DecisionType.EMERGENCY,
            target="security_agent",
            confidence=1.0,
            reason="Evento crÃ­tico",
            data={"event": event.payload if hasattr(event, 'payload') else {}}
        )


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\consciousness\intent_classifier.py ====================
"""Classificador de intenÃ§Ãµes."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
import re


class IntentClassifier:
    """
    Classifica intenÃ§Ãµes a partir de texto e contexto.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, consciousness_manager):
        self.consciousness = consciousness_manager
        self.logger = consciousness_manager.logger
        
        # PadrÃµes de intenÃ§Ã£o
        self._patterns = {
            "emergency": [
                r"urgente", r"emergÃªncia", r"perigo", r"fogo", r"acidente",
                r"ajuda", r"socorro", r"rÃ¡pido", r"agora"
            ],
            "command": [
                r"faÃ§a", r"execute", r"ligue", r"desligue", r"abra", r"feche",
                r"ative", r"desative", r"mude", r"configure", r"inicie", r"pare"
            ],
            "question": [
                r"o que", r"quem", r"quando", r"onde", r"como", r"por que",
                r"qual", r"quanto", r"pode", r"poderia", r"seria"
            ],
            "planning": [
                r"planejar", r"planeje", r"organizar", r"organize",
                r"projeto", r"construir", r"criar", r"desenvolver"
            ],
            "learning": [
                r"aprender", r"ensinar", r"explicar", r"estudar",
                r"pesquisar", r"descobrir", r"entender"
            ],
            "conversation": [
                r"oi", r"olÃ¡", r"e aÃ­", r"tudo bem", r"como vai",
                r"bom dia", r"boa tarde", r"boa noite"
            ]
        }
    
    async def classify(self, text: str, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Classifica a intenÃ§Ã£o de um texto.
        """
        text_lower = text.lower()
        
        # Calcula scores para cada intenÃ§Ã£o
        scores = {}
        for intent, patterns in self._patterns.items():
            score = 0
            for pattern in patterns:
                if pattern in text_lower:
                    score += 1
            scores[intent] = score
        
        # Encontra a intenÃ§Ã£o com maior score
        if scores:
            max_score = max(scores.values())
            if max_score > 0:
                # Pode ter mÃºltiplas intenÃ§Ãµes
                top_intents = [
                    (intent, score / len(self._patterns[intent]))
                    for intent, score in scores.items()
                    if score > 0
                ]
                
                # Ordena por score
                top_intents.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
                
                primary_intent = top_intents[0][0]
                confidence = min(1.0, top_intents[0][1] * 2)
                
                # Ajusta confianÃ§a baseada no comprimento do texto
                if len(text) < 3:
                    confidence = min(confidence, 0.5)
                
                # Ajusta confianÃ§a baseada no contexto
                context_intent = context.get("intent", {}).get("current")
                if context_intent and context_intent == primary_intent:
                    confidence = min(1.0, confidence + 0.2)
                
                return {
                    "type": primary_intent,
                    "confidence": confidence,
                    "all_intents": top_intents,
                    "text": text
                }
        
        # Se nÃ£o classificou, assume conversaÃ§Ã£o
        return {
            "type": "conversation",
            "confidence": 0.3,
            "all_intents": [],
            "text": text
        }
    
    def _get_context_intent(self, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m a intenÃ§Ã£o atual do contexto."""
        return context.get("conversation", {}).get("current_intent")


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\consciousness\intent_router.py ====================
"""Roteador de intenÃ§Ãµes."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime

from src.kernel.events import Event
from src.consciousness.decision_engine import Decision, DecisionType


class IntentRouter:
    """
    Roteia intenÃ§Ãµes para o destino apropriado.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, consciousness_manager):
        self.consciousness = consciousness_manager
        self.logger = consciousness_manager.logger
        self.event_bus = consciousness_manager.event_bus
    
    async def route(self, decision: Decision, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """
        Roteia uma decisÃ£o para o destino apropriado.
        """
        self.logger.debug(f"Roteando decisÃ£o: {decision.type.value} -> {decision.target}")
        
        if decision.type == DecisionType.ROUTE_TO_AGENT:
            await self._route_to_agent(decision, context)
        
        elif decision.type == DecisionType.CREATE_PLAN:
            await self._route_to_planner(decision, context)
        
        elif decision.type == DecisionType.EXECUTE_TOOL:
            await self._route_to_tool(decision, context)
        
        elif decision.type == DecisionType.RESPOND:
            await self._route_to_response(decision, context)
        
        elif decision.type == DecisionType.ASK_CLARIFICATION:
            await self._ask_clarification(decision, context)
        
        elif decision.type == DecisionType.EMERGENCY:
            await self._route_emergency(decision, context)
        
        elif decision.type == DecisionType.REFLECT:
            await self._route_to_reflection(decision, context)
        
        else:
            self.logger.warning(f"Tipo de decisÃ£o nÃ£o reconhecido: {decision.type}")
    
    async def _route_to_agent(self, decision: Decision, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Roteia para um agente."""
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            f"agent.{decision.target}",
            {
                "decision": decision.to_dict(),
                "context": context,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="consciousness"
        )
        
        self.logger.info(f"Roteado para agente: {decision.target}")
    
    async def _route_to_planner(self, decision: Decision, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Roteia para o Planner."""
        goal_data = {
            "title": decision.data.get("question", "Objetivo"),
            "description": decision.data.get("description", decision.reason),
            "goal_type": "oneshot",
            "priority": 5,
            "created_by": "consciousness",
            "context": decision.data
        }
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "planner.create_goal",
            {
                "goal": goal_data,
                "context": context,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="consciousness"
        )
        
        self.logger.info(f"Roteado para Planner: {goal_data['title']}")
    
    async def _route_to_tool(self, decision: Decision, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Roteia para uma ferramenta."""
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "tool.execute",
            {
                "tool": decision.target,
                "params": decision.data.get("params", {}),
                "caller": "consciousness",
                "context": context,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="consciousness"
        )
        
        self.logger.info(f"Roteado para ferramenta: {decision.target}")
    
    async def _route_to_response(self, decision: Decision, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Roteia para resposta direta."""
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "consciousness.response",
            {
                "response": decision.data.get("response", ""),
                "context": context,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="consciousness"
        )
    
    async def _ask_clarification(self, decision: Decision, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Pede clarificaÃ§Ã£o ao usuÃ¡rio."""
        message = decision.data.get("message", "NÃ£o entendi. Pode reformular?")
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "user.message.response",
            {
                "text": message,
                "context": context,
                "requesting_clarification": True,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="consciousness"
        )
        
        self.logger.info(f"Solicitando clarificaÃ§Ã£o: {message}")
    
    async def _route_emergency(self, decision: Decision, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Roteia emergÃªncia."""
        # Prioridade mÃ¡xima
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "emergency.trigger",
            {
                "decision": decision.to_dict(),
                "context": context,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="consciousness",
            priority=0  # CRITICAL
        )
        
        self.logger.warning(f"EMERGÃŠNCIA roteada: {decision.reason}")
    
    async def _route_to_reflection(self, decision: Decision, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Roteia para reflexÃ£o."""
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "consciousness.reflect",
            {
                "decision": decision.to_dict(),
                "context": context,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="consciousness"
        )


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\consciousness\priority_arbiter.py ====================
"""Ãrbitro de prioridades."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime


class PriorityArbiter:
    """
    Arbitra prioridades entre mÃºltiplas demandas.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, consciousness_manager):
        self.consciousness = consciousness_manager
        self.logger = consciousness_manager.logger
    
    def arbitrate(self, demands: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Arbitra entre mÃºltiplas demandas.
        Retorna a demanda de maior prioridade.
        """
        if not demands:
            return {}
        
        if len(demands) == 1:
            return demands[0]
        
        # Calcula score para cada demanda
        scored = []
        for demand in demands:
            score = self._calculate_score(demand)
            scored.append((score, demand))
        
        # Ordena por score (maior primeiro)
        scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
        
        return scored[0][1]
    
    def _calculate_score(self, demand: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
        """
        Calcula score de prioridade.
        """
        score = 0.0
        
        # 1. Prioridade explÃ­cita
        priority = demand.get("priority", 5)
        score += (10 - priority) * 0.5
        
        # 2. UrgÃªncia
        urgency = demand.get("urgency", 0)
        score += urgency * 0.3
        
        # 3. Fonte (usuÃ¡rio tem mais peso)
        source = demand.get("source", "system")
        if source == "user":
            score += 2.0
        elif source == "admin":
            score += 3.0
        elif source == "system":
            score += 1.0
        
        # 4. Tempo de espera
        created_at = demand.get("created_at")
        if created_at:
            wait_time = (datetime.now() - datetime.fromisoformat(created_at)).total_seconds()
            score += min(wait_time / 60, 5.0)  # Max 5 pontos por espera
        
        # 5. Tipo de demanda
        demand_type = demand.get("type", "unknown")
        type_weights = {
            "emergency": 10.0,
            "command": 8.0,
            "planning": 6.0,
            "question": 4.0,
            "conversation": 2.0
        }
        score += type_weights.get(demand_type, 1.0)
        
        return score

"""Motor de reflexÃ£o da ConsciÃªncia."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime


class ReflectionEngine:
    """
    Motor de reflexÃ£o - auto-anÃ¡lise e aprendizado.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Analisar decisÃµes passadas
    - Identificar padrÃµes
    - Aprender com erros
    - Sugerir melhorias
    """
    
    def __init__(self, consciousness_manager):
        self.consciousness = consciousness_manager
        self.logger = consciousness_manager.logger
        self.event_bus = consciousness_manager.event_bus
        
        self._reflection_count = 0
        self._insights: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    
    async def reflect(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executa um ciclo de reflexÃ£o.
        """
        self.logger.info("Iniciando ciclo de reflexÃ£o...")
        
        insights = []
        
        # 1. Analisa decisÃµes recentes
        decisions = self.consciousness.get_decision_history(50)
        decision_insights = self._analyze_decisions(decisions)
        if decision_insights:
            insights.extend(decision_insights)
        
        # 2. Analisa padrÃµes de intenÃ§Ã£o
        intent_insights = self._analyze_intent_patterns(decisions)
        if intent_insights:
            insights.extend(intent_insights)
        
        # 3. Analisa eficiÃªncia
        efficiency_insights = self._analyze_efficiency(decisions)
        if efficiency_insights:
            insights.extend(efficiency_insights)
        
        # 4. Gera recomendaÃ§Ãµes
        recommendations = self._generate_recommendations(insights)
        
        self._reflection_count += 1
        
        # Armazena insights
        for insight in insights:
            insight["reflection_id"] = self._reflection_count
            insight["timestamp"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
            self._insights.append(insight)
        
        # Limita histÃ³rico
        if len(self._insights) > 1000:
            self._insights = self._insights[-1000:]
        
        result = {
            "reflection_id": self._reflection_count,
            "insights": insights,
            "recommendations": recommendations,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        # Publica resultado
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "consciousness.reflection_complete",
            result,
            source="reflection_engine"
        )
        
        self.logger.info(f"Ciclo de reflexÃ£o concluÃ­do: {len(insights)} insights")
        
        return result
    
    def _analyze_decisions(self, decisions: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Analisa decisÃµes passadas."""
        insights = []
        
        if not decisions:
            return insights
        
        # Conta tipos de decisÃ£o
        types = {}
        for d in decisions:
            decision = d.get("decision", {})
            decision_type = decision.get("type", "unknown")
            types[decision_type] = types.get(decision_type, 0) + 1
        
        if types:
            most_common = max(types.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])
            insights.append({
                "type": "decision_pattern",
                "message": f"Tipo de decisÃ£o mais comum: {most_common[0]} ({most_common[1]} vezes)",
                "data": types
            })
        
        # Identifica erros/baixa confianÃ§a
        low_confidence = [
            d for d in decisions
            if d.get("decision", {}).get("confidence", 1.0) < 0.5
        ]
        
        if low_confidence:
            insights.append({
                "type": "low_confidence",
                "message": f"{len(low_confidence)} decisÃµes com baixa confianÃ§a",
                "data": {
                    "count": len(low_confidence),
                    "examples": [
                        d.get("decision", {}).get("reason", "")
                        for d in low_confidence[:3]
                    ]
                }
            })
        
        return insights
    
    def _analyze_intent_patterns(self, decisions: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Analisa padrÃµes de intenÃ§Ã£o."""
        insights = []
        
        if not decisions:
            return insights
        
        # Conta intenÃ§Ãµes
        intents = {}
        for d in decisions:
            context = d.get("context", {})
            conversation = context.get("conversation", {})
            intent = conversation.get("intent", {}).get("type", "unknown")
            intents[intent] = intents.get(intent, 0) + 1
        
        if intents and len(intents) > 1:
            most_common = max(intents.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])
            insights.append({
                "type": "intent_pattern",
                "message": f"IntenÃ§Ã£o mais comum: {most_common[0]} ({most_common[1]} vezes)",
                "data": intents
            })
        
        return insights
    
    def _analyze_efficiency(self, decisions: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Analisa eficiÃªncia das decisÃµes."""
        insights = []
        
        if not decisions:
            return insights
        
        # Calcula tempo mÃ©dio entre decisÃµes
        timestamps = []
        for d in decisions:
            ts = d.get("timestamp")
            if ts:
                try:
                    timestamps.append(datetime.fromisoformat(ts))
                except:
                    pass
        
        if len(timestamps) > 1:
            avg_gap = sum(
                (timestamps[i] - timestamps[i-1]).total_seconds()
                for i in range(1, len(timestamps))
            ) / (len(timestamps) - 1)
            
            if avg_gap > 60:
                insights.append({
                    "type": "efficiency",
                    "message": f"Tempo mÃ©dio entre decisÃµes: {avg_gap:.1f}s",
                    "data": {"avg_gap": avg_gap}
                })
        
        return insights
    
    def _generate_recommendations(self, insights: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[str]:
        """Gera recomendaÃ§Ãµes baseadas nos insights."""
        recommendations = []
        
        for insight in insights:
            if insight.get("type") == "low_confidence":
                recommendations.append(
                    "Considere melhorar o classificador de intenÃ§Ãµes para aumentar confianÃ§a"
                )
            
            if insight.get("type") == "efficiency":
                recommendations.append(
                    "Considere otimizar o tempo de resposta para decisÃµes"
                )
        
        return recommendations
    
    async def learn_from_plan(self, plan_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """
        Aprende com um plano concluÃ­do.
        """
        self.logger.info(f"Aprendendo com plano: {plan_data.get('plan_id', '')[:8]}")
        
        # TODO: Extrair aprendizado do plano
        pass

"""Orquestrador de estados do sistema."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime

from src.core.state_manager import SystemState


class StateOrchestrator:
    """
    Orquestra os estados do sistema.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, consciousness_manager):
        self.consciousness = consciousness_manager
        self.logger = consciousness_manager.logger
        self.state_manager = consciousness_manager.context_manager.system._data
    
    async def transition_to(self, new_state: SystemState, reason: str = "",
                            data: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> bool:
        """
        Transiciona para um novo estado.
        """
        # Valida transiÃ§Ã£o
        if not self._can_transition(new_state):
            self.logger.warning(f"TransiÃ§Ã£o invÃ¡lida: {self._get_current_state()} -> {new_state}")
            return False
        
        # Executa transiÃ§Ã£o
        old_state = self._get_current_state()
        
        # Notifica sistema
        await self.consciousness.event_bus.publish_async(
            "system.state.pre_transition",
            {
                "from": old_state,
                "to": new_state,
                "reason": reason,
                "data": data
            },
            source="consciousness"
        )
        
        # Muda estado
        self.state_manager["state"] = new_state.value
        self.state_manager["last_transition"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
        
        self.logger.info(f"Estado alterado: {old_state} -> {new_state} ({reason})")
        
        # Notifica sistema
        await self.consciousness.event_bus.publish_async(
            "system.state.transitioned",
            {
                "from": old_state,
                "to": new_state,
                "reason": reason,
                "data": data,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="consciousness"
        )
        
        return True
    
    def _can_transition(self, new_state: SystemState) -> bool:
        """Verifica se a transiÃ§Ã£o Ã© vÃ¡lida."""
        current = self._get_current_state()
        
        # Regras de transiÃ§Ã£o
        valid = {
            "IDLE": ["LISTENING", "THINKING", "UPDATING", "MAINTENANCE"],
            "LISTENING": ["THINKING", "IDLE"],
            "THINKING": ["PLANNING", "LISTENING"],
            "PLANNING": ["EXECUTING", "THINKING"],
            "EXECUTING": ["LEARNING", "LISTENING"],
            "LEARNING": ["IDLE", "LISTENING"],
            "UPDATING": ["IDLE"],
            "MAINTENANCE": ["IDLE"],
            "ERROR": ["RECOVERING"],
            "RECOVERING": ["IDLE"]
        }
        
        current_name = current if isinstance(current, str) else current.value
        new_name = new_state.value
        
        return new_name in valid.get(current_name, [])
    
    def _get_current_state(self) -> str:
        """ObtÃ©m o estado atual."""
        return self.state_manager.get("state", "IDLE")
    
    async def handle_error(self, error: str, source: str = "system") -> None:
        """
        Manipula um erro, transicionando para ERROR se necessÃ¡rio.
        """
        current = self._get_current_state()
        
        if current != "ERROR":
            await self.transition_to(
                SystemState.ERROR,
                reason=f"Erro de {source}: {error[:100]}",
                data={"error": error, "source": source}
            )
    
    async def recover(self) -> bool:
        """
        Tenta se recuperar de um estado de erro.
        """
        current = self._get_current_state()
        
        if current != "ERROR":
            return False
        
        await self.transition_to(
            SystemState.RECOVERING,
            reason="Iniciando recuperaÃ§Ã£o"
        )
        
        # TODO: Executar recuperaÃ§Ã£o
        
        await self.transition_to(
            SystemState.IDLE,
            reason="RecuperaÃ§Ã£o concluÃ­da"
        )
        
        return True

"""Agregador de contexto."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime


class ContextAggregator:
    """
    Agrega todas as camadas de contexto em um Ãºnico objeto.
    Prepara o contexto para uso por IA, agentes e ferramentas.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, context_manager):
        self.context_manager = context_manager
    
    def aggregate(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Agrega todo o contexto em um Ãºnico objeto estruturado.
        
        Retorna:
        {
            "user": {...},
            "environment": {...},
            "conversation": {...},
            "temporal": {...},
            "system": {...},
            "summary": "...",  # Texto resumido para IA
            "timestamp": "..."
        }
        """
        # Coleta de todas as camadas
        user = self.context_manager.user.get_all()
        environment = self.context_manager.environment.get_all()
        conversation = self.context_manager.conversation.get_all()
        temporal = self.context_manager.temporal.get_all()
        system = self.context_manager.system.get_all()
        
        # ConstrÃ³i o objeto agregado
        aggregated = {
            "user": self._extract_user_summary(user),
            "environment": self._extract_environment_summary(environment),
            "conversation": self._extract_conversation_summary(conversation),
            "temporal": self._extract_temporal_summary(temporal),
            "system": self._extract_system_summary(system),
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        # Gera texto resumido para IA
        aggregated["summary"] = self._generate_summary(aggregated)
        
        return aggregated
    
    def _extract_user_summary(self, user: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Extrai resumo do usuÃ¡rio."""
        return {
            "current": user.get("current_user"),
            "role": "admin" if user.get("current_user") == "alberto" else "user",
            "preferences": user.get("users", {}).get(
                user.get("current_user", ""), {}
            ).get("preferences", {}),
            "active_users": user.get("active_users", [])
        }
    
    def _extract_environment_summary(self, env: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Extrai resumo do ambiente."""
        return {
            "location": env.get("location", "Casa"),
            "room": env.get("room"),
            "temperature": env.get("temperature", 0.0),
            "light_status": self._get_light_status(env),
            "devices": len(env.get("devices", {})),
            "sensors": len(env.get("sensors", {}))
        }
    
    def _get_light_status(self, env: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """ObtÃ©m resumo do status das luzes."""
        lights = env.get("lights", {})
        if not lights:
            return "desconhecido"
        
        on_count = sum(1 for l in lights.values() if l.get("on", False))
        total = len(lights)
        
        if on_count == 0:
            return "todas apagadas"
        elif on_count == total:
            return "todas acesas"
        else:
            return f"{on_count}/{total} acesas"
    
    def _extract_conversation_summary(self, conv: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Extrai resumo da conversa."""
        return {
            "topic": conv.get("current_topic"),
            "intent": conv.get("detected_intent"),
            "message_count": conv.get("message_count", 0),
            "last_messages": conv.get("history", [])[-3:] if conv.get("history") else []
        }
    
    def _extract_temporal_summary(self, temporal: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Extrai resumo temporal."""
        return {
            "time": temporal.get("time"),
            "date": temporal.get("date"),
            "period": temporal.get("period"),
            "day": temporal.get("day_name"),
            "is_weekend": temporal.get("is_weekend", False),
            "is_school_day": temporal.get("is_school_day", False),
            "season": temporal.get("season")
        }
    
    def _extract_system_summary(self, system: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Extrai resumo do sistema."""
        return {
            "state": system.get("state", "UNKNOWN"),
            "uptime": system.get("uptime", 0),
            "resources": {
                "cpu": system.get("cpu_usage", 0),
                "memory": system.get("memory_usage", 0),
                "disk": system.get("disk_usage", 0)
            },
            "active_modules": system.get("active_modules", [])
        }
    
    def _generate_summary(self, aggregated: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """
        Gera um texto resumido para uso em IA.
        """
        parts = []
        
        # UsuÃ¡rio
        user = aggregated["user"]
        if user.get("current"):
            parts.append(f"UsuÃ¡rio: {user['current']} ({user.get('role', 'guest')})")
        
        # Temporal
        temporal = aggregated["temporal"]
        parts.append(f"HorÃ¡rio: {temporal.get('time')} - {temporal.get('period')}")
        parts.append(f"Dia: {temporal.get('day')}")
        if temporal.get("is_weekend"):
            parts.append("(Fim de semana)")
        if temporal.get("is_school_day"):
            parts.append("(Dia de escola)")
        
        # Ambiente
        env = aggregated["environment"]
        if env.get("room"):
            parts.append(f"Local: {env['room']}")
        if env.get("temperature"):
            parts.append(f"Temperatura: {env['temperature']:.1f}Â°C")
        if env.get("light_status"):
            parts.append(f"Luzes: {env['light_status']}")
        
        # Conversa
        conv = aggregated["conversation"]
        if conv.get("topic"):
            parts.append(f"TÃ³pico: {conv['topic']}")
        if conv.get("intent"):
            parts.append(f"IntenÃ§Ã£o: {conv['intent']}")
        
        # Sistema
        system = aggregated["system"]
        parts.append(f"Sistema: {system.get('state')}")
        
        return "\n".join(parts)
    
    def to_string(self) -> str:
        """Gera uma string de contexto para uso em LLM."""
        aggregated = self.aggregate()
        return aggregated.get("summary", "")
