"""Gerenciador de contexto - FASE 1 do pipeline cognitivo."""

import re
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from src.core.event_types import EventType
from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus


@dataclass
class ContextData:
    """Dados de contexto processados."""
    message: str
    user_id: str = "unknown"
    session_id: str = "default"
    intent: str = "unknown"
    entities: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    query_type: str = "general"
    language: str = "pt-BR"
    timestamp: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())
    raw_metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


class ContextManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de Contexto - FASE 1 do pipeline cognitivo.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Extrair intenÃ§Ã£o da mensagem
    - Identificar entidades (nomes, datas, lugares)
    - Classificar tipo de pergunta
    - Preparar contexto para prÃ³ximas fases
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self._running = False
        
        # PadrÃµes para extraÃ§Ã£o
        self._intent_patterns = {
            "question": [
                r"\?",
                r"o que",
                r"quem",
                r"quando",
                r"onde",
                r"como",
                r"por que",
                r"qual"
            ],
            "command": [
                r"faÃ§a",
                r"execute",
                r"ligue",
                r"desligue",
                r"abra",
                r"feche",
                r"ative",
                r"desative"
            ],
            "planning": [
                r"planejar",
                r"planeje",
                r"organizar",
                r"organize",
                r"projeto",
                r"construir"
            ],
            "learning": [
                r"aprender",
                r"ensinar",
                r"estudar",
                r"pesquisar",
                r"explicar"
            ]
        }
        
        self._entity_patterns = {
            "name": r"(?:meu nome Ã©|chamo[- ]me|sou)\s+(\w+)",
            "date": r"(\d{2}[/-]\d{2}[/-]\d{4}|\d{4}[/-]\d{2}[/-]\d{2})",
            "time": r"(\d{2}:\d{2})",
            "number": r"\b(\d+)\b"
        }
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o ContextManager."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [ContextManager] Inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [ContextManager] Inicializado")
    
    async def process(self, message: str, user_id: str = "unknown", 
                     session_id: str = "default") -> ContextData:
        """
        Processa uma mensagem e extrai contexto.
        
        Args:
            message: Mensagem do usuÃ¡rio
            user_id: ID do usuÃ¡rio
            session_id: ID da sessÃ£o
            
        Returns:
            ContextData com contexto processado
        """
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§  [ContextManager] Processando: '{message[:50]}...'")
        
        # 1. Extrai intenÃ§Ã£o
        intent = self._extract_intent(message)
        
        # 2. Extrai entidades
        entities = self._extract_entities(message)
        
        # 3. Classifica tipo
        query_type = self._classify_type(message, intent)
        
        # 4. Monta contexto
        context = ContextData(
            message=message,
            user_id=user_id,
            session_id=session_id,
            intent=intent,
            entities=entities,
            query_type=query_type,
            timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat()
        )
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§  [ContextManager] IntenÃ§Ã£o: {intent}, Tipo: {query_type}")
        
        # Publica evento
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            EventType.CONTEXT_READY,
            {
                "context": context.__dict__,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="context_manager"
        )
        
        return context
    
    def _extract_intent(self, message: str) -> str:
        """Extrai intenÃ§Ã£o da mensagem."""
        message_lower = message.lower()
        
        for intent, patterns in self._intent_patterns.items():
            for pattern in patterns:
                if pattern in message_lower:
                    return intent
        
        return "conversation"
    
    def _extract_entities(self, message: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Extrai entidades da mensagem."""
        entities = {}
        
        for entity_type, pattern in self._entity_patterns.items():
            match = re.search(pattern, message, re.IGNORECASE)
            if match:
                entities[entity_type] = match.group(1)
        
        return entities
    
    def _classify_type(self, message: str, intent: str) -> str:
        """Classifica o tipo de pergunta/comando."""
        if intent == "question":
            if "?" in message:
                return "direct_question"
            return "indirect_question"
        elif intent == "command":
            return "direct_command"
        elif intent == "planning":
            return "planning_request"
        elif intent == "learning":
            return "learning_request"
        else:
            return "general"
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o ContextManager."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [ContextManager] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [ContextManager] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        """Health check."""
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "intent_patterns": len(self._intent_patterns),
            "entity_patterns": len(self._entity_patterns)
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\context\conversation_context.py ====================
"""Contexto da conversa atual."""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime
from dataclasses import dataclass, field


@dataclass
class Message:
    """Uma mensagem na conversa."""
    sender: str
    content: str
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


class ConversationContext:
    """
    Contexto da conversa atual.
    
    MantÃ©m:
    - HistÃ³rico recente
    - Assunto atual
    - IntenÃ§Ã£o detectada
    - ReferÃªncias ("isso", "ele", "aquele")
    - TÃ³picos anteriores
    """
    
    def __init__(self, max_history: int = 50):
        self.max_history = max_history
        self._messages: List[Message] = []
        self._data: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "current_topic": None,
            "previous_topics": [],
            "detected_intent": None,
            "entities": {},
            "referents": {},  # "isso" -> "o que foi mencionado"
            "last_update": None
        }
    
    def add_message(self, sender: str, content: str, metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> None:
        """Adiciona uma mensagem ao histÃ³rico."""
        message = Message(
            sender=sender,
            content=content,
            metadata=metadata or {}
        )
        self._messages.append(message)
        
        # Limita histÃ³rico
        if len(self._messages) > self.max_history:
            self._messages = self._messages[-self.max_history:]
        
        # Atualiza metadados
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
        
        # Detecta tÃ³pico (simples)
        self._detect_topic(content)
    
    def get_history(self, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m histÃ³rico recente."""
        messages = self._messages[-limit:] if limit > 0 else self._messages
        return [
            {
                "sender": m.sender,
                "content": m.content,
                "timestamp": m.timestamp.isoformat(),
                "metadata": m.metadata
            }
            for m in messages
        ]
    
    def get_last_n_messages(self, n: int = 5) -> List[Message]:
        """ObtÃ©m as Ãºltimas N mensagens."""
        return self._messages[-n:] if n > 0 else self._messages
    
    def get_conversation_chain(self, n: int = 10) -> str:
        """Gera a cadeia de conversaÃ§Ã£o."""
        messages = self._messages[-n:] if n > 0 else self._messages
        chain = []
        for msg in messages:
            chain.append(f"[{msg.sender}] {msg.content}")
        return "\n".join(chain)
    
    def get_current_topic(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m o tÃ³pico atual."""
        return self._data.get("current_topic")
    
    def get_detected_intent(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m a intenÃ§Ã£o detectada."""
        return self._data.get("detected_intent")
    
    def set_intent(self, intent: str) -> None:
        """Define a intenÃ§Ã£o detectada."""
        self._data["detected_intent"] = intent
    
    def set_topic(self, topic: str) -> None:
        """Define o tÃ³pico atual."""
        if topic and topic != self._data["current_topic"]:
            if self._data["current_topic"]:
                self._data["previous_topics"].append(self._data["current_topic"])
            self._data["current_topic"] = topic
    
    def resolve_referent(self, word: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """Resolve uma referÃªncia (ex: "isso" -> "o que foi mencionado")."""
        return self._data.get("referents", {}).get(word.lower())
    
    def _detect_topic(self, content: str) -> None:
        """Detecta tÃ³pico a partir do conteÃºdo (simples)."""
        # TODO: Implementar detecÃ§Ã£o de tÃ³pico com IA
        # Por enquanto, usa regras simples
        content_lower = content.lower()
        
        topics = {
            "luz": "Controle de luz",
            "temperatura": "Controle de temperatura",
            "mÃºsica": "MÃºsica e entretenimento",
            "lembrete": "Lembretes",
            "programar": "ProgramaÃ§Ã£o",
            "estudar": "Estudo",
            "compras": "Lista de compras"
        }
        
        for keyword, topic in topics.items():
            if keyword in content_lower:
                self.set_topic(topic)
                break
    
    def set(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
        """Define um valor no contexto."""
        self._data[key] = value
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def get(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        """ObtÃ©m um valor do contexto."""
        return self._data.get(key, default)
    
    def get_all(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m todo o contexto."""
        result = self._data.copy()
        result["history"] = self.get_history(10)
        result["message_count"] = len(self._messages)
        return result
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Limpa o contexto."""
        self._messages.clear()
        self._data = {
            "current_topic": None,
            "previous_topics": [],
            "detected_intent": None,
            "entities": {},
            "referents": {},
            "last_update": datetime.now()
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\context\conversation_flow.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de Fluxo de Conversa da EVA."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import uuid


@dataclass
class ConversationThread:
    """Uma thread de conversa."""
    id: str
    topic: str
    messages: List[Dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    last_position: int = 0
    is_active: bool = True
    is_complete: bool = False
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    paused_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    resumed_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    context: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    priority: int = 5  # 0-10, quanto menor mais prioritÃ¡rio


class ConversationFlow:
    """
    Gerenciador de Fluxo de Conversa.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Gerenciar mÃºltiplas threads de conversa
    - Permitir interrupÃ§Ãµes
    - Continuar de onde parou
    - Manter contexto paralelo
    """
    
    def __init__(self, logger):
        self.logger = logger
        self._threads: Dict[str, ConversationThread] = {}
        self._current_thread_id: Optional[str] = None
        self._pause_stack: List[str] = []
        self._history_stack: List[str] = []  # HistÃ³rico de threads visitadas
    
    def start_thread(self, topic: str, context: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> str:
        """Inicia uma nova thread de conversa."""
        thread_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
        
        # Se jÃ¡ existe uma thread ativa, pausa
        if self._current_thread_id:
            self.pause_thread(self._current_thread_id)
        
        self._threads[thread_id] = ConversationThread(
            id=thread_id,
            topic=topic,
            context=context or {}
        )
        self._current_thread_id = thread_id
        self._history_stack.append(thread_id)
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§µ Nova thread: {topic} ({thread_id})")
        return thread_id
    
    def add_to_thread(self, thread_id: str, message: str, sender: str) -> None:
        """Adiciona uma mensagem a uma thread."""
        thread = self._threads.get(thread_id)
        if not thread:
            return
        
        thread.messages.append({
            "sender": sender,
            "content": message,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
        thread.last_position = len(thread.messages) - 1
    
    def pause_thread(self, thread_id: str) -> bool:
        """Pausa uma thread (interrupÃ§Ã£o)."""
        thread = self._threads.get(thread_id)
        if thread and thread.is_active:
            thread.is_active = False
            thread.paused_at = datetime.now()
            if thread_id not in self._pause_stack:
                self._pause_stack.append(thread_id)
            self.logger.info(f"â¸ï¸ Thread pausada: {thread.topic}")
            return True
        return False
    
    def resume_thread(self, thread_id: str) -> bool:
        """Resume uma thread pausada."""
        thread = self._threads.get(thread_id)
        if thread and not thread.is_active:
            # Pausa a thread atual primeiro
            if self._current_thread_id and self._current_thread_id != thread_id:
                self.pause_thread(self._current_thread_id)
            
            thread.is_active = True
            thread.paused_at = None
            thread.resumed_at = datetime.now()
            
            if thread_id in self._pause_stack:
                self._pause_stack.remove(thread_id)
            
            self._current_thread_id = thread_id
            self._history_stack.append(thread_id)
            
            self.logger.info(f"â–¶ï¸ Thread resumida: {thread.topic}")
            return True
        return False
    
    def switch_to_thread(self, thread_id: str) -> bool:
        """Troca para outra thread."""
        if thread_id in self._threads:
            # Pausa a atual
            if self._current_thread_id:
                self.pause_thread(self._current_thread_id)
            
            self._current_thread_id = thread_id
            self._threads[thread_id].is_active = True
            self._history_stack.append(thread_id)
            
            self.logger.info(f"ðŸ”„ Switch para: {self._threads[thread_id].topic}")
            return True
        return False
    
    def get_last_thread(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m a Ãºltima thread visitada (histÃ³rico)."""
        if len(self._history_stack) >= 2:
            return self._history_stack[-2]
        return None
    
    def get_thread(self, thread_id: str) -> Optional[ConversationThread]:
        """ObtÃ©m uma thread pelo ID."""
        return self._threads.get(thread_id)
    
    def get_current_thread(self) -> Optional[ConversationThread]:
        """ObtÃ©m a thread atual."""
        if self._current_thread_id:
            return self._threads.get(self._current_thread_id)
        return None
    
    def get_active_threads(self) -> List[ConversationThread]:
        """Lista threads ativas."""
        return [t for t in self._threads.values() if t.is_active and not t.is_complete]
    
    def get_paused_threads(self) -> List[ConversationThread]:
        """Lista threads pausadas."""
        return [t for t in self._threads.values() if not t.is_active and not t.is_complete]
    
    def close_thread(self, thread_id: str) -> None:
        """Fecha uma thread."""
        if thread_id in self._threads:
            self._threads[thread_id].is_complete = True
            if thread_id == self._current_thread_id:
                # Tenta voltar para a Ãºltima thread
                last = self.get_last_thread()
                if last and last != thread_id:
                    self.switch_to_thread(last)
                else:
                    self._current_thread_id = None
            
            if thread_id in self._pause_stack:
                self._pause_stack.remove(thread_id)
            
            self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§¹ Thread fechada: {thread_id}")
    
    def get_context_for_prompt(self, thread_id: str = None, limit: int = 10) -> str:
        """Gera contexto para o prompt."""
        if not thread_id:
            thread_id = self._current_thread_id
        
        thread = self._threads.get(thread_id) if thread_id else None
        if not thread:
            return ""
        
        messages = thread.messages[-limit:]
        context = []
        
        if messages:
            context.append(f"[TÃ³pico atual: {thread.topic}]")
            for msg in messages:
                sender = "UsuÃ¡rio" if msg["sender"] == "user" else "EVA"
                context.append(f"{sender}: {msg['content']}")
        
        # Adiciona threads pausadas como contexto secundÃ¡rio
        if self._pause_stack:
            paused_context = []
            for tid in self._pause_stack:
                paused = self._threads.get(tid)
                if paused and paused.messages:
                    last = paused.messages[-1]
                    paused_context.append(f"[Pausado] {paused.topic}: {last['content'][:80]}...")
            
            if paused_context:
                context.append("\n--- Threads pausadas ---")
                context.extend(paused_context)
                context.append("---\n")
        
        return "\n".join(context)
    
    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m status do fluxo."""
        return {
            "total_threads": len(self._threads),
            "active_threads": len(self.get_active_threads()),
            "paused_threads": len(self.get_paused_threads()),
            "current_thread": self._current_thread_id,
            "threads": [
                {
                    "id": t.id,
                    "topic": t.topic,
                    "active": t.is_active,
                    "complete": t.is_complete,
                    "message_count": len(t.messages),
                    "paused_at": t.paused_at.isoformat() if t.paused_at else None
                }
                for t in self._threads.values()
            ]
        }

"""Contexto do ambiente."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime


class EnvironmentContext:
    """
    Contexto do ambiente fÃ­sico e digital.
    
    MantÃ©m:
    - LocalizaÃ§Ã£o
    - Dispositivos e sensores
    - Estado da casa
    - Temperatura, luzes, etc.
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._data: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "location": "Casa",
            "room": None,
            "devices": {},
            "sensors": {},
            "lights": {},
            "temperature": 0.0,
            "humidity": 0.0,
            "weather": {
                "temp": 0.0,
                "condition": "unknown",
                "humidity": 0.0
            },
            "network_status": "unknown",
            "server_status": "unknown",
            "energy": {
                "consumption": 0.0,
                "solar_production": 0.0,
                "battery_level": 0.0
            },
            "last_update": None
        }
    
    def set_room(self, room: str) -> None:
        """Define a sala atual."""
        self._data["room"] = room
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def get_room(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m a sala atual."""
        return self._data.get("room")
    
    def set_device(self, device_id: str, status: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Define o estado de um dispositivo."""
        if "devices" not in self._data:
            self._data["devices"] = {}
        self._data["devices"][device_id] = {
            **status,
            "last_update": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def get_device(self, device_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m o estado de um dispositivo."""
        return self._data.get("devices", {}).get(device_id)
    
    def get_devices(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m todos os dispositivos."""
        return self._data.get("devices", {})
    
    def set_sensor(self, sensor_id: str, value: Any) -> None:
        """Define o valor de um sensor."""
        if "sensors" not in self._data:
            self._data["sensors"] = {}
        self._data["sensors"][sensor_id] = {
            "value": value,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
        
        # Atualiza atalhos
        if sensor_id == "temperature":
            self._data["temperature"] = value
        elif sensor_id == "humidity":
            self._data["humidity"] = value
    
    def get_sensor(self, sensor_id: str) -> Optional[Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m o valor de um sensor."""
        sensor = self._data.get("sensors", {}).get(sensor_id)
        return sensor.get("value") if sensor else None
    
    def get_sensors(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m todos os sensores."""
        return self._data.get("sensors", {})
    
    def set_light(self, light_id: str, status: bool) -> None:
        """Define o estado de uma luz."""
        if "lights" not in self._data:
            self._data["lights"] = {}
        self._data["lights"][light_id] = {
            "on": status,
            "last_update": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def get_light(self, light_id: str) -> Optional[bool]:
        """ObtÃ©m o estado de uma luz."""
        light = self._data.get("lights", {}).get(light_id)
        return light.get("on") if light else None
    
    def set_temperature(self, temp: float) -> None:
        """Define a temperatura."""
        self._data["temperature"] = temp
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def set_weather(self, weather: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Define o clima."""
        self._data["weather"] = weather
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def set(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
        """Define um valor no contexto."""
        self._data[key] = value
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def get(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        """ObtÃ©m um valor do contexto."""
        return self._data.get(key, default)
    
    def get_all(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m todo o contexto."""
        result = self._data.copy()
        return result

"""Contexto da famÃ­lia."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime


class FamilyContext:
    """Contexto sobre a famÃ­lia."""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._data = {
            "members": [],
            "current_member": None,
            "family_events": [],
            "family_values": [],
            "active_members": []
        }
    
    def set(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
        self._data[key] = value
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def get(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        return self._data.get(key, default)
    
    def add_member(self, name: str) -> None:
        if name not in self._data["members"]:
            self._data["members"].append(name)
    
    def set_current_member(self, name: str) -> None:
        self._data["current_member"] = name
        if name not in self._data["active_members"]:
            self._data["active_members"].append(name)
    
    def get_all(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:

"""Contexto do sistema."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime


class SystemContext:
    """
    Contexto do sistema EVA.
    
    MantÃ©m:
    - Estado interno da EVA
    - CPU, RAM, Disco
    - ServiÃ§os e containers
    - Banco de dados
    - MÃ³dulos ativos
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._data: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "state": "OFFLINE",
            "version": "1.0.0",
            "uptime": 0,
            "memory_usage": 0.0,
            "cpu_usage": 0.0,
            "disk_usage": 0.0,
            "active_modules": [],
            "services": {},
            "last_boot": None,
            "errors": [],
            "warnings": [],
            "last_update": None
        }
    
    def set_state(self, state: str) -> None:
        """Define o estado do sistema."""
        self._data["state"] = state
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def get_state(self) -> str:
        """ObtÃ©m o estado do sistema."""
        return self._data.get("state", "UNKNOWN")
    
    def set_uptime(self, seconds: float) -> None:
        """Define o uptime do sistema."""
        self._data["uptime"] = seconds
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def set_resources(self, cpu: float, memory: float, disk: float) -> None:
        """Define o uso de recursos."""
        self._data["cpu_usage"] = cpu
        self._data["memory_usage"] = memory
        self._data["disk_usage"] = disk
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def add_module(self, module_name: str) -> None:
        """Adiciona um mÃ³dulo ativo."""
        if module_name not in self._data["active_modules"]:
            self._data["active_modules"].append(module_name)
            self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def remove_module(self, module_name: str) -> None:
        """Remove um mÃ³dulo ativo."""
        if module_name in self._data["active_modules"]:
            self._data["active_modules"].remove(module_name)
            self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def get_active_modules(self) -> List[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m mÃ³dulos ativos."""
        return self._data.get("active_modules", [])
    
    def set_service_status(self, service_name: str, status: str) -> None:
        """Define o status de um serviÃ§o."""
        if "services" not in self._data:
            self._data["services"] = {}
        self._data["services"][service_name] = {
            "status": status,
            "last_update": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def get_service_status(self, service_name: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m o status de um serviÃ§o."""
        service = self._data.get("services", {}).get(service_name)
        return service.get("status") if service else None
    
    def add_error(self, error: str) -> None:
        """Adiciona um erro."""
        self._data["errors"].append({
            "error": error,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
        if len(self._data["errors"]) > 50:
            self._data["errors"] = self._data["errors"][-50:]
    
    def add_warning(self, warning: str) -> None:
        """Adiciona um aviso."""
        self._data["warnings"].append({
            "warning": warning,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
        if len(self._data["warnings"]) > 50:
            self._data["warnings"] = self._data["warnings"][-50:]
    
    def set(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
        """Define um valor no contexto."""
        self._data[key] = value
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def get(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        """ObtÃ©m um valor do contexto."""
        return self._data.get(key, default)
    
    def get_all(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m todo o contexto."""
        return self._data.copy()

"""Contexto temporal."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime, time, timedelta
import calendar


class TemporalContext:
    """
    Contexto temporal.
    
    MantÃ©m:
    - Data e hora atual
    - PerÃ­odo do dia (manhÃ£, tarde, noite, madrugada)
    - Dia da semana
    - Fim de semana ou dia Ãºtil
    - Rotina escolar
    - FÃ©rias
    - AniversÃ¡rios
    - Eventos importantes
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._data: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "timestamp": None,
            "date": None,
            "time": None,
            "period": None,  # dawn, morning, afternoon, evening, night
            "day_of_week": None,  # 0-6 (segunda=0)
            "day_name": None,
            "is_weekend": False,
            "is_holiday": False,
            "is_vacation": False,
            "is_school_day": True,
            "season": None,
            "year": None,
            "month": None,
            "day": None,
            "hour": None,
            "minute": None,
            "last_update": None
        }
        
        # PerÃ­odos
        self._periods = {
            "dawn": (0, 5),      # 00:00 - 05:59
            "morning": (6, 11),  # 06:00 - 11:59
            "afternoon": (12, 17), # 12:00 - 17:59
            "evening": (18, 20), # 18:00 - 20:59
            "night": (21, 23)    # 21:00 - 23:59
        }
        
        # Dias da semana em portuguÃªs
        self._weekday_names = [
            "segunda-feira",
            "terÃ§a-feira",
            "quarta-feira",
            "quinta-feira",
            "sexta-feira",
            "sÃ¡bado",
            "domingo"
        ]
        
        # EstaÃ§Ãµes do ano (Sul)
        self._seasons = {
            (1, 2): "verÃ£o",
            (3, 4, 5): "outono",
            (6, 7, 8): "inverno",
            (9, 10, 11): "primavera",
            (12,): "verÃ£o"
        }
    
    def update(self) -> None:
        """Atualiza o contexto temporal com a data/hora atual."""
        now = datetime.now()
        self._data["timestamp"] = now.isoformat()
        self._data["date"] = now.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        self._data["time"] = now.strftime("%H:%M:%S")
        self._data["year"] = now.year
        self._data["month"] = now.month
        self._data["day"] = now.day
        self._data["hour"] = now.hour
        self._data["minute"] = now.minute
        
        # PerÃ­odo do dia
        self._data["period"] = self._get_period(now.hour)
        
        # Dia da semana
        weekday = now.weekday()  # 0=segunda, 6=domingo
        self._data["day_of_week"] = weekday
        self._data["day_name"] = self._weekday_names[weekday]
        self._data["is_weekend"] = weekday in [5, 6]  # sÃ¡bado=5, domingo=6
        
        # EstaÃ§Ã£o
        self._data["season"] = self._get_season(now.month)
        
        # HorÃ¡rio escolar (dias Ãºteis, 7-17h)
        self._data["is_school_day"] = (
            not self._data["is_weekend"] and
            7 <= now.hour < 17
        )
        
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def _get_period(self, hour: int) -> str:
        """Determina o perÃ­odo do dia com base na hora."""
        for period, (start, end) in self._periods.items():
            if start <= hour <= end:
                return period
        return "unknown"
    
    def _get_season(self, month: int) -> str:
        """Determina a estaÃ§Ã£o do ano (Hemisferio Sul)."""
        for months, season in self._seasons.items():
            if month in months:
                return season
        return "unknown"
    
    def get_period(self) -> str:
        """ObtÃ©m o perÃ­odo atual."""
        return self._data.get("period", "unknown")
    
    def is_weekend(self) -> bool:
        """Verifica se Ã© fim de semana."""
        return self._data.get("is_weekend", False)
    
    def is_school_day(self) -> bool:
        """Verifica se Ã© dia de escola."""
        return self._data.get("is_school_day", True)
    
    def get_time_until(self, target_hour: int, target_minute: int = 0) -> float:
        """Tempo em horas atÃ© um horÃ¡rio especÃ­fico."""
        now = datetime.now()
        target = now.replace(hour=target_hour, minute=target_minute, second=0, microsecond=0)
        if target <= now:
            target += timedelta(days=1)
        return (target - now).total_seconds() / 3600
    
    def is_between(self, start_hour: int, end_hour: int) -> bool:
        """Verifica se a hora atual estÃ¡ entre dois horÃ¡rios."""
        now = datetime.now()
        start = now.replace(hour=start_hour, minute=0, second=0)
        end = now.replace(hour=end_hour, minute=0, second=0)
        if end < start:
            end += timedelta(days=1)
        return start <= now <= end
    
    def set(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
        """Define um valor no contexto."""
        self._data[key] = value
        self._data["last_update"] = datetime.now()
    
    def get(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        """ObtÃ©m um valor do contexto."""
        return self._data.get(key, default)
    
    def get_all(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m todo o contexto."""
        self.update()  # Atualiza antes de retornar
        return self._data.copy()

"""Contexto do usuÃ¡rio."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime


class UserContext:
    """
    Contexto do usuÃ¡rio.
    
    MantÃ©m:
    - Quem estÃ¡ falando
    - PermissÃµes do usuÃ¡rio
    - PreferÃªncias
    - Perfil
    - HistÃ³rico de interaÃ§Ãµes
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._data: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "current_user": None,
            "users": {},  # user_id -> user_data
            "active_users": [],
            "last_interaction": None
        }
        
        # UsuÃ¡rios padrÃ£o
        self._init_default_users()
    
    def _init_default_users(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa usuÃ¡rios padrÃ£o."""
        self._data["users"] = {
            "alberto": {
                "name": "Alberto",
                "role": "admin",
                "preferences": {
                    "voice": True,
                    "language": "pt-BR",
                    "lights_auto": False,
                    "temperature": 22.0,
                    "notifications": True
                },
                "profile": {
                    "age": 35,
                    "occupation": "Engenheiro de Software",
                    "interests": ["programaÃ§Ã£o", "eletrÃ´nica", "IA"]
                }
            },
            "juliana": {
                "name": "Juliana",
                "role": "user",
                "preferences": {
                    "voice": True,
                    "language": "pt-BR",
                    "lights_auto": True,
                    "temperature": 24.0,
                    "notifications": True
                },
                "profile": {
                    "age": 32,
                    "occupation": "Designer",
                    "interests": ["design", "arte", "fotografia"]
                }
            },
            "nicolas": {
                "name": "Nicolas",
                "role": "child",
                "preferences": {
                    "voice": True,
                    "language": "pt-BR",
                    "lights_auto": False,
                    "temperature": 24.0,
                    "notifications": False
                },
                "profile": {
                    "age": 7,
                    "occupation": "Estudante",
                    "interests": ["jogos", "desenhos", "robÃ´s"]
                }
            }
        }
    
    def set_current_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        """Define o usuÃ¡rio atual."""
        if user_id in self._data["users"]:
            self._data["current_user"] = user_id
            if user_id not in self._data["active_users"]:
                self._data["active_users"].append(user_id)
            self._data["last_interaction"] = datetime.now()
    
    def get_current_user(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m o usuÃ¡rio atual."""
        return self._data.get("current_user")
    
    def get_current_user_data(self) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m os dados do usuÃ¡rio atual."""
        user_id = self.get_current_user()
        if user_id:
            return self._data["users"].get(user_id)
        return None
    
    def get_user(self, user_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m dados de um usuÃ¡rio."""
        return self._data["users"].get(user_id)
    
    def get_user_preference(self, user_id: str, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        """ObtÃ©m uma preferÃªncia de um usuÃ¡rio."""
        user = self.get_user(user_id)
        if user:
            return user.get("preferences", {}).get(key, default)
        return default
    
    def get_user_role(self, user_id: str) -> str:
        """ObtÃ©m o papel de um usuÃ¡rio."""
        user = self.get_user(user_id)
        if user:
            return user.get("role", "guest")
        return "guest"
    
    def has_permission(self, user_id: str, permission: str) -> bool:
        """Verifica se um usuÃ¡rio tem uma permissÃ£o."""
        role = self.get_user_role(user_id)
        
        # Mapeamento de papÃ©is para permissÃµes
        role_permissions = {
            "admin": ["all"],
            "user": ["conversation", "control_lights", "control_temperature", "set_reminders"],
            "child": ["conversation", "play_music", "set_reminders"],
            "guest": ["conversation"]
        }
        
        perms = role_permissions.get(role, [])
        return "all" in perms or permission in perms
    
    def set_user_preference(self, user_id: str, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
        """Define uma preferÃªncia de um usuÃ¡rio."""
        if user_id in self._data["users"]:
            if "preferences" not in self._data["users"][user_id]:
                self._data["users"][user_id]["preferences"] = {}
            self._data["users"][user_id]["preferences"][key] = value
    
    def get_all_users(self) -> List[str]:
        """Lista todos os usuÃ¡rios."""
        return list(self._data["users"].keys())
    
    def get_active_users(self) -> List[str]:
        """Lista usuÃ¡rios ativos."""
        return self._data.get("active_users", [])
    
    def set(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
        """Define um valor no contexto."""
        self._data[key] = value
    
    def get(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        """ObtÃ©m um valor do contexto."""
        return self._data.get(key, default)
    
    def get_all(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m todo o contexto."""
        result = self._data.copy()
        # Remove dados sensÃ­veis em produÃ§Ã£o
        # result["users"] = {k: v for k, v in result.get("users", {}).items()}
        return result
