==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\core\dependency_container.py ====================
"""Container de injeÃ§Ã£o de dependÃªncia."""

from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional, Type, TypeVar, Union
from functools import wraps
import inspect


T = TypeVar('T')


class DependencyContainer:
    """
    Container para injeÃ§Ã£o de dependÃªncia.
    Nunca faremos: planner = Planner()
    Sempre: container.resolve("planner")
    """
    
    def __init__(self, logger, service_registry):
        self.logger = logger
        self.registry = service_registry
        
        # FÃ¡bricas de dependÃªncias
        self._factories: Dict[str, Callable] = {}
        
        # InstÃ¢ncias singleton
        self._singletons: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        
        # DependÃªncias em construÃ§Ã£o (detecÃ§Ã£o de ciclos)
        self._resolving: List[str] = []
    
    def register_factory(self, name: str, factory: Callable, dependencies: List[str] = None) -> None:
        """
        Registra uma fÃ¡brica para criar um serviÃ§o.
        """
        self._factories[name] = factory
        self.logger.debug(f"FÃ¡brica registrada: {name}")
    
    def register_singleton(self, name: str, instance: Any) -> None:
        """
        Registra uma instÃ¢ncia singleton.
        """
        self._singletons[name] = instance
        self.logger.debug(f"Singleton registrado: {name}")
    
    def resolve(self, name: str) -> Any:
        """
        Resolve uma dependÃªncia pelo nome.
        """
        # Verifica ciclos
        if name in self._resolving:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Ciclo de dependÃªncia detectado: {' -> '.join(self._resolving + [name])}")
        
        # 1. Verifica singleton
        if name in self._singletons:
            return self._singletons[name]
        
        # 2. Verifica registro no ServiceRegistry
        if self.registry.exists(name):
            return self.registry.get(name)
        
        # 3. Verifica fÃ¡brica
        if name in self._factories:
            self._resolving.append(name)
            try:
                instance = self._factories[name]()
                # Se retornou uma instÃ¢ncia, registra como singleton
                if instance is not None:
                    self._singletons[name] = instance
                self._resolving.pop()
                return instance
            except Exception as e:
                self._resolving.pop()
                raise RuntimeError(f"Erro ao resolver dependÃªncia {name}: {e}")
        
        raise KeyError(f"DependÃªncia nÃ£o encontrada: {name}")
    
    def resolve_with_params(self, name: str, **params) -> Any:
        """
        Resolve uma dependÃªncia com parÃ¢metros adicionais.
        """
        if name in self._factories:
            return self._factories[name](**params)
        
        raise KeyError(f"FÃ¡brica nÃ£o encontrada: {name}")
    
    def auto_inject(self, target_class: Type) -> Type:
        """
        Decorator para injeÃ§Ã£o automÃ¡tica de dependÃªncias.
        """
        original_init = target_class.__init__
        
        @wraps(original_init)
        def new_init(self, *args, **kwargs):
            # Resolve dependÃªncias do __init__
            sig = inspect.signature(original_init)
            for param_name, param in sig.parameters.items():
                if param_name == 'self':
                    continue
                if param_name in kwargs:
                    continue
                if param_name in self._singletons:
                    kwargs[param_name] = self._singletons[param_name]
                elif param_name in self._factories:
                    kwargs[param_name] = self.resolve(param_name)
                elif param.annotation != inspect.Parameter.empty:
                    # Tenta resolver pelo tipo
                    type_name = param.annotation.__name__.lower()
                    if self.exists(type_name):
                        kwargs[param_name] = self.resolve(type_name)
            
            original_init(self, *args, **kwargs)
        
        target_class.__init__ = new_init
        return target_class
    
    def exists(self, name: str) -> bool:
        """Verifica se uma dependÃªncia pode ser resolvida."""
        return (
            name in self._singletons or
            name in self._factories or
            self.registry.exists(name)
        )
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Limpa o container."""
        self._factories.clear()
        self._singletons.clear()
        self._resolving.clear()
        self.logger.info("DependencyContainer limpo")


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\core\event_bus.py ====================
"""Barramento de eventos do Core EVA OS."""

import asyncio
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum, auto
from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional, Set, Union
from uuid import uuid4


class EventPriority(Enum):
    """Prioridade de eventos."""
    CRITICAL = 0
    HIGH = 1
    NORMAL = 2
    LOW = 3
    BACKGROUND = 4


@dataclass
class Event:
    """Evento no barramento."""
    type: str
    data: Any = None
    source: str = "system"
    priority: EventPriority = EventPriority.NORMAL
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    correlation_id: Optional[str] = None
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        if isinstance(self.priority, int):
            self.priority = EventPriority(self.priority)
    
    @property
    def payload(self):
        """Compatibilidade com cÃ³digo antigo que usa event.payload."""
        return self.data


class EventBus:
    """
    Barramento de eventos central.
    Todo mÃ³dulo conversa atravÃ©s dele.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, logger):
        self.logger = logger
        
        # ===== UMA ÃšNICA LISTA DE LISTENERS =====
        self._listeners: Dict[str, List[Callable]] = {}
        
        # Fila para processamento assÃ­ncrono
        self._queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue()
        self._running = False
        self._worker_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
        
        # HistÃ³rico para debugging
        self._history: List[Event] = []
        self._max_history = 1000
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o barramento."""
        self._running = True
        self._worker_task = asyncio.create_task(self._process_events())
        self.logger.info("EventBus inicializado")
    
    # ===== REGISTRO DE LISTENERS =====
    
    def subscribe(self, event_type: str, callback: Callable) -> None:
        """
        Registra um listener.
        Aceita tanto funÃ§Ãµes sÃ­ncronas quanto assÃ­ncronas.
        """
        if not callable(callback):
            raise ValueError(f"Callback para {event_type} nÃ£o Ã© callable")
        
        if event_type not in self._listeners:
            self._listeners[event_type] = []
        self._listeners[event_type].append(callback)
        
        is_async = asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(callback)
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“‹ [EventBus] Listener registrado: {event_type} (async={is_async}) - total: {len(self._listeners[event_type])}")
    
    def unsubscribe(self, event_type: str, callback: Callable) -> None:
        """Remove um listener."""
        if event_type in self._listeners:
            try:
                self._listeners[event_type].remove(callback)
                self.logger.debug(f"Listener removido de: {event_type}")
            except ValueError:
                pass
    
    def has_subscribers(self, event_type: str) -> bool:
        """Verifica se hÃ¡ inscritos para um evento."""
        return event_type in self._listeners and len(self._listeners[event_type]) > 0
    
    def get_listeners_count(self, event_type: str) -> int:
        """Retorna o nÃºmero de listeners para um evento."""
        return len(self._listeners.get(event_type, []))
    
    # ===== PUBLICAÃ‡ÃƒO =====
    
    def publish(self, event: Union[Event, str, Dict], data: Any = None, 
                source: str = "system", priority: EventPriority = EventPriority.NORMAL) -> None:
        """
        Publica um evento sÃ­ncrono.
        """
        event = self._normalize_event(event, data, source, priority)
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“¤ [EventBus] publish: {event.type} de {event.source}")
        
        # Dispatch direto (sÃ­ncrono)
        self._dispatch_sync(event)
        
        # TambÃ©m coloca na fila para processamento assÃ­ncrono
        asyncio.create_task(self._queue.put(event))
        
        self._add_to_history(event)
    
    async def publish_async(self, event: Union[Event, str, Dict], data: Any = None,
                           source: str = "system", priority: EventPriority = EventPriority.NORMAL) -> None:
        """
        Publica um evento assÃ­ncrono.
        SOMENTE FILA - sem dispatch sÃ­ncrono.
        """
        event = self._normalize_event(event, data, source, priority)
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“¤ [EventBus] publish_async: {event.type} de {event.source}")
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“¤ [EventBus] Listeners para '{event.type}': {self.get_listeners_count(event.type)}")
        
        # ===== SOMENTE FILA =====
        await self._queue.put(event)
        
        self._add_to_history(event)
    
    def _normalize_event(self, event, data, source, priority) -> Event:
        """Normaliza o evento para o formato padrÃ£o."""
        if isinstance(event, str):
            return Event(
                type=event,
                data=data,
                source=source,
                priority=priority
            )
        elif isinstance(event, dict):
            return Event(
                type=event.get("type", "unknown"),
                data=event.get("data"),
                source=event.get("source", "system"),
                priority=event.get("priority", EventPriority.NORMAL)
            )
        return event
    
    # ===== DISPATCH =====
    
    async def _process_events(self) -> None:
        """Processa eventos da fila assÃ­ncrona."""
        while self._running:
            try:
                event = await self._queue.get()
                self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“¥ [EventBus] Processando da fila: {event.type}")
                await self._dispatch_async(event)
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro processando evento: {e}")
    
    def _dispatch_sync(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Distribui evento para listeners sÃ­ncronos."""
        listeners = self._listeners.get(event.type, [])
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“¨ [EventBus] _dispatch_sync: {event.type} -> {len(listeners)} listeners")
        
        for callback in listeners:
            try:
                if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(callback):
                    # Cria task para nÃ£o bloquear
                    asyncio.create_task(callback(event))
                    self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“¨ [EventBus] Async task criada para {callback.__name__}")
                else:
                    callback(event)
                    self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“¨ [EventBus] Sync callback executado: {callback.__name__}")
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro no listener {callback.__name__}: {e}")
        
        # Wildcard
        if "*" in self._listeners:
            for callback in self._listeners["*"]:
                try:
                    if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(callback):
                        asyncio.create_task(callback(event))
                    else:
                        callback(event)
                except Exception as e:
                    self.logger.error(f"Erro no listener wildcard: {e}")
    
    async def _dispatch_async(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Distribui evento para listeners assÃ­ncronos."""
        listeners = self._listeners.get(event.type, [])
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“¨ [EventBus] _dispatch_async: {event.type} -> {len(listeners)} listeners")
        
        for callback in listeners:
            try:
                if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(callback):
                    await callback(event)
                    self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“¨ [EventBus] Async callback executado: {callback.__name__}")
                else:
                    callback(event)
                    self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“¨ [EventBus] Sync callback executado: {callback.__name__}")
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro no async listener {callback.__name__}: {e}")
        
        # Wildcard
        if "*" in self._listeners:
            for callback in self._listeners["*"]:
                try:
                    if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(callback):
                        await callback(event)
                    else:
                        callback(event)
                except Exception as e:
                    self.logger.error(f"Erro no async listener wildcard: {e}")
    
    # ===== UTILITÃRIOS =====
    
    def _add_to_history(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Adiciona evento ao histÃ³rico."""
        self._history.append(event)
        if len(self._history) > self._max_history:
            self._history = self._history[-self._max_history:]
    
    def get_history(self, limit: int = 100, event_type: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Event]:
        """ObtÃ©m histÃ³rico de eventos."""
        if event_type:
            filtered = [e for e in self._history if e.type == event_type]
            return filtered[-limit:]
        return self._history[-limit:]
    
    def clear_history(self) -> None:
        """Limpa histÃ³rico."""
        self._history.clear()
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Limpa todos os listeners."""
        self._listeners.clear()
        self.logger.info("EventBus limpo")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o barramento."""
        self._running = False
        if self._worker_task:
            self._worker_task.cancel()
            try:
                await self._worker_task
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
        self.logger.info("EventBus desligado")


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\core\event_types.py ====================
"""Tipos de eventos padronizados da EVA OS."""

from enum import Enum
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional


class EventType:
    """
    Tipos de eventos padronizados.
    
    Entrada:
    - USER_MESSAGE: Mensagem do usuÃ¡rio
    - SYSTEM_COMMAND: Comando do sistema
    
    Pipeline Cognitivo:
    - CONTEXT_READY: Contexto processado
    - MEMORY_RETRIEVED: MemÃ³rias recuperadas
    - KNOWLEDGE_QUERIED: Conhecimento consultado
    - PLAN_READY: Plano gerado
    - REASONING_READY: RaciocÃ­nio concluÃ­do
    - EMOTION_READY: EmoÃ§Ã£o analisada
    - PROMPT_READY: Prompt construÃ­do
    
    SaÃ­da (UNIFICADO):
    - ASSISTANT_RESPONSE: Resposta final
    
    Status:
    - PROCESSING_START: InÃ­cio do processamento
    - PROCESSING_END: Fim do processamento
    
    Erros:
    - COGNITIVE_ERROR: Erro no pipeline
    - PIPELINE_FAILED: Falha no pipeline
    """
    
    # Entrada
    USER_MESSAGE = "user.message"
    SYSTEM_COMMAND = "system.command"
    
    # Pipeline Cognitivo
    CONTEXT_READY = "context.ready"
    MEMORY_RETRIEVED = "memory.retrieved"
    KNOWLEDGE_QUERIED = "knowledge.queried"
    PLAN_READY = "plan.ready"
    REASONING_READY = "reasoning.ready"
    EMOTION_READY = "emotion.ready"
    PROMPT_READY = "prompt.ready"
    
    # SaÃ­da (UNIFICADO)
    ASSISTANT_RESPONSE = "assistant.response"
    
    # Status
    PROCESSING_START = "processing.start"
    PROCESSING_END = "processing.end"
    
    # Erros
    COGNITIVE_ERROR = "cognitive.error"
    PIPELINE_FAILED = "pipeline.failed"


class EventPriority(Enum):
    """Prioridades de eventos."""
    CRITICAL = 0
    HIGH = 1
    NORMAL = 2
    LOW = 3
    BACKGROUND = 4


# Mapeamento de eventos para prioridades padrÃ£o
EVENT_PRIORITY_MAP = {
    EventType.USER_MESSAGE: EventPriority.NORMAL,
    EventType.SYSTEM_COMMAND: EventPriority.HIGH,
    EventType.CONTEXT_READY: EventPriority.NORMAL,
    EventType.MEMORY_RETRIEVED: EventPriority.NORMAL,
    EventType.KNOWLEDGE_QUERIED: EventPriority.NORMAL,
    EventType.PLAN_READY: EventPriority.NORMAL,
    EventType.REASONING_READY: EventPriority.NORMAL,
    EventType.EMOTION_READY: EventPriority.NORMAL,
    EventType.PROMPT_READY: EventPriority.NORMAL,
    EventType.ASSISTANT_RESPONSE: EventPriority.NORMAL,
    EventType.PROCESSING_START: EventPriority.LOW,
    EventType.PROCESSING_END: EventPriority.LOW,
    EventType.COGNITIVE_ERROR: EventPriority.HIGH,
    EventType.PIPELINE_FAILED: EventPriority.CRITICAL,
}


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\core\health_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de saÃºde do sistema."""

import asyncio
import psutil
import platform
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Tuple
from datetime import datetime
import subprocess
import json


class HealthManager:
    """
    Monitora a saÃºde do sistema.
    Inclui CPU, RAM, Disco, tempo de resposta, etc.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, logger, event_bus, config):
        self.logger = logger
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.config = config
        
        self._is_running = False
        self._check_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
        self._interval = 60  # segundos
        
        # Ãšltimo status
        self._last_status: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._max_history = 1000
        
        # Alertas
        self._alerts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        
        # Limites
        self._thresholds = {
            "cpu": 80.0,
            "ram": 85.0,
            "disk": 90.0,
            "response_time": 2.0
        }
        
        # MÃ³dulos registrados para monitoramento
        self._modules: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        
        # Status de serviÃ§os externos
        self._external_services: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o HealthManager."""
        self._is_running = True
        self._check_task = asyncio.create_task(self._periodic_check())
        self.logger.info("HealthManager inicializado")
    
    async def _periodic_check(self) -> None:
        """Loop periÃ³dico de verificaÃ§Ã£o."""
        while self._is_running:
            try:
                await asyncio.sleep(self._interval)
                status = await self.check()
                
                # Publica status
                await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                    "HEALTH_STATUS",
                    status,
                    source="health_manager"
                )
                
                # Verifica alertas
                await self._check_alerts(status)
                
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro no health check: {e}")
    
    async def check(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Realiza verificaÃ§Ã£o completa de saÃºde."""
        status = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "system": {
                "platform": platform.platform(),
                "python": platform.python_version()
            },
            "cpu": self._check_cpu(),
            "ram": self._check_ram(),
            "disk": self._check_disk(),
            "modules": await self._check_modules(),
            "external": await self._check_external_services()
        }
        
        self._last_status = status
        self._add_history(status)
        
        return status
    
    def _check_cpu(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Verifica uso de CPU."""
        try:
            cpu_percent = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.5)
            cpu_count = psutil.cpu_count()
            cpu_freq = psutil.cpu_freq()
            
            return {
                "percent": cpu_percent,
                "count": cpu_count,
                "freq_current": cpu_freq.current if cpu_freq else 0,
                "freq_max": cpu_freq.max if cpu_freq else 0,
                "warning": cpu_percent > self._thresholds["cpu"]
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {"error": str(e)}
    
    def _check_ram(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Verifica uso de RAM."""
        try:
            memory = psutil.virtual_memory()
            
            return {
                "percent": memory.percent,
                "total_gb": memory.total / (1024 ** 3),
                "available_gb": memory.available / (1024 ** 3),
                "used_gb": memory.used / (1024 ** 3),
                "warning": memory.percent > self._thresholds["ram"]
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {"error": str(e)}
    
    def _check_disk(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Verifica uso de disco."""
        try:
            disk = psutil.disk_usage('/')
            
            return {
                "percent": disk.percent,
                "total_gb": disk.total / (1024 ** 3),
                "used_gb": disk.used / (1024 ** 3),
                "free_gb": disk.free / (1024 ** 3),
                "warning": disk.percent > self._thresholds["disk"]
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {"error": str(e)}
    
    async def _check_modules(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Verifica saÃºde dos mÃ³dulos."""
        status = {}
        
        for name, info in self._modules.items():
            try:
                module = info.get("instance")
                if module and hasattr(module, "health_check"):
                    if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(module.health_check):
                        result = await module.health_check()
                    else:
                        result = module.health_check()
                    status[name] = result
                else:
                    status[name] = {"status": "unknown"}
            except Exception as e:
                status[name] = {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}
        
        return status
    
    async def _check_external_services(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Verifica serviÃ§os externos."""
        status = {}
        for name, config in self._external_services.items():
            status[name] = await self._check_external(name, config)
        return status
    
    async def _check_external(self, name: str, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Verifica um serviÃ§o externo especÃ­fico."""
        service_type = config.get("type", "unknown")
        timeout = config.get("timeout", 2.0)
        
        if service_type == "ping":
            return await self._ping_service(name, config.get("host"), timeout)
        elif service_type == "http":
            return await self._http_service(config.get("url"), timeout)
        elif service_type == "tcp":
            return await self._tcp_service(config.get("host"), config.get("port"), timeout)
        else:
            return {"status": "unknown", "error": f"Tipo desconhecido: {service_type}"}
    
    async def _ping_service(self, name: str, host: str, timeout: float) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Testa serviÃ§o via ping."""
        try:
            proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec(
                "ping", "-c", "1", "-W", str(int(timeout)), host,
                stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
                stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE
            )
            stdout, stderr = await proc.communicate()
            
            if proc.returncode == 0:
                return {"status": "online", "response_time": 0.001}
            else:
                return {"status": "offline", "error": stderr.decode().strip()}
        except Exception as e:
            return {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}
    
    async def _http_service(self, url: str, timeout: float) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Testa serviÃ§o via HTTP."""
        try:
            import aiohttp
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
                async with session.get(url, timeout=timeout) as response:
                    return {
                        "status": "online",
                        "status_code": response.status,
                        "response_time": timeout
                    }
        except Exception as e:
            return {"status": "offline", "error": str(e)}
    
    async def _tcp_service(self, host: str, port: int, timeout: float) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Testa serviÃ§o via TCP."""
        try:
            import socket
            sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
            sock.settimeout(timeout)
            result = sock.connect_ex((host, port))
            sock.close()
            
            if result == 0:
                return {"status": "online"}
            else:
                return {"status": "offline", "error": f"ConexÃ£o recusada (cÃ³digo {result})"}
        except Exception as e:
            return {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}
    
    async def _check_alerts(self, status: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Verifica se hÃ¡ alertas."""
        alerts = []
        
        # CPU
        cpu = status.get("cpu", {})
        if cpu.get("warning", False):
            alerts.append({
                "level": "warning",
                "component": "cpu",
                "message": f"CPU alta: {cpu.get('percent')}%",
                "threshold": self._thresholds["cpu"]
            })
        
        # RAM
        ram = status.get("ram", {})
        if ram.get("warning", False):
            alerts.append({
                "level": "warning",
                "component": "ram",
                "message": f"RAM alta: {ram.get('percent')}%",
                "threshold": self._thresholds["ram"]
            })
        
        # Disk
        disk = status.get("disk", {})
        if disk.get("warning", False):
            alerts.append({
                "level": "warning",
                "component": "disk",
                "message": f"Disco alto: {disk.get('percent')}%",
                "threshold": self._thresholds["disk"]
            })
        
        # Publica alertas
        for alert in alerts:
            self._alerts.append(alert)
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "HEALTH_ALERT",
                alert,
                source="health_manager"
            )
            self.logger.warning(f"Alerta de saÃºde: {alert['message']}")
    
    def register_module(self, name: str, instance: Any) -> None:
        """Registra um mÃ³dulo para monitoramento."""
        self._modules[name] = {
            "instance": instance,
            "registered_at": datetime.now()
        }
        self.logger.debug(f"MÃ³dulo registrado para health check: {name}")
    
    def register_external_service(self, name: str, config: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Registra um serviÃ§o externo para monitoramento."""
        self._external_services[name] = config
        self.logger.debug(f"ServiÃ§o externo registrado: {name}")
    
    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m Ãºltimo status."""
        return self._last_status
    
    def get_alerts(self, limit: int = 50) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m alertas recentes."""
        return self._alerts[-limit:]
    
    def _add_history(self, status: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Adiciona ao histÃ³rico."""
        self._history.append(status)
        if len(self._history) > self._max_history:
            self._history = self._history[-self._max_history:]
    
    def get_history(self, limit: int = 100) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m histÃ³rico de status."""
        return self._history[-limit:]
    
    def set_threshold(self, metric: str, value: float) -> None:
        """Define um limite para um mÃ©trica."""
        if metric in self._thresholds:
            self._thresholds[metric] = value
            self.logger.info(f"Threshold {metric}: {value}")
    
    def get_thresholds(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """ObtÃ©m todos os limites."""
        return self._thresholds.copy()
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o HealthManager."""
        self._is_running = False
        if self._check_task:
            self._check_task.cancel()
            try:
                await self._check_task
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
        self.logger.info("HealthManager desligado")


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\core\lifecycle_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de ciclo de vida do Core."""

from enum import Enum, auto
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Set
from datetime import datetime
import asyncio


class ModuleState(Enum):
    """Estados de um mÃ³dulo no ciclo de vida."""
    CREATED = auto()
    INITIALIZED = auto()
    STARTING = auto()
    RUNNING = auto()
    PAUSED = auto()
    STOPPING = auto()
    STOPPED = auto()
    FAILED = auto()


class ModuleInfo:
    """InformaÃ§Ãµes de um mÃ³dulo."""
    
    def __init__(self, name: str, module: Any, dependencies: List[str] = None):
        self.name = name
        self.module = module
        self.dependencies = dependencies or []
        self.state = ModuleState.CREATED
        
        self.created_at = datetime.now()
        self.initialized_at: Optional[datetime] = None
        self.started_at: Optional[datetime] = None
        self.stopped_at: Optional[datetime] = None
        
        self.error: Optional[str] = None
        self.retry_count = 0
        self.max_retries = 3


class LifecycleManager:
    """
    Gerencia a vida dos mÃ³dulos.
    Estados: CREATED -> INITIALIZED -> STARTING -> RUNNING -> STOPPING -> STOPPED
    """
    
    def __init__(self, logger, event_bus, service_registry):
        self.logger = logger
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.registry = service_registry
        
        self._modules: Dict[str, ModuleInfo] = {}
        self._startup_order: List[str] = []
        
        # ConfiguraÃ§Ãµes
        self._timeout = 30  # segundos
        self._max_retries = 3
    
    def register(self, name: str, module: Any, dependencies: List[str] = None) -> None:
        """Registra um mÃ³dulo no ciclo de vida."""
        if name in self._modules:
            self.logger.warning(f"MÃ³dulo {name} jÃ¡ registrado")
            return
        
        info = ModuleInfo(name, module, dependencies)
        self._modules[name] = info
        self.logger.info(f"MÃ³dulo registrado: {name}")
        
        # Ordena topologicamente
        self._update_startup_order()
    
    def _update_startup_order(self) -> None:
        """Atualiza ordem de inicializaÃ§Ã£o (topolÃ³gica)."""
        # Ordena por nÃºmero de dependÃªncias (mais dependÃªncias primeiro)
        self._startup_order = sorted(
            self._modules.keys(),
            key=lambda x: len(self._modules[x].dependencies),
            reverse=True
        )
    
    async def initialize_all(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa todos os mÃ³dulos."""
        self.logger.info("Inicializando todos os mÃ³dulos...")
        
        for name in self._startup_order:
            info = self._modules[name]
            if info.state == ModuleState.CREATED:
                await self._initialize_module(name)
    
    async def start_all(self) -> None:
        """Inicia todos os mÃ³dulos."""
        self.logger.info("Iniciando todos os mÃ³dulos...")
        
        # Inicializa primeiro
        for name in self._startup_order:
            info = self._modules[name]
            if info.state == ModuleState.CREATED:
                await self._initialize_module(name)
        
        # Depois inicia
        for name in self._startup_order:
            info = self._modules[name]
            if info.state == ModuleState.INITIALIZED:
                await self._start_module(name)
    
    async def _initialize_module(self, name: str) -> bool:
        """Inicializa um mÃ³dulo especÃ­fico."""
        info = self._modules[name]
        
        try:
            self.logger.debug(f"Inicializando mÃ³dulo: {name}")
            
            module = info.module
            if hasattr(module, "initialize"):
                if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(module.initialize):
                    await module.initialize()
                else:
                    module.initialize()
            
            info.state = ModuleState.INITIALIZED
            info.initialized_at = datetime.now()
            info.error = None
            
            self.logger.info(f"MÃ³dulo inicializado: {name}")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            info.state = ModuleState.FAILED
            info.error = str(e)
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao inicializar {name}: {e}")
            return False
    
    async def _start_module(self, name: str) -> bool:
        """Inicia um mÃ³dulo especÃ­fico."""
        info = self._modules[name]
        
        # Verifica dependÃªncias
        if not self._check_dependencies(name):
            self.logger.error(f"DependÃªncias nÃ£o satisfeitas para {name}")
            info.state = ModuleState.FAILED
            return False
        
        try:
            self.logger.debug(f"Iniciando mÃ³dulo: {name}")
            
            module = info.module
            if hasattr(module, "start"):
                if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(module.start):
                    await module.start()
                else:
                    module.start()
            
            info.state = ModuleState.RUNNING
            info.started_at = datetime.now()
            info.error = None
            
            self.logger.info(f"MÃ³dulo iniciado: {name}")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            info.state = ModuleState.FAILED
            info.error = str(e)
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao iniciar {name}: {e}")
            return False
    
    def _check_dependencies(self, name: str) -> bool:
        """Verifica se todas as dependÃªncias estÃ£o running."""
        info = self._modules[name]
        
        for dep in info.dependencies:
            if dep not in self._modules:
                self.logger.error(f"DependÃªncia {dep} nÃ£o registrada para {name}")
                return False
            
            dep_info = self._modules[dep]
            if dep_info.state != ModuleState.RUNNING:
                self.logger.error(f"DependÃªncia {dep} nÃ£o estÃ¡ running para {name}")
                return False
        
        return True
    
    async def pause(self, name: str) -> bool:
        """Pausa um mÃ³dulo."""
        if name not in self._modules:
            return False
        
        info = self._modules[name]
        if info.state != ModuleState.RUNNING:
            return False
        
        try:
            module = info.module
            if hasattr(module, "pause"):
                if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(module.pause):
                    await module.pause()
                else:
                    module.pause()
            
            info.state = ModuleState.PAUSED
            self.logger.info(f"MÃ³dulo pausado: {name}")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao pausar {name}: {e}")
            return False
    
    async def resume(self, name: str) -> bool:
        """Retoma um mÃ³dulo pausado."""
        if name not in self._modules:
            return False
        
        info = self._modules[name]
        if info.state != ModuleState.PAUSED:
            return False
        
        try:
            module = info.module
            if hasattr(module, "resume"):
                if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(module.resume):
                    await module.resume()
                else:
                    module.resume()
            
            info.state = ModuleState.RUNNING
            self.logger.info(f"MÃ³dulo retomado: {name}")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao retomar {name}: {e}")
            return False
    
    async def stop(self, name: str) -> bool:
        """Para um mÃ³dulo."""
        if name not in self._modules:
            return False
        
        info = self._modules[name]
        
        try:
            self.logger.debug(f"Parando mÃ³dulo: {name}")
            
            module = info.module
            if hasattr(module, "shutdown"):
                if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(module.shutdown):
                    await module.shutdown()
                else:
                    module.shutdown()
            
            info.state = ModuleState.STOPPED
            info.stopped_at = datetime.now()
            
            self.logger.info(f"MÃ³dulo parado: {name}")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao parar {name}: {e}")
            info.state = ModuleState.FAILED
            info.error = str(e)
            return False
    
    async def stop_all(self) -> None:
        """Para todos os mÃ³dulos (ordem reversa)."""
        self.logger.info("Parando todos os mÃ³dulos...")
        
        for name in reversed(self._startup_order):
            await self.stop(name)
    
    async def restart(self, name: str) -> bool:
        """Reinicia um mÃ³dulo."""
        if name not in self._modules:
            return False
        
        if not await self.stop(name):
            return False
        
        # Aguarda um pouco
        await asyncio.sleep(1)
        
        if not await self._initialize_module(name):
            return False
        
        return await self._start_module(name)
    
    def get_state(self, name: str) -> Optional[ModuleState]:
        """ObtÃ©m estado de um mÃ³dulo."""
        info = self._modules.get(name)
        return info.state if info else None
    
    def get_module_info(self, name: str) -> Optional[ModuleInfo]:
        """ObtÃ©m informaÃ§Ãµes de um mÃ³dulo."""
        return self._modules.get(name)
    
    def list_modules(self) -> List[str]:
        """Lista todos os mÃ³dulos registrados."""
        return list(self._modules.keys())
    
    def list_by_state(self, state: ModuleState) -> List[str]:
        """Lista mÃ³dulos por estado."""
        return [name for name, info in self._modules.items() if info.state == state]
    
    def get_status_report(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Gera relatÃ³rio de status."""
        return {
            "total": len(self._modules),
            "states": {
                state.name: len([m for m in self._modules.values() if m.state == state])
                for state in ModuleState
            },
            "modules": {
                name: {
                    "state": info.state.name,
                    "dependencies": info.dependencies,
                    "error": info.error
                }
                for name, info in self._modules.items()
            }
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\core\module_loader.py ====================
"""Carregador automÃ¡tico de mÃ³dulos."""

import importlib
import inspect
import pkgutil
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Type
import sys


class ModuleLoader:
    """
    Carrega mÃ³dulos automaticamente.
    Procura mÃ³dulos em diretÃ³rios e registra no ServiceRegistry.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, logger, service_registry):
        self.logger = logger
        self.registry = service_registry
        
        self._loaded_modules: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._module_paths: List[Path] = []
    
    def add_path(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Adiciona um caminho para scanear."""
        if path.exists() and path.is_dir():
            self._module_paths.append(path)
            self.logger.debug(f"Caminho adicionado: {path}")
    
    def add_package(self, package_name: str) -> None:
        """Adiciona um pacote para scanear."""
        try:
            package = importlib.import_module(package_name)
            if hasattr(package, "__path__"):
                self._module_paths.append(Path(package.__path__[0]))
                self.logger.debug(f"Pacote adicionado: {package_name}")
        except ImportError:
            self.logger.warning(f"Pacote nÃ£o encontrado: {package_name}")
    
    def scan(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Escaneia todos os caminhos e carrega mÃ³dulos.
        Retorna mÃ³dulos carregados.
        """
        self.logger.info("Scanando mÃ³dulos...")
        
        for path in self._module_paths:
            self._scan_directory(path)
        
        self.logger.info(f"Scan concluÃ­do: {len(self._loaded_modules)} mÃ³dulos encontrados")
        return self._loaded_modules
    
    def _scan_directory(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Escaneia um diretÃ³rio por mÃ³dulos Python."""
        if not path.exists():
            return
        
        # Procura por arquivos .py
        for py_file in path.glob("*.py"):
            if py_file.name.startswith("__"):
                continue
            
            module_name = py_file.stem
            self._load_module_from_file(path, module_name)
        
        # Procura por subdiretÃ³rios com __init__.py
        for subdir in path.iterdir():
            if subdir.is_dir() and (subdir / "__init__.py").exists():
                # Ã‰ um pacote
                module_name = subdir.name
                self._load_module_from_package(path, module_name)
    
    def _load_module_from_file(self, path: Path, module_name: str) -> bool:
        """Carrega um mÃ³dulo de um arquivo."""
        try:
            # ConstrÃ³i o nome completo do mÃ³dulo
            full_name = self._build_module_name(path, module_name)
            
            # Importa o mÃ³dulo
            module = importlib.import_module(full_name)
            
            # Registra classes encontradas
            self._register_classes(module)
            
            self._loaded_modules[module_name] = module
            self.logger.debug(f"MÃ³dulo carregado: {module_name}")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao carregar {module_name}: {e}")
            return False
    
    def _load_module_from_package(self, path: Path, package_name: str) -> bool:
        """Carrega um pacote."""
        try:
            full_name = self._build_module_name(path, package_name)
            module = importlib.import_module(full_name)
            
            self._loaded_modules[package_name] = module
            self.logger.debug(f"Pacote carregado: {package_name}")
            
            # Escaneia submÃ³dulos
            if hasattr(module, "__path__"):
                for subpath in module.__path__:
                    self._scan_directory(Path(subpath))
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao carregar pacote {package_name}: {e}")
            return False
    
    def _build_module_name(self, path: Path, module_name: str) -> str:
        """ConstrÃ³i o nome completo do mÃ³dulo."""
        # Remove o caminho base
        rel_path = path.relative_to(Path.cwd())
        
        # Converte para nome de pacote
        parts = list(rel_path.parts)
        
        # Remove 'src' se presente
        if parts and parts[0] == "src":
            parts = parts[1:]
        
        # Adiciona o nome do mÃ³dulo
        parts.append(module_name)
        
        return ".".join(parts)
    
    def _register_classes(self, module: Any) -> None:
        """Registra classes do mÃ³dulo no ServiceRegistry."""
        for name, obj in inspect.getmembers(module):
            if inspect.isclass(obj):
                # Pula classes internas
                if name.startswith("_"):
                    continue
                
                # Verifica se tem um mÃ©todo initialize ou start
                if hasattr(obj, "initialize") or hasattr(obj, "start"):
                    class_name = name.lower()
                    
                    # Tenta instanciar (se possÃ­vel)
                    try:
                        # Verifica se o construtor nÃ£o requer parÃ¢metros obrigatÃ³rios
                        sig = inspect.signature(obj.__init__)
                        params = list(sig.parameters.keys())
                        
                        # Remove self e params com default
                        required = [p for p in params[1:] if sig.parameters[p].default == inspect.Parameter.empty]
                        
                        if len(required) == 0:
                            instance = obj()
                            self.registry.register(class_name, instance)
                            self.logger.debug(f"Classe registrada: {class_name}")
                    except Exception as e:
                        # NÃ£o conseguiu instanciar, apenas registra a classe
                        self.registry.register(class_name, obj)
                        self.logger.debug(f"Classe registrada (como tipo): {class_name}")
    
    def load(self, module_name: str) -> Optional[Any]:
        """Carrega um mÃ³dulo especÃ­fico."""
        try:
            module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
            self._loaded_modules[module_name] = module
            return module
        except ImportError as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao importar {module_name}: {e}")
            return None
    
    def reload(self, module_name: str) -> Optional[Any]:
        """Recarrega um mÃ³dulo."""
        if module_name in sys.modules:
            importlib.reload(sys.modules[module_name])
            return sys.modules[module_name]
        return self.load(module_name)
    
    def unload(self, module_name: str) -> bool:
        """Descarrega um mÃ³dulo."""
        if module_name in sys.modules:
            del sys.modules[module_name]
            self._loaded_modules.pop(module_name, None)
            self.logger.info(f"MÃ³dulo descarregado: {module_name}")
            return True
        return False
    
    def list_modules(self) -> List[str]:
        """Lista mÃ³dulos carregados."""
        return list(self._loaded_modules.keys())

"""Plugin Manager - Sistema de carregamento dinÃ¢mico de mÃ³dulos para EVA OS."""

import asyncio
import importlib
import inspect
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Type, Set, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum, auto
from uuid import uuid4
import hashlib
import json


class PluginStatus(Enum):
    """Status de um plugin."""
    DISCOVERED = auto()
    LOADING = auto()
    LOADED = auto()
    INITIALIZED = auto()
    STARTED = auto()
    PAUSED = auto()
    STOPPED = auto()
    ERROR = auto()
    DEPRECATED = auto()
    INCOMPATIBLE = auto()


class PluginType(Enum):
    """Tipo de plugin."""
    CORE = auto()           # Componentes do Core
    MODULE = auto()         # MÃ³dulo funcional
    AGENT = auto()          # Agente
    TOOL = auto()           # Ferramenta
    INTERFACE = auto()      # Interface
    EXTENSION = auto()      # ExtensÃ£o de terceiros
    LEGACY = auto()         # Compatibilidade


@dataclass
class PluginManifest:
    """Manifesto de um plugin."""
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    name: str = ""
    version: str = "1.0.0"
    description: str = ""
    author: str = "EVA OS"
    
    plugin_type: PluginType = PluginType.MODULE
    
    # DependÃªncias
    requires: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # Nomes de plugins
    requires_version: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)  # Nome -> versÃ£o mÃ­nima
    
    # Compatibilidade
    eva_version_min: str = "1.0.0"
    eva_version_max: str = "99.0.0"
    
    # Entry points
    main_class: Optional[str] = None  # Nome da classe principal
    initialize_func: Optional[str] = None
    start_func: Optional[str] = None
    stop_func: Optional[str] = None
    
    # Metadata
    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    capabilities: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    # Assinatura/VerificaÃ§Ã£o
    checksum: Optional[str] = None
    signature: Optional[str] = None
    
    # Timestamps
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Converte para dicionÃ¡rio."""
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "name": self.name,
            "version": self.version,
            "description": self.description,
            "author": self.author,
            "plugin_type": self.plugin_type.name,
            "requires": self.requires,
            "requires_version": self.requires_version,
            "eva_version_min": self.eva_version_min,
            "eva_version_max": self.eva_version_max,
            "main_class": self.main_class,
            "tags": self.tags,
            "capabilities": self.capabilities,
            "checksum": self.checksum
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> "PluginManifest":
        """Cria a partir de dicionÃ¡rio."""
        return cls(
            id=data.get("id", str(uuid4())),
            name=data.get("name", ""),
            version=data.get("version", "1.0.0"),
            description=data.get("description", ""),
            author=data.get("author", "EVA OS"),
            plugin_type=PluginType[data.get("plugin_type", "MODULE")],
            requires=data.get("requires", []),
            requires_version=data.get("requires_version", {}),
            eva_version_min=data.get("eva_version_min", "1.0.0"),
            eva_version_max=data.get("eva_version_max", "99.0.0"),
            main_class=data.get("main_class"),
            tags=data.get("tags", []),
            capabilities=data.get("capabilities", []),
            checksum=data.get("checksum")
        )


@dataclass
class Plugin:
    """Plugin carregado."""
    manifest: PluginManifest
    path: Path
    module: Optional[Any] = None
    instance: Optional[Any] = None
    status: PluginStatus = PluginStatus.DISCOVERED
    error: Optional[str] = None
    
    # DependÃªncias resolvidas
    dependencies: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    dependents: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    # Timestamps
    discovered_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    loaded_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    initialized_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    started_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    
    # MÃ©tricas
    load_time: float = 0.0
    import_time: float = 0.0
    init_time: float = 0.0
    
    def is_core(self) -> bool:
        """Verifica se Ã© um plugin core."""
        return self.manifest.plugin_type == PluginType.CORE
    
    def is_compatible(self, eva_version: str) -> bool:
        """Verifica compatibilidade com versÃ£o da EVA."""
        # VersÃ£o simplificada - idealmente usar semver
        return True  # TODO: Implementar verificaÃ§Ã£o semver


class PluginManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de Plugins da EVA.
    
    CaracterÃ­sticas:
    - Descoberta automÃ¡tica de mÃ³dulos
    - ResoluÃ§Ã£o de dependÃªncias
    - Carregamento dinÃ¢mico
    - Hot reload
    - Sandbox (futuro)
    - VerificaÃ§Ã£o de assinatura (futuro)
    - Compatibilidade entre versÃµes
    """
    
    def __init__(self, logger, event_bus, config, service_registry):
        self.logger = logger
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.config = config
        self.registry = service_registry
        
        # Plugins
        self._plugins: Dict[str, Plugin] = {}
        self._plugins_by_name: Dict[str, str] = {}  # nome -> id
        
        # Caminhos de scan
        self._scan_paths: List[Path] = []
        
        # PadrÃµes de exclusÃ£o
        self._exclude_patterns = {
            "__pycache__",
            "tests",
            "test",
            "venv",
            ".venv",
            "env",
            ".git",
            ".idea",
            ".vscode",
            "node_modules",
            "dist",
            "build"
        }
        
        # Plugins ignorados
        self._ignored_plugins: Set[str] = set()
        
        # Plugins carregados na ordem
        self._load_order: List[str] = []
        
        # ConfiguraÃ§Ã£o
        self._auto_load = config.get("features.auto_load_plugins", True)
        self._enable_hot_reload = config.get("features.hot_reload", False)
        
        # Estado
        self._is_scanning = False
        self._is_loading = False
        
        self.logger.info("PluginManager inicializado")
    
    def add_scan_path(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Adiciona um caminho para scanear."""
        if path.exists() and path.is_dir():
            self._scan_paths.append(path.resolve())
            self.logger.debug(f"Caminho adicionado: {path}")
        else:
            self.logger.warning(f"Caminho invÃ¡lido: {path}")
    
    def add_exclude_pattern(self, pattern: str) -> None:
        """Adiciona um padrÃ£o de exclusÃ£o."""
        self._exclude_patterns.add(pattern)
    
    def ignore_plugin(self, name: str) -> None:
        """Ignora um plugin especÃ­fico."""
        self._ignored_plugins.add(name)
        self.logger.debug(f"Plugin ignorado: {name}")
    
    async def scan(self) -> Dict[str, Plugin]:
        """
        Escaneia todos os caminhos em busca de plugins.
        Retorna dicionÃ¡rio de plugins encontrados.
        """
        if self._is_scanning:
            self.logger.warning("Scan jÃ¡ em andamento")
            return self._plugins
        
        self._is_scanning = True
        self.logger.info("Iniciando scan de plugins...")
        
        found = {}
        
        for path in self._scan_paths:
            plugins = await self._scan_directory(path)
            found.update(plugins)
        
        self._is_scanning = False
        self.logger.info(f"Scan concluÃ­do: {len(found)} plugins encontrados")
        
        # Atualiza plugins
        for plugin_id, plugin in found.items():
            if plugin_id not in self._plugins:
                self._plugins[plugin_id] = plugin
                self._plugins_by_name[plugin.manifest.name] = plugin_id
            else:
                # Atualiza existente
                existing = self._plugins[plugin_id]
                existing.status = plugin.status
                existing.path = plugin.path
        
        return self._plugins
    
    async def _scan_directory(self, path: Path, parent: Optional[Path] = None) -> Dict[str, Plugin]:
        """Escaneia um diretÃ³rio recursivamente."""
        plugins = {}
        
        try:
            for item in path.iterdir():
                # Verifica exclusÃµes
                if item.name in self._exclude_patterns:
                    continue
                
                if item.name.startswith("."):
                    continue
                
                if item.is_dir():
                    # Verifica se Ã© um pacote Python (tem __init__.py)
                    init_file = item / "__init__.py"
                    if init_file.exists():
                        # Ã‰ um pacote - procura por plugins dentro
                        sub_plugins = await self._scan_directory(item, path)
                        plugins.update(sub_plugins)
                    else:
                        # DiretÃ³rio normal - pode ter arquivos .py
                        for py_file in item.glob("*.py"):
                            if py_file.name.startswith("__"):
                                continue
                            plugin = await self._discover_plugin(py_file, item)
                            if plugin:
                                plugins[plugin.manifest.id] = plugin
                
                elif item.is_file() and item.suffix == ".py":
                    if item.name.startswith("__"):
                        continue
                    plugin = await self._discover_plugin(item, parent or path)
                    if plugin:
                        plugins[plugin.manifest.id] = plugin
        
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao scanear {path}: {e}")
        
        return plugins
    
    async def _discover_plugin(self, file_path: Path, base_path: Path) -> Optional[Plugin]:
        """
        Descobre um plugin a partir de um arquivo Python.
        Procura por classes que podem ser plugins.
        """
        try:
            # Calcula nome do mÃ³dulo
            rel_path = file_path.relative_to(base_path)
            module_parts = []
            
            # ConstrÃ³i o caminho do mÃ³dulo a partir da estrutura
            # ex: src/agents/general_agent.py -> agents.general_agent
            current = file_path.parent
            while current != base_path and current.name != "src":
                module_parts.insert(0, current.name)
                current = current.parent
            
            # Adiciona o nome do arquivo (sem .py)
            module_parts.append(file_path.stem)
            
            # Se o mÃ³dulo estÃ¡ dentro de src, remove src
            if module_parts and module_parts[0] == "src":
                module_parts = module_parts[1:]
            
            module_name = ".".join(module_parts)
            
            # Pula se jÃ¡ estÃ¡ carregado ou ignorado
            if module_name in self._ignored_plugins:
                return None
            
            # Tenta importar para descobrir a classe principal
            try:
                module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.debug(f"NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel importar {module_name}: {e}")
                return None
            
            # Procura por classes que podem ser plugins
            plugin_class = self._find_plugin_class(module)
            
            if plugin_class:
                # Cria manifest
                manifest = self._create_manifest_from_class(plugin_class, module_name, file_path)
                
                if manifest:
                    plugin = Plugin(
                        manifest=manifest,
                        path=file_path,
                        module=module,
                        status=PluginStatus.DISCOVERED
                    )
                    return plugin
            
            return None
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.debug(f"Erro ao descobrir plugin {file_path}: {e}")
            return None
    
    def _find_plugin_class(self, module: Any) -> Optional[Type]:
        """Encontra a classe principal do plugin."""
        # Procura por classes que sÃ£o subclasses de Plugin ou tÃªm atributos especÃ­ficos
        for name, obj in inspect.getmembers(module):
            if name.startswith("_"):
                continue
            
            if inspect.isclass(obj):
                # Verifica se Ã© uma classe que parece ser um plugin
                # CritÃ©rios:
                # 1. Tem mÃ©todo initialize ou start
                # 2. Ã‰ uma classe concreta (nÃ£o abstrata)
                # 3. NÃ£o Ã© uma classe interna
                
                if hasattr(obj, "initialize") or hasattr(obj, "start"):
                    # Verifica se tem atributos de plugin
                    if hasattr(obj, "__plugin_name__") or hasattr(obj, "__plugin_version__"):
                        return obj
                    
                    # Ou verifica se Ã© uma classe que pode ser instanciada
                    try:
                        sig = inspect.signature(obj.__init__)
                        params = list(sig.parameters.keys())
                        required = [p for p in params[1:] if sig.parameters[p].default == inspect.Parameter.empty]
                        
                        # Se nÃ£o requer parÃ¢metros ou requer apenas serviÃ§os injetÃ¡veis
                        if len(required) <= 2:  # Pode ter event_bus, logger, config
                            return obj
                    except Exception:
                        pass
        
        return None
    
    def _create_manifest_from_class(self, cls: Type, module_name: str, file_path: Path) -> Optional[PluginManifest]:
        """Cria um manifesto a partir de uma classe."""
        try:
            manifest = PluginManifest(
                name=getattr(cls, "__plugin_name__", module_name),
                version=getattr(cls, "__plugin_version__", "1.0.0"),
                description=getattr(cls, "__plugin_description__", ""),
                author=getattr(cls, "__plugin_author__", "EVA OS"),
                main_class=cls.__name__,
                plugin_type=self._detect_plugin_type(cls, module_name)
            )
            
            # Detecta dependÃªncias
            if hasattr(cls, "__plugin_requires__"):
                manifest.requires = cls.__plugin_requires__
            
            # Detecta tags
            if hasattr(cls, "__plugin_tags__"):
                manifest.tags = cls.__plugin_tags__
            
            # Detecta capabilities
            if hasattr(cls, "__plugin_capabilities__"):
                manifest.capabilities = cls.__plugin_capabilities__
            
            return manifest
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao criar manifesto para {cls.__name__}: {e}")
            return None
    
    def _detect_plugin_type(self, cls: Type, module_name: str) -> PluginType:
        """Detecta o tipo do plugin baseado no nome e classe."""
        type_map = {
            "agent": PluginType.AGENT,
            "tool": PluginType.TOOL,
            "interface": PluginType.INTERFACE,
            "core": PluginType.CORE,
            "memory": PluginType.MODULE,
            "knowledge": PluginType.MODULE,
            "planner": PluginType.MODULE,
            "consciousness": PluginType.MODULE
        }
        
        # Verifica por nome
        module_lower = module_name.lower()
        for key, plugin_type in type_map.items():
            if key in module_lower:
                return plugin_type
        
        # Verifica por mÃ©todos
        if hasattr(cls, "execute") or hasattr(cls, "run"):
            return PluginType.TOOL
        
        if hasattr(cls, "process") or hasattr(cls, "decide"):
            return PluginType.AGENT
        
        return PluginType.MODULE
    
    async def load_all(self) -> Dict[str, bool]:
        """
        Carrega todos os plugins descobertos.
        Resolve dependÃªncias e carrega na ordem correta.
        """
        if self._is_loading:
            self.logger.warning("Carregamento jÃ¡ em andamento")
            return {}
        
        self._is_loading = True
        self.logger.info("Carregando plugins...")
        
        results = {}
        
        # 1. Resolve dependÃªncias
        self._resolve_dependencies()
        
        # 2. Ordena topologicamente
        self._load_order = self._topological_sort()
        
        # 3. Carrega na ordem
        for plugin_id in self._load_order:
            plugin = self._plugins.get(plugin_id)
            if plugin:
                success = await self._load_plugin(plugin)
                results[plugin.manifest.name] = success
                
                # Publica evento
                await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                    "PLUGIN_LOADED" if success else "PLUGIN_LOAD_FAILED",
                    {
                        "plugin": plugin.manifest.name,
                        "version": plugin.manifest.version,
                        "success": success,
                        "error": plugin.error if plugin.error else None
                    },
                    source="plugin_manager"
                )
        
        self._is_loading = False
        self.logger.info(f"Carregamento concluÃ­do: {sum(results.values())}/{len(results)} plugins")
        
        return results
    
    async def _load_plugin(self, plugin: Plugin) -> bool:
        """Carrega um plugin especÃ­fico."""
        if plugin.status in [PluginStatus.LOADED, PluginStatus.INITIALIZED, PluginStatus.STARTED]:
            return True
        
        if plugin.manifest.name in self._ignored_plugins:
            plugin.status = PluginStatus.STOPPED
            plugin.error = "Plugin ignorado"
            return False
        
        try:
            plugin.status = PluginStatus.LOADING
            
            # Verifica dependÃªncias
            for dep_name in plugin.manifest.requires:
                if dep_name not in self._plugins_by_name:
                    plugin.status = PluginStatus.ERROR
                    plugin.error = f"DependÃªncia nÃ£o encontrada: {dep_name}"
                    self.logger.error(f"Plugin {plugin.manifest.name}: {plugin.error}")
                    return False
                
                dep_id = self._plugins_by_name[dep_name]
                dep = self._plugins.get(dep_id)
                if dep and dep.status not in [PluginStatus.LOADED, PluginStatus.INITIALIZED, PluginStatus.STARTED]:
                    plugin.status = PluginStatus.ERROR
                    plugin.error = f"DependÃªncia nÃ£o carregada: {dep_name}"
                    self.logger.error(f"Plugin {plugin.manifest.name}: {plugin.error}")
                    return False
            
            # Importa o mÃ³dulo se ainda nÃ£o foi
            if not plugin.module:
                # ReconstrÃ³i nome do mÃ³dulo
                module_name = self._get_module_name_from_path(plugin.path)
                plugin.module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
            
            # Encontra a classe principal
            if plugin.manifest.main_class and plugin.module:
                cls = getattr(plugin.module, plugin.manifest.main_class, None)
                if cls:
                    # Instancia a classe
                    instance = await self._instantiate_plugin(cls, plugin)
                    if instance:
                        plugin.instance = instance
                        plugin.status = PluginStatus.LOADED
                        plugin.loaded_at = datetime.now()
                        
                        self.logger.info(f"Plugin carregado: {plugin.manifest.name} v{plugin.manifest.version}")
                        
                        # Registra no ServiceRegistry
                        self.registry.register(
                            plugin.manifest.name,
                            instance,
                            plugin.manifest.requires
                        )
                        
                        # Inicializa
                        if hasattr(instance, "initialize"):
                            try:
                                if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(instance.initialize):
                                    await instance.initialize()
                                else:
                                    instance.initialize()
                                plugin.status = PluginStatus.INITIALIZED
                                plugin.initialized_at = datetime.now()
                            except Exception as e:
                                self.logger.error(f"Erro ao inicializar {plugin.manifest.name}: {e}")
                                plugin.error = str(e)
                                plugin.status = PluginStatus.ERROR
                                return False
                        
                        # Inicia
                        if hasattr(instance, "start"):
                            try:
                                if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(instance.start):
                                    await instance.start()
                                else:
                                    instance.start()
                                plugin.status = PluginStatus.STARTED
                                plugin.started_at = datetime.now()
                            except Exception as e:
                                self.logger.error(f"Erro ao iniciar {plugin.manifest.name}: {e}")
                                plugin.error = str(e)
                                plugin.status = PluginStatus.ERROR
                                return False
                        
                        return True
            
            plugin.status = PluginStatus.ERROR
            plugin.error = "Classe principal nÃ£o encontrada"
            return False
            
        except Exception as e:
            plugin.status = PluginStatus.ERROR
            plugin.error = str(e)
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao carregar plugin {plugin.manifest.name}: {e}")
            return False
    
    async def _instantiate_plugin(self, cls: Type, plugin: Plugin) -> Optional[Any]:
        """Instancia um plugin com injeÃ§Ã£o de dependÃªncias."""
        try:
            sig = inspect.signature(cls.__init__)
            params = list(sig.parameters.keys())
            
            kwargs = {}
            for param in params[1:]:  # Pula self
                param_type = sig.parameters[param].annotation
                if param_type != inspect.Parameter.empty:
                    # Tenta resolver pelo tipo
                    type_name = param_type.__name__.lower()
                    if self.registry.exists(type_name):
                        kwargs[param] = self.registry.get(type_name)
                    elif self.registry.exists(param):
                        kwargs[param] = self.registry.get(param)
                elif param == "event_bus":
                    kwargs[param] = self.registry.get("event_bus")
                elif param == "logger":
                    kwargs[param] = self.registry.get("logger")
                elif param == "config":
                    kwargs[param] = self.registry.get("config")
                elif param == "service_registry":
                    kwargs[param] = self.registry.get("service_registry")
            
            return cls(**kwargs)
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao instanciar {cls.__name__}: {e}")
            return None
    
    def _get_module_name_from_path(self, path: Path) -> str:
        """Converte caminho para nome de mÃ³dulo."""
        # Remove a extensÃ£o
        name = path.stem
        
        # Tenta descobrir o caminho relativo
        parts = []
        current = path.parent
        while current.name and current.name not in ["src", "eva_os"]:
            parts.insert(0, current.name)
            current = current.parent
        
        if parts and parts[0] == "src":
            parts = parts[1:]
        
        parts.append(name)
        return ".".join(parts)
    
    def _resolve_dependencies(self) -> None:
        """Resolve dependÃªncias entre plugins."""
        for plugin in self._plugins.values():
            plugin.dependencies = []
            for dep_name in plugin.manifest.requires:
                if dep_name in self._plugins_by_name:
                    dep_id = self._plugins_by_name[dep_name]
                    plugin.dependencies.append(dep_id)
        
        # Atualiza dependents
        for plugin in self._plugins.values():
            plugin.dependents = []
            for other in self._plugins.values():
                if plugin.manifest.id in other.dependencies:
                    plugin.dependents.append(other.manifest.id)
    
    def _topological_sort(self) -> List[str]:
        """
        Ordena plugins topologicamente por dependÃªncias.
        Plugins com menos dependÃªncias sÃ£o carregados primeiro.
        """
        # Kahn's algorithm
        graph = {}
        in_degree = {}
        
        for plugin_id, plugin in self._plugins.items():
            graph[plugin_id] = plugin.dependencies.copy()
            in_degree[plugin_id] = len(plugin.dependencies)
        
        # Fila com plugins que nÃ£o tÃªm dependÃªncias
        queue = [pid for pid, deg in in_degree.items() if deg == 0]
        result = []
        
        while queue:
            # Ordena por tipo (Core primeiro)
            queue.sort(key=lambda pid: 0 if self._plugins[pid].is_core() else 1)
            plugin_id = queue.pop(0)
            result.append(plugin_id)
            
            # Remove dependÃªncias de outros
            for other_id, deps in graph.items():
                if plugin_id in deps:
                    deps.remove(plugin_id)
                    in_degree[other_id] -= 1
                    if in_degree[other_id] == 0:
                        queue.append(other_id)
        
        # Verifica ciclos
        if len(result) != len(self._plugins):
            self.logger.warning("Ciclo de dependÃªncia detectado!")
            # Tenta carregar mesmo assim (ordem atual)
            return list(self._plugins.keys())
        
        return result
    
    async def reload(self, plugin_name: str) -> bool:
        """Recarrega um plugin especÃ­fico (hot reload)."""
        if not self._enable_hot_reload:
            self.logger.warning("Hot reload desabilitado")
            return False
        
        if plugin_name not in self._plugins_by_name:
            self.logger.warning(f"Plugin nÃ£o encontrado: {plugin_name}")
            return False
        
        plugin_id = self._plugins_by_name[plugin_name]
        plugin = self._plugins[plugin_id]
        
        # Para o plugin
        if plugin.instance and hasattr(plugin.instance, "shutdown"):
            try:
                if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(plugin.instance.shutdown):
                    await plugin.instance.shutdown()
                else:
                    plugin.instance.shutdown()
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro ao parar {plugin_name}: {e}")
        
        # Recarrega o mÃ³dulo
        if plugin.module:
            try:
                importlib.reload(plugin.module)
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro ao recarregar mÃ³dulo {plugin_name}: {e}")
                return False
        
        # Recarrega o plugin
        plugin.status = PluginStatus.DISCOVERED
        plugin.loaded_at = None
        plugin.initialized_at = None
        plugin.started_at = None
        plugin.instance = None
        
        return await self._load_plugin(plugin)
    
    def get_plugin(self, name: str) -> Optional[Plugin]:
        """ObtÃ©m um plugin pelo nome."""
        plugin_id = self._plugins_by_name.get(name)
        return self._plugins.get(plugin_id) if plugin_id else None
    
    def get_plugins(self, status: Optional[PluginStatus] = None) -> List[Plugin]:
        """Lista plugins, opcionalmente filtrando por status."""
        if status:
            return [p for p in self._plugins.values() if p.status == status]
        return list(self._plugins.values())
    
    def get_status_report(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Gera relatÃ³rio de status dos plugins."""
        return {
            "total": len(self._plugins),
            "by_status": {
                status.name: len([p for p in self._plugins.values() if p.status == status])
                for status in PluginStatus
            },
            "plugins": {
                name: {
                    "version": p.manifest.version,
                    "status": p.status.name,
                    "type": p.manifest.plugin_type.name,
                    "error": p.error,
                    "dependencies": p.manifest.requires,
                    "load_time": p.load_time
                }
                for name, p in self._plugins.items()
            },
            "load_order": self._load_order,
            "scan_paths": [str(p) for p in self._scan_paths]
        }
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga todos os plugins (ordem reversa)."""
        self.logger.info("Desligando plugins...")
        
        # Para na ordem reversa
        for plugin_id in reversed(self._load_order):
            plugin = self._plugins.get(plugin_id)
            if plugin and plugin.instance and hasattr(plugin.instance, "shutdown"):
                try:
                    if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(plugin.instance.shutdown):
                        await plugin.instance.shutdown()
                    else:
                        plugin.instance.shutdown()
                    plugin.status = PluginStatus.STOPPED
                    self.logger.debug(f"Plugin parado: {plugin.manifest.name}")
                except Exception as e:
                    self.logger.error(f"Erro ao parar {plugin.manifest.name}: {e}")
        
        self.logger.info("Plugins desligados")
