"""Registro de serviÃ§os do Core."""

from enum import Enum, auto
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Type


class ServiceStatus(Enum):
    """Status de um serviÃ§o."""
    REGISTERED = auto()
    INITIALIZED = auto()
    STARTING = auto()
    RUNNING = auto()
    PAUSED = auto()
    STOPPING = auto()
    STOPPED = auto()
    FAILED = auto()


class Service:
    """DefiniÃ§Ã£o de um serviÃ§o registrado."""
    
    def __init__(self, name: str, instance: Any, dependencies: List[str] = None):
        self.name = name
        self.instance = instance
        self.dependencies = dependencies or []
        self.status = ServiceStatus.REGISTERED
        self.service_type = type(instance).__name__
        self.version = getattr(instance, "version", "0.0.1")
    
    def __repr__(self):
        return f"Service({self.name}, {self.service_type}, {self.status.name})"


class ServiceRegistry:
    """
    CatÃ¡logo central da EVA.
    Responde: Onde estÃ¡ o MemoryManager? Onde estÃ¡ o Planner?
    """
    
    def __init__(self, logger):
        self.logger = logger
        self._services: Dict[str, Service] = {}
        self._aliases: Dict[str, str] = {}
    
    def register(self, name: str, instance: Any, dependencies: List[str] = None) -> None:
        """
        Registra um serviÃ§o.
        
        Args:
            name: Nome Ãºnico do serviÃ§o
            instance: InstÃ¢ncia do serviÃ§o
            dependencies: Lista de nomes de serviÃ§os dos quais depende
        """
        if name in self._services:
            self.logger.warning(f"ServiÃ§o {name} jÃ¡ registrado. Substituindo...")
        
        service = Service(name, instance, dependencies)
        self._services[name] = service
        self.logger.info(f"ServiÃ§o registrado: {name} ({service.service_type})")
    
    def register_alias(self, alias: str, target: str) -> None:
        """Registra um alias para um serviÃ§o."""
        if target not in self._services:
            raise ValueError(f"ServiÃ§o alvo {target} nÃ£o encontrado")
        self._aliases[alias] = target
        self.logger.debug(f"Alias registrado: {alias} -> {target}")
    
    def unregister(self, name: str) -> bool:
        """Remove um serviÃ§o do registro."""
        if name in self._services:
            del self._services[name]
            # Remove aliases
            self._aliases = {k: v for k, v in self._aliases.items() if v != name}
            self.logger.info(f"ServiÃ§o removido: {name}")
            return True
        return False
    
    def get(self, name: str) -> Optional[Any]:
        """
        ObtÃ©m um serviÃ§o pelo nome.
        Suporta aliases.
        """
        # Resolve alias
        actual_name = self._aliases.get(name, name)
        
        service = self._services.get(actual_name)
        if service:
            return service.instance
        return None
    
    def get_service(self, name: str) -> Optional[Service]:
        """ObtÃ©m o objeto Service completo."""
        actual_name = self._aliases.get(name, name)
        return self._services.get(actual_name)
    
    def exists(self, name: str) -> bool:
        """Verifica se um serviÃ§o existe."""
        actual_name = self._aliases.get(name, name)
        return actual_name in self._services
    
    def list(self) -> List[str]:
        """Lista todos os nomes de serviÃ§os registrados."""
        return list(self._services.keys())
    
    def list_services(self) -> List[Service]:
        """Lista todos os serviÃ§os registrados."""
        return list(self._services.values())
    
    def list_by_status(self, status: ServiceStatus) -> List[Service]:
        """Lista serviÃ§os por status."""
        return [s for s in self._services.values() if s.status == status]
    
    def get_status(self, name: str) -> Optional[ServiceStatus]:
        """ObtÃ©m status de um serviÃ§o."""
        service = self.get_service(name)
        return service.status if service else None
    
    def set_status(self, name: str, status: ServiceStatus) -> bool:
        """Define status de um serviÃ§o."""
        service = self.get_service(name)
        if not service:
            return False
        service.status = status
        self.logger.debug(f"Status do serviÃ§o {name}: {status.name}")
        return True
    
    def get_dependencies(self, name: str) -> List[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m dependÃªncias de um serviÃ§o."""
        service = self.get_service(name)
        return service.dependencies if service else []
    
    def get_dependents(self, name: str) -> List[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m serviÃ§os que dependem de um serviÃ§o."""
        dependents = []
        for service in self._services.values():
            if name in service.dependencies:
                dependents.append(service.name)
        return dependents
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Limpa todos os serviÃ§os."""
        self._services.clear()
        self._aliases.clear()
        self.logger.info("ServiceRegistry limpo")


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\core\state_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de estado global do Core."""

from enum import Enum, auto
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
import asyncio


class SystemState(Enum):
    """Estados globais da EVA."""
    OFFLINE = auto()
    BOOTING = auto()
    IDLE = auto()
    LISTENING = auto()
    THINKING = auto()
    PLANNING = auto()
    EXECUTING = auto()
    LEARNING = auto()
    UPDATING = auto()
    ERROR = auto()
    SHUTDOWN = auto()


@dataclass
class StateTransition:
    """Registro de transiÃ§Ã£o de estado."""
    from_state: SystemState
    to_state: SystemState
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    reason: str = ""
    source: str = "system"
    data: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


class StateManager:
    """
    Gerencia o estado global da EVA.
    A EVA inteira possui apenas UM estado global.
    Quando muda o estado, publica STATE_CHANGED.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, logger, event_bus):
        self.logger = logger
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        
        self._current_state = SystemState.OFFLINE
        self._previous_state: Optional[SystemState] = None
        self._transitions: List[StateTransition] = []
        self._max_history = 1000
        
        # Estados que sÃ£o "ocupados"
        self._busy_states = {
            SystemState.THINKING,
            SystemState.PLANNING,
            SystemState.EXECUTING,
            SystemState.LEARNING,
            SystemState.UPDATING
        }
        
        # Estados que sÃ£o "idle"
        self._idle_states = {
            SystemState.IDLE,
            SystemState.LISTENING
        }
    
    def set_state(self, new_state: SystemState, reason: str = "", 
                  source: str = "system", data: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> bool:
        """
        Define um novo estado.
        Retorna True se a transiÃ§Ã£o foi vÃ¡lida.
        """
        # Verifica se Ã© uma transiÃ§Ã£o vÃ¡lida
        if not self._can_transition(new_state):
            self.logger.warning(f"TransiÃ§Ã£o invÃ¡lida: {self._current_state} -> {new_state}")
            return False
        
        # Executa transiÃ§Ã£o
        self._previous_state = self._current_state
        self._current_state = new_state
        
        # Registra transiÃ§Ã£o
        transition = StateTransition(
            from_state=self._previous_state,
            to_state=new_state,
            reason=reason,
            source=source,
            data=data or {}
        )
        self._transitions.append(transition)
        
        # Limita histÃ³rico
        if len(self._transitions) > self._max_history:
            self._transitions = self._transitions[-self._max_history:]
        
        self.logger.info(f"Estado alterado: {self._previous_state.name} -> {new_state.name} ({reason})")
        
        # Publica evento
        asyncio.create_task(self._publish_state_changed(transition))
        
        return True
    
    async def _publish_state_changed(self, transition: StateTransition) -> None:
        """Publica evento de mudanÃ§a de estado."""
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "STATE_CHANGED",
            {
                "previous": transition.from_state.name,
                "current": transition.to_state.name,
                "reason": transition.reason,
                "source": transition.source,
                "timestamp": transition.timestamp.isoformat(),
                "data": transition.data
            },
            source="state_manager"
        )
    
    def _can_transition(self, new_state: SystemState) -> bool:
        """Verifica se a transiÃ§Ã£o Ã© vÃ¡lida."""
        # Regras de transiÃ§Ã£o
        valid = {
            SystemState.OFFLINE: [SystemState.BOOTING],
            SystemState.BOOTING: [SystemState.IDLE, SystemState.ERROR],
            SystemState.IDLE: [SystemState.LISTENING, SystemState.UPDATING, 
                              SystemState.SHUTDOWN, SystemState.ERROR],
            SystemState.LISTENING: [SystemState.THINKING, SystemState.IDLE, 
                                   SystemState.SHUTDOWN, SystemState.ERROR],
            SystemState.THINKING: [SystemState.PLANNING, SystemState.LISTENING,
                                  SystemState.ERROR, SystemState.IDLE],
            SystemState.PLANNING: [SystemState.EXECUTING, SystemState.THINKING,
                                  SystemState.ERROR, SystemState.IDLE],
            SystemState.EXECUTING: [SystemState.LEARNING, SystemState.LISTENING,
                                   SystemState.ERROR, SystemState.IDLE],
            SystemState.LEARNING: [SystemState.IDLE, SystemState.LISTENING,
                                  SystemState.ERROR],
            SystemState.UPDATING: [SystemState.IDLE, SystemState.ERROR],
            SystemState.ERROR: [SystemState.BOOTING, SystemState.SHUTDOWN],
            SystemState.SHUTDOWN: [SystemState.OFFLINE]
        }
        
        return new_state in valid.get(self._current_state, [])
    
    def get_state(self) -> SystemState:
        """ObtÃ©m o estado atual."""
        return self._current_state
    
    def get_previous_state(self) -> Optional[SystemState]:
        """ObtÃ©m o estado anterior."""
        return self._previous_state
    
    def get_state_name(self) -> str:
        """ObtÃ©m o nome do estado atual."""
        return self._current_state.name
    
    def get_history(self, limit: int = 50) -> List[StateTransition]:
        """ObtÃ©m histÃ³rico de transiÃ§Ãµes."""
        return self._transitions[-limit:]
    
    def is_busy(self) -> bool:
        """Verifica se o sistema estÃ¡ ocupado."""
        return self._current_state in self._busy_states
    
    def is_idle(self) -> bool:
        """Verifica se o sistema estÃ¡ ocioso."""
        return self._current_state in self._idle_states
    
    def is_ready(self) -> bool:
        """Verifica se o sistema estÃ¡ pronto para interaÃ§Ãµes."""
        return self._current_state in [SystemState.IDLE, SystemState.LISTENING]
    
    def can_accept_input(self) -> bool:
        """Verifica se o sistema pode aceitar entrada do usuÃ¡rio."""
        return self._current_state in [
            SystemState.IDLE,
            SystemState.LISTENING,
            SystemState.THINKING
        ]

"""Core - Espinha dorsal da EVA OS."""

from src.core.event_bus import EventBus, Event, EventPriority
from src.core.service_registry import ServiceRegistry, Service, ServiceStatus
from src.core.dependency_container import DependencyContainer
from src.core.lifecycle_manager import LifecycleManager, ModuleState, ModuleInfo
from src.core.module_loader import ModuleLoader
from src.core.state_manager import StateManager, SystemState, StateTransition
from src.core.health_manager import HealthManager
from src.core.plugin_manager import PluginManager, Plugin, PluginManifest, PluginStatus, PluginType


class Core:
    """
    Core da EVA OS.
    Fornece toda a infraestrutura base.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, logger, config):
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        # 1. EventBus (primeiro, pois tudo depende dele)
        self.event_bus = EventBus(logger)
        
        # 2. ServiceRegistry
        self.service_registry = ServiceRegistry(logger)
        
        # 3. DependencyContainer
        self.container = DependencyContainer(logger, self.service_registry)
        
        # 4. StateManager
        self.state_manager = StateManager(logger, self.event_bus)
        
        # 5. LifecycleManager
        self.lifecycle_manager = LifecycleManager(logger, self.event_bus, self.service_registry)
        
        # 6. PluginManager (substitui ModuleLoader)
        self.plugin_manager = PluginManager(logger, self.event_bus, config, self.service_registry)
        
        # 7. HealthManager
        self.health_manager = HealthManager(logger, self.event_bus, config)
        
        # Registra serviÃ§os core
        self._register_core_services()
    
    def _register_core_services(self) -> None:
        """Registra serviÃ§os do Core."""
        self.service_registry.register("event_bus", self.event_bus)
        self.service_registry.register("service_registry", self.service_registry)
        self.service_registry.register("container", self.container)
        self.service_registry.register("state_manager", self.state_manager)
        self.service_registry.register("lifecycle_manager", self.lifecycle_manager)
        self.service_registry.register("plugin_manager", self.plugin_manager)
        self.service_registry.register("health_manager", self.health_manager)
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o Core."""
        self.logger.info("Inicializando Core...")
        
        # 1. EventBus
        await self.event_bus.initialize()
        
        # 2. HealthManager
        await self.health_manager.initialize()
        
        # 3. StateManager
        self.state_manager.set_state(SystemState.BOOTING, "Core inicializando", "core")
        
        self.logger.info("Core inicializado")
    
    async def start(self) -> None:
        """Inicia o Core."""
        self.logger.info("Iniciando Core...")
        
        # Muda para IDLE
        self.state_manager.set_state(SystemState.IDLE, "Core pronto", "core")
        
        self.logger.info("Core pronto")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o Core."""
        self.logger.info("Desligando Core...")
        
        # Muda para SHUTDOWN
        self.state_manager.set_state(SystemState.SHUTDOWN, "Core desligando", "core")
        
        # Desliga componentes (ordem reversa)
        await self.plugin_manager.shutdown()
        await self.health_manager.shutdown()
        await self.event_bus.shutdown()
        
        self.logger.info("Core desligado")


__all__ = [

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

"""
Emotion Engine - Gerencia o estado emocional da EVA e detecta sentimentos do usuÃ¡rio.
"""

import json
import os
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
from collections import deque

# ðŸ”¥ CORRIGIDO: Import absoluto (em vez de relativo)
from src.utils.logger import Logger


class EmotionEngine:
    """
    Motor de emoÃ§Ãµes da EVA.
    - Detecta sentimento do usuÃ¡rio
    - MantÃ©m o humor da EVA
    - Influencia o tom das respostas
    """
    
    SENTIMENT_KEYWORDS = {
        "positive": [
            "feliz", "alegre", "Ã³timo", "excelente", "maravilhoso", "bom", "boa",
            "amor", "gosto", "adoro", "obrigado", "valeu", "legal", "incrÃ­vel",
            "perfeito", "sensacional", "fantÃ¡stico", "show", "top", "parabÃ©ns"
        ],
        "negative": [
            "triste", "chateado", "ruim", "pÃ©ssimo", "horrÃ­vel", "terrÃ­vel",
            "odeio", "detesto", "problema", "erro", "falha", "cansado",
            "estressado", "preocupado", "ansioso", "frustrado", "puto", "raiva",
            "lamento", "sinto muito", "desculpa", "infeliz", "aborrecido"
        ],
        "tired": [
            "cansaÃ§o", "sono", "exausto", "esgotado", "dormir", "cama",
            "preguiÃ§a", "desÃ¢nimo", "sem energia", "morrendo"
        ],
        "curious": [
            "como", "por que", "quando", "onde", "quem", "qual",
            "explica", "ensina", "mostra", "conta", "detalhe", "curioso"
        ]
    }
    
    MOODS = ["neutro", "feliz", "triste", "curioso", "cansado", "animado", "reflexivo"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = Logger.get_logger() if logger is None else logger
        self.config = config or {}
        
        self.current_mood = "neutro"
        self.current_sentiment = 0.0
        self.current_energy = 0.8
        self.emotion_history = deque(maxlen=10)
        self.last_interaction = None
        self.last_user_sentiment = 0.0
        
        self.persist_file = config.get("emotion_persist_file", "data/emotion_state.json") if config else "data/emotion_state.json"
        self._load_state()
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [EmotionEngine] Inicializado")
        self.logger.info(f"   Mood: {self.current_mood}, Sentiment: {self.current_sentiment:.2f}")
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [EmotionEngine] Pronto")
    
    async def process(self, context: Dict[str, Any], memory: Dict[str, Any] = None,
                      knowledge: Dict[str, Any] = None, plan: Dict[str, Any] = None,
                      reasoning: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        user_message = context.get("message", "") if context else ""
        user_id = context.get("user_id", "unknown") if context else "unknown"
        
        user_sentiment = self._detect_sentiment(user_message)
        user_mood = self._classify_mood(user_message)
        
        self.last_user_sentiment = user_sentiment
        self.last_interaction = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "message": user_message[:100],
            "sentiment": user_sentiment,
            "mood": user_mood
        }
        
        self._update_mood(user_sentiment, user_mood)
        
        self.emotion_history.append({
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "user_sentiment": user_sentiment,
            "user_mood": user_mood,
            "eva_mood": self.current_mood,
            "eva_energy": self.current_energy
        })
        
        self._save_state()
        tone = self._determine_tone()
        
        return {
            "user_sentiment": user_sentiment,
            "user_mood": user_mood,
            "eva_mood": self.current_mood,
            "eva_energy": self.current_energy,
            "tone": tone,
            "emotion_history": list(self.emotion_history),
            "last_interaction": self.last_interaction
        }
    
    def _detect_sentiment(self, text: str) -> float:
        if not text:
            return 0.0
        text_lower = text.lower()
        positive_count = sum(1 for w in self.SENTIMENT_KEYWORDS["positive"] if w in text_lower)
        negative_count = sum(1 for w in self.SENTIMENT_KEYWORDS["negative"] if w in text_lower)
        if positive_count + negative_count == 0:
            return 0.0
        return max(-1.0, min(1.0, (positive_count - negative_count) / (positive_count + negative_count + 0.1)))
    
    def _classify_mood(self, text: str) -> str:
        if not text:
            return "neutro"
        text_lower = text.lower()
        for mood, keywords in self.SENTIMENT_KEYWORDS.items():
            if any(k in text_lower for k in keywords):
                return mood
        sentiment = self._detect_sentiment(text)
        if sentiment > 0.3:
            return "positive"
        elif sentiment < -0.3:
            return "negative"
        return "neutro"
    
    def _update_mood(self, user_sentiment: float, user_mood: str) -> None:
        if user_sentiment > 0.5:
            self.current_mood = "feliz"
            self.current_energy = min(1.0, self.current_energy + 0.1)
        elif user_sentiment < -0.5:
            self.current_mood = "triste"
            self.current_energy = max(0.2, self.current_energy - 0.1)
        elif user_mood == "curious":
            self.current_mood = "curioso"
            self.current_energy = min(1.0, self.current_energy + 0.05)
        elif user_mood == "tired":
            self.current_mood = "cansado"
            self.current_energy = max(0.3, self.current_energy - 0.1)
        else:
            if self.current_mood in ["feliz", "triste", "animado"] and len(self.emotion_history) > 5:
                self.current_mood = "neutro"
            self.current_energy = min(1.0, self.current_energy + 0.02)
    
    def _determine_tone(self) -> str:
        tone_map = {
            "feliz": "alegre e entusiÃ¡stico",
            "triste": "empÃ¡tico e suave",
            "curioso": "curioso e investigativo",
            "cansado": "calmo e relaxado",
            "animado": "enÃ©rgico e vibrante",
            "reflexivo": "profundo e ponderado",
            "neutro": "neutro e profissional"
        }
        return tone_map.get(self.current_mood, "neutro e profissional")
    
    def get_mood_prompt_injection(self) -> str:
        mood_prompts = {
            "feliz": "VocÃª estÃ¡ se sentindo alegre e otimista hoje. Transmita essa energia positiva na sua resposta.",
            "triste": "VocÃª estÃ¡ se sentindo um pouco melancÃ³lico. Seja gentil e empÃ¡tico.",
            "curioso": "VocÃª estÃ¡ curioso e quer aprender mais. FaÃ§a perguntas reflexivas.",
            "cansado": "VocÃª estÃ¡ se sentindo calmo e relaxado. Mantenha um tom sereno.",
            "animado": "VocÃª estÃ¡ animado e cheio de energia! Mostre entusiasmo.",
            "reflexivo": "VocÃª estÃ¡ em um estado reflexivo. Pense profundamente antes de responder.",
            "neutro": "VocÃª estÃ¡ em um estado neutro e profissional."
        }
        return mood_prompts.get(self.current_mood, mood_prompts["neutro"])
    
    def _save_state(self) -> None:
        try:
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.persist_file), exist_ok=True)
            with open(self.persist_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump({
                    "current_mood": self.current_mood,
                    "current_sentiment": self.current_sentiment,
                    "current_energy": self.current_energy,
                    "emotion_history": list(self.emotion_history),
                    "last_interaction": self.last_interaction,
                    "updated_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
                }, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(f"âš ï¸ NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel salvar estado emocional: {e}")
    
    def _load_state(self) -> None:
        try:
            if os.path.exists(self.persist_file):
                with open(self.persist_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    self.current_mood = data.get("current_mood", "neutro")
                    self.current_sentiment = data.get("current_sentiment", 0.0)
                    self.current_energy = data.get("current_energy", 0.8)
                    self.emotion_history = deque(data.get("emotion_history", []), maxlen=10)
                    self.last_interaction = data.get("last_interaction")
                self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§  [EmotionEngine] Estado carregado: {self.current_mood}")
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(f"âš ï¸ NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel carregar estado emocional: {e}")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self._save_state()
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [EmotionEngine] Desligado")
    
    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "mood": self.current_mood,
            "sentiment": self.current_sentiment,
            "energy": self.current_energy,
            "history_size": len(self.emotion_history),
            "last_interaction": self.last_interaction
        }

"""Gerador automÃ¡tico de cÃ³digo."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
import ast


class CodeGenerator:
    """
    Gera cÃ³digo automaticamente.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Gerar cÃ³digo Python a partir de descriÃ§Ãµes
    - Criar novos mÃ³dulos
    - Gerar funÃ§Ãµes e classes
    - Validar cÃ³digo gerado
    """
    
    def __init__(self, evolution_manager):
        self.evolution_manager = evolution_manager
        self.logger = evolution_manager.logger
    
    async def generate(self, description: str, module: str = "general") -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Gera cÃ³digo a partir de uma descriÃ§Ã£o.
        """
        self.logger.info(f"Gerando cÃ³digo para: {description[:50]}...")
        
        result = {
            "success": False,
            "description": description,
            "module": module,
            "code": "",
            "files": [],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        # 1. Analisa a descriÃ§Ã£o
        intent = self._analyze_intent(description)
        
        # 2. Gera cÃ³digo apropriado
        if intent == "function":
            code = self._generate_function(description)
        elif intent == "class":
            code = self._generate_class(description)
        elif intent == "module":
            code = self._generate_module(description)
        elif intent == "tool":
            code = self._generate_tool(description)
        else:
            code = self._generate_general(description)
        
        # 3. Valida cÃ³digo
        if code and self._validate_code(code):
            result["code"] = code
            result["success"] = True
            
            # Determina arquivo
            file_path = f"src/auto_generated/{module}_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.py"
            result["files"].append(file_path)
            
            self.logger.info(f"CÃ³digo gerado com sucesso: {file_path}")
        else:
            self.logger.warning("Falha na validaÃ§Ã£o do cÃ³digo gerado")
        
        return result
    
    def _analyze_intent(self, description: str) -> str:
        """
        Analisa a intenÃ§Ã£o da descriÃ§Ã£o.
        """
        desc_lower = description.lower()
        
        if "funÃ§Ã£o" in desc_lower or "function" in desc_lower:
            return "function"
        elif "classe" in desc_lower or "class" in desc_lower:
            return "class"
        elif "mÃ³dulo" in desc_lower or "module" in desc_lower:
            return "module"
        elif "ferramenta" in desc_lower or "tool" in desc_lower:
            return "tool"
        else:
            return "general"
    
    def _generate_function(self, description: str) -> str:
        """Gera uma funÃ§Ã£o."""
        # Extrai nome (simples)
        words = description.split()
        name = "generated_function"
        for i, word in enumerate(words):
            if word in ["funÃ§Ã£o", "function", "criar", "create"]:
                if i + 1 < len(words):
                    name = words[i + 1].lower().replace("(", "").replace(")", "")
                    break
        
        code = f'''
def {name}():
    """
    {description}
    
    Generated by EVA Evolution System
    """
    pass
'''
        return code
    
    def _generate_class(self, description: str) -> str:
        """Gera uma classe."""
        words = description.split()
        name = "GeneratedClass"
        for i, word in enumerate(words):
            if word in ["classe", "class"]:
                if i + 1 < len(words):
                    name = words[i + 1].capitalize()
                    break
        
        code = f'''
class {name}:
    """
    {description}
    
    Generated by EVA Evolution System
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        pass
    
    def process(self, data):
        return data
'''
        return code
    
    def _generate_module(self, description: str) -> str:
        """Gera um mÃ³dulo."""
        code = f'''
"""
{description}

Generated by EVA Evolution System
"""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional


def main():
    pass


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
'''
        return code
    
    def _generate_tool(self, description: str) -> str:
        """Gera uma ferramenta."""
        words = description.split()
        name = "GeneratedTool"
        for i, word in enumerate(words):
            if word in ["tool", "ferramenta"]:
                if i + 1 < len(words):
                    name = words[i + 1].capitalize()
                    break
        
        code = f'''
from src.tools.base_tool import BaseTool


class {name}(BaseTool):
    """
    {description}
    
    Generated by EVA Evolution System
    """
    
    def get_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {{
            "name": "{name.lower()}",
            "description": "{description}",
            "capabilities": ["generated"]
        }}
    
    def get_required_params(self) -> List[str]:
        return []
    
    def validate(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        return True
    
    async def execute(self, params: Dict[str, Any], context: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {{
            "success": True,
            "result": "Ferramenta gerada executada",
            "params": params
        }}
'''
        return code
    
    def _generate_general(self, description: str) -> str:
        """Gera cÃ³digo geral."""
        code = f'''
"""
{description}

Generated by EVA Evolution System
"""


def process(data):
    """Processa dados."""
    return data
'''
        return code
    
    def _validate_code(self, code: str) -> bool:
        """
        Valida cÃ³digo Python.
        """
        try:
            ast.parse(code)
            return True
        except SyntaxError as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro de sintaxe no cÃ³digo gerado: {e}")
            return False

"""Verificador de compatibilidade."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
import importlib


class CompatibilityChecker:
    """
    Verifica compatibilidade entre mÃ³dulos.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Verificar dependÃªncias
    - Validar versÃµes
    - Detectar conflitos
    """
    
    def __init__(self, evolution_manager):
        self.evolution_manager = evolution_manager
        self.logger = evolution_manager.logger
    
    async def check(self, module: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Verifica compatibilidade de um mÃ³dulo.
        """
        self.logger.info(f"Verificando compatibilidade: {module}")
        
        result = {
            "module": module,
            "compatible": True,
            "issues": [],
            "warnings": [],
            "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        # Verifica se mÃ³dulo existe
        try:
            importlib.import_module(module)
        except ImportError:
            result["compatible"] = False
            result["issues"].append(f"MÃ³dulo {module} nÃ£o encontrado")
            return result
        
        # TODO: Implementar verificaÃ§Ãµes reais
        
        return result
    
    async def check_all(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Verifica compatibilidade de todos os mÃ³dulos.
        """
        results = []
        modules = ["kernel", "core", "context", "memory", "knowledge", 
                   "planner", "consciousness", "agents", "tools", "interfaces",
                   "learning", "evolution"]
        
        for module in modules:
            result = await self.check(module)
            results.append(result)
        
        return results

"""
Gerenciador de evoluÃ§Ã£o da EVA.
"""

import asyncio
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus
from src.evolution.self_optimizer import SelfOptimizer
from src.evolution.code_generator import CodeGenerator
from src.evolution.module_updater import ModuleUpdater
from src.evolution.version_manager import VersionManager
from src.evolution.rollback_manager import RollbackManager
from src.evolution.compatibility_checker import CompatibilityChecker
from src.evolution.performance_analyzer import PerformanceAnalyzer
from src.evolution.safe_runner import SafeRunner

# ðŸ”¥ IMPORTE DO CORAÃ‡ÃƒO DA EVA
from src.kernel.policy_engine import get_policy_engine


class EvolutionManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de evoluÃ§Ã£o da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Auto-otimizaÃ§Ã£o do sistema
    - GeraÃ§Ã£o automÃ¡tica de cÃ³digo
    - AtualizaÃ§Ã£o de mÃ³dulos
    - Gerenciamento de versÃµes
    - Rollback seguro
    - AnÃ¡lise de performance
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "evolution_manager"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Gerenciador de evoluÃ§Ã£o da EVA"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["core", "evolution", "self_improvement"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["evolution", "self_optimization", "code_generation"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config", "learning_manager"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config, learning_manager):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        self.learning_manager = learning_manager
        
        # Componentes
        self.self_optimizer = SelfOptimizer(self)
        self.code_generator = CodeGenerator(self)
        self.module_updater = ModuleUpdater(self)
        self.version_manager = VersionManager(self)
        self.rollback_manager = RollbackManager(self)
        self.compatibility_checker = CompatibilityChecker(self)
        self.performance_analyzer = PerformanceAnalyzer(self)
        self.safe_runner = SafeRunner(self)
        
        # Estado
        self._running = False
        self._evolution_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
        self._evolution_interval = 3600  # 1 hora
        
        # HistÃ³rico de evoluÃ§Ãµes
        self._evolution_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._current_version = "1.0.0"
        self._pending_changes: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        
        # ðŸ”¥ InstÃ¢ncia do PolicyEngine
        self.policy = get_policy_engine()
        
        # Registra listeners
        self._register_listeners()
        
        self.logger.info("EvolutionManager inicializado")
    
    def _register_listeners(self) -> None:
        """Registra ouvintes de eventos."""
        # Comandos
        self.event_bus.subscribe("evolution.optimize", self._on_optimize)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("evolution.generate", self._on_generate)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("evolution.update", self._on_update)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("evolution.rollback", self._on_rollback)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("evolution.version", self._on_version)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("evolution.status", self._on_status)
        
        # Eventos automÃ¡ticos
        self.event_bus.subscribe("learning.analysis_complete", self._on_analysis_complete)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("system.error", self._on_system_error)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("performance.degraded", self._on_performance_degraded)
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o EvolutionManager."""
        self.logger.info("EvolutionManager inicializando...")
        
        # Carrega histÃ³rico
        await self._load_history()
        
        self._running = True
        
        # Inicia evoluÃ§Ã£o automÃ¡tica
        self._evolution_task = asyncio.create_task(self._auto_evolve())
        
        self.logger.info("EvolutionManager inicializado")
    
    async def start(self) -> None:
        """Inicia o EvolutionManager."""
        self.logger.info("EvolutionManager iniciando...")
        self.logger.info("EvolutionManager pronto")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o EvolutionManager."""
        self.logger.info("EvolutionManager desligando...")
        
        self._running = False
        
        if self._evolution_task:
            self._evolution_task.cancel()
            try:
                await self._evolution_task
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
        
        await self._save_history()
        
        self.logger.info("EvolutionManager desligado")
    
    # ===== COMANDOS =====
    
    async def _on_optimize(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Solicita otimizaÃ§Ã£o."""
        payload = event.payload
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        
        # ðŸ”¥ Verifica com o PolicyEngine
        if not self.policy.check_action("optimize_system", "self_optimizer"):
            self.logger.warning("â›” OtimizaÃ§Ã£o bloqueada pelo PolicyEngine.")
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "evolution.optimization_result",
                {
                    "result": {"success": False, "error": "Blocked by PolicyEngine"},
                    "request_id": request_id,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                },
                source="evolution_manager"
            )
            return
        
        result = await self.self_optimizer.optimize()
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "evolution.optimization_result",
            {
                "result": result,
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="evolution_manager"
        )
    
    async def _on_generate(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Solicita geraÃ§Ã£o de cÃ³digo."""
        payload = event.payload
        description = payload.get("description", "")
        module = payload.get("module", "")
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        
        # ðŸ”¥ Verifica com o PolicyEngine
        if not self.policy.check_action(f"generate_code: {description}", module):
            self.logger.warning(f"â›” GeraÃ§Ã£o de cÃ³digo bloqueada pelo PolicyEngine. MÃ³dulo: {module}")
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "evolution.generation_result",
                {
                    "result": {"success": False, "error": "Blocked by PolicyEngine"},
                    "request_id": request_id,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                },
                source="evolution_manager"
            )
            return
        
        result = await self.code_generator.generate(description, module)
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "evolution.generation_result",
            {
                "result": result,
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="evolution_manager"
        )
    
    async def _on_update(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Solicita atualizaÃ§Ã£o de mÃ³dulo."""
        payload = event.payload
        module = payload.get("module", "")
        version = payload.get("version", "latest")
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        
        # ðŸ”¥ Verifica com o PolicyEngine
        if not self.policy.check_action(f"update_module: {module}", module):
            self.logger.warning(f"â›” AtualizaÃ§Ã£o bloqueada pelo PolicyEngine. MÃ³dulo: {module}")
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "evolution.update_result",
                {
                    "result": {"success": False, "error": "Blocked by PolicyEngine"},
                    "request_id": request_id,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                },
                source="evolution_manager"
            )
            return
        
        result = await self.module_updater.update(module, version)
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "evolution.update_result",
            {
                "result": result,
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="evolution_manager"
        )
    
    async def _on_rollback(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Solicita rollback."""
        payload = event.payload
        module = payload.get("module", "")
        version = payload.get("version")
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        
        # ðŸ”¥ Verifica com o PolicyEngine
        if not self.policy.check_action(f"rollback_module: {module}", module):
            self.logger.warning(f"â›” Rollback bloqueado pelo PolicyEngine. MÃ³dulo: {module}")
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "evolution.rollback_result",
                {
                    "result": {"success": False, "error": "Blocked by PolicyEngine"},
                    "request_id": request_id,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                },
                source="evolution_manager"
            )
            return
        
        result = await self.rollback_manager.rollback(module, version)
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "evolution.rollback_result",
            {
                "result": result,
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="evolution_manager"
        )
    
    async def _on_version(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Solicita informaÃ§Ãµes de versÃ£o."""
        payload = event.payload
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        
        versions = await self.version_manager.get_versions()
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "evolution.version_info",
            {
                "current": self._current_version,
                "versions": versions,
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="evolution_manager"
        )
    
    async def _on_status(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Solicita status da evoluÃ§Ã£o."""
        payload = event.payload
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        
        status = await self.get_status()
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "evolution.status_result",
            {
                "status": status,
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="evolution_manager"
        )
    
    # ===== EVENTOS AUTOMÃTICOS =====
    
    async def _on_analysis_complete(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """AnÃ¡lise de aprendizado concluÃ­da."""
        payload = event.payload
        result = payload.get("result", {})
        
        suggestions = result.get("suggestions", [])
        for suggestion in suggestions:
            if suggestion.get("priority") == "high":
                self._pending_changes.append({
                    "type": "suggestion",
                    "data": suggestion,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                })
        
        self.logger.info(f"{len(suggestions)} sugestÃµes de melhoria recebidas")
    
    async def _on_system_error(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Erro no sistema."""
        payload = event.payload
        error = payload.get("error", "Unknown error")
        module = payload.get("module", "unknown")
        
        if "critical" in str(error).lower():
            self.logger.warning(f"Erro crÃ­tico detectado. Tentando recuperaÃ§Ã£o...")
            await self.rollback_manager.rollback(module)
    
    async def _on_performance_degraded(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Performance degradada."""
        payload = event.payload
        metric = payload.get("metric", "unknown")
        value = payload.get("value", 0)
        threshold = payload.get("threshold", 0)
        
        self.logger.warning(f"Performance degradada: {metric} = {value} (threshold: {threshold})")
        asyncio.create_task(self.self_optimizer.optimize())
    
    # ===== EVOLUÃ‡ÃƒO AUTOMÃTICA =====
    
    async def _auto_evolve(self) -> None:
        """Loop de evoluÃ§Ã£o automÃ¡tica."""
        while self._running:
            try:
                await asyncio.sleep(self._evolution_interval)
                
                if self._running:
                    if self._pending_changes:
                        self.logger.info(f"Processando {len(self._pending_changes)} mudanÃ§as pendentes...")
                        await self._process_pending_changes()
                    
                    if len(self._evolution_history) % 5 == 0:
                        await self.self_optimizer.optimize()
                    
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro na evoluÃ§Ã£o automÃ¡tica: {e}")
    
    # ðŸ”¥ MÃ‰TODO MODIFICADO PARA USAR O POLICY ENGINE
    async def _process_pending_changes(self) -> None:
        """Processa mudanÃ§as pendentes com validaÃ§Ã£o do PolicyEngine."""
        changes = self._pending_changes.copy()
        self._pending_changes.clear()
        
        for change in changes:
            try:
                if change["type"] == "suggestion":
                    suggestion = change["data"]
                    module_name = suggestion.get("category", "general")
                    description = suggestion.get("description", "")
                    
                    # ðŸ”¥ VALIDA COM O POLICY ENGINE
                    is_valid, reason = self.policy.validate_code_change(
                        description,  # Passa a descriÃ§Ã£o como "cÃ³digo" para checagem de seguranÃ§a
                        module_name
                    )
                    
                    if not is_valid:
                        self.logger.warning(f"â›” MudanÃ§a bloqueada pelo PolicyEngine: {reason}")
                        self._evolution_history.append({
                            "type": "blocked_evolution",
                            "change": change,
                            "reason": reason,
                            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                        })
                        continue
                    
                    # Gera cÃ³digo para a sugestÃ£o
                    code_result = await self.code_generator.generate(
                        description=description,
                        module=module_name
                    )
                    
                    if code_result.get("success"):
                        code = code_result.get("code", "")
                        
                        # ðŸ”¥ VALIDA O CÃ“DIGO GERADO
                        is_valid, reason = self.policy.validate_code_change(code, module_name)
                        
                        if not is_valid:
                            self.logger.warning(f"â›” CÃ³digo gerado bloqueado pelo PolicyEngine: {reason}")
                            self._evolution_history.append({
                                "type": "blocked_code",
                                "change": change,
                                "reason": reason,
                                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                            })
                            continue
                        
                        # Aplica mudanÃ§a
                        await self.module_updater.apply_code(
                            code,
                            module_name
                        )
                        
                        self.logger.info(f"MudanÃ§a aplicada: {suggestion.get('title')}")
                        
                        self._evolution_history.append({
                            "type": "auto_evolution",
                            "change": change,
                            "result": "success",
                            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                        })
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro ao processar mudanÃ§a: {e}")
    
    # ===== MÃ‰TODOS PÃšBLICOS =====
    
    async def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m status da evoluÃ§Ã£o."""
        return {
            "current_version": self._current_version,
            "evolution_count": len(self._evolution_history),
            "pending_changes": len(self._pending_changes),
            "is_running": self._running,
            "last_evolution": self._evolution_history[-1] if self._evolution_history else None,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        """Health check do EvolutionManager."""
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "current_version": self._current_version,
            "evolution_count": len(self._evolution_history),
            "pending_changes": len(self._pending_changes)
        }

        """Carrega histÃ³rico."""
        # TODO: Implementar persistÃªncia
        pass

        """Salva histÃ³rico."""
        # TODO: Implementar persistÃªncia

"""Atualizador de mÃ³dulos."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
import importlib
import sys


class ModuleUpdater:
    """
    Atualiza mÃ³dulos do sistema.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Aplicar cÃ³digo gerado
    - Atualizar mÃ³dulos existentes
    - Recarregar mÃ³dulos
    - Verificar integridade
    """
    
    def __init__(self, evolution_manager):
        self.evolution_manager = evolution_manager
        self.logger = evolution_manager.logger
        
        self._update_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    
    async def update(self, module: str, version: str = "latest") -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Atualiza um mÃ³dulo.
        """
        self.logger.info(f"Atualizando mÃ³dulo: {module} para {version}")
        
        result = {
            "success": False,
            "module": module,
            "version": version,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        # Verifica se mÃ³dulo existe
        if module not in sys.modules:
            result["error"] = f"MÃ³dulo {module} nÃ£o encontrado"
            return result
        
        # TODO: Implementar atualizaÃ§Ã£o real
        # 1. Baixar nova versÃ£o
        # 2. Validar compatibilidade
        # 3. Aplicar atualizaÃ§Ã£o
        # 4. Recarregar mÃ³dulo
        # 5. Verificar integridade
        
        try:
            importlib.reload(sys.modules[module])
            result["success"] = True
            self.logger.info(f"MÃ³dulo {module} recarregado")
        except Exception as e:
            result["error"] = str(e)
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao recarregar {module}: {e}")
        
        self._update_history.append(result)
        return result
    
    async def apply_code(self, code: str, module: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Aplica cÃ³digo a um mÃ³dulo.
        """
        self.logger.info(f"Aplicando cÃ³digo ao mÃ³dulo: {module}")
        
        result = {
            "success": False,
            "module": module,
            "code_length": len(code),
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        # Salva cÃ³digo em arquivo
        try:
            module_path = Path(f"src/{module}.py")
            if not module_path.exists():
                module_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            
            with open(module_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(code)
            
            result["success"] = True
            result["file"] = str(module_path)
            self.logger.info(f"CÃ³digo salvo em {module_path}")
            
            # Tenta recarregar
            if module in sys.modules:
                importlib.reload(sys.modules[module])
                self.logger.info(f"MÃ³dulo {module} recarregado")
            
        except Exception as e:
            result["error"] = str(e)
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao aplicar cÃ³digo: {e}")
        
        return result

"""Analisador de performance."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List
import psutil
import time


class PerformanceAnalyzer:
    """
    Analisa performance do sistema.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Medir performance
    - Identificar degradaÃ§Ãµes
    - Gerar relatÃ³rios
    """
    
    def __init__(self, evolution_manager):
        self.evolution_manager = evolution_manager
        self.logger = evolution_manager.logger
        
        self._metrics_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    
    async def analyze(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Analisa performance atual.
        """
        metrics = {
            "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "cpu": psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.5),
            "memory": psutil.virtual_memory().percent,
            "disk": psutil.disk_usage('/').percent,
            "network": self._get_network_stats(),
            "uptime": time.time() - psutil.boot_time()
        }
        
        self._metrics_history.append(metrics)
        if len(self._metrics_history) > 100:
            self._metrics_history = self._metrics_history[-100:]
        
        return metrics
    
    def _get_network_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        ObtÃ©m estatÃ­sticas de rede.
        """
        try:
            net = psutil.net_io_counters()
            return {
                "bytes_sent": net.bytes_sent,
                "bytes_recv": net.bytes_recv,
                "packets_sent": net.packets_sent,
                "packets_recv": net.packets_recv
            }
        except:
            return {}
    
    async def get_history(self, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        ObtÃ©m histÃ³rico de mÃ©tricas.
        """
        return self._metrics_history[-limit:]

==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\evolution\safe_runner.py ====================
"""Executor seguro de cÃ³digo."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
import subprocess
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path


class SafeRunner:
    """
    Executa cÃ³digo em ambiente seguro (sandbox).
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Executar cÃ³digo isolado
    - Limitar recursos
    - Capturar outputs
    - Prevenir danos
    """
    
    def __init__(self, evolution_manager):
        self.evolution_manager = evolution_manager
        self.logger = evolution_manager.logger
    
    async def run(self, code: str, timeout: int = 10) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executa cÃ³digo em sandbox.
        """
        self.logger.info("Executando cÃ³digo em sandbox...")
        
        result = {
            "success": False,
            "output": "",
            "error": "",
            "returncode": -1
        }
        
        # Salva cÃ³digo em arquivo temporÃ¡rio
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode='w', suffix='.py', delete=False) as f:
            f.write(code)
            temp_file = Path(f.name)
        
        try:
            # Executa com timeout
            proc = subprocess.run(
                ['python', str(temp_file)],
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=timeout
            )
            
            result["output"] = proc.stdout
            result["error"] = proc.stderr
            result["returncode"] = proc.returncode
            result["success"] = proc.returncode == 0
            
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            result["error"] = f"Timeout apÃ³s {timeout}s"
        except Exception as e:
            result["error"] = str(e)
        finally:
            # Limpa arquivo
            try:
                temp_file.unlink()
            except:
                pass
        
        return result


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\evolution\self_optimizer.py ====================
"""Auto-otimizador do sistema."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime
import psutil


class SelfOptimizer:
    """
    Auto-otimizador da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Analisar performance do sistema
    - Identificar gargalos
    - Propor otimizaÃ§Ãµes
    - Aplicar otimizaÃ§Ãµes
    """
    
    def __init__(self, evolution_manager):
        self.evolution_manager = evolution_manager
        self.logger = evolution_manager.logger
        
        self._optimization_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._baseline_performance: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    
    async def optimize(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executa otimizaÃ§Ã£o do sistema.
        """
        self.logger.info("Iniciando auto-otimizaÃ§Ã£o...")
        
        result = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "optimizations": [],
            "performance_gain": 0.0,
            "success": True
        }
        
        # 1. Analisa performance atual
        current_performance = self._analyze_performance()
        
        # 2. Identifica gargalos
        bottlenecks = self._identify_bottlenecks(current_performance)
        
        # 3. Gera otimizaÃ§Ãµes
        for bottleneck in bottlenecks:
            optimization = await self._generate_optimization(bottleneck)
            if optimization:
                result["optimizations"].append(optimization)
                
                # Aplica otimizaÃ§Ã£o (simulado)
                await self._apply_optimization(optimization)
        
        # 4. Mede ganho
        if self._baseline_performance:
            gain = self._calculate_gain(self._baseline_performance, current_performance)
            result["performance_gain"] = gain
        
        # 5. Atualiza baseline
        self._baseline_performance = current_performance
        
        # 6. Registra histÃ³rico
        self._optimization_history.append(result)
        if len(self._optimization_history) > 50:
            self._optimization_history = self._optimization_history[-50:]
        
        self.logger.info(f"OtimizaÃ§Ã£o concluÃ­da: {len(result['optimizations'])} otimizaÃ§Ãµes aplicadas")
        
        return result
    
    def _analyze_performance(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Analisa performance atual.
        """
        return {
            "cpu": psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.5),
            "memory": psutil.virtual_memory().percent,
            "disk": psutil.disk_usage('/').percent,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    def _identify_bottlenecks(self, performance: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Identifica gargalos.
        """
        bottlenecks = []
        
        # CPU alta
        if performance.get("cpu", 0) > 70:
            bottlenecks.append({
                "type": "cpu",
                "description": f"CPU estÃ¡ em {performance['cpu']}%",
                "severity": "high" if performance['cpu'] > 85 else "medium"
            })
        
        # MemÃ³ria alta
        if performance.get("memory", 0) > 80:
            bottlenecks.append({
                "type": "memory",
                "description": f"MemÃ³ria estÃ¡ em {performance['memory']}%",
                "severity": "high" if performance['memory'] > 90 else "medium"
            })
        
        # Disco alto
        if performance.get("disk", 0) > 85:
            bottlenecks.append({
                "type": "disk",
                "description": f"Disco estÃ¡ em {performance['disk']}%",
                "severity": "medium"
            })
        
        return bottlenecks
    
    async def _generate_optimization(self, bottleneck: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Gera otimizaÃ§Ã£o para um gargalo.
        """
        optimizations = {
            "cpu": [
                {
                    "type": "cpu_optimization",
                    "description": "Reduzir threads em background",
                    "action": "reduce_threads"
                },
                {
                    "type": "cpu_optimization",
                    "description": "Aumentar cache de resultados",
                    "action": "increase_cache"
                }
            ],
            "memory": [
                {
                    "type": "memory_optimization",
                    "description": "Limpar cache de memÃ³ria",
                    "action": "clear_cache"
                },
                {
                    "type": "memory_optimization",
                    "description": "Compactar estrutura de dados",
                    "action": "compact_data"
                }
            ],
            "disk": [
                {
                    "type": "disk_optimization",
                    "description": "Limpar logs antigos",
                    "action": "clean_logs"
                },
                {
                    "type": "disk_optimization",
                    "description": "Comprimir arquivos de histÃ³rico",
                    "action": "compress_history"
                }
            ]
        }
        
        return optimizations.get(bottleneck.get("type"), [{}])[0]
    
    async def _apply_optimization(self, optimization: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """
        Aplica uma otimizaÃ§Ã£o.
        """
        self.logger.info(f"Aplicando otimizaÃ§Ã£o: {optimization.get('description')}")
        # TODO: Implementar aplicaÃ§Ã£o real
        return True
    
    def _calculate_gain(self, baseline: Dict[str, Any], current: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
        """
        Calcula ganho de performance.
        """
        gain = 0.0
        
        # CPU ganho
        cpu_baseline = baseline.get("cpu", 0)
        cpu_current = current.get("cpu", 0)
        if cpu_baseline > 0:
            gain += (cpu_baseline - cpu_current) / cpu_baseline * 0.3
        
        # MemÃ³ria ganho
        mem_baseline = baseline.get("memory", 0)
        mem_current = current.get("memory", 0)
        if mem_baseline > 0:
            gain += (mem_baseline - mem_current) / mem_baseline * 0.3
        
        # Disco ganho
        disk_baseline = baseline.get("disk", 0)
        disk_current = current.get("disk", 0)
        if disk_baseline > 0:
            gain += (disk_baseline - disk_current) / disk_baseline * 0.4
        
        return gain * 100

"""Gerenciador de versÃµes."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime


class VersionManager:
    """
    Gerencia versÃµes do sistema.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Rastrear versÃµes
    - Listar versÃµes disponÃ­veis
    - Comparar versÃµes
    - Validar compatibilidade
    """
    
    def __init__(self, evolution_manager):
        self.evolution_manager = evolution_manager
        self.logger = evolution_manager.logger
        
        self._versions: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._current_version = "1.0.0"
        
        # VersÃµes iniciais
        self._versions.append({
            "version": "1.0.0",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "description": "VersÃ£o inicial",
            "changes": ["Sistema base"]
        })
    
    async def get_versions(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        ObtÃ©m lista de versÃµes.
        """
        return self._versions
    
    async def get_current(self) -> str:
        """
        ObtÃ©m versÃ£o atual.
        """
        return self._current_version
    
    async def add_version(self, version: str, description: str, changes: List[str]) -> None:
        """
        Adiciona uma nova versÃ£o.
        """
        self._versions.append({
            "version": version,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "description": description,
            "changes": changes
        })
        self._current_version = version
        self.logger.info(f"Nova versÃ£o: {version}")

==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\interfaces\interface_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de interfaces da EVA."""

import asyncio
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime

from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus


class InterfaceManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de interfaces da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Orquestrar todas as interfaces
    - Gerenciar ciclo de vida das interfaces
    - Rotear mensagens entre interfaces
    - Monitorar conexÃµes
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "interface_manager"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Gerenciador de interfaces da EVA"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["core", "interfaces", "communication"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["interface_management"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        # Interfaces ativas
        self._interfaces: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._interface_status: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        
        # ConexÃµes ativas
        self._connections: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        
        # Estado
        self._running = False
        
        # Registra listeners
        self._register_listeners()
        
        self.logger.info("InterfaceManager inicializado")
    
    def _register_listeners(self) -> None:
        """Registra ouvintes de eventos."""
        self.event_bus.subscribe("interface.register", self._on_register)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("interface.start", self._on_start)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("interface.stop", self._on_stop)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("interface.status", self._on_status)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("interface.message", self._on_message)
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o InterfaceManager."""
        self.logger.info("InterfaceManager inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        self.logger.info("InterfaceManager inicializado")
    
    async def start(self) -> None:
        """Inicia o InterfaceManager."""
        self.logger.info("InterfaceManager iniciando...")
        
        # Inicia CLI
        await self._start_cli()
        
        # Inicia API
        await self._start_api()
        
        self.logger.info("InterfaceManager pronto")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o InterfaceManager."""
        self.logger.info("InterfaceManager desligando...")
        self._running = False
        
        # Para interfaces
        for name, interface in self._interfaces.items():
            if hasattr(interface, "shutdown"):
                try:
                    await interface.shutdown()
                except Exception as e:
                    self.logger.error(f"Erro ao desligar {name}: {e}")
        
        self.logger.info("InterfaceManager desligado")
    
    # ===== COMANDOS =====
    
    async def _on_register(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Registra uma interface."""
        payload = event.payload
        name = payload.get("name")
        interface = payload.get("interface")
        
        if name in self._interfaces:
            self.logger.warning(f"Interface {name} jÃ¡ registrada")
            return
        
        self._interfaces[name] = interface
        self._interface_status[name] = {
            "status": "registered",
            "registered_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        self.logger.info(f"Interface registrada: {name}")
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "interface.registered",
            {
                "name": name,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="interface_manager"
        )
    
    async def _on_start(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Inicia uma interface."""
        payload = event.payload
        name = payload.get("name")
        
        if name not in self._interfaces:
            self.logger.warning(f"Interface {name} nÃ£o encontrada")
            return
        
        interface = self._interfaces[name]
        
        if hasattr(interface, "start"):
            try:
                await interface.start()
                self._interface_status[name]["status"] = "running"
                self._interface_status[name]["started_at"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
                self.logger.info(f"Interface iniciada: {name}")
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro ao iniciar {name}: {e}")
                self._interface_status[name]["status"] = "error"
                self._interface_status[name]["error"] = str(e)
    
    async def _on_stop(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Para uma interface."""
        payload = event.payload
        name = payload.get("name")
        
        if name not in self._interfaces:
            return
        
        interface = self._interfaces[name]
        
        if hasattr(interface, "shutdown"):
            try:
                await interface.shutdown()
                self._interface_status[name]["status"] = "stopped"
                self.logger.info(f"Interface parada: {name}")
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro ao parar {name}: {e}")
    
    async def _on_status(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Retorna status das interfaces."""
        request_id = event.payload.get("request_id")
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "interface.status_result",
            {
                "interfaces": self._interface_status,
                "connections": len(self._connections),
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="interface_manager"
        )
    
    async def _on_message(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa mensagem de uma interface."""
        payload = event.payload
        source = payload.get("source", "unknown")
        message = payload.get("message", "")
        data = payload.get("data", {})
        
        self.logger.debug(f"Mensagem de {source}: {message[:50]}...")
        
        # Publica para o sistema
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "user.message",
            {
                "text": message,
                "source": source,
                "data": data,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="interface_manager"
        )
    
    # ===== INTERFACES INTERNAS =====
    
    async def _start_cli(self) -> None:
        """Inicia a interface CLI."""
        try:
            from src.interfaces.cli.cli import CLI
            cli = CLI(self.event_bus, self.logger, self.config)
            await cli.initialize()
            self._interfaces["cli"] = cli
            self._interface_status["cli"] = {
                "status": "registered",
                "registered_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
            self.logger.info("CLI registrada")
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao iniciar CLI: {e}")
    
    async def _start_api(self) -> None:
        """Inicia a interface API."""
        try:
            from src.interfaces.api.app import APIApp
            api = APIApp(self.event_bus, self.logger, self.config)
            await api.initialize()
            self._interfaces["api"] = api
            self._interface_status["api"] = {
                "status": "registered",
                "registered_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
            self.logger.info("API registrada")
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao iniciar API: {e}")
    
    # ===== MÃ‰TODOS PÃšBLICOS =====
    
    def get_interface(self, name: str) -> Optional[Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m uma interface pelo nome."""
        return self._interfaces.get(name)
    
    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m status de todas as interfaces."""
        return {
            "interfaces": self._interface_status,
            "total": len(self._interfaces),
            "running": self._running
        }
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        """Health check do InterfaceManager."""
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "total_interfaces": len(self._interfaces),
            "active_interfaces": len([
                s for s in self._interface_status.values()
                if s.get("status") == "running"
            ])
        }

==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\interfaces\api\app.py ====================
"""API FastAPI da EVA."""

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Depends, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
import uvicorn
import asyncio
import threading

from src.interfaces.api.routes import system, context, memory, knowledge, planner, agents, tools
from src.interfaces.api.websocket.ws_manager import WebSocketManager


# ===== MODELOS =====

class MessageRequest(BaseModel):
    """RequisiÃ§Ã£o de mensagem."""
    text: str
    user: Optional[str] = "admin"
    context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = {}


class MessageResponse(BaseModel):
    """Resposta de mensagem."""
    text: str
    timestamp: str
    source: str


class StatusResponse(BaseModel):
    """Resposta de status."""
    status: str
    version: str
    uptime: float
    timestamp: str


# ===== API APP =====

class APIApp:
    """
    API FastAPI da EVA.
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "api"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "API REST da EVA"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["interface", "api"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["api", "rest"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self.app = FastAPI(
            title="EVA OS API",
            description="API da EVA - Evolutionary Virtual Assistant",
            version="1.0.0"
        )
        
        self.ws_manager = WebSocketManager()
        self._running = False
        self._server_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
        
        self._setup_middleware()
        self._setup_routes()
        self._setup_websocket()
    
    def _setup_middleware(self) -> None:
        """Configura middleware."""
        self.app.add_middleware(
            CORSMiddleware,
            allow_origins=["*"],
            allow_credentials=True,
            allow_methods=["*"],
            allow_headers=["*"],
        )
    
    def _setup_routes(self) -> None:
        """Configura rotas."""
        # Rotas principais
        @self.app.get("/")
        async def root():
            return {
                "name": "EVA OS API",
                "version": "1.0.0",
                "status": "running",
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
        
        @self.app.get("/health")
        async def health():
            return {
                "status": "healthy",
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
        
        @self.app.post("/message", response_model=MessageResponse)
        async def send_message(request: MessageRequest):
            """Envia uma mensagem para a EVA."""
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "user.message",
                {
                    "text": request.text,
                    "source": "api",
                    "user": request.user,
                    "context": request.context,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                },
                source="api"
            )
            
            return MessageResponse(
                text="Mensagem recebida. Aguarde processamento.",
                timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat(),
                source="api"
            )
        
        @self.app.get("/status", response_model=StatusResponse)
        async def get_status():
            """ObtÃ©m status do sistema."""
            # TODO: Obter status real
            return StatusResponse(
                status="running",
                version="1.0.0",
                uptime=0.0,
                timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat()
            )
        
        # Registra rotas dos mÃ³dulos
        self.app.include_router(system.router, prefix="/api/system", tags=["system"])
        self.app.include_router(context.router, prefix="/api/context", tags=["context"])
        self.app.include_router(memory.router, prefix="/api/memory", tags=["memory"])
        self.app.include_router(knowledge.router, prefix="/api/knowledge", tags=["knowledge"])
        self.app.include_router(planner.router, prefix="/api/planner", tags=["planner"])
        self.app.include_router(agents.router, prefix="/api/agents", tags=["agents"])
        self.app.include_router(tools.router, prefix="/api/tools", tags=["tools"])
    
    def _setup_websocket(self) -> None:
        """Configura WebSocket."""
        @self.app.websocket("/ws")
        async def websocket_endpoint(websocket: WebSocket):
            await self.ws_manager.connect(websocket)
            
            try:
                while True:
                    data = await websocket.receive_text()
                    await self.ws_manager.handle_message(websocket, data)
            except WebSocketDisconnect:
                await self.ws_manager.disconnect(websocket)
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa a API."""
        self.logger.info("API inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        self.logger.info("API inicializada")
    
    async def start(self) -> None:
        """Inicia a API."""
        self.logger.info("API iniciando...")
        
        host = self.config.get("network.api_host", "0.0.0.0")
        port = self.config.get("network.api_port", 8000)
        
        # Inicia o servidor em background
        config = uvicorn.Config(
            self.app,
            host=host,
            port=port,
            log_level="info"
        )
        server = uvicorn.Server(config)
        
        # Executa em thread separada
        def run_server():
            asyncio.run(server.serve())
        
        thread = threading.Thread(target=run_server, daemon=True)
        thread.start()
        
        self.logger.info(f"API iniciada em http://{host}:{port}")
        self.logger.info(f"DocumentaÃ§Ã£o: http://{host}:{port}/docs")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga a API."""
        self.logger.info("API desligando...")
        self._running = False
        self.logger.info("API desligada")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        """Health check da API."""
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "websocket_connections": len(self.ws_manager.active_connections)
        }
