==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\interfaces\api\websocket\ws_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de WebSocket."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List, Set
from fastapi import WebSocket
import json
from datetime import datetime


class WebSocketManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de conexÃµes WebSocket.
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.active_connections: Set[WebSocket] = set()
        self.connection_data: Dict[WebSocket, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
    async def connect(self, websocket: WebSocket) -> None:
        """Aceita uma conexÃ£o."""
        await websocket.accept()
        self.active_connections.add(websocket)
        self.connection_data[websocket] = {
            "connected_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "messages_received": 0,
            "messages_sent": 0
        }
    
    async def disconnect(self, websocket: WebSocket) -> None:
        """Remove uma conexÃ£o."""
        self.active_connections.remove(websocket)
        if websocket in self.connection_data:
            del self.connection_data[websocket]
    
    async def handle_message(self, websocket: WebSocket, message: str) -> None:
        """Processa uma mensagem recebida."""
        try:
            data = json.loads(message)
            self.connection_data[websocket]["messages_received"] += 1
            
            # Echo
            response = {
                "type": "echo",
                "data": data,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
            
            await self.send_message(websocket, response)
            
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            await self.send_message(websocket, {
                "type": "error",
                "message": "JSON invÃ¡lido",
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            })
    
    async def send_message(self, websocket: WebSocket, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Envia uma mensagem."""
        try:
            await websocket.send_json(data)
            if websocket in self.connection_data:
                self.connection_data[websocket]["messages_sent"] += 1
        except Exception:
            pass
    
    async def broadcast(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Transmite uma mensagem para todas as conexÃµes."""
        for connection in self.active_connections:
            await self.send_message(connection, data)
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m estatÃ­sticas."""
        return {
            "active_connections": len(self.active_connections),
            "connections": [
                {
                    "connected_at": data.get("connected_at"),
                    "messages_received": data.get("messages_received", 0),
                    "messages_sent": data.get("messages_sent", 0)
                }
                for data in self.connection_data.values()
            ]
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\interfaces\cli\cli.py ====================
"""Interface CLI da EVA."""

import asyncio
import sys
import threading
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

from src.interfaces.cli.console import EVAConsole


class CLI:
    """Interface de linha de comando da EVA."""
    
    __plugin_name__ = "cli"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Interface de linha de comando"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["interface", "cli"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["cli"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self.console = None
        self._running = False
        self._loop = None
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa a CLI."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [CLI] initialize INICIANDO")
        self._running = True
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [CLI] initialize FINALIZADO")
    
    async def start(self) -> None:
        """Inicia a CLI."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [CLI] start INICIANDO")
        
        self._loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§  [CLI] Loop: {self._loop}")
        
        def run_console():
            self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [CLI] run_console INICIADO")
            try:
                # CONSOLE SEM BRAIN - 100% VIA EVENTBUS
                self.console = EVAConsole(
                    self.event_bus, 
                    self.logger, 
                    self.config, 
                    self._loop
                )
                self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [CLI] Console criado (sem Brain, via EventBus)")
                self.console.cmdloop()
                self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [CLI] cmdloop finalizado")
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"ðŸ§  [CLI] Erro no console: {e}")
                import traceback
                traceback.print_exc()
        
        thread = threading.Thread(target=run_console, daemon=True)
        thread.start()
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [CLI] Thread iniciada")
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [CLI] start FINALIZADO")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga a CLI."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [CLI] shutdown")
        self._running = False
        if self.console:
            self.console._running = False
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [CLI] shutdown concluÃ­do")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        """Health check da CLI."""
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "console_running": self.console is not None
        }

"""Comandos da CLI da EVA."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime


class CLICommands:
    """
    Comandos disponÃ­veis na CLI.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
    
    async def execute(self, command: str, args: List[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executa um comando.
        """
        if command == "status":
            return await self._cmd_status()
        elif command == "modules":
            return await self._cmd_modules()
        elif command == "context":
            return await self._cmd_context()
        elif command == "memory":
            return await self._cmd_memory()
        elif command == "knowledge":
            return await self._cmd_knowledge()
        elif command == "planner":
            return await self._cmd_planner()
        elif command == "agents":
            return await self._cmd_agents()
        elif command == "tools":
            return await self._cmd_tools()
        elif command == "help":
            return self._cmd_help()
        elif command == "clear":
            return self._cmd_clear()
        elif command == "exit":
            return {"action": "exit"}
        else:
            return {"error": f"Comando desconhecido: {command}"}
    
    async def _cmd_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Status do sistema."""
        return {
            "command": "status",
            "result": "Sistema operacional",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _cmd_modules(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Lista mÃ³dulos."""
        return {
            "command": "modules",
            "result": ["kernel", "core", "context", "memory", "knowledge", 
                       "planner", "consciousness", "agents", "tools", "interfaces"],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _cmd_context(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Contexto atual."""
        return {
            "command": "context",
            "result": "Contexto solicitado",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _cmd_memory(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """MemÃ³ria de trabalho."""
        return {
            "command": "memory",
            "result": "MemÃ³ria de trabalho",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _cmd_knowledge(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Conhecimento."""
        return {
            "command": "knowledge",
            "result": "Base de conhecimento",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _cmd_planner(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Planner."""
        return {
            "command": "planner",
            "result": "Sistema de planejamento",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _cmd_agents(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Agentes."""
        return {
            "command": "agents",
            "result": ["general", "knowledge", "memory", "planner", 
                       "home_automation", "information", "learning", "security"],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def _cmd_tools(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Ferramentas."""
        return {
            "command": "tools",
            "result": ["python", "shell", "file", "network", "system", "date"],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    def _cmd_help(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Ajuda."""
        return {
            "command": "help",
            "result": """
Comandos disponÃ­veis:
  status      - Status do sistema
  modules     - Lista mÃ³dulos carregados
  context     - Contexto atual
  memory      - MemÃ³ria de trabalho
  knowledge   - Base de conhecimento
  planner     - Sistema de planejamento
  agents      - Lista agentes
  tools       - Lista ferramentas
  help        - Esta ajuda
  clear       - Limpa a tela
  exit        - Sai do console
""",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    def _cmd_clear(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Limpa a tela."""
        import os
        os.system('cls' if os.name == 'nt' else 'clear')
        return {"command": "clear", "action": "clear"}


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\interfaces\cli\console.py ====================
"""Console interativo da EVA - Pipeline Cognitivo."""

import asyncio
import sys
import cmd
from datetime import datetime

from src.core.event_types import EventType


class EVAConsole(cmd.Cmd):
    """Console interativo da EVA."""
    
    intro = """
â•”â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•—
â•‘         Console Interativo da EVA            â•‘
â•šâ•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
    
Digite 'help' para ver os comandos.
Digite 'sair' para encerrar.
"""
    prompt = "EVA> "
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus, logger, config, loop=None):
        super().__init__()
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        self.loop = loop or asyncio.get_event_loop()
        self._running = True
        self._waiting = False
        
        # ===== EVENTOS UNIFICADOS =====
        self.event_bus.subscribe(EventType.ASSISTANT_RESPONSE, self._on_response)
        self.event_bus.subscribe(EventType.PROCESSING_START, self._on_processing_start)
        self.event_bus.subscribe(EventType.PROCESSING_END, self._on_processing_end)
        self.event_bus.subscribe(EventType.COGNITIVE_ERROR, self._on_error)
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [CONSOLE] Listeners registrados")
    
    def _on_response(self, event) -> None:
        """Mostra resposta da EVA."""
        data = event.data
        text = data.get("text", "")
        metadata = data.get("metadata", {})
        
        if text:
            self._waiting = False
            sys.stdout.write("\r\033[K")
            print(f"\nðŸ¤– EVA: {text}")
            
            # Mostra metadados em modo debug
            if metadata:
                sys.stdout.write("\033[90m")
                print(f"   [intenÃ§Ã£o: {metadata.get('intent', '?')} | tipo: {metadata.get('query_type', '?')}]")
                sys.stdout.write("\033[0m")
            
            sys.stdout.write(self.prompt)
            sys.stdout.flush()
    
    def _on_processing_start(self, event) -> None:
        """Mostra inÃ­cio do processamento."""
        self.logger.debug(f"ðŸ§  [CONSOLE] Processando...")
    
    def _on_processing_end(self, event) -> None:
        """Mostra fim do processamento."""
        self.logger.debug(f"ðŸ§  [CONSOLE] Processamento concluÃ­do")
    
    def _on_error(self, event) -> None:
        """Mostra erro."""
        data = event.data
        error = data.get("error", "Erro desconhecido")
        self._waiting = False
        sys.stdout.write("\r\033[K")
        print(f"\nâŒ Erro: {error}")
        sys.stdout.write(self.prompt)
        sys.stdout.flush()
    
    def default(self, line: str) -> None:
        """Envia mensagem."""
        if line.strip():
            if line.lower() in ["sair", "exit", "quit"]:
                return self.do_exit(line)
            
            self._waiting = True
            sys.stdout.write("\r\033[K")
            print(f"â³ Processando...")
            sys.stdout.write(self.prompt)
            sys.stdout.flush()
            
            asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(
                self._publish_message(line),
                self.loop
            )
    
    async def _publish_message(self, text: str) -> None:
        """Publica mensagem."""
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§  [CONSOLE] Publicando: '{text[:50]}'")
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            EventType.USER_MESSAGE,
            {
                "text": text,
                "source": "cli",
                "user": "admin",
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="cli"
        )
        
        # Aguarda resposta
        timeout = 60
        start = datetime.now()
        while self._waiting and (datetime.now() - start).total_seconds() < timeout:
            await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
        
        if self._waiting:
            self._waiting = False
            sys.stdout.write("\r\033[K")
            print("\nâ° Tempo esgotado!")
            sys.stdout.write(self.prompt)
            sys.stdout.flush()
    
    def do_help(self, arg: str) -> None:
        print("\nComandos: help, sair, status, memory\n")
    
    def do_status(self, arg: str) -> None:
        print("\nâœ… EVA OS rodando")
        print("   Pipeline: Context â†’ Memory â†’ Knowledge â†’ Planner â†’ Consciousness â†’ LLM\n")
    
    def do_memory(self, arg: str) -> None:
        print("\nðŸ’¾ HistÃ³rico da conversa:\n")
        try:
            from src.core.service_registry import ServiceRegistry
            registry = ServiceRegistry()
            brain = registry.get("brain")
            if brain and brain._conversation_history:
                for msg in brain._conversation_history[-10:]:
                    role = "ðŸ‘¤" if msg["role"] == "user" else "ðŸ¤–"
                    print(f"  {role} {msg['content'][:100]}...")
            else:
                print("  (Nenhum histÃ³rico)")
        except:
            print("  (NÃ£o disponÃ­vel)")
        print()
    
    def do_exit(self, arg: str) -> None:
        print("ðŸ‘‹ AtÃ© logo!")
        self._running = False
        return True
    
    def do_EOF(self, arg: str) -> None:
        print()
        return self.do_exit(arg)


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\interfaces\multimodal\multimodal_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador multimodal da EVA."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
import asyncio

from src.kernel.events import EventBus


class MultimodalManager:
    """
    Integra voz, visÃ£o e texto em uma interface unificada.
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "multimodal_manager"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Gerenciador multimodal da EVA"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["interface", "multimodal"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["multimodal", "integration"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self._voice_manager = None
        self._vision_manager = None
        self._running = False
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o MultimodalManager."""
        self.logger.info("MultimodalManager inicializando...")
        
        # Tenta obter voice manager
        try:
            from src.core.service_registry import ServiceRegistry
            registry = ServiceRegistry()
            self._voice_manager = registry.get("voice_manager")
            self._vision_manager = registry.get("vision_manager")
        except:
            self.logger.warning("Voice/Vision managers nÃ£o disponÃ­veis")
        
        self._running = True
        self.logger.info("MultimodalManager inicializado")
    
    async def start(self) -> None:
        """Inicia o MultimodalManager."""
        self.logger.info("MultimodalManager iniciando...")
        self.logger.info("MultimodalManager pronto")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o MultimodalManager."""
        self.logger.info("MultimodalManager desligando...")
        self._running = False
        self.logger.info("MultimodalManager desligado")
    
    # ===== COMANDOS MULTIMODAIS =====
    
    async def process_voice_command(self, text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Processa um comando de voz.
        
        Args:
            text: Texto transcrito
            
        Returns:
            Resultado do processamento
        """
        self.logger.debug(f"Comando de voz: {text[:50]}...")
        
        # Publica como mensagem de usuÃ¡rio
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "user.message",
            {
                "text": text,
                "source": "voice",
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="multimodal_manager"
        )
        
        return {
            "processed": True,
            "text": text,
            "source": "voice",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def process_visual_input(self, image_path: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Processa uma entrada visual.
        
        Args:
            image_path: Caminho da imagem
            
        Returns:
            Resultado do processamento
        """
        self.logger.debug(f"Entrada visual: {image_path}")
        
        if self._vision_manager:
            # Detecta objetos
            objects = await self._vision_manager.detect_objects(image_path)
            
            # Reconhece faces
            faces = await self._vision_manager.recognize_faces(image_path)
            
            return {
                "processed": True,
                "objects": [{"label": o.label, "confidence": o.confidence} for o in objects],
                "faces": len(faces),
                "source": "vision",
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
        
        return {
            "processed": False,
            "error": "Vision Manager nÃ£o disponÃ­vel",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    async def process_multimodal_input(self, text: str = None, 
                                      image_path: str = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Processa entrada multimodal.
        
        Args:
            text: Texto opcional
            image_path: Imagem opcional
            
        Returns:
            Resultado combinado
        """
        result = {
            "processed": True,
            "sources": [],
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        if text:
            text_result = await self.process_voice_command(text)
            result["text"] = text_result
            result["sources"].append("voice")
        
        if image_path:
            vision_result = await self.process_visual_input(image_path)
            result["vision"] = vision_result
            result["sources"].append("vision")
        
        # Publica evento multimodal
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "multimodal.input",
            result,
            source="multimodal_manager"
        )
        
        return result
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        """Health check do MultimodalManager."""
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "voice_available": self._voice_manager is not None,
            "vision_available": self._vision_manager is not None
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\interfaces\vision\vision_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de visÃ£o da EVA (com fallbacks)."""

import asyncio
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List, Callable
from datetime import datetime
from dataclasses import dataclass

from src.kernel.events import EventBus


@dataclass
class DetectedObject:
    """Objeto detectado."""
    label: str
    confidence: float
    x: int
    y: int
    width: int
    height: int


class VisionManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de visÃ£o da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Capturar imagens de cÃ¢meras
    - Detectar objetos (YOLO)
    - Reconhecer faces
    - Processar imagens
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "vision_manager"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Gerenciador de visÃ£o da EVA"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["interface", "vision", "camera"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["vision", "object_detection", "face_recognition"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self._running = False
        self._cameras: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._camera_tasks: Dict[str, asyncio.Task] = {}
        
        self._detector = None
        self._detector_available = False
        self._face_recognizer = None
        self._face_available = False
        self._cv2_available = False
        
        self._on_detection_callback: Optional[Callable] = None
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o VisionManager."""
        self.logger.info("VisionManager inicializando...")
        
        # Tenta carregar OpenCV
        try:
            import cv2
            self._cv2 = cv2
            self._cv2_available = True
            self.logger.info("OpenCV disponÃ­vel")
        except ImportError:
            self.logger.warning("OpenCV nÃ£o disponÃ­vel")
            self._cv2_available = False
        
        # Inicializa detector
        await self._init_detector()
        
        # Inicializa reconhecimento facial
        await self._init_face_recognizer()
        
        self._running = True
        self.logger.info(f"VisionManager inicializado (Detector: {self._detector_available}, Face: {self._face_available})")
    
    async def start(self) -> None:
        """Inicia o VisionManager."""
        self.logger.info("VisionManager iniciando...")
        self.logger.info("VisionManager pronto")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o VisionManager."""
        self.logger.info("VisionManager desligando...")
        self._running = False
        
        for camera_id in list(self._camera_tasks.keys()):
            await self.stop_camera(camera_id)
        
        self.logger.info("VisionManager desligado")
    
    # ===== DETECTOR (YOLO) =====
    
    async def _init_detector(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o detector de objetos."""
        try:
            from ultralytics import YOLO
            model_name = self.config.get("vision.detection_model", "yolov8n.pt")
            self._detector = YOLO(model_name)
            self._detector_available = True
            self.logger.info(f"Detector carregado: {model_name}")
        except ImportError:
            self.logger.warning("Ultralytics/YOLO nÃ£o disponÃ­vel. DetecÃ§Ã£o desativada.")
            self._detector_available = False
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao carregar detector: {e}")
            self._detector_available = False
    
    async def _init_face_recognizer(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o reconhecimento facial."""
        try:
            import face_recognition
            self._face_recognizer = face_recognition
            self._face_available = True
            self.logger.info("Reconhecimento facial disponÃ­vel")
        except ImportError:
            self.logger.warning("face_recognition nÃ£o disponÃ­vel. Reconhecimento facial desativado.")
            self._face_available = False
    
    async def detect_objects(self, image_path: Path) -> List[DetectedObject]:
        """
        Detecta objetos em uma imagem.
        """
        if not self._detector_available:
            self.logger.warning("Detector nÃ£o disponÃ­vel")
            return []
        
        try:
            results = self._detector(str(image_path))
            
            objects = []
            for r in results:
                boxes = r.boxes
                if boxes is not None:
                    for box in boxes:
                        x1, y1, x2, y2 = box.xyxy[0].tolist()
                        conf = float(box.conf[0])
                        cls = int(box.cls[0])
                        label = self._detector.names[cls]
                        
                        objects.append(DetectedObject(
                            label=label,
                            confidence=conf,
                            x=int(x1),
                            y=int(y1),
                            width=int(x2 - x1),
                            height=int(y2 - y1)
                        ))
            
            return objects
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro na detecÃ§Ã£o: {e}")
            return []
    
    async def recognize_faces(self, image_path: Path) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Reconhece faces em uma imagem.
        """
        if not self._face_available:
            self.logger.warning("Reconhecimento facial nÃ£o disponÃ­vel")
            return []
        
        try:
            import face_recognition
            
            image = face_recognition.load_image_file(str(image_path))
            face_locations = face_recognition.face_locations(image)
            face_encodings = face_recognition.face_encodings(image, face_locations)
            
            results = []
            for location, encoding in zip(face_locations, face_encodings):
                results.append({
                    "location": {
                        "top": location[0],
                        "right": location[1],
                        "bottom": location[2],
                        "left": location[3]
                    },
                    "encoding": encoding.tolist() if hasattr(encoding, 'tolist') else None
                })
            
            return results
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro no reconhecimento facial: {e}")
            return []
    
    # ===== CÃ‚MERAS (com fallback) =====
    
    async def start_camera(self, camera_id: str, source: int = 0) -> bool:
        """
        Inicia uma cÃ¢mera.
        """
        if not self._cv2_available:
            self.logger.warning("OpenCV nÃ£o disponÃ­vel. CÃ¢mera simulada.")
            return True
        
        if camera_id in self._camera_tasks:
            self.logger.warning(f"CÃ¢mera {camera_id} jÃ¡ estÃ¡ rodando")
            return False
        
        try:
            cap = self._cv2.VideoCapture(source)
            if not cap.isOpened():
                self.logger.error(f"NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel abrir cÃ¢mera {camera_id}")
                return False
            
            self._cameras[camera_id] = cap
            self._camera_tasks[camera_id] = asyncio.create_task(
                self._camera_loop(camera_id)
            )
            
            self.logger.info(f"CÃ¢mera {camera_id} iniciada (fonte: {source})")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao iniciar cÃ¢mera {camera_id}: {e}")
            return False
    
    async def stop_camera(self, camera_id: str) -> bool:
        """Para uma cÃ¢mera."""
        if camera_id not in self._camera_tasks:
            return False
        
        task = self._camera_tasks.pop(camera_id)
        task.cancel()
        try:
            await task
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            pass
        
        if camera_id in self._cameras:
            cap = self._cameras.pop(camera_id)
            if self._cv2_available:
                cap.release()
        
        self.logger.info(f"CÃ¢mera {camera_id} parada")
        return True
    
    async def _camera_loop(self, camera_id: str) -> None:
        """Loop de captura de uma cÃ¢mera."""
        if not self._cv2_available:
            # Fallback: loop simulado
            while self._running and camera_id in self._cameras:
                await asyncio.sleep(1)
                self.logger.debug(f"[FALLBACK] CÃ¢mera {camera_id} simulada...")
            return
        
        cap = self._cameras.get(camera_id)
        if not cap:
            return
        
        frame_interval = 1.0 / self.config.get("vision.fps", 15)
        
        while self._running and camera_id in self._cameras:
            try:
                ret, frame = cap.read()
                if not ret:
                    await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
                    continue
                
                await self._process_frame(camera_id, frame)
                await asyncio.sleep(frame_interval)
                
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro na cÃ¢mera {camera_id}: {e}")
                await asyncio.sleep(1)
    
    async def _process_frame(self, camera_id: str, frame: Any) -> None:
        """Processa um frame da cÃ¢mera."""
        if not self._detector_available:
            return
        
        try:
            results = self._detector(frame)
            
            objects = []
            for r in results:
                boxes = r.boxes
                if boxes is not None:
                    for box in boxes:
                        x1, y1, x2, y2 = box.xyxy[0].tolist()
                        conf = float(box.conf[0])
                        cls = int(box.cls[0])
                        label = self._detector.names[cls]
                        
                        objects.append({
                            "label": label,
                            "confidence": conf,
                            "x": int(x1),
                            "y": int(y1),
                            "width": int(x2 - x1),
                            "height": int(y2 - y1)
                        })
            
            if objects:
                await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                    "vision.detection",
                    {
                        "camera": camera_id,
                        "objects": objects,
                        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                    },
                    source="vision_manager"
                )
                
                if self._on_detection_callback:
                    if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(self._on_detection_callback):
                        await self._on_detection_callback(camera_id, objects)
                    else:
                        self._on_detection_callback(camera_id, objects)
                        
        except Exception as e:
            pass
    
    # ===== MÃ‰TODOS PÃšBLICOS =====
    
    async def capture_frame(self, camera_id: str) -> Optional[Any]:
        """Captura um frame de uma cÃ¢mera."""
        if not self._cv2_available:
            return None
        
        cap = self._cameras.get(camera_id)
        if not cap:
            return None
        
        ret, frame = cap.read()
        if not ret:
            return None
        
        return frame
    
    async def save_frame(self, camera_id: str, output_path: Path) -> bool:
        """Salva um frame de uma cÃ¢mera."""
        if not self._cv2_available:
            return False
        
        frame = await self.capture_frame(camera_id)
        if frame is None:
            return False
        
        output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._cv2.imwrite(str(output_path), frame)
        return True
    
    def set_detection_callback(self, callback: Callable) -> None:
        """Define callback para detecÃ§Ãµes."""
        self._on_detection_callback = callback
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        """Health check do VisionManager."""
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "cv2_available": self._cv2_available,
            "detector_available": self._detector_available,
            "face_recognizer_available": self._face_available,
            "active_cameras": list(self._cameras.keys()),
            "camera_count": len(self._cameras)
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\interfaces\voice\audio_utils.py ====================
"""UtilitÃ¡rios de Ã¡udio (com fallbacks)."""

import wave
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional
from datetime import datetime
import os


class AudioUtils:
    """
    UtilitÃ¡rios para manipulaÃ§Ã£o de Ã¡udio.
    """
    
    _pyaudio_available = False
    _playsound_available = False
    _librosa_available = False
    _soundfile_available = False
    
    @classmethod
    def _check_imports(cls) -> None:
        """Verifica dependÃªncias disponÃ­veis."""
        if not hasattr(cls, '_checked'):
            try:
                import pyaudio
                cls._pyaudio_available = True
            except:
                pass
            
            try:
                import playsound
                cls._playsound_available = True
            except:
                pass
            
            try:
                import librosa
                cls._librosa_available = True
            except:
                pass
            
            try:
                import soundfile
                cls._soundfile_available = True
            except:
                pass
            
            cls._checked = True
    
    @staticmethod
    def record_audio(duration: float, rate: int = 16000, channels: int = 1) -> Optional[Path]:
        """
        Grava Ã¡udio do microfone.
        """
        AudioUtils._check_imports()
        
        if not AudioUtils._pyaudio_available:
            print("[FALLBACK] PyAudio nÃ£o disponÃ­vel. Criando Ã¡udio simulado...")
            # Cria um arquivo de Ã¡udio simulado (silÃªncio)
            temp_file = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / f"eva_record_{datetime.now().timestamp()}.wav"
            with wave.open(str(temp_file), 'wb') as wf:
                wf.setnchannels(channels)
                wf.setsampwidth(2)
                wf.setframerate(rate)
                wf.writeframes(b'\x00' * int(rate * duration * channels * 2))
            return temp_file
        
        try:
            import pyaudio
            import numpy as np
            
            chunk = 1024
            format = pyaudio.paInt16
            
            p = pyaudio.PyAudio()
            stream = p.open(format=format, channels=channels, rate=rate,
                          input=True, frames_per_buffer=chunk)
            
            frames = []
            
            for _ in range(0, int(rate / chunk * duration)):
                data = stream.read(chunk)
                frames.append(data)
            
            stream.stop_stream()
            stream.close()
            p.terminate()
            
            temp_file = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / f"eva_record_{datetime.now().timestamp()}.wav"
            
            with wave.open(str(temp_file), 'wb') as wf:
                wf.setnchannels(channels)
                wf.setsampwidth(p.get_sample_size(format))
                wf.setframerate(rate)
                wf.writeframes(b''.join(frames))
            
            return temp_file
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Erro ao gravar Ã¡udio: {e}")
            return None
    
    @staticmethod
    def play_audio(audio_path: Path) -> bool:
        """
        Reproduz um arquivo de Ã¡udio.
        """
        AudioUtils._check_imports()
        
        if not AudioUtils._playsound_available:
            print(f"[FALLBACK] Ãudio salvo em: {audio_path}")
            return True
        
        try:
            import playsound
            playsound.playsound(str(audio_path))
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Erro ao reproduzir Ã¡udio: {e}")
            return False
    
    @staticmethod
    def convert_to_wav(input_path: Path, output_path: Optional[Path] = None) -> Optional[Path]:
        """
        Converte Ã¡udio para WAV.
        """
        AudioUtils._check_imports()
        
        if not AudioUtils._librosa_available or not AudioUtils._soundfile_available:
            print("[FALLBACK] Librosa/Soundfile nÃ£o disponÃ­vel. Copiando arquivo...")
            if output_path is None:
                output_path = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / f"eva_converted_{datetime.now().timestamp()}.wav"
            
            # Copia o arquivo
            import shutil
            shutil.copy(input_path, output_path)
            return output_path
        
        try:
            import librosa
            import soundfile as sf
            
            if output_path is None:
                output_path = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / f"eva_converted_{datetime.now().timestamp()}.wav"
            
            audio, sr = librosa.load(str(input_path), sr=16000)
            sf.write(str(output_path), audio, sr)
            
            return output_path
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Erro na conversÃ£o: {e}")
            return None
    
    @staticmethod
    def get_audio_duration(audio_path: Path) -> float:
        """
        ObtÃ©m duraÃ§Ã£o de um arquivo de Ã¡udio.
        """
        AudioUtils._check_imports()
        
        if AudioUtils._librosa_available:
            try:
                import librosa
                duration = librosa.get_duration(filename=str(audio_path))
                return duration
            except:
                pass
        
        try:
            import wave
            with wave.open(str(audio_path), 'rb') as wf:
                frames = wf.getnframes()
                rate = wf.getframerate()
                return frames / float(rate)
        except:
            return 0.0


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\interfaces\voice\voice_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de voz da EVA (com fallbacks)."""

import asyncio
import os
import tempfile
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, Callable
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from src.kernel.events import EventBus


class VoiceManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de voz da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Capturar Ã¡udio do microfone
    - Converter fala em texto (STT)
    - Converter texto em fala (TTS)
    - Gerenciar dispositivos de Ã¡udio
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "voice_manager"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Gerenciador de voz da EVA"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["interface", "voice", "speech"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["voice", "stt", "tts"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self._stt = None
        self._stt_available = False
        self._tts = None
        self._tts_available = False
        self._running = False
        self._listening = False
        self._record_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
        
        self._on_phrase_callback: Optional[Callable] = None
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o VoiceManager."""
        self.logger.info("VoiceManager inicializando...")
        
        # Inicializa STT (tenta carregar)
        await self._init_stt()
        
        # Inicializa TTS (tenta carregar)
        await self._init_tts()
        
        self._running = True
        self.logger.info(f"VoiceManager inicializado (STT: {self._stt_available}, TTS: {self._tts_available})")
    
    async def start(self) -> None:
        """Inicia o VoiceManager."""
        self.logger.info("VoiceManager iniciando...")
        self.logger.info("VoiceManager pronto")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o VoiceManager."""
        self.logger.info("VoiceManager desligando...")
        self._running = False
        
        if self._record_task:
            self._record_task.cancel()
            try:
                await self._record_task
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
        
        self.logger.info("VoiceManager desligado")
    
    # ===== STT (Speech to Text) =====
    
    async def _init_stt(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o STT com fallback."""
        try:
            import whisper
            model_name = self.config.get("voice.stt_model", "base")
            self._stt = whisper.load_model(model_name)
            self._stt_available = True
            self.logger.info(f"STT carregado: {model_name}")
        except ImportError:
            self.logger.warning("Whisper nÃ£o disponÃ­vel. STT desativado.")
            self._stt_available = False
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao carregar STT: {e}")
            self._stt_available = False
    
    async def speech_to_text(self, audio_path: Path) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        Converte Ã¡udio para texto.
        
        Args:
            audio_path: Caminho do arquivo de Ã¡udio
            
        Returns:
            Texto transcrito ou None se falhar
        """
        if not self._stt_available:
            self.logger.warning("STT nÃ£o disponÃ­vel")
            return None
        
        try:
            result = self._stt.transcribe(str(audio_path))
            text = result.get("text", "").strip()
            if text:
                self.logger.debug(f"STT: {text[:50]}...")
                return text
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro no STT: {e}")
            return None
    
    # ===== TTS (Text to Speech) =====
    
    async def _init_tts(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o TTS com fallback."""
        try:
            from TTS.api import TTS
            
            model_name = self.config.get("voice.tts_model", "tts_models/pt-br/fairseq/vits")
            self._tts = TTS(model_name)
            self._tts_available = True
            self.logger.info(f"TTS carregado: {model_name}")
        except ImportError:
            self.logger.warning("TTS nÃ£o disponÃ­vel. TTS desativado.")
            self._tts_available = False
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao carregar TTS: {e}")
            self._tts_available = False
    
    async def text_to_speech(self, text: str, output_path: Optional[Path] = None) -> Optional[Path]:
        """
        Converte texto para fala.
        
        Args:
            text: Texto a ser falado
            output_path: Caminho para salvar o Ã¡udio
            
        Returns:
            Caminho do arquivo de Ã¡udio ou None se falhar
        """
        if not self._tts_available:
            self.logger.warning("TTS nÃ£o disponÃ­vel")
            # Fallback: apenas log
            self.logger.info(f"[FALLBACK] TTS: {text[:100]}...")
            return None
        
        try:
            if output_path is None:
                output_path = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / f"eva_tts_{datetime.now().timestamp()}.wav"
            
            self._tts.tts_to_file(text=text, file_path=str(output_path))
            self.logger.debug(f"TTS gerado: {output_path}")
            return output_path
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro no TTS: {e}")
            return None
    
    # ===== GRAVAÃ‡ÃƒO (com fallback) =====
    
    async def start_listening(self, callback: Optional[Callable] = None) -> None:
        """
        Inicia escuta contÃ­nua do microfone.
        """
        if self._listening:
            return
        
        self._listening = True
        self._on_phrase_callback = callback
        
        try:
            self._record_task = asyncio.create_task(self._continuous_listen())
            self.logger.info("Escuta contÃ­nua iniciada")
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao iniciar escuta: {e}")
            self._listening = False
    
    async def stop_listening(self) -> None:
        """Para a escuta contÃ­nua."""
        self._listening = False
        
        if self._record_task:
            self._record_task.cancel()
            try:
                await self._record_task
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
            self._record_task = None
        
        self.logger.info("Escuta contÃ­nua parada")
    
    async def _continuous_listen(self) -> None:
        """Loop de escuta contÃ­nua (com fallback)."""
        try:
            import pyaudio
            import wave
            import numpy as np
            
            chunk = 1024
            format = pyaudio.paInt16
            channels = 1
            rate = 16000
            silence_threshold = 500
            silence_duration = 2.0
            
            p = pyaudio.PyAudio()
            stream = p.open(format=format, channels=channels, rate=rate,
                          input=True, frames_per_buffer=chunk)
            
            frames = []
            silent_frames = 0
            max_silent_frames = int(rate / chunk * silence_duration)
            recording = False
            
            self.logger.info("Microfone ativo. Aguardando fala...")
            
            while self._listening:
                data = stream.read(chunk, exception_on_overflow=False)
                audio_data = np.frombuffer(data, dtype=np.int16)
                volume = np.abs(audio_data).mean()
                
                if volume > silence_threshold:
                    if not recording:
                        recording = True
                        frames = []
                        self.logger.debug("InÃ­cio da fala detectado")
                    frames.append(data)
                    silent_frames = 0
                elif recording:
                    frames.append(data)
                    silent_frames += 1
                    
                    if silent_frames > max_silent_frames:
                        recording = False
                        self.logger.debug("Fim da fala detectado")
                        
                        temp_file = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / f"eva_speech_{datetime.now().timestamp()}.wav"
                        
                        with wave.open(str(temp_file), 'wb') as wf:
                            wf.setnchannels(channels)
                            wf.setsampwidth(p.get_sample_size(format))
                            wf.setframerate(rate)
                            wf.writeframes(b''.join(frames))
                        
                        asyncio.create_task(self._process_audio(temp_file))
                        frames = []
                
                await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
            
            stream.stop_stream()
            stream.close()
            p.terminate()
            
        except ImportError:
            self.logger.warning("PyAudio nÃ£o disponÃ­vel. Escuta contÃ­nua desativada.")
            self._listening = False
            # MantÃ©m rodando em modo sleep
            while self._listening:
                await asyncio.sleep(1)
                self.logger.debug("[FALLBACK] Escuta simulada...")
                
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro na escuta contÃ­nua: {e}")
            self._listening = False
    
    async def _process_audio(self, audio_path: Path) -> None:
        """Processa Ã¡udio capturado."""
        try:
            text = await self.speech_to_text(audio_path)
            
            if text:
                await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                    "voice.input",
                    {
                        "text": text,
                        "source": "voice",
                        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                    },
                    source="voice_manager"
                )
                
                if self._on_phrase_callback:
                    if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(self._on_phrase_callback):
                        await self._on_phrase_callback(text)
                    else:
                        self._on_phrase_callback(text)
            
            try:
                audio_path.unlink()
            except:
                pass
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao processar Ã¡udio: {e}")
    
    # ===== MÃ‰TODOS PÃšBLICOS =====
    
    async def say(self, text: str) -> bool:
        """
        Faz a EVA falar um texto.
        """
        if not self._tts_available:
            self.logger.info(f"[FALLBACK] EVA diria: {text[:100]}...")
            return True
        
        audio_path = await self.text_to_speech(text)
        
        if audio_path:
            try:
                import playsound
                playsound.playsound(str(audio_path))
                try:
                    audio_path.unlink()
                except:
                    pass
                return True
            except ImportError:
                self.logger.warning("playsound nÃ£o disponÃ­vel. Ãudio salvo em: {audio_path}")
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro ao reproduzir Ã¡udio: {e}")
                return False
        
        return False
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        """Health check do VoiceManager."""
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "stt_available": self._stt_available,
            "tts_available": self._tts_available,
            "listening": self._listening
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\interfaces\web\web_server.py ====================
"""Servidor Web para a EVA OS."""

import asyncio
import json
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, Set
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

from fastapi import FastAPI, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, JSONResponse
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
from pydantic import BaseModel
import uvicorn

from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus
from src.core.event_types import EventType


class MessageRequest(BaseModel):
    text: str
    user: str = "Alberto"
    mode: str = "normal"


class WebServer:
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self.app = FastAPI(title="EVA OS Web Interface", version="2.0.0")
        self._active_websockets: Set[WebSocket] = set()
        self._running = False
        self._shutdown_in_progress = False
        
        self._setup_routes()
        self._setup_websocket()
        self._setup_static()
        
        # Registra listeners
        self.event_bus.subscribe(EventType.ASSISTANT_RESPONSE, self._on_response)
        self.event_bus.subscribe(EventType.PROCESSING_START, self._on_processing_start)
        self.event_bus.subscribe(EventType.PROCESSING_END, self._on_processing_end)
        self.event_bus.subscribe(EventType.COGNITIVE_ERROR, self._on_error)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("reminder.notification", self._on_reminder)
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ [WebServer] Inicializado")
    
    def _setup_static(self) -> None:
        web_path = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.parent / "web"
        if web_path.exists():
            self.app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=str(web_path)), name="static")
    
    def _setup_routes(self) -> None:
        @self.app.get("/")
        async def index():
            web_path = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.parent / "web" / "index.html"
            if web_path.exists():
                return FileResponse(str(web_path))
            return JSONResponse({"error": "Interface nÃ£o encontrada"}, status_code=404)
        
        @self.app.get("/health")
        async def health():
            return {"status": "ok", "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()}
        
        @self.app.post("/message")
        async def message(request: MessageRequest):
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                EventType.USER_MESSAGE,
                {
                    "text": request.text,
                    "user": request.user,
                    "mode": request.mode,
                    "source": "web",
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                },
                source="web_server"
            )
            return {"status": "received", "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()}
        
        @self.app.get("/status")
        async def status():
            return {
                "status": "running",
                "websockets": len(self._active_websockets),
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
    
    def _setup_websocket(self) -> None:
        @self.app.websocket("/ws")
        async def websocket_endpoint(websocket: WebSocket):
            await websocket.accept()
            self._active_websockets.add(websocket)
            self.logger.info(f"ðŸ”Œ WebSocket conectado: {len(self._active_websockets)}")
            
            try:
                while True:
                    data = await websocket.receive_text()
                    await self._handle_websocket_message(websocket, data)
            except WebSocketDisconnect:
                if websocket in self._active_websockets:
                    self._active_websockets.remove(websocket)
                self.logger.info(f"ðŸ”Œ WebSocket desconectado: {len(self._active_websockets)}")
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"âŒ Erro no WebSocket: {e}")
                if websocket in self._active_websockets:
                    self._active_websockets.remove(websocket)
    
    async def _handle_websocket_message(self, websocket: WebSocket, data: str) -> None:
        try:
            message = json.loads(data)
            
            if message.get("type") == "user.message":
                text = message.get("text", "")
                user = message.get("user", "Alberto")
                mode = message.get("mode", "normal")
                
                await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                    EventType.USER_MESSAGE,
                    {
                        "text": text,
                        "user": user,
                        "mode": mode,
                        "source": "web",
                        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                    },
                    source="web_server"
                )
            elif message.get("type") == "mode.change":
                mode = message.get("mode", "normal")
                self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“± Modo alterado: {mode}")
            else:
                self.logger.warning(f"âš ï¸ Tipo de mensagem desconhecido: {message.get('type')}")
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            self.logger.error(f"âŒ JSON invÃ¡lido: {data}")
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"âŒ Erro ao processar mensagem: {e}")
    
    # ===== EVENTBUS LISTENERS =====
    
    async def _on_response(self, event: Event) -> None:
        await self._broadcast({
            "type": "assistant.response",
            "text": event.data.get("text", ""),
            "user": event.data.get("user", "EVA"),
            "metadata": event.data.get("metadata", {}),
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
    
    async def _on_processing_start(self, event: Event) -> None:
        await self._broadcast({"type": "processing.start"})
    
    async def _on_processing_end(self, event: Event) -> None:
        await self._broadcast({"type": "processing.end"})
    
    async def _on_error(self, event: Event) -> None:
        await self._broadcast({
            "type": "error",
            "message": event.data.get("error", "Erro desconhecido")
        })
    
    async def _on_reminder(self, event: Event) -> None:
        await self._broadcast({
            "type": "assistant.response",
            "text": f"ðŸ”” {event.data.get('title', 'Lembrete')}\n{event.data.get('description', '')}",
            "user": "EVA",
            "metadata": {"type": "reminder"},
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
    
    async def _broadcast(self, message: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        if not self._active_websockets:
            return
        
        data = json.dumps(message)
        disconnected = set()
        
        for ws in self._active_websockets:
            try:
                await ws.send_text(data)
            except Exception:
                disconnected.add(ws)
        
        for ws in disconnected:
            if ws in self._active_websockets:
                self._active_websockets.remove(ws)
    
    # ===== SERVIDOR =====
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ [WebServer] Inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        self._shutdown_in_progress = False
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ [WebServer] Inicializado")
    
    async def start(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ [WebServer] Iniciando...")
        
        host = self.config.get("network.api_host", "0.0.0.0")
        port = self.config.get("network.api_port", 8000)
        
        config = uvicorn.Config(self.app, host=host, port=port, log_level="info")
        server = uvicorn.Server(config)
        
        def run_server():
            asyncio.run(server.serve())
        
        import threading
        thread = threading.Thread(target=run_server, daemon=True)
        thread.start()
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸŒ [WebServer] Rodando em http://{host}:{port}")
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“± Interface: http://{host}:{port}")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ [WebServer] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        self._shutdown_in_progress = True
        
        for ws in list(self._active_websockets):
            try:
                await ws.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
        self._active_websockets.clear()
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ [WebServer] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "websockets": len(self._active_websockets)
        }

"""Boot - O maestro da EVA. Inicializa todo o sistema."""

import asyncio
import sys
import signal
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional, List
from datetime import datetime

from src.kernel.config import KernelConfig
from src.kernel.logger import KernelLogger
from src.core import Core, SystemState, PluginStatus


class Boot:
    """
    O maestro da EVA.
    Ãšnica responsabilidade: Inicializar a EVA.
    NÃ£o executa IA, nÃ£o executa agentes, nÃ£o sabe memÃ³ria.
    Apenas inicia.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config_path: Optional[Path] = None):
        self.config_path = config_path or Path("./config/kernel.json")
        
        # Componentes
        self.config: Optional[KernelConfig] = None
        self.logger: Optional[KernelLogger] = None
        self.core: Optional[Core] = None
        
        # Estado
        self._boot_state = "OFFLINE"
        self._start_time: Optional[datetime] = None
        self._is_shutting_down = False
        
        # MÃ³dulos carregados
        self._loaded_modules: List[str] = []

        """
        print(banner)
    
    def _print_boot_progress(self, step: str, status: str = "âœ“") -> None:
        """Exibe progresso do boot."""
        print(f"  {status} {step}")
    
    def _set_boot_state(self, state: str) -> None:
        """Atualiza estado do boot."""
        self._boot_state = state
        if self.logger:
            self.logger.debug(f"Boot state: {state}")
    
    async def boot(self) -> bool:
        """
        Executa toda a sequÃªncia de inicializaÃ§Ã£o.
        Retorna True se bem-sucedido.
        """
        self._start_time = datetime.now()
        self._is_shutting_down = False
        
        # Banner
        self._print_banner()
        print("=" * 50)
        print(f"EVA OS v1.0")
        print(f"Iniciando em: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        print("=" * 50)
        print()
        
        try:
            # ====== 1. CONFIGURAÃ‡ÃƒO ======
            self._set_boot_state("LOADING_CONFIG")
            self._print_boot_progress("Carregando configuraÃ§Ãµes...")
            
            self.config = KernelConfig(self.config_path)
            self.config.load()
            
            # ====== 2. LOGGER ======
            self._set_boot_state("INITIALIZING_LOGGER")
            self._print_boot_progress("Inicializando Logger...")
            
            self.logger = KernelLogger(self.config)
            self.logger.info("=" * 50)
            self.logger.info("EVA OS v1.0 - Inicializando")
            self.logger.info("=" * 50)
            
            # ====== 3. CORE ======
            self._set_boot_state("INITIALIZING_CORE")
            self._print_boot_progress("Inicializando Core...")
            
            self.core = Core(self.logger, self.config)
            await self.core.initialize()
            
            # ====== 4. EVENT BUS ======
            self._set_boot_state("STARTING_EVENT_BUS")
            self._print_boot_progress("Iniciando EventBus...")
            
            # JÃ¡ foi iniciado pelo Core
            self.logger.info("EventBus ativo")
            
            # ====== 5. SERVICE REGISTRY ======
            self._set_boot_state("STARTING_SERVICE_REGISTRY")
            self._print_boot_progress("Iniciando Service Registry...")
            
            # JÃ¡ foi iniciado pelo Core
            self.logger.info("Service Registry ativo")
            
            # ====== 6. PLUGIN MANAGER - ADICIONAR CAMINHOS ======
            self._set_boot_state("CONFIGURING_PLUGIN_MANAGER")
            self._print_boot_progress("Configurando Plugin Manager...")
            
            # Adiciona caminhos para scanear
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_scan_path(Path("./src/consciousness"))
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_scan_path(Path("./src/planner"))
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_scan_path(Path("./src/memory"))
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_scan_path(Path("./src/knowledge"))
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_scan_path(Path("./src/agents"))
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_scan_path(Path("./src/tools"))
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_scan_path(Path("./src/security"))
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_scan_path(Path("./src/context"))
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_scan_path(Path("./src/interfaces"))
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_scan_path(Path("./src/api"))
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_scan_path(Path("./src/personality"))
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_scan_path(Path("./src/learning"))
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_scan_path(Path("./src/evolution"))
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_scan_path(Path("./src/hardware"))
            
            # Adiciona padrÃµes de exclusÃ£o
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_exclude_pattern("__pycache__")
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_exclude_pattern("tests")
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_exclude_pattern("test")
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_exclude_pattern("venv")
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_exclude_pattern(".venv")
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_exclude_pattern("env")
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_exclude_pattern(".git")
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_exclude_pattern(".idea")
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_exclude_pattern(".vscode")
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_exclude_pattern("node_modules")
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_exclude_pattern("dist")
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_exclude_pattern("build")
            self.core.plugin_manager.add_exclude_pattern("__init__.py")
            
            self.logger.info("Plugin Manager configurado")
            
            # ====== 7. SCANEAR PLUGINS ======
            self._set_boot_state("SCANNING_PLUGINS")
            self._print_boot_progress("Escaneando plugins...")
            
            plugins = await self.core.plugin_manager.scan()
            
            self.logger.info(f"Plugins encontrados: {len(plugins)}")
            
            # ====== 8. CARREGAR PLUGINS ======
            self._set_boot_state("LOADING_PLUGINS")
            self._print_boot_progress("Carregando plugins...")
            
            results = await self.core.plugin_manager.load_all()
            
            loaded_count = sum(1 for success in results.values() if success)
            self.logger.info(f"Plugins carregados: {loaded_count}/{len(results)}")
            
            # Atualiza lista de mÃ³dulos carregados
            self._loaded_modules = [
                p.manifest.name for p in self.core.plugin_manager.get_plugins()
                if p.status in [PluginStatus.LOADED, PluginStatus.INITIALIZED, PluginStatus.STARTED]
            ]
            
            # ====== 9. REGISTRAR SERVIÃ‡OS ADICIONAIS ======
            self._set_boot_state("REGISTERING_SERVICES")
            self._print_boot_progress("Registrando serviÃ§os...")
            
            # Registra serviÃ§os do Kernel
            self.core.service_registry.register("kernel_boot", self)
            self.core.service_registry.register("kernel_config", self.config)
            self.core.service_registry.register("kernel_logger", self.logger)
            
            services = self.core.service_registry.list()
            self.logger.info(f"ServiÃ§os registrados: {len(services)}")
            
            # ====== 10. HEALTH CHECK ======
            self._set_boot_state("CHECKING_HEALTH")
            self._print_boot_progress("Verificando saÃºde do sistema...")
            
            health = await self.core.health_manager.check()
            
            # Verifica se tudo estÃ¡ ok
            cpu_ok = not health.get("cpu", {}).get("warning", False)
            ram_ok = not health.get("ram", {}).get("warning", False)
            disk_ok = not health.get("disk", {}).get("warning", False)
            
            if cpu_ok and ram_ok and disk_ok:
                self.logger.info("Health Check: OK")
                self._print_boot_progress("Health Check: OK", "âœ“")
            else:
                warnings = []
                if not cpu_ok:
                    warnings.append(f"CPU: {health['cpu'].get('percent')}%")
                if not ram_ok:
                    warnings.append(f"RAM: {health['ram'].get('percent')}%")
                if not disk_ok:
                    warnings.append(f"DISK: {health['disk'].get('percent')}%")
                self.logger.warning(f"Health Check: Alertas - {', '.join(warnings)}")
                self._print_boot_progress(f"Health Check: Alertas - {', '.join(warnings)}", "âš ")
            
            # ====== 11. MUDAR ESTADO PARA IDLE ======
            self._set_boot_state("READY")
            
            self.core.state_manager.set_state(
                SystemState.IDLE,
                reason="Sistema inicializado com sucesso",
                source="kernel",
                data={
                    "version": "1.0.0",
                    "plugins": len(self._loaded_modules),
                    "boot_time": self._start_time.isoformat()
                }
            )
            
            # ====== 12. PUBLICAR SYSTEM_READY ======
            self._set_boot_state("SYSTEM_READY")
            
            await self.core.event_bus.publish_async(
                "SYSTEM_READY",
                {
                    "version": "1.0.0",
                    "plugins": self._loaded_modules,
                    "started_at": self._start_time.isoformat()
                },
                source="kernel"
            )
            
            self.logger.info("SYSTEM_READY publicado")
            
            # ====== 13. EXECUTAR TAREFAS DE STARTUP ======
            if hasattr(self.core, 'scheduler'):
                self._set_boot_state("RUNNING_STARTUP_TASKS")
                self._print_boot_progress("Executando tarefas de startup...")
                self.core.scheduler.run_startup_tasks()
            
            # ====== 14. FINALIZADO ======
            self._set_boot_state("IDLE")
            
            boot_time = (datetime.now() - self._start_time).total_seconds()
            
            print()
            print("=" * 50)
            print(f"âœ… EVA OS v1.0 - Inicializado com sucesso!")
            print(f"   Tempo de boot: {boot_time:.2f}s")
            print(f"   Plugins: {len(self._loaded_modules)}")
            print(f"   Estado: {self.core.state_manager.get_state_name()}")
            print("=" * 50)
            print()
            print("   Aguardando eventos...")
            print()
            print("=" * 50)
            
            self.logger.info(f"Boot concluÃ­do em {boot_time:.2f}s")
            self.logger.info(f"Estado: {self.core.state_manager.get_state_name()}")
            
            # ====== 15. REGISTRA NO WATCHDOG ======
            if hasattr(self.core, 'health_manager'):
                self.core.health_manager.register_module("kernel", self)
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            self._set_boot_state("ERROR")
            
            print()
            print("=" * 50)
            print(f"âŒ ERRO durante o boot: {e}")
            print("=" * 50)
            
            if self.logger:
                self.logger.error(f"Boot falhou: {e}")
                self.logger.exception(e)
            
            # Tenta entrar em modo de erro
            if self.core:
                self.core.state_manager.set_state(
                    SystemState.ERROR,
                    reason=f"Boot falhou: {e}",
                    source="kernel"
                )
            
            return False
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga a EVA de forma segura."""
        if self._is_shutting_down:
            return
        
        self._is_shutting_down = True
        
        self.logger.info("=" * 50)
        self.logger.info("EVA OS - Desligando...")
        self.logger.info("=" * 50)
        
        print()
        print("=" * 50)
        print("Desligando EVA OS...")
        print("=" * 50)
        
        # Executa tarefas de shutdown
        if hasattr(self.core, 'scheduler'):
            await self.core.scheduler.run_shutdown_tasks()
        
        # Publica evento de desligamento
        if self.core:
            await self.core.event_bus.publish_async(
                "SYSTEM_SHUTDOWN",
                {"reason": "Desligamento solicitado"},
                source="kernel"
            )
            
            # Muda estado
            self.core.state_manager.set_state(
                SystemState.SHUTDOWN,
                reason="Desligamento",
                source="kernel"
            )
            
            # Desliga Core
            await self.core.shutdown()
        
        if self.logger:
            self.logger.info("EVA OS desligado")
        
        print("âœ… EVA OS desligado com sucesso")
        print("=" * 50)
    
    async def restart(self) -> bool:
        """Reinicia a EVA."""
        self.logger.info("Reiniciando EVA OS...")
        
        print()
        print("=" * 50)
        print("Reiniciando EVA OS...")
        print("=" * 50)
        
        await self.shutdown()
        
        # Aguarda um momento
        await asyncio.sleep(2)
        
        # Reinicia
        return await self.boot()
    
    async def safe_mode(self) -> bool:
        """Inicia em modo seguro (apenas Core)."""
        self._print_boot_progress("Modo seguro ativado", "âš ")
        self.logger.warning("Iniciando em modo seguro (Core apenas)")
        
        try:
            self.config = KernelConfig(self.config_path)
            self.config.load()
            
            self.logger = KernelLogger(self.config)
            
            self.core = Core(self.logger, self.config)
            await self.core.initialize()
            
            self.core.state_manager.set_state(
                SystemState.IDLE,
                reason="Modo seguro",
                source="kernel"
            )
            
            print()
            print("=" * 50)
            print("âš  Modo Seguro ativo")
            print("   Apenas Core carregado")
            print("   Plugins desativados")
            print("=" * 50)
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"âŒ Erro no modo seguro: {e}")
            return False
    
    def get_boot_state(self) -> str:
        """ObtÃ©m estado atual do boot."""
        return self._boot_state
    
    def get_boot_info(self) -> dict:
        """ObtÃ©m informaÃ§Ãµes do boot."""
        return {
            "state": self._boot_state,
            "start_time": self._start_time.isoformat() if self._start_time else None,
            "plugins": self._loaded_modules,
            "plugin_count": len(self._loaded_modules),
            "core_initialized": self.core is not None
        }
    
    def get_loaded_modules(self) -> List[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m lista de mÃ³dulos carregados."""
        return self._loaded_modules.copy()
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        """Health check do Boot."""
        return {
            "status": "alive" if self._boot_state != "ERROR" else "error",
            "boot_state": self._boot_state,
            "uptime": (datetime.now() - self._start_time).total_seconds() if self._start_time else 0,
            "plugins": len(self._loaded_modules)
        }

class KernelConfig:
    """

    # ConfiguraÃ§Ã£o padrÃ£o
    DEFAULT_CONFIG = {
        "system": {
