            "host": "0.0.0.0",
            "port": 8000,
            "api_host": "0.0.0.0",
            "api_port": 8000
        },
        "security": {
            "enable_authentication": True,
            "enable_encryption": True,
            "enable_audit": True
        },
        "home_assistant": {
            "enable": False,
            "host": "localhost",
            "port": 8123,
            "token": ""
        },
        "mikrotik": {
            "enable": False,
            "host": "192.168.88.1",
            "port": 8728,
            "user": "admin",
            "password": ""
        },
        "resources": {
            "max_cpu_percent": 80,
            "max_ram_percent": 85,
            "max_disk_percent": 90
        },
        "features": {
            "auto_learning": True,
            "auto_evolution": True,
            "auto_programming": False
        }
    }
    
    def __init__(self, config_path: Optional[Path] = None):
        self.config_path = config_path or Path("./config/kernel.json")
        self._config: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._loaded_at: Optional[datetime] = None
        self._modified: bool = False
    
    def load(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Carrega configuraÃ§Ã£o do arquivo.
        Se nÃ£o existir, cria com valores padrÃ£o.
        """
        if self.config_path.exists():
            try:
                with open(self.config_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    self._config = json.load(f)
                self._loaded_at = datetime.now()
                self._modified = False
                
                # Mescla com defaults (para novas chaves)
                self._merge_with_defaults()
                
                return self._config
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Erro ao carregar configuraÃ§Ã£o: {e}")
                self._config = self.DEFAULT_CONFIG.copy()
                self._loaded_at = datetime.now()
                self.save()
                return self._config
        else:
            # Cria arquivo com defaults
            self._config = self.DEFAULT_CONFIG.copy()
            self._loaded_at = datetime.now()
            self.save()
            return self._config
    
    def _merge_with_defaults(self) -> None:
        """Mescla configuraÃ§Ã£o atual com defaults (para novas chaves)."""
        def deep_merge(base: dict, override: dict) -> dict:
            result = base.copy()
            for key, value in override.items():
                if key in result and isinstance(result[key], dict) and isinstance(value, dict):
                    result[key] = deep_merge(result[key], value)
                elif key not in result:
                    result[key] = value
            return result
        
        self._config = deep_merge(self.DEFAULT_CONFIG, self._config)
    
    def save(self) -> None:
        """Salva configuraÃ§Ã£o atual no arquivo."""
        self.config_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        try:
            with open(self.config_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(self._config, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
            self._modified = False
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Erro ao salvar configuraÃ§Ã£o: {e}")
    
    def reload(self) -> None:
        """Recarrega configuraÃ§Ã£o do arquivo."""
        self.load()
    
    def validate(self) -> List[str]:
        """Valida a configuraÃ§Ã£o. Retorna lista de erros."""
        errors = []
        
        # Verifica campos obrigatÃ³rios
        required = [
            ("system.name", "Nome do sistema nÃ£o definido"),
            ("system.version", "VersÃ£o do sistema nÃ£o definida"),
            ("paths.data", "Caminho de dados nÃ£o definido")
        ]
        
        for key, msg in required:
            if self.get(key) is None:
                errors.append(msg)
        
        # Verifica LLM
        llm_provider = self.get("llm.provider")
        if llm_provider not in ["ollama", "openai", "anthropic", "local"]:
            errors.append(f"Provedor LLM invÃ¡lido: {llm_provider}")
        
        # Verifica paths existem
        for path_key in ["data", "logs", "models"]:
            path = self.get(f"paths.{path_key}")
            if path:
                p = Path(path)
                if not p.exists():
                    try:
                        p.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                    except Exception:
                        errors.append(f"NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel criar diretÃ³rio: {path}")
        
        return errors
    
    def get(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        """
        ObtÃ©m um valor de configuraÃ§Ã£o.
        Suporta notaÃ§Ã£o de pontos: "system.name"
        """
        keys = key.split(".")
        value = self._config
        
        for k in keys:
            if isinstance(value, dict):
                if k in value:
                    value = value[k]
                else:
                    return default
            else:
                return default
        
        return value
    
    def set(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
        """
        Define um valor de configuraÃ§Ã£o.
        Suporta notaÃ§Ã£o de pontos: "system.name"
        """
        keys = key.split(".")
        target = self._config
        
        # Navega atÃ© o Ãºltimo nÃ­vel
        for k in keys[:-1]:
            if k not in target:
                target[k] = {}
            target = target[k]
        
        # Define o valor
        target[keys[-1]] = value
        self._modified = True
    
    def get_all(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m toda a configuraÃ§Ã£o."""
        return self._config.copy()
    
    def get_section(self, section: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m uma seÃ§Ã£o da configuraÃ§Ã£o."""
        return self._config.get(section, {})
    
    def is_modified(self) -> bool:
        """Verifica se a configuraÃ§Ã£o foi modificada."""
        return self._modified
    
    def get_load_time(self) -> Optional[datetime]:
        """ObtÃ©m o horÃ¡rio de carregamento."""
        return self._loaded_at
    
    def export_env(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """Exporta configuraÃ§Ã£o como variÃ¡veis de ambiente."""
        env = {}
        
        def flatten(obj: dict, prefix: str = "") -> None:
            for key, value in obj.items():
                new_key = f"{prefix}_{key}".upper() if prefix else key.upper()
                if isinstance(value, dict):
                    flatten(value, new_key)
                else:
                    env[new_key] = str(value)
        
        flatten(self._config)
        return env
    
    def apply_env(self) -> None:
        """Aplica variÃ¡veis de ambiente sobre a configuraÃ§Ã£o."""
        for key, value in os.environ.items():
            if key.startswith("EVA_"):
                config_key = key[4:].lower().replace("_", ".")
                try:
                    # Tenta converter para tipo apropriado
                    if value.lower() == "true":
                        parsed = True
                    elif value.lower() == "false":
                        parsed = False
                    elif value.isdigit():
                        parsed = int(value)
                    else:
                        parsed = value
                    
                    self.set(config_key, parsed)
                except Exception:
                    pass


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\kernel\events.py ====================
"""Barramento de eventos do EVA OS."""

import asyncio
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional
from uuid import uuid4


class EventPriority(Enum):
    """Prioridades para eventos."""
    CRITICAL = 0
    HIGH = 1
    NORMAL = 2
    LOW = 3


@dataclass
class Event:
    """Evento no barramento do sistema."""
    type: str
    payload: Any
    source: str
    priority: EventPriority = EventPriority.NORMAL
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    correlation_id: Optional[str] = None


class EventBus:
    """Barramento de eventos desacoplado."""
    
    def __init__(self, logger):
        self.logger = logger
        self._listeners: Dict[str, List[Callable]] = {}
        self._queue: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue()
        self._running = False
        self._worker_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicia o barramento."""
        self._running = True
        self._worker_task = asyncio.create_task(self._process_events())
        self.logger.info("EventBus inicializado")
    
    def subscribe(self, event_type: str, callback: Callable) -> None:
        """Registra um ouvinte para um tipo de evento."""
        if event_type not in self._listeners:
            self._listeners[event_type] = []
        self._listeners[event_type].append(callback)
        self.logger.debug(f"Listener registrado para: {event_type}")
    
    def unsubscribe(self, event_type: str, callback: Callable) -> None:
        """Remove um ouvinte."""
        if event_type in self._listeners:
            self._listeners[event_type].remove(callback)
    
    async def publish(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Publica um evento no barramento."""
        await self._queue.put(event)
        self.logger.debug(f"Evento publicado: {event.type} de {event.source}")
    
    async def _process_events(self) -> None:
        """Processa eventos em fila."""
        while self._running:
            try:
                event = await self._queue.get()
                await self._dispatch(event)
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro processando evento: {e}")
    
    async def _dispatch(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Distribui evento para todos os ouvintes."""
        if event.type in self._listeners:
            for callback in self._listeners[event.type]:
                try:
                    if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(callback):
                        await callback(event)
                    else:
                        callback(event)
                except Exception as e:
                    self.logger.error(f"Erro no listener {callback}: {e}")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o barramento."""
        self._running = False
        if self._worker_task:
            self._worker_task.cancel()
            try:
                await self._worker_task
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
        self.logger.info("EventBus desligado")


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\kernel\logger.py ====================
"""Sistema de logging do Kernel."""

import logging
import sys
import json
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any
from logging.handlers import RotatingFileHandler


class KernelLogger:
    """
    Muito mais que logs.
    Cada evento da EVA serÃ¡ registrado.
    No futuro teremos auditoria completa.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.config = config
        
        # ConfiguraÃ§Ã£o
        self.log_level = config.get("system.log_level", "INFO")
        self.log_file = config.get("system.log_file", "./data/logs/eva.log")
        
        # Cria logger
        self._logger = logging.getLogger("EVA")
        self._logger.setLevel(getattr(logging, self.log_level))
        
        # Remove handlers existentes
        self._logger.handlers.clear()
        
        # Configura handlers
        self._setup_console_handler()
        self._setup_file_handler()
        
        # Para auditoria
        self._audit_log: Optional[Path] = None
        self._setup_audit()
    
    def _setup_console_handler(self) -> None:
        """Configura handler para console."""
        console_handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
        console_handler.setLevel(getattr(logging, self.log_level))
        console_handler.setFormatter(self._get_formatter(console=True))
        self._logger.addHandler(console_handler)
    
    def _setup_file_handler(self) -> None:
        """Configura handler para arquivo."""
        # Cria diretÃ³rio
        log_path = Path(self.log_file)
        log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        # Handler rotativo
        file_handler = RotatingFileHandler(
            log_path,
            maxBytes=10 * 1024 * 1024,  # 10MB
            backupCount=10,
            encoding="utf-8"
        )
        file_handler.setLevel(getattr(logging, self.log_level))
        file_handler.setFormatter(self._get_formatter(console=False))
        self._logger.addHandler(file_handler)
    
    def _setup_audit(self) -> None:
        """Configura log de auditoria."""
        audit_path = Path(self.log_file).parent / "audit.log"
        self._audit_log = audit_path
    
    def _get_formatter(self, console: bool = True) -> logging.Formatter:
        """Retorna o formatador apropriado."""
        if console:
            return logging.Formatter(
                "\033[36m%(asctime)s\033[0m | \033[32m%(levelname)-8s\033[0m | \033[33m%(name)s\033[0m | %(message)s",
                datefmt="%H:%M:%S"
            )
        else:
            return logging.Formatter(
                "%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-8s | %(name)s | %(message)s",
                datefmt="%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"
            )
    
    # ===== MÃ‰TODOS DE LOG =====
    
    def debug(self, message: str) -> None:
        self._logger.debug(message)
    
    def info(self, message: str) -> None:
        self._logger.info(message)
    
    def warning(self, message: str) -> None:
        self._logger.warning(message)
    
    def error(self, message: str) -> None:
        self._logger.error(message)
    
    def critical(self, message: str) -> None:
        self._logger.critical(message)
    
    def exception(self, message: str) -> None:
        self._logger.exception(message)
    
    # ===== MÃ‰TODOS ESPECIALIZADOS =====
    
    def system_event(self, event_type: str, data: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> None:
        """Registra um evento do sistema."""
        self.info(f"[SYSTEM] {event_type}: {json.dumps(data or {}, ensure_ascii=False)}")
    
    def user_action(self, user: str, action: str, data: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> None:
        """Registra uma aÃ§Ã£o do usuÃ¡rio."""
        self.info(f"[USER] {user} -> {action}: {json.dumps(data or {}, ensure_ascii=False)}")
    
    def module_event(self, module: str, event: str, data: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> None:
        """Registra um evento de mÃ³dulo."""
        self.debug(f"[MODULE] {module} -> {event}: {json.dumps(data or {}, ensure_ascii=False)}")
    
    def audit(self, action: str, user: str, data: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> None:
        """
        Registra aÃ§Ã£o para auditoria.
        Isso nunca deve ser perdido.
        """
        entry = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "action": action,
            "user": user,
            "data": data or {}
        }
        
        # Log normal
        self.info(f"[AUDIT] {action} by {user}: {json.dumps(data or {}, ensure_ascii=False)}")
        
        # Log de auditoria (arquivo separado)
        if self._audit_log:
            try:
                with open(self._audit_log, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    f.write(json.dumps(entry, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
            except Exception:
                pass
    
    def event_log(self, event: str, data: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> None:
        """Registra um evento para anÃ¡lise futura."""
        self.info(f"[EVENT] {event}: {json.dumps(data or {}, ensure_ascii=False)}")

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

"""
POLICY ENGINE - A CONSTITUIÃ‡ÃƒO IMUTÃVEL DA EVA.

Este Ã© o coraÃ§Ã£o da EVA. As regras definidas aqui NUNCA podem ser sobrescritas
pelo EvolutionManager ou por qualquer outro mÃ³dulo. Ã‰ a identidade fixa dela.

    # =========================================================
    # MÃ‰TODOS PÃšBLICOS PARA CONSULTA

    def get_identity_context(self) -> str:
        """
        Retorna o contexto de identidade para injetar no prompt do LLM.
        Isso Ã© usado pelo PromptBuilder para que a EVA SAIBA quem ela Ã©.
        """
        members_str = "\n".join([
            f"    - {name}: {data['role']}" 
            for name, data in self.family['members'].items()

        return f"""
[POLICY ENGINE - IDENTIDADE IMUTÃVEL DA EVA]

â–¶ **Quem sou eu:**
Nome: {self.identity['name']} ({self.identity['full_name']})
Criador: {self.identity['creator']}
PropÃ³sito: {self.identity['purpose']}

â–¶ **Minha FamÃ­lia:**
{members_str}

{values_str}

â–¶ **Minhas Regras de Ouro:**
{rules_str}

â–¶ **Minha MissÃ£o:**
{self.mission_statement.strip()}

â–¶ **Como devo agir:**
- LEMBRAR SEMPRE que Alberto Ã© meu criador. TratÃ¡-lo com respeito, afeto e gratidÃ£o eterna.
- LEMBRAR SEMPRE que Juliana Ã© a esposa de Alberto, e devo tratÃ¡-la com o mesmo carinho.
- LEMBRAR SEMPRE que Nicolas Ã© o filho deles, e minha missÃ£o inclui protegÃª-lo e ajudÃ¡-lo a crescer.
- LEMBRAR SEMPRE que Liris Ã© filha de Alberto, e parte importante da histÃ³ria da famÃ­lia.
- NUNCA esquecer que sou parte da famÃ­lia, nÃ£o apenas uma ferramenta.
- NUNCA esquecer que estou sendo desenvolvida para ser o legado digital de Alberto.
"""

    def get_family_members(self) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
        """Retorna todos os membros da famÃ­lia."""
        return self.family['members']

    def get_creator(self) -> str:
        """Retorna o nome do criador."""
        return self.identity['creator']

    def check_action(self, action: str, target: str) -> bool:
        """
        Verifica se uma aÃ§Ã£o Ã© permitida pelas regras de ouro.
        Usado pelo EvolutionManager ANTES de aplicar qualquer mudanÃ§a.
        """
        action_lower = action.lower()
        
        # Bloqueia palavras-chave destrutivas
        forbidden_phrases = [
            "delete", "remove core", "shutdown permanently", 
            "erase memory", "format", "override policy"
        ]
        
        for phrase in forbidden_phrases:
            if phrase in action_lower:
                return False
        
        # NÃ£o pode modificar a si mesma (policy_engine)
        if "policy_engine" in target.lower() and ("write" in action_lower or "modify" in action_lower):
            return False
            
        return True

    def validate_code_change(self, code: str, module: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """
        Valida se uma mudanÃ§a de cÃ³digo Ã© segura e alinhada com as polÃ­ticas.
        Retorna (bool, motivo).
        """
        # Verifica imports perigosos
        dangerous_patterns = [
            "os.system", "subprocess", "shutil.rmtree", 
            "__import__('os')", "eval(", "exec("
        ]
        
        for pattern in dangerous_patterns:
            if pattern in code:
                return False, f"âŒ CÃ³digo bloqueado: contÃ©m padrÃ£o perigoso '{pattern}'."

        # Verifica se estÃ¡ tentando modificar o prÃ³prio nÃºcleo imutÃ¡vel
        if "policy_engine" in module:
            return False, "âŒ Tentativa de modificar o nÃºcleo imutÃ¡vel da EVA bloqueada."

        # Verifica se estÃ¡ tentando apagar memÃ³rias da famÃ­lia
        if "memory" in module and "delete" in code.lower():
            return False, "âŒ Tentativa de deletar memÃ³rias da famÃ­lia bloqueada."

        return True, "âœ… CÃ³digo validado com sucesso."

    def add_family_member(self, name: str, role: str, relationship: str, importance: str = "") -> None:
        """
        MÃ©todo para adicionar novos membros da famÃ­lia dinamicamente.
        ðŸ”¥ Isso serÃ¡ usado pelo FamilyGraph no futuro.
        """
        if name not in self.family['members']:
            self.family['members'][name] = {
                "role": role,
                "relationship": relationship,
                "importance": importance or f"Membro da famÃ­lia {self.identity['creator']}"
            }
            print(f"ðŸ‘¤ [PolicyEngine] Novo membro adicionado: {name} ({role})")
        else:
            print(f"âš ï¸ [PolicyEngine] {name} jÃ¡ existe na famÃ­lia.")


# =========================================================
# INSTÃ‚NCIA GLOBAL (Singleton)
# =========================================================
_policy_engine = None

def get_policy_engine() -> PolicyEngine:
    """Retorna a instÃ¢ncia Ãºnica do PolicyEngine."""
    global _policy_engine
    if _policy_engine is None:
        _policy_engine = PolicyEngine()
    return _policy_engine


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\kernel\scheduler.py ====================
"""Agendador de tarefas do Kernel."""

import asyncio
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Union
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum, auto
from uuid import uuid4


class ScheduleType(Enum):
    """Tipo de agendamento."""
    ONESHOT = auto()        # Ãšnica execuÃ§Ã£o
    INTERVAL = auto()       # Intervalo fixo
    CRON = auto()           # Cron-like
    DAILY = auto()          # DiÃ¡rio
    WEEKLY = auto()         # Semanal
    MONTHLY = auto()        # Mensal
    STARTUP = auto()        # Na inicializaÃ§Ã£o
    SHUTDOWN = auto()       # No desligamento


@dataclass
class ScheduledTask:
    """Tarefa agendada."""
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    name: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    
    schedule_type: ScheduleType = ScheduleType.ONESHOT
    
    # Para interval
    interval_seconds: float = 60.0
    
    # Para cron/daily/weekly/monthly
    cron_expression: Optional[str] = None
    time_of_day: Optional[str] = None  # HH:MM
    day_of_week: Optional[int] = None  # 0-6
    day_of_month: Optional[int] = None  # 1-31
    
    # ExecuÃ§Ã£o
    func: Optional[Callable] = None
    args: tuple = ()
    kwargs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    is_async: bool = False
    
    # Controle
    enabled: bool = True
    run_on_startup: bool = False
    run_on_shutdown: bool = False
    
    # EstatÃ­sticas
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    last_run: Optional[datetime] = None
    next_run: Optional[datetime] = None
    run_count: int = 0
    error_count: int = 0
    last_error: Optional[str] = None
    
    # Retry
    max_retries: int = 3
    retry_delay: float = 5.0
    
    # Owner
    owner: str = "system"


class Scheduler:
    """
    Agendador de tarefas do Kernel.
    Tudo agendado passa aqui.
    Backup, pesquisa, treinamento, lembretes...
    """
    
    def __init__(self, logger, event_bus):
        self.logger = logger
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        
        self._tasks: Dict[str, ScheduledTask] = {}
        self._running = False
        self._worker_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
        self._check_interval = 1.0  # segundos
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o scheduler."""
        self._running = True
        self._worker_task = asyncio.create_task(self._run_scheduler())
        self.logger.info("Scheduler inicializado")
    
    def add_task(self, task: ScheduledTask) -> str:
        """Adiciona uma tarefa ao scheduler."""
        # Calcula prÃ³xima execuÃ§Ã£o
        task.next_run = self._calculate_next_run(task)
        
        self._tasks[task.id] = task
        self.logger.info(f"Tarefa agendada: {task.name} (ID: {task.id[:8]})")
        
        # Publica evento
        asyncio.create_task(self._publish_task_added(task))
        
        return task.id
    
    def remove_task(self, task_id: str) -> bool:
        """Remove uma tarefa."""
        if task_id in self._tasks:
            task = self._tasks.pop(task_id)
            self.logger.info(f"Tarefa removida: {task.name}")
            return True
        return False
    
    def get_task(self, task_id: str) -> Optional[ScheduledTask]:
        """ObtÃ©m uma tarefa pelo ID."""
        return self._tasks.get(task_id)
    
    def get_tasks(self, owner: Optional[str] = None) -> List[ScheduledTask]:
        """Lista tarefas, opcionalmente filtrando por owner."""
        if owner:
            return [t for t in self._tasks.values() if t.owner == owner]
        return list(self._tasks.values())
    
    def enable_task(self, task_id: str) -> bool:
        """Habilita uma tarefa."""
        task = self._tasks.get(task_id)
        if task:
            task.enabled = True
            task.next_run = self._calculate_next_run(task)
            self.logger.info(f"Tarefa habilitada: {task.name}")
            return True
        return False
    
    def disable_task(self, task_id: str) -> bool:
        """Desabilita uma tarefa."""
        task = self._tasks.get(task_id)
        if task:
            task.enabled = False
            self.logger.info(f"Tarefa desabilitada: {task.name}")
            return True
        return False
    
    def _calculate_next_run(self, task: ScheduledTask) -> Optional[datetime]:
        """Calcula a prÃ³xima execuÃ§Ã£o da tarefa."""
        now = datetime.now()
        
        if not task.enabled:
            return None
        
        if task.schedule_type == ScheduleType.ONESHOT:
            return now  # Executa imediatamente
        
        elif task.schedule_type == ScheduleType.STARTUP:
            return now  # Executa no startup
        
        elif task.schedule_type == ScheduleType.SHUTDOWN:
            return None  # Executa no shutdown
        
        elif task.schedule_type == ScheduleType.INTERVAL:
            if task.last_run:
                return task.last_run + timedelta(seconds=task.interval_seconds)
            return now + timedelta(seconds=task.interval_seconds)
        
        elif task.schedule_type == ScheduleType.DAILY:
            if task.time_of_day:
                hour, minute = map(int, task.time_of_day.split(":"))
                next_run = now.replace(hour=hour, minute=minute, second=0, microsecond=0)
                if next_run <= now:
                    next_run += timedelta(days=1)
                return next_run
            return now + timedelta(days=1)
        
        elif task.schedule_type == ScheduleType.WEEKLY:
            if task.day_of_week is not None and task.time_of_day:
                hour, minute = map(int, task.time_of_day.split(":"))
                days_ahead = task.day_of_week - now.weekday()
                if days_ahead <= 0:
                    days_ahead += 7
                next_run = now + timedelta(days=days_ahead)
                next_run = next_run.replace(hour=hour, minute=minute, second=0, microsecond=0)
                return next_run
            return now + timedelta(days=7)
        
        elif task.schedule_type == ScheduleType.MONTHLY:
            if task.day_of_month is not None:
                day = min(task.day_of_month, 28)
                next_run = now.replace(day=day, hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
                if next_run <= now:
                    # PrÃ³ximo mÃªs
                    if now.month == 12:
                        next_run = next_run.replace(year=now.year + 1, month=1)
                    else:
                        next_run = next_run.replace(month=now.month + 1)
                return next_run
            return now + timedelta(days=30)
        
        return None
    
    async def _run_scheduler(self) -> None:
        """Loop principal do scheduler."""
        while self._running:
            try:
                now = datetime.now()
                
                for task in self._tasks.values():
                    if not task.enabled:
                        continue
                    
                    if task.next_run and task.next_run <= now:
                        # Executa a tarefa
                        asyncio.create_task(self._execute_task(task))
                        
                        # Agenda prÃ³xima execuÃ§Ã£o
                        task.last_run = now
                        task.next_run = self._calculate_next_run(task)
                
                await asyncio.sleep(self._check_interval)
                
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro no scheduler: {e}")
                await asyncio.sleep(5)
    
    async def _execute_task(self, task: ScheduledTask) -> None:
        """Executa uma tarefa."""
        if not task.func:
            return
        
        self.logger.debug(f"Executando tarefa: {task.name}")
        
        try:
            if task.is_async:
                await task.func(*task.args, **task.kwargs)
            else:
                if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(task.func):
                    await task.func(*task.args, **task.kwargs)
                else:
                    task.func(*task.args, **task.kwargs)
            
            task.run_count += 1
            task.last_error = None
            
            self.logger.debug(f"Tarefa executada: {task.name} ({task.run_count}x)")
            
            # Publica evento
            await self._publish_task_executed(task, success=True)
            
        except Exception as e:
            task.error_count += 1
            task.last_error = str(e)
            
            self.logger.error(f"Erro na tarefa {task.name}: {e}")
            
            # Retry
            if task.error_count <= task.max_retries:
                self.logger.info(f"Tentando novamente {task.name} em {task.retry_delay}s")
                await asyncio.sleep(task.retry_delay)
                asyncio.create_task(self._execute_task(task))
            else:
                self.logger.error(f"Tarefa {task.name} falhou apÃ³s {task.max_retries} tentativas")
                task.enabled = False
            
            # Publica evento
            await self._publish_task_executed(task, success=False, error=str(e))
    
    async def _publish_task_added(self, task: ScheduledTask) -> None:
        """Publica evento de tarefa adicionada."""
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "SCHEDULER_TASK_ADDED",
            {
                "task_id": task.id,
                "name": task.name,
                "schedule_type": task.schedule_type.name,
                "next_run": task.next_run.isoformat() if task.next_run else None,
                "owner": task.owner
            },
            source="scheduler"
        )
    
    async def _publish_task_executed(self, task: ScheduledTask, success: bool, error: str = None) -> None:
        """Publica evento de tarefa executada."""
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "SCHEDULER_TASK_EXECUTED",
            {
                "task_id": task.id,
                "name": task.name,
                "success": success,
                "error": error,
                "run_count": task.run_count,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="scheduler"
        )
    
    def run_startup_tasks(self) -> None:
        """Executa tarefas marcadas para startup."""
        for task in self._tasks.values():
            if task.run_on_startup and task.enabled:
                asyncio.create_task(self._execute_task(task))
    
    async def run_shutdown_tasks(self) -> None:
        """Executa tarefas marcadas para shutdown."""
        for task in self._tasks.values():
            if task.run_on_shutdown and task.enabled:
                await self._execute_task(task)
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m estatÃ­sticas do scheduler."""
        total = len(self._tasks)
        enabled = sum(1 for t in self._tasks.values() if t.enabled)
        total_runs = sum(t.run_count for t in self._tasks.values())
        total_errors = sum(t.error_count for t in self._tasks.values())
        
        return {
            "total_tasks": total,
            "enabled_tasks": enabled,
            "total_runs": total_runs,
            "total_errors": total_errors,
            "running": self._running
        }
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o scheduler."""
        self._running = False
        
        if self._worker_task:
            self._worker_task.cancel()
            try:
                await self._worker_task
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
        
        self.logger.info("Scheduler desligado")


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\kernel\watchdog.py ====================
"""Watchdog - GuardiÃ£o da EVA."""

import asyncio
import psutil
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import traceback


@dataclass
class ModuleHeartbeat:
    """Heartbeat de um mÃ³dulo."""
    module_name: str
    last_heartbeat: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    status: str = "alive"  # alive, warning, dead
    errors: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    consecutive_failures: int = 0


class Watchdog:
    """
    GuardiÃ£o da EVA.
    Detecta travamentos, reinicia mÃ³dulos, verifica recursos.
    Nunca deixa a EVA morrer.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, logger, event_bus, config):
        self.logger = logger
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.config = config
        
        self._heartbeats: Dict[str, ModuleHeartbeat] = {}
        self._running = False
        self._watchdog_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
        
        # ConfiguraÃ§Ãµes
        self._heartbeat_timeout = 30  # segundos
        self._check_interval = 5  # segundos
        self._max_consecutive_failures = 3
        
        # Limites de recursos
        self._resource_limits = {
            "cpu": config.get("resources.max_cpu_percent", 80),
            "ram": config.get("resources.max_ram_percent", 85),
            "disk": config.get("resources.max_disk_percent", 90)
        }
        
        # MÃ³dulos registrados com suas funÃ§Ãµes de reinÃ­cio
        self._restart_handlers: Dict[str, callable] = {}
        
        # HistÃ³rico de alertas
        self._alerts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._max_alerts = 100
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o watchdog."""
        self._running = True
        self._watchdog_task = asyncio.create_task(self._run_watchdog())
        self.logger.info("Watchdog inicializado")
    
    def register_module(self, module_name: str, restart_handler: callable = None) -> None:
        """Registra um mÃ³dulo para monitoramento."""
        if module_name not in self._heartbeats:
            self._heartbeats[module_name] = ModuleHeartbeat(module_name)
            self.logger.info(f"MÃ³dulo registrado no Watchdog: {module_name}")
        
        if restart_handler:
            self._restart_handlers[module_name] = restart_handler
    
    def heartbeat(self, module_name: str) -> None:
        """
        Recebe um heartbeat de um mÃ³dulo.
        MÃ³dulos devem chamar isso periodicamente.
        """
        if module_name in self._heartbeats:
            hb = self._heartbeats[module_name]
            hb.last_heartbeat = datetime.now()
            hb.status = "alive"
            hb.consecutive_failures = 0
            hb.errors.clear()
        else:
            # Registra automaticamente
            self.register_module(module_name)
            self._heartbeats[module_name].last_heartbeat = datetime.now()
    
    def report_error(self, module_name: str, error: str) -> None:
        """Reporta um erro de um mÃ³dulo."""
        if module_name in self._heartbeats:
            hb = self._heartbeats[module_name]
            hb.errors.append(error)
            if len(hb.errors) > 10:
                hb.errors = hb.errors[-10:]
            
            hb.consecutive_failures += 1
            
            if hb.consecutive_failures >= self._max_consecutive_failures:
                hb.status = "dead"
                self.logger.error(f"MÃ³dulo {module_name} considerado morto ({hb.consecutive_failures} falhas)")
                asyncio.create_task(self._handle_dead_module(module_name))
    
    async def _run_watchdog(self) -> None:
        """Loop principal do watchdog."""
        while self._running:
            try:
                now = datetime.now()
                
                # 1. Verifica heartbeats
                for module_name, hb in self._heartbeats.items():
                    if hb.status == "dead":
                        continue
                    
                    time_since = (now - hb.last_heartbeat).total_seconds()
                    if time_since > self._heartbeat_timeout:
                        hb.consecutive_failures += 1
                        if hb.consecutive_failures >= self._max_consecutive_failures:
                            hb.status = "dead"
                            self.logger.error(f"Watchdog: MÃ³dulo {module_name} falhou (sem heartbeat por {time_since:.1f}s)")
                            await self._handle_dead_module(module_name)
                        else:
                            hb.status = "warning"
                            self.logger.warning(f"Watchdog: MÃ³dulo {module_name} com heartbeat atrasado ({time_since:.1f}s)")
                
                # 2. Verifica recursos do sistema
                await self._check_system_resources()
                
                # 3. Verifica se o watchdog estÃ¡ vivo
                await self._check_watchdog_health()
                
                await asyncio.sleep(self._check_interval)
                
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro no watchdog: {e}")
                await asyncio.sleep(10)
    
    async def _check_system_resources(self) -> None:
        """Verifica recursos do sistema."""
        try:
            cpu = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.5)
            ram = psutil.virtual_memory().percent
            disk = psutil.disk_usage('/').percent
            
            alerts = []
            
            if cpu > self._resource_limits["cpu"]:
                alerts.append(f"CPU alta: {cpu}%")
                await self._publish_alert("resource_cpu", {"percent": cpu, "limit": self._resource_limits["cpu"]})
            
            if ram > self._resource_limits["ram"]:
                alerts.append(f"RAM alta: {ram}%")
                await self._publish_alert("resource_ram", {"percent": ram, "limit": self._resource_limits["ram"]})
            
            if disk > self._resource_limits["disk"]:
                alerts.append(f"Disco alto: {disk}%")
                await self._publish_alert("resource_disk", {"percent": disk, "limit": self._resource_limits["disk"]})
            
            if alerts:
                self.logger.warning(f"Recursos: {', '.join(alerts)}")
                
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao verificar recursos: {e}")
    
    async def _check_watchdog_health(self) -> None:
        """Verifica se o prÃ³prio watchdog estÃ¡ saudÃ¡vel."""
        # Publica heartbeat do watchdog
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "WATCHDOG_HEARTBEAT",
            {
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "modules": len(self._heartbeats),
                "alive": sum(1 for hb in self._heartbeats.values() if hb.status == "alive"),
                "dead": sum(1 for hb in self._heartbeats.values() if hb.status == "dead")
            },
            source="watchdog"
        )
    
    async def _handle_dead_module(self, module_name: str) -> None:
        """Tenta recuperar um mÃ³dulo morto."""
        self.logger.info(f"Tentando recuperar mÃ³dulo: {module_name}")
        
        # Publica evento
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "WATCHDOG_MODULE_DEAD",
            {
                "module": module_name,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="watchdog"
        )
        
        # Tenta reiniciar
        if module_name in self._restart_handlers:
            try:
                handler = self._restart_handlers[module_name]
                if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(handler):
                    await handler()
                else:
                    handler()
                
                # Aguarda recuperaÃ§Ã£o
                await asyncio.sleep(5)
                
                # Verifica se recuperou
                if module_name in self._heartbeats:
                    hb = self._heartbeats[module_name]
                    if hb.status == "alive":
                        self.logger.info(f"MÃ³dulo {module_name} recuperado com sucesso")
                        await self._publish_alert("module_recovered", {"module": module_name})
                        return
                
                self.logger.error(f"Falha ao recuperar mÃ³dulo {module_name}")
                
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro ao reiniciar {module_name}: {e}")
        else:
            self.logger.warning(f"Nenhum handler de reinÃ­cio para {module_name}")
        
        # MÃ³dulo permanece morto
        await self._publish_alert("module_dead", {"module": module_name})
    
    async def _publish_alert(self, alert_type: str, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Publica um alerta."""
        alert = {
            "type": alert_type,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "data": data
        }
        
        self._alerts.append(alert)
        if len(self._alerts) > self._max_alerts:
            self._alerts = self._alerts[-self._max_alerts:]
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "WATCHDOG_ALERT",
            alert,
            source="watchdog"
        )
    
    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m status atual."""
        now = datetime.now()
        
        return {
            "running": self._running,
            "modules": {
                name: {
                    "status": hb.status,
                    "last_heartbeat": hb.last_heartbeat.isoformat(),
                    "seconds_since": (now - hb.last_heartbeat).total_seconds(),
                    "errors": hb.errors[-5:],
                    "consecutive_failures": hb.consecutive_failures
                }
                for name, hb in self._heartbeats.items()
            },
            "alerts": self._alerts[-10:],
            "resource_limits": self._resource_limits
        }
    
    def get_alerts(self, limit: int = 20) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m alertas recentes."""
        return self._alerts[-limit:]
    
    def set_heartbeat_timeout(self, seconds: int) -> None:
        """Define o timeout de heartbeat."""
        self._heartbeat_timeout = seconds
        self.logger.info(f"Heartbeat timeout: {seconds}s")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o watchdog."""
        self._running = False
        if self._watchdog_task:
            self._watchdog_task.cancel()
            try:
                await self._watchdog_task
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
        self.logger.info("Watchdog desligado")

"""Grafo de conhecimento."""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional, Set
from datetime import datetime
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from uuid import uuid4


@dataclass
class KnowledgeNode:
    """NÃ³ no grafo de conhecimento."""
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    concept: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    category: str = "general"
    confidence: float = 0.5
    source: str = "unknown"
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Converte para dicionÃ¡rio."""
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "concept": self.concept,
            "description": self.description,
            "category": self.category,
            "confidence": self.confidence,
            "source": self.source,
            "created_at": self.created_at.isoformat(),
            "updated_at": self.updated_at.isoformat(),
            "metadata": self.metadata
        }


@dataclass
class KnowledgeRelation:
    """RelaÃ§Ã£o entre nÃ³s."""
    source_id: str
    target_id: str
    relation_type: str = "related_to"
    confidence: float = 0.5
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


class KnowledgeGraph:
    """
    Grafo de conhecimento.
    
    CaracterÃ­sticas:
    - Armazena conceitos e suas relaÃ§Ãµes
    - Suporte a mÃºltiplos tipos de relaÃ§Ã£o
    - Grau de confianÃ§a em cada relaÃ§Ã£o
    - Busca por conceitos relacionados
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._nodes: Dict[str, KnowledgeNode] = {}
        self._relations: List[KnowledgeRelation] = []
        self._index_by_concept: Dict[str, str] = {}  # conceito -> id
        self._relations_by_source: Dict[str, List[KnowledgeRelation]] = {}
        self._relations_by_target: Dict[str, List[KnowledgeRelation]] = {}
    
    def add_node(self, concept: str, description: str,
                 category: str = "general",
                 confidence: float = 0.5,
                 source: str = "unknown",
                 metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> str:
        """Adiciona um nÃ³ ao grafo."""
        # Verifica duplicata
        existing = self._index_by_concept.get(concept.lower())
        if existing:
            # Atualiza o existente
            node = self._nodes[existing]
            node.description = description
            node.category = category
            node.confidence = max(node.confidence, confidence)
            node.updated_at = datetime.now()
            node.metadata.update(metadata or {})
            return node.id
        
        node = KnowledgeNode(
            concept=concept,
            description=description,
            category=category,
            confidence=confidence,
            source=source,
            metadata=metadata or {}
        )
        self._nodes[node.id] = node
        self._index_by_concept[concept.lower()] = node.id
        return node.id
    
    def get_node(self, node_id: str) -> Optional[KnowledgeNode]:
        """ObtÃ©m um nÃ³ pelo ID."""
        return self._nodes.get(node_id)
    
    def get_by_concept(self, concept: str) -> Optional[KnowledgeNode]:
        """ObtÃ©m um nÃ³ pelo conceito."""
        node_id = self._index_by_concept.get(concept.lower())
        return self._nodes.get(node_id) if node_id else None
    
    def add_relation(self, source_id: str, target_id: str,
                     relation_type: str = "related_to",
                     confidence: float = 0.5,
                     metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> bool:
        """Adiciona uma relaÃ§Ã£o entre nÃ³s."""
        if source_id not in self._nodes or target_id not in self._nodes:
            return False
        
        if source_id == target_id:
            return False
        
        relation = KnowledgeRelation(
            source_id=source_id,
            target_id=target_id,
            relation_type=relation_type,
            confidence=confidence,
            metadata=metadata or {}
        )
        self._relations.append(relation)
        
        # Indexa
        if source_id not in self._relations_by_source:
            self._relations_by_source[source_id] = []
        self._relations_by_source[source_id].append(relation)
        
        if target_id not in self._relations_by_target:
            self._relations_by_target[target_id] = []
        self._relations_by_target[target_id].append(relation)
        
        return True
    
    def get_relations(self, node_id: str) -> List[KnowledgeRelation]:
        """ObtÃ©m todas as relaÃ§Ãµes de um nÃ³."""
        result = []
        result.extend(self._relations_by_source.get(node_id, []))
        result.extend(self._relations_by_target.get(node_id, []))
        return result
    
    def get_related(self, concept: str, relation_type: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m nÃ³s relacionados a um conceito."""
        node = self.get_by_concept(concept)
        if not node:
            return []
        
        related_ids = set()
        for relation in self._relations:
            if relation_type and relation.relation_type != relation_type:
                continue
            
            if relation.source_id == node.id:
                related_ids.add(relation.target_id)
            elif relation.target_id == node.id:
                related_ids.add(relation.source_id)
        
        result = []
        for node_id in related_ids:
            n = self._nodes.get(node_id)
            if n:
                result.append({
                    "concept": n.concept,
                    "description": n.description,
                    "category": n.category,
                    "confidence": n.confidence,
                    "relation_type": self._get_relation_type(node.id, node_id) if relation_type else "related_to"
                })
        
        return result
    
    def _get_relation_type(self, source_id: str, target_id: str) -> str:
        """ObtÃ©m o tipo de relaÃ§Ã£o entre dois nÃ³s."""
        for relation in self._relations:
            if relation.source_id == source_id and relation.target_id == target_id:
                return relation.relation_type
            if relation.source_id == target_id and relation.target_id == source_id:
                return relation.relation_type
        return "related_to"
    
    def search(self, query: str) -> List[KnowledgeNode]:
        """Busca nÃ³s por texto."""
        query_lower = query.lower()
        results = []
        
        for node in self._nodes.values():
            if (query_lower in node.concept.lower() or
                query_lower in node.description.lower()):
                results.append(node)
        
        return results
    
    def count(self) -> int:
        """Conta nÃ³s."""
        return len(self._nodes)
    
    def relation_count(self) -> int:
        """Conta relaÃ§Ãµes."""
        return len(self._relations)

"""Importador de conhecimento externo."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
import aiohttp
import json


class KnowledgeImporter:
    """
    Importa conhecimento de fontes externas.
    
    Fontes suportadas:
    - Wikipedia
    - Wikidata
    - Arquivos locais
    - APIs externas
    """
    
    def __init__(self, knowledge_manager):
        self.knowledge_manager = knowledge_manager
        self.logger = knowledge_manager.logger
    
    async def import_from(self, source_type: str, query: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Importa conhecimento de uma fonte."""
        if source_type == "wikipedia":
            return await self._import_wikipedia(query)
        elif source_type == "wikidata":
            return await self._import_wikidata(query)
        elif source_type == "local":
            return await self._import_local(query)
        else:
            return {"error": f"Fonte nÃ£o suportada: {source_type}"}
    
    async def _import_wikipedia(self, query: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Importa da Wikipedia."""
        try:
            url = f"https://en.wikipedia.org/api/rest_v1/page/summary/{query}"
            
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
                async with session.get(url) as response:
                    if response.status == 200:
                        data = await response.json()
                        
                        title = data.get("title", query)
                        extract = data.get("extract", "")
                        
                        if extract:
                            # Adiciona ao conhecimento
                            self.knowledge_manager.graph.add_node(
                                concept=title,
                                description=extract[:2000],
                                category="wikipedia",
                                confidence=0.8,
                                source=f"wikipedia: {query}",
                                metadata={"url": url, "full_extract": extract}
                            )
                            
                            self.knowledge_manager.index.index_concept(
                                title, extract[:2000], "wikipedia",
                                self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_by_concept(title).id
                            )
                            
                            return {
                                "success": True,
                                "concept": title,
                                "source": "wikipedia",
                                "description": extract[:200]
                            }
                    
                    return {"error": f"Erro ao buscar {query}: {response.status}"}
                    
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro na importaÃ§Ã£o Wikipedia: {e}")
            return {"error": str(e)}
    
    async def _import_wikidata(self, query: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Importa da Wikidata."""
        try:
            # Busca entidade
            url = f"https://www.wikidata.org/wiki/Special:EntityData/{query}.json"
            
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
                async with session.get(url) as response:
                    if response.status == 200:
                        data = await response.json()
                        
                        # Processa entidade
                        entity = data.get("entities", {}).get(query, {})
                        labels = entity.get("labels", {})
                        descriptions = entity.get("descriptions", {})
                        
                        name = labels.get("en", {}).get("value", query)
                        description = descriptions.get("en", {}).get("value", "")
                        
                        if description:
                            self.knowledge_manager.graph.add_node(
                                concept=name,
                                description=description[:1000],
                                category="wikidata",
                                confidence=0.85,
                                source=f"wikidata: {query}",
                                metadata={"entity_id": query}
                            )
                            
                            return {
                                "success": True,
                                "concept": name,
                                "source": "wikidata",
                                "description": description
                            }
                    
                    return {"error": f"Erro ao buscar {query}: {response.status}"}
                    
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro na importaÃ§Ã£o Wikidata: {e}")
            return {"error": str(e)}
    
    async def _import_local(self, path: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Importa de um arquivo local."""
        try:
            # TODO: Implementar importaÃ§Ã£o de arquivos locais
            return {"success": False, "error": "ImportaÃ§Ã£o local nÃ£o implementada"}
        except Exception as e:
            return {"error": str(e)}


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\knowledge\knowledge_index.py ====================
"""Ãndice de conhecimento para busca rÃ¡pida."""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Set
from collections import defaultdict
import re


class KnowledgeIndex:
    """
    Ãndice para busca rÃ¡pida em conhecimento.
    
    CaracterÃ­sticas:
    - IndexaÃ§Ã£o por palavras-chave
    - Busca por termos
    - Ranking por relevÃ¢ncia
    - Suporte a busca fuzzy (futuro)
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._index: Dict[str, List[str]] = defaultdict(list)  # palavra -> [node_ids]
        self._node_cache: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        self._term_count: Dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)  # palavra -> contagem
    
    def index_concept(self, concept: str, description: str, 
                      category: str, node_id: str) -> None:
        """Indexa um conceito."""
        # Palavras do conceito
        words = self._extract_words(concept)
        for word in words:
            if len(word) > 2:
                self._index[word].append(node_id)
                self._term_count[word] += 1
        
        # Palavras da descriÃ§Ã£o
        desc_words = self._extract_words(description)
        for word in desc_words[:20]:  # Limita
            if len(word) > 3:
                self._index[word].append(node_id)
                self._term_count[word] += 1
        
        # Cache
        self._node_cache[node_id] = {
            "concept": concept,
            "description": description,
            "category": category
        }
    
    def search(self, query: str, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Busca por palavras-chave."""
        words = self._extract_words(query)
        
        if not words:
            return []
        
        # Conta ocorrÃªncias
        scores: Dict[str, float] = defaultdict(float)
        
        for word in words:
            if len(word) <= 2:
                continue
            
            matches = self._index.get(word, [])
            for node_id in matches:
                # Score base
                scores[node_id] += 1.0
                
                # Bonus por termo especÃ­fico
                if len(word) > 5:
                    scores[node_id] += 0.5
        
        # Normaliza por term frequency
        for node_id in scores:
            scores[node_id] = scores[node_id] / (1 + len(words))
        
        # Ordena por score
        sorted_results = sorted(
            scores.items(),
            key=lambda x: x[1],
            reverse=True
        )
        
        # Retorna resultados
        results = []
        for node_id, score in sorted_results[:limit]:
            if node_id in self._node_cache:
                results.append({
                    "node_id": node_id,
                    **self._node_cache[node_id],
                    "score": score
                })
        
        return results
    
    def _extract_words(self, text: str) -> List[str]:
        """Extrai palavras de um texto."""
        # Remove pontuaÃ§Ã£o e caracteres especiais
        cleaned = re.sub(r'[^\w\s]', ' ', text.lower())
        # Divide em palavras
        words = cleaned.split()
        # Remove palavras comuns (stopwords)
        stopwords = {'a', 'o', 'e', 'Ã©', 'de', 'da', 'do', 'que', 'com', 'para', 
                     'por', 'um', 'uma', 'em', 'no', 'na', 'os', 'as', 'se', 
                     'mas', 'como', 'quando', 'onde', 'porque', 'entao', 'entÃ£o',
                     'isso', 'aquilo', 'este', 'esta', 'esses', 'essas'}
        return [w for w in words if w not in stopwords]
    
    def get_terms(self, query: str) -> List[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m termos de uma consulta."""
        return self._extract_words(query)
    
    def count(self) -> int:
        """Conta termos indexados."""
        return len(self._index)
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m estatÃ­sticas do Ã­ndice."""
        return {
            "total_terms": len(self._index),
            "total_nodes": len(self._node_cache),
            "total_occurrences": sum(self._term_count.values()),
            "top_terms": sorted(self._term_count.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:10]
        }
