"""Gerenciador de Conhecimento - FASE 3 do pipeline cognitivo."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re

from src.core.event_types import EventType
from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus


class KnowledgeManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de Conhecimento - FASE 3 do pipeline cognitivo.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Armazenar conhecimento estruturado
    - Consultar conhecimento relevante
    - Adicionar novo conhecimento
    - Manter base de conhecimento
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        self._running = False
        
        # Base de conhecimento em memÃ³ria
        self._knowledge_base: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "facts": {},
            "concepts": {},
            "categories": {}
        }
        
        # Caminho para persistÃªncia
        data_path = Path(config.get("paths.data", "./data"))
        self._knowledge_path = data_path / "knowledge" / "knowledge_base.json"
        
        # Carrega conhecimento base
        self._load_base_knowledge()
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“š [KnowledgeManager] Inicializado")
    
    def _load_base_knowledge(self) -> None:
        """Carrega conhecimento base (hardcoded para testes)."""
        self._knowledge_base["facts"] = {
            "brasilia": {
                "capital": "BrasÃ­lia",
                "pais": "Brasil",
                "inauguracao": 1960,
                "populacao": "3.4 milhÃµes",
                "estado": "Distrito Federal",
                "regiao": "Centro-Oeste"
            },
            "brasil": {
                "capital": "BrasÃ­lia",
                "idioma": "PortuguÃªs",
                "moeda": "Real (BRL)",
                "populacao": "203 milhÃµes",
                "area": "8.5 milhÃµes kmÂ²"
            },
            "python": {
                "criador": "Guido van Rossum",
                "ano": 1991,
                "tipo": "Linguagem de programaÃ§Ã£o",
                "paradigma": "Multi-paradigma",
                "site": "python.org"
            },
            "eva": {
                "nome": "EVA OS",
                "significado": "Evolutionary Virtual Assistant",
                "versao": "1.0.0",
                "criador": "Alberto Fernandes"
            }
        }
        
        self._knowledge_base["concepts"] = {
            "capital": "Cidade onde estÃ¡ localizada a sede do governo de um paÃ­s ou estado.",
            "populacao": "NÃºmero total de habitantes de uma determinada regiÃ£o.",
            "linguagem_programacao": "Conjunto de regras e sintaxe para escrever programas de computador.",
            "ia": "InteligÃªncia Artificial - Ã¡rea da computaÃ§Ã£o que cria sistemas capazes de aprender e raciocinar."
        }
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o KnowledgeManager."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“š [KnowledgeManager] Inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        
        # Carrega conhecimento persistido
        await self._load_knowledge()
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“š [KnowledgeManager] Inicializado com {len(self._knowledge_base['facts'])} fatos")
    
    async def process(self, context: Dict[str, Any], memory: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Processa o contexto e memÃ³ria para consultar conhecimento.
        
        Args:
            context: Contexto da mensagem
            memory: Dados de memÃ³ria recuperados
            
        Returns:
            Dados de conhecimento consultados
        """
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“š [KnowledgeManager] Processando...")
        
        message = context.get("message", "")
        intent = context.get("intent", "unknown")
        entities = context.get("entities", {})
        
        # 1. Extrai termos-chave da mensagem
        keywords = self._extract_keywords(message)
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“š [KnowledgeManager] Palavras-chave: {keywords}")
        
        # 2. Busca fatos relevantes
        facts = self._search_facts(keywords)
        
        # 3. Busca conceitos relevantes
        concepts = self._search_concepts(keywords)
        
        # 4. Busca por entidades especÃ­ficas
        entity_knowledge = self._search_entities(entities)
        
        knowledge_data = {
            "keywords": keywords,
            "facts": facts,
            "concepts": concepts,
            "entity_knowledge": entity_knowledge,
            "total_results": len(facts) + len(concepts) + len(entity_knowledge),
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“š [KnowledgeManager] {knowledge_data['total_results']} resultados encontrados")
        
        # Publica evento
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            EventType.KNOWLEDGE_QUERIED,
            {
                "context": context,
                "memory": memory or {},
                "knowledge": knowledge_data,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="knowledge_manager"
        )
        
        return knowledge_data
    
    def _extract_keywords(self, message: str) -> List[str]:
        """Extrai palavras-chave da mensagem."""
        # Remove pontuaÃ§Ã£o
        clean = re.sub(r'[^\w\s]', ' ', message.lower())
        words = clean.split()
        
        # Filtra palavras curtas e comuns
        stopwords = {'a', 'o', 'e', 'Ã©', 'de', 'da', 'do', 'que', 'com', 'para',
                     'por', 'um', 'uma', 'em', 'no', 'na', 'os', 'as', 'se',
                     'mas', 'como', 'quando', 'onde', 'porque', 'entao', 'entÃ£o',
                     'isso', 'aquilo', 'este', 'esta', 'esses', 'essas', 'qual',
                     'sua', 'seu', 'meu', 'minha', 'nosso', 'nossa'}
        
        keywords = [w for w in words if len(w) > 3 and w not in stopwords]
        
        # Adiciona variaÃ§Ãµes
        result = []
        for word in keywords:
            result.append(word)
            # Plural
            if word.endswith('s') and len(word) > 4:
                result.append(word[:-1])
            # AcentuaÃ§Ã£o simplificada
            if 'Ã§' in word:
                result.append(word.replace('Ã§', 'c'))
        
        return list(set(result))
    
    def _search_facts(self, keywords: List[str]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Busca fatos relevantes."""
        results = []
        facts = self._knowledge_base.get("facts", {})
        
        for key, fact in facts.items():
            # Verifica se a palavra-chave estÃ¡ no nome do fato
            if any(keyword in key.lower() for keyword in keywords):
                results.append({
                    "type": "fact",
                    "key": key,
                    "data": fact,
                    "relevance": 1.0
                })
                continue
            
            # Verifica se a palavra-chave estÃ¡ nos valores do fato
            for value in fact.values():
                if isinstance(value, str):
                    if any(keyword in value.lower() for keyword in keywords):
                        results.append({
                            "type": "fact",
                            "key": key,
                            "data": fact,
                            "relevance": 0.8
                        })
                        break
        
        return results[:5]  # Limita a 5 resultados
    
    def _search_concepts(self, keywords: List[str]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Busca conceitos relevantes."""
        results = []
        concepts = self._knowledge_base.get("concepts", {})
        
        for key, definition in concepts.items():
            if any(keyword in key.lower() for keyword in keywords):
                results.append({
                    "type": "concept",
                    "key": key,
                    "definition": definition,
                    "relevance": 1.0
                })
        
        return results[:3]
    
    def _search_entities(self, entities: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Busca conhecimento sobre entidades especÃ­ficas."""
        results = []
        facts = self._knowledge_base.get("facts", {})
        
        for entity_type, entity_value in entities.items():
            if entity_type == "name":
                for key, fact in facts.items():
                    if entity_value.lower() in key.lower():
                        results.append({
                            "type": "entity",
                            "entity_type": entity_type,
                            "entity_value": entity_value,
                            "data": fact,
                            "relevance": 1.0
                        })
        
        return results[:3]
    
    async def add_knowledge(self, key: str, data: Dict[str, Any], category: str = "facts") -> bool:
        """Adiciona novo conhecimento Ã  base."""
        try:
            if category not in self._knowledge_base:
                self._knowledge_base[category] = {}
            
            self._knowledge_base[category][key] = data
            await self._save_knowledge()
            self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“š [KnowledgeManager] Conhecimento adicionado: {key}")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao adicionar conhecimento: {e}")
            return False
    
    async def _load_knowledge(self) -> None:
        """Carrega conhecimento do disco."""
        try:
            if self._knowledge_path.exists():
                with open(self._knowledge_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    loaded = json.load(f)
                    self._knowledge_base.update(loaded)
                self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“š [KnowledgeManager] Carregado conhecimento persistido")
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(f"Erro ao carregar conhecimento: {e}")
    
    async def _save_knowledge(self) -> None:
        """Salva conhecimento no disco."""
        try:
            self._knowledge_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with open(self._knowledge_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(self._knowledge_base, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao salvar conhecimento: {e}")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o KnowledgeManager."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“š [KnowledgeManager] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        await self._save_knowledge()
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“š [KnowledgeManager] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "facts": len(self._knowledge_base.get("facts", {})),
            "concepts": len(self._knowledge_base.get("concepts", {}))
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\knowledge\knowledge_query.py ====================
"""Linguagem de consulta para conhecimento."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
import re


class KnowledgeQuery:
    """
    Linguagem de consulta para conhecimento.
    
    Suporta:
    - Busca por conceito: "O que Ã© X?"
    - Busca por relaÃ§Ã£o: "O que X tem a ver com Y?"
    - Busca por categoria: "Conceitos sobre tecnologia"
    - Consulta hierÃ¡rquica: "O que Ã© parte de X?"
    """
    
    def __init__(self, knowledge_manager):
        self.knowledge_manager = knowledge_manager
        self.logger = knowledge_manager.logger
    
    async def execute(self, query: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Executa uma consulta."""
        query_lower = query.lower().strip()
        
        # Detecta tipo de consulta
        if self._is_definition_query(query_lower):
            return await self._handle_definition(query_lower)
        elif self._is_relationship_query(query_lower):
            return await self._handle_relationship(query_lower)
        elif self._is_category_query(query_lower):
            return await self._handle_category(query_lower)
        elif self._is_hierarchy_query(query_lower):
            return await self._handle_hierarchy(query_lower)
        else:
            return await self._handle_general(query_lower)
    
    def _is_definition_query(self, query: str) -> bool:
        """Verifica se Ã© uma consulta de definiÃ§Ã£o."""
        patterns = [
            r"^o que (Ã©|e) ",
            r"^quem (Ã©|e) ",
            r"^defina ",
            r"^explique (o que|quem) ",
            r"^o que significa "
        ]
        return any(re.search(p, query) for p in patterns)
    
    def _is_relationship_query(self, query: str) -> bool:
        """Verifica se Ã© uma consulta de relaÃ§Ã£o."""
        patterns = [
            r" relaÃ§Ã£o entre ",
            r" tem a ver com ",
            r" diferente de ",
            r" similar a "
        ]
        return any(p in query for p in patterns)
    
    def _is_category_query(self, query: str) -> bool:
        """Verifica se Ã© uma consulta por categoria."""
        patterns = [
            r"conceitos sobre ",
            r"categor[aÃ¡] ",
            r"tipos de "
        ]
        return any(p in query for p in patterns)
    
    def _is_hierarchy_query(self, query: str) -> bool:
        """Verifica se Ã© uma consulta hierÃ¡rquica."""
        patterns = [
            r"parte de ",
            r"subcategoria",
            r"tipo de ",
            r"hierarquia"
        ]
        return any(p in query for p in patterns)
    
    async def _handle_definition(self, query: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Processa consulta de definiÃ§Ã£o."""
        # Extrai o conceito
        words = query.split()
        concept = None
        
        for i, word in enumerate(words):
            if word in ["Ã©", "e", "o", "a", "que"]:
                if i + 1 < len(words):
                    concept = words[i + 1]
                    break
        
        if not concept:
            # Tenta pegar a Ãºltima palavra
            concept = words[-1] if words else None
        
        if concept:
            node = self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_by_concept(concept)
            if node:
                return {
                    "type": "definition",
                    "query": query,
                    "concept": concept,
                    "found": True,
                    "definition": node.description,
                    "category": node.category,
                    "confidence": node.confidence,
                    "source": node.source
                }
        
        return {
            "type": "definition",
            "query": query,
            "found": False,
            "message": f"NÃ£o encontrei definiÃ§Ã£o para: {concept or query}"
        }
    
    async def _handle_relationship(self, query: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Processa consulta de relaÃ§Ã£o."""
        # Busca conceitos na consulta
        concepts = []
        words = query.split()
        
        for word in words:
            if len(word) > 3:
                node = self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_by_concept(word)
                if node:
                    concepts.append(word)
                elif len(concepts) < 2:
                    # Verifica se Ã© uma palavra composta
                    pass
        
        if len(concepts) >= 2:
            source = concepts[0]
            target = concepts[1]
            
            relations = self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_related(source)
            target_info = None
            
            for rel in relations:
                if rel["concept"] == target:
                    target_info = rel
                    break
            
            if target_info:
                return {
                    "type": "relationship",
                    "query": query,
                    "source": source,
                    "target": target,
                    "found": True,
                    "relation_type": target_info.get("relation_type", "related_to"),
                    "confidence": target_info.get("confidence", 0.5)
                }
        
        return {
            "type": "relationship",
            "query": query,
            "found": False,
            "message": "NÃ£o encontrei relaÃ§Ã£o entre os conceitos"
        }
    
    async def _handle_category(self, query: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Processa consulta por categoria."""
        # Extrai categoria
        words = query.split()
        category = None
        
        for i, word in enumerate(words):
            if word in ["sobre", "de", "em"]:
                if i + 1 < len(words):
                    category = words[i + 1]
                    break
        
        if not category:
            category = words[-1] if words else None
        
        if category:
            # Busca conceitos por categoria
            nodes = self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_by_category(category)
            
            if nodes:
                return {
                    "type": "category",
                    "query": query,
                    "category": category,
                    "found": True,
                    "concepts": [n.concept for n in nodes[:10]],
                    "count": len(nodes)
                }
        
        return {
            "type": "category",
            "query": query,
            "found": False,
            "message": f"NÃ£o encontrei conceitos na categoria: {category or query}"
        }
    
    async def _handle_hierarchy(self, query: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Processa consulta hierÃ¡rquica."""
        # Extrai conceito
        words = query.split()
        concept = None
        
        for word in words:
            if len(word) > 3:
                node = self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_by_concept(word)
                if node:
                    concept = word
                    break
        
        if concept:
            hierarchy = self.knowledge_manager.relationship.get_hierarchy(concept)
            if hierarchy.get("parents") or hierarchy.get("children"):
                return {
                    "type": "hierarchy",
                    "query": query,
                    "concept": concept,
                    "found": True,
                    "parents": hierarchy.get("parents", []),
                    "children": hierarchy.get("children", [])
                }
        
        return {
            "type": "hierarchy",
            "query": query,
            "found": False,
            "message": f"NÃ£o encontrei hierarquia para: {concept or query}"
        }
    
    async def _handle_general(self, query: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Processa consulta geral."""
        # Busca no Ã­ndice
        results = self.knowledge_manager.index.search(query, limit=5)
        
        if results:
            return {
                "type": "general",
                "query": query,
                "found": True,
                "results": results,
                "count": len(results)
            }
        
        return {
            "type": "general",
            "query": query,
            "found": False,
            "message": "NÃ£o encontrei resultados para sua consulta"
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\knowledge\knowledge_relationship.py ====================
"""Gerenciamento de relacionamentos entre conceitos."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional


class KnowledgeRelationship:
    """
    Gerencia relacionamentos entre conceitos.
    
    Tipos de relacionamento:
    - is_a: hierarquia
    - part_of: composiÃ§Ã£o
    - depends_on: dependÃªncia
    - causes: causalidade
    - related_to: associaÃ§Ã£o
    - opposite_of: oposiÃ§Ã£o
    - similar_to: similaridade
    """
    
    def __init__(self, knowledge_manager):
        self.knowledge_manager = knowledge_manager
        self.logger = knowledge_manager.logger
        
        # Tipos de relacionamento vÃ¡lidos
        self.valid_types = {
            "is_a", "part_of", "depends_on", "causes", "prevents",
            "improves", "reduces", "related_to", "similar_to",
            "opposite_of", "example_of", "definition_of",
            "used_for", "created_by"
        }
    
    def add_relation(self, source: str, target: str, 
                     relation_type: str = "related_to") -> bool:
        """Adiciona uma relaÃ§Ã£o entre conceitos."""
        # Valida
        if relation_type not in self.valid_types:
            self.logger.warning(f"Tipo de relaÃ§Ã£o invÃ¡lido: {relation_type}")
            return False
        
        source_node = self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_by_concept(source)
        target_node = self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_by_concept(target)
        
        if not source_node or not target_node:
            self.logger.warning(f"Conceito nÃ£o encontrado: {source} ou {target}")
            return False
        
        # Adiciona relaÃ§Ã£o
        return self.knowledge_manager.graph.add_relation(
            source_id=source_node.id,
            target_id=target_node.id,
            relation_type=relation_type
        )
    
    def get_relations(self, concept: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m todas as relaÃ§Ãµes de um conceito."""
        node = self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_by_concept(concept)
        if not node:
            return []
        
        relations = self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_relations(node.id)
        
        result = []
        for rel in relations:
            source = self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_node(rel.source_id)
            target = self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_node(rel.target_id)
            
            if source and target:
                direction = "source" if source.concept == concept else "target"
                result.append({
                    "source": source.concept,
                    "target": target.concept,
                    "type": rel.relation_type,
                    "confidence": rel.confidence,
                    "direction": direction
                })
        
        return result
    
    def get_hierarchy(self, concept: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m a hierarquia de um conceito."""
        node = self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_by_concept(concept)
        if not node:
            return {}
        
        result = {
            "concept": concept,
            "parents": [],
            "children": [],
            "siblings": []
        }
        
        # Busca is_a relations
        for rel in self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_relations(node.id):
            if rel.relation_type == "is_a":
                source = self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_node(rel.source_id)
                target = self.knowledge_manager.graph.get_node(rel.target_id)
                
                if source and target:
                    if source.id == node.id:
                        result["parents"].append(target.concept)
                    else:
                        result["children"].append(source.concept)
        
        return result


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\knowledge\knowledge_source.py ====================
"""Gerenciamento de fontes de conhecimento."""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
import json


class KnowledgeSource:
    """
    Gerencia fontes de conhecimento.
    
    Cada conhecimento deve ter sua fonte.
    Isso permite rastrear a origem e a confiabilidade.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, storage_path: Path):
        self.storage_path = storage_path
        self.storage_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._sources: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        self._load()
    
    def _load(self) -> None:
        """Carrega fontes do disco."""
        source_file = self.storage_path / "sources.json"
        if source_file.exists():
            try:
                with open(source_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    self._sources = json.load(f)
            except Exception:
                pass
    
    def _save(self) -> None:
        """Salva fontes no disco."""
        source_file = self.storage_path / "sources.json"
        try:
            with open(source_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(self._sources, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Erro ao salvar fontes: {e}")
    
    def add_source(self, concept: str, source: str, 
                   source_type: str = "manual",
                   metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> str:
        """Adiciona uma fonte para um conceito."""
        source_key = f"{concept}_{source}_{datetime.now().isoformat()}"
        
        self._sources[source_key] = {
            "concept": concept,
            "source": source,
            "source_type": source_type,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "metadata": metadata or {},
            "confidence": self._calculate_confidence(source, source_type)
        }
        self._save()
        return source_key
    
    def get_sources(self, concept: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m todas as fontes de um conceito."""
        return [
            data for data in self._sources.values()
            if data["concept"] == concept
        ]
    
    def get_trusted_sources(self, min_confidence: float = 0.7) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m fontes com alta confianÃ§a."""
        return [
            data for data in self._sources.values()
            if data.get("confidence", 0) >= min_confidence
        ]
    
    def _calculate_confidence(self, source: str, source_type: str) -> float:
        """Calcula confianÃ§a de uma fonte."""
        confidence_map = {
            "wikipedia": 0.8,
            "wikidata": 0.85,
            "system": 0.9,
            "expert": 0.9,
            "book": 0.85,
            "article": 0.7,
            "course": 0.75,
            "manual": 0.6,
            "imported": 0.5,
            "learned": 0.4
        }
        
        # Ajusta pelo tipo de fonte
        base = confidence_map.get(source_type, 0.5)
        
        # Ajusta pela fonte especÃ­fica
        if "wikipedia.org" in source:
            base += 0.1
        elif "gov.br" in source:
            base += 0.1
        elif ".edu" in source:
            base += 0.05
        
        return min(1.0, max(0.0, base))
    
    def update_confidence(self, concept: str, source: str, 
                          new_confidence: float) -> bool:
        """Atualiza confianÃ§a de uma fonte."""
        for key, data in self._sources.items():
            if data["concept"] == concept and data["source"] == source:
                data["confidence"] = max(0.0, min(1.0, new_confidence))
                self._save()
                return True
        return False


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\knowledge\knowledge_validator.py ====================
"""Validador de conhecimento."""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
import re


class KnowledgeValidator:
    """
    Valida conhecimento antes de adicionar ao sistema.
    
    CritÃ©rios:
    - Conceito nÃ£o vazio
    - DescriÃ§Ã£o nÃ£o vazia
    - Fonte vÃ¡lida
    - ConfianÃ§a mÃ­nima
    - NÃ£o duplicado (verificado no grafo)
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._min_confidence = 0.3
        self._max_description_length = 10000
        self._min_description_length = 10
    
    def validate_concept(self, concept: str, description: str, 
                         source: str) -> bool:
        """Valida um conceito."""
        # Conceito nÃ£o vazio
        if not concept or not concept.strip():
            return False
        
        # DescriÃ§Ã£o nÃ£o vazia
        if not description or not description.strip():
            return False
        
        # DescriÃ§Ã£o muito curta
        if len(description) < self._min_description_length:
            return False
        
        # DescriÃ§Ã£o muito longa
        if len(description) > self._max_description_length:
            return False
        
        # Fonte vÃ¡lida
        if not source or source == "unknown":
            # Permite source unknown com confianÃ§a baixa
            pass
        
        return True
    
    def validate_description(self, description: str) -> bool:
        """Valida uma descriÃ§Ã£o."""
        if not description or not description.strip():
            return False
        
        if len(description) < self._min_description_length:
            return False
        
        if len(description) > self._max_description_length:
            return False
        
        return True
    
    def validate_relation(self, source: str, target: str, 
                          relation_type: str) -> bool:
        """Valida uma relaÃ§Ã£o."""
        if not source or not target:
            return False
        
        if source == target:
            return False
        
        if not relation_type or not relation_type.strip():
            return False
        
        valid_types = [
            "related_to", "is_a", "part_of", "depends_on",
            "causes", "prevents", "improves", "reduces",
            "similar_to", "opposite_of", "example_of",
            "definition_of", "used_for", "created_by"
        ]
        
        return relation_type in valid_types
    
    def check_consistency(self, node) -> List[str]:
        """Verifica consistÃªncia de um nÃ³."""
        issues = []
        
        # Verifica conceito
        if not node.concept or not node.concept.strip():
            issues.append("Conceito vazio")
        
        # Verifica descriÃ§Ã£o
        if not node.description or not node.description.strip():
            issues.append("DescriÃ§Ã£o vazia")
        elif len(node.description) < self._min_description_length:
            issues.append(f"DescriÃ§Ã£o muito curta ({len(node.description)} caracteres)")
        
        # Verifica confianÃ§a
        if node.confidence < self._min_confidence:
            issues.append(f"ConfianÃ§a muito baixa ({node.confidence})")
        
        # Verifica fonte
        if node.source == "unknown":
            issues.append("Fonte desconhecida")
        
        return issues
    
    def validate_source(self, source: str) -> bool:
        """Valida uma fonte."""
        if not source or not source.strip():
            return False
        
        # Lista de fontes vÃ¡lidas
        valid_sources = [
            "wikipedia", "wikidata", "system", "manual", 
            "imported", "learned", "book", "article",
            "video", "course", "expert"
        ]
        
        return source in valid_sources or source.startswith("https://")


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\knowledge\source_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de fontes de conhecimento."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from uuid import uuid4


@dataclass
class KnowledgeSource:
    """Fonte de conhecimento."""
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    name: str = ""
    type: str = ""  # book, article, video, course, etc.
    author: str = ""
    url: Optional[str] = None
    trust_score: float = 0.5  # 0.0 a 1.0
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


class SourceManager:
    """
    Gerencia fontes de conhecimento.
    Cada conhecimento deve ter sua fonte.
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._sources: Dict[str, KnowledgeSource] = {}
    
    def add_source(self, source: KnowledgeSource) -> str:
        """Adiciona uma fonte."""
        self._sources[source.id] = source
        return source.id
    
    def get_source(self, source_id: str) -> Optional[KnowledgeSource]:
        """ObtÃ©m uma fonte pelo ID."""
        return self._sources.get(source_id)
    
    def get_by_type(self, source_type: str) -> List[KnowledgeSource]:
        """ObtÃ©m fontes por tipo."""
        return [s for s in self._sources.values() if s.type == source_type]
    
    def get_trusted_sources(self, min_trust: float = 0.7) -> List[KnowledgeSource]:
        """ObtÃ©m fontes com alto grau de confianÃ§a."""
        return [s for s in self._sources.values() if s.trust_score >= min_trust]
    
    def update_trust(self, source_id: str, new_score: float) -> None:
        """Atualiza o grau de confianÃ§a de uma fonte."""
        if source_id in self._sources:
            self._sources[source_id].trust_score = max(0.0, min(1.0, new_score))

"""Loop de feedback."""

from typing import Dict, Any


class FeedbackLoop:
    """Processa feedback do usuÃ¡rio."""
    
    def __init__(self, learning_manager):
        self.learning_manager = learning_manager
        self.logger = learning_manager.logger
        self._feedback_history = []
    
    async def process(self, feedback_type: str, content: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Processa feedback."""
        self._feedback_history.append({
            "type": feedback_type,
            "content": content,
            "timestamp": __import__('datetime').datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
        self.logger.info(f"Feedback recebido: {feedback_type}")
    
    async def process_agent_response(self, agent: str, response: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Processa resposta de agente."""
        if response.get("success") == False:
            self.logger.warning(f"Agente {agent} falhou: {response.get('error')}")

==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\learning\knowledge_extractor.py ====================
"""Extrator de conhecimento."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime
import re


class KnowledgeExtractor:
    """
    Extrai conhecimento de conversas e interaÃ§Ãµes.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, learning_manager):
        self.learning_manager = learning_manager
        self.logger = learning_manager.logger
        self.knowledge_manager = learning_manager.knowledge_manager
        
        self._extracted: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    
    async def extract_from_text(self, text: str, source: str = "unknown") -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Extrai conhecimento de um texto.
        """
        knowledge = []
        
        # 1. Extrai definiÃ§Ãµes
        definitions = self._extract_definitions(text)
        for concept, definition in definitions:
            knowledge.append({
                "type": "definition",
                "concept": concept,
                "description": definition,
                "source": source
            })
        
        # 2. Extrai fatos
        facts = self._extract_facts(text)
        for fact in facts:
            knowledge.append({
                "type": "fact",
                "concept": fact["subject"],
                "description": fact["predicate"],
                "source": source
            })
        
        # 3. Extrai procedimentos
        procedures = self._extract_procedures(text)
        for procedure in procedures:
            knowledge.append({
                "type": "procedure",
                "name": procedure["name"],
                "steps": procedure["steps"],
                "source": source
            })
        
        # Armazena no KnowledgeManager
        for item in knowledge:
            if item["type"] == "definition":
                await self.knowledge_manager._on_add(
                    self._create_event({
                        "concept": item["concept"],
                        "description": item["description"],
                        "source": item["source"],
                        "confidence": 0.5
                    })
                )
        
        self._extracted.extend(knowledge)
        return knowledge
    
    async def extract_from_dict(self, data: Dict[str, Any], source: str = "unknown") -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Extrai conhecimento de um dicionÃ¡rio.
        """
        knowledge = []
        
        if "concept" in data and "description" in data:
            knowledge.append({
                "type": "definition",
                "concept": data["concept"],
                "description": data["description"],
                "source": source
            })
        
        if "facts" in data:
            for fact in data["facts"]:
                knowledge.append({
                    "type": "fact",
                    "concept": fact.get("subject", ""),
                    "description": fact.get("predicate", ""),
                    "source": source
                })
        
        return knowledge
    
    async def extract(self, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Retorna conhecimento extraÃ­do recentemente.
        """
        return self._extracted[-limit:]
    
    def _extract_definitions(self, text: str) -> List[tuple]:
        """
        Extrai definiÃ§Ãµes no formato "X Ã© Y".
        """
        definitions = []
        
        # PadrÃµes: "X Ã© Y" ou "X significa Y" ou "X Ã© quando Y"
        patterns = [
            r'([A-Za-zÃ€-Ã¿\s]{2,30})\s+[Ã©e]\s+([A-Za-zÃ€-Ã¿\s,;]{5,100})',
            r'([A-Za-zÃ€-Ã¿\s]{2,30})\s+significa\s+([A-Za-zÃ€-Ã¿\s,;]{5,100})',
            r'([A-Za-zÃ€-Ã¿\s]{2,30})\s+[Ã©e]\s+quando\s+([A-Za-zÃ€-Ã¿\s,;]{5,100})'
        ]
        
        for pattern in patterns:
            matches = re.findall(pattern, text)
            for match in matches:
                concept = match[0].strip()
                definition = match[1].strip()
                if len(concept) > 2 and len(definition) > 10:
                    definitions.append((concept, definition))
        
        return definitions[:3]
    
    def _extract_facts(self, text: str) -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
        """
        Extrai fatos no formato "X Ã© Y" ou "X tem Y".
        """
        facts = []
        
        patterns = [
            r'([A-Za-zÃ€-Ã¿\s]{2,30})\s+[Ã©e]\s+([A-Za-zÃ€-Ã¿\s,;]{3,50})',
            r'([A-Za-zÃ€-Ã¿\s]{2,30})\s+tem\s+([A-Za-zÃ€-Ã¿\s,;]{3,50})',
        ]
        
        for pattern in patterns:
            matches = re.findall(pattern, text)
            for match in matches:
                facts.append({
                    "subject": match[0].strip(),
                    "predicate": match[1].strip()
                })
        
        return facts[:5]
    
    def _extract_procedures(self, text: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Extrai procedimentos (passos).
        """
        procedures = []
        
        # Detecta listas de passos
        if "primeiro" in text and "depois" in text:
            steps = re.findall(r'(?:primeiro|depois|em seguida|entÃ£o|finalmente)\s+([A-Za-zÃ€-Ã¿\s,;]{5,50})', text)
            if steps:
                procedures.append({
                    "name": "Procedimento extraÃ­do",
                    "steps": steps
                })
        
        return procedures
    
    def _create_event(self, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
        """Cria um evento para o KnowledgeManager."""
        from src.kernel.events import Event
        return Event(
            type="knowledge.add",
            payload=data,
            source="knowledge_extractor"
        )

"""Gerenciador de aprendizado da EVA."""

import asyncio
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path

from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus
from src.learning.pattern_extractor import PatternExtractor
from src.learning.knowledge_extractor import KnowledgeExtractor
from src.learning.behavior_analyzer import BehaviorAnalyzer
from src.learning.feedback_loop import FeedbackLoop
from src.learning.skill_learner import SkillLearner
from src.learning.anomaly_detector import AnomalyDetector
from src.learning.improvement_suggester import ImprovementSuggester


class LearningManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de aprendizado da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Extrair padrÃµes de interaÃ§Ãµes
    - Extrair conhecimento de conversas
    - Analisar comportamentos
    - Loop de feedback
    - Aprender novas habilidades
    - Detectar anomalias
    - Sugerir melhorias
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "learning_manager"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Gerenciador de aprendizado da EVA"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["core", "learning", "intelligence"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["learning", "pattern_extraction", "feedback"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config", "memory_manager", "knowledge_manager"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config, memory_manager, knowledge_manager):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        self.memory_manager = memory_manager
        self.knowledge_manager = knowledge_manager
        
        # Componentes
        self.pattern_extractor = PatternExtractor(self)
        self.knowledge_extractor = KnowledgeExtractor(self)
        self.behavior_analyzer = BehaviorAnalyzer(self)
        self.feedback_loop = FeedbackLoop(self)
        self.skill_learner = SkillLearner(self)
        self.anomaly_detector = AnomalyDetector(self)
        self.improvement_suggester = ImprovementSuggester(self)
        
        # Estado
        self._running = False
        self._learning_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
        self._learning_interval = 300  # 5 minutos
        
        # HistÃ³rico de aprendizado
        self._learning_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._patterns: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._insights: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        
        # Registra listeners
        self._register_listeners()
        
        self.logger.info("LearningManager inicializado")
    
    def _register_listeners(self) -> None:
        """Registra ouvintes de eventos."""
        self.event_bus.subscribe("learning.analyze", self._on_analyze)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("learning.extract_patterns", self._on_extract_patterns)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("learning.feedback", self._on_feedback)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("learning.suggest", self._on_suggest)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("learning.get_insights", self._on_get_insights)
        
        # Eventos automÃ¡ticos
        self.event_bus.subscribe("user.message", self._on_user_message)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("plan.completed", self._on_plan_completed)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("task.completed", self._on_task_completed)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("agent.response", self._on_agent_response)
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o LearningManager."""
        self.logger.info("LearningManager inicializando...")
        
        # Carrega histÃ³rico
        await self._load_history()
        
        self._running = True
        
        # Inicia aprendizado automÃ¡tico
        self._learning_task = asyncio.create_task(self._auto_learn())
        
        self.logger.info("LearningManager inicializado")
    
    async def start(self) -> None:
        """Inicia o LearningManager."""
        self.logger.info("LearningManager iniciando...")
        self.logger.info("LearningManager pronto")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o LearningManager."""
        self.logger.info("LearningManager desligando...")
        
        self._running = False
        
        if self._learning_task:
            self._learning_task.cancel()
            try:
                await self._learning_task
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
        
        await self._save_history()
        
        self.logger.info("LearningManager desligado")
    
    # ===== COMANDOS =====
    
    async def _on_analyze(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Analisa interaÃ§Ãµes."""
        payload = event.payload
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        
        result = await self.analyze()
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "learning.analysis_result",
            {
                "result": result,
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="learning_manager"
        )
    
    async def _on_extract_patterns(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Extrai padrÃµes."""
        payload = event.payload
        limit = payload.get("limit", 50)
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        
        patterns = await self.pattern_extractor.extract(limit)
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "learning.patterns",
            {
                "patterns": patterns,
                "count": len(patterns),
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="learning_manager"
        )
    
    async def _on_feedback(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa feedback."""
        payload = event.payload
        feedback_type = payload.get("type", "general")
        content = payload.get("content", {})
        
        await self.feedback_loop.process(feedback_type, content)
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "learning.feedback_processed",
            {
                "type": feedback_type,
                "success": True,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="learning_manager"
        )
    
    async def _on_suggest(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Sugere melhorias."""
        payload = event.payload
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        
        suggestions = await self.improvement_suggester.suggest()
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "learning.suggestions",
            {
                "suggestions": suggestions,
                "count": len(suggestions),
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="learning_manager"
        )
    
    async def _on_get_insights(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Retorna insights."""
        payload = event.payload
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        limit = payload.get("limit", 10)
        
        insights = self._insights[-limit:]
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "learning.insights",
            {
                "insights": insights,
                "count": len(insights),
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="learning_manager"
        )
    
    # ===== EVENTOS AUTOMÃTICOS =====
    
    async def _on_user_message(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa mensagem do usuÃ¡rio."""
        payload = event.payload
        text = payload.get("text", "")
        user = payload.get("user", "unknown")
        
        # Extrai conhecimento da mensagem
        await self.knowledge_extractor.extract_from_text(text, source=f"user_{user}")
        
        # Analisa padrÃµes
        await self.pattern_extractor.process_message(text, user)
    
    async def _on_plan_completed(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa conclusÃ£o de plano."""
        payload = event.payload
        plan_id = payload.get("plan_id")
        result = payload.get("result", {})
        
        # Aprende com o plano
        await self.skill_learner.learn_from_plan(plan_id, result)
        
        # Extrai conhecimento
        if result.get("knowledge"):
            await self.knowledge_extractor.extract_from_dict(
                result.get("knowledge"),
                source=f"plan_{plan_id}"
            )
    
    async def _on_task_completed(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa conclusÃ£o de tarefa."""
        payload = event.payload
        task_id = payload.get("task_id")
        result = payload.get("result", {})
        
        # Atualiza padrÃµes
        await self.pattern_extractor.process_task(task_id, result)
    
    async def _on_agent_response(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Processa resposta de agente."""
        payload = event.payload
        agent = payload.get("agent", "unknown")
        response = payload.get("result", {})
        
        # Feedback loop
        if response.get("success") is not None:
            await self.feedback_loop.process_agent_response(agent, response)
    
    # ===== APRENDIZADO AUTOMÃTICO =====
    
    async def _auto_learn(self) -> None:
        """Loop de aprendizado automÃ¡tico."""
        while self._running:
            try:
                await asyncio.sleep(self._learning_interval)
                
                if self._running:
                    await self.analyze()
                    
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro no aprendizado automÃ¡tico: {e}")
    
    # ===== MÃ‰TODOS PRINCIPAIS =====
    
    async def analyze(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executa anÃ¡lise completa de aprendizado.
        """
        self.logger.info("Iniciando anÃ¡lise de aprendizado...")
        
        result = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "patterns": [],
            "knowledge": [],
            "insights": [],
            "anomalies": [],
            "suggestions": []
        }
        
        # 1. Extrai padrÃµes
        patterns = await self.pattern_extractor.extract(limit=20)
        result["patterns"] = patterns
        
        # 2. Extrai conhecimento
        knowledge = await self.knowledge_extractor.extract(limit=10)
        result["knowledge"] = knowledge
        
        # 3. Analisa comportamento
        behavior = await self.behavior_analyzer.analyze()
        result["behavior"] = behavior
        
        # 4. Detecta anomalias
        anomalies = await self.anomaly_detector.detect()
        result["anomalies"] = anomalies
        
        # 5. Gera insights
        insights = await self._generate_insights(result)
        result["insights"] = insights
        self._insights.extend(insights)
        
        # 6. Sugere melhorias
        suggestions = await self.improvement_suggester.suggest()
        result["suggestions"] = suggestions
        
        # Registra histÃ³rico
        self._learning_history.append(result)
        if len(self._learning_history) > 100:
            self._learning_history = self._learning_history[-100:]
        
        self.logger.info(f"AnÃ¡lise concluÃ­da: {len(patterns)} padrÃµes, {len(insights)} insights")
        
        # Publica evento
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "learning.analysis_complete",
            {
                "result": result,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="learning_manager"
        )
        
        return result
    
    async def _generate_insights(self, analysis: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Gera insights a partir da anÃ¡lise.
        """
        insights = []
        
        # PadrÃµes interessantes
        for pattern in analysis.get("patterns", [])[:5]:
            if pattern.get("frequency", 0) > 3:
                insights.append({
                    "type": "pattern",
                    "title": f"PadrÃ£o detectado: {pattern.get('name', 'desconhecido')}",
                    "description": pattern.get("description", ""),
                    "confidence": pattern.get("confidence", 0.5),
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                })
        
        # Anomalias
        for anomaly in analysis.get("anomalies", []):
            insights.append({
                "type": "anomaly",
                "title": f"Anomalia: {anomaly.get('type', 'desconhecida')}",
                "description": anomaly.get("description", ""),
                "severity": anomaly.get("severity", "medium"),
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            })
        
        # Comportamentos
        behavior = analysis.get("behavior", {})
        if behavior.get("common_topics"):
            insights.append({
                "type": "behavior",
                "title": "TÃ³picos mais frequentes",
                "description": f"Os tÃ³picos mais comuns sÃ£o: {', '.join(behavior['common_topics'][:3])}",
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            })
        
        return insights
    
    # ===== PERSISTÃŠNCIA =====
    
    async def _load_history(self) -> None:
        """Carrega histÃ³rico."""
        # TODO: Implementar persistÃªncia
        pass
    
    async def _save_history(self) -> None:
        """Salva histÃ³rico."""
        # TODO: Implementar persistÃªncia
        pass
    
    # ===== MÃ‰TODOS PÃšBLICOS =====
    
    def get_insights(self, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m insights recentes."""
        return self._insights[-limit:]
    
    def get_patterns(self, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m padrÃµes recentes."""
        return self._patterns[-limit:]
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        """Health check do LearningManager."""
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "patterns": len(self._patterns),
            "insights": len(self._insights),
            "history": len(self._learning_history)
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\learning\pattern_extractor.py ====================
"""Extrator de padrÃµes."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from collections import defaultdict, Counter
import re


class PatternExtractor:
    """
    Extrai padrÃµes de interaÃ§Ãµes.
    
    Tipos de padrÃµes:
    - FrequÃªncia de palavras
    - TÃ³picos recorrentes
    - PadrÃµes de horÃ¡rio
    - SequÃªncias de aÃ§Ãµes
    - PadrÃµes de perguntas
    """
    
    def __init__(self, learning_manager):
        self.learning_manager = learning_manager
        self.logger = learning_manager.logger
        
        self._message_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._patterns: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        self._word_freq: Counter = Counter()
        self._topic_freq: Counter = Counter()
        self._hourly_freq: Counter = Counter()
        self._question_patterns: List[str] = []
    
    async def process_message(self, text: str, user: str) -> None:
        """
        Processa uma mensagem para extraÃ§Ã£o de padrÃµes.
        """
        timestamp = datetime.now()
        
        self._message_history.append({
            "text": text,
            "user": user,
            "timestamp": timestamp
        })
        
        # Limita histÃ³rico
        if len(self._message_history) > 1000:
            self._message_history = self._message_history[-1000:]
        
        # Atualiza frequÃªncia de palavras
        words = re.findall(r'\w+', text.lower())
        for word in words:
            if len(word) > 3:
                self._word_freq[word] += 1
        
        # Atualiza frequÃªncia de tÃ³picos
        topics = self._extract_topics(text)
        for topic in topics:
            self._topic_freq[topic] += 1
        
        # Atualiza frequÃªncia horÃ¡ria
        hour = timestamp.hour
        self._hourly_freq[hour] += 1
        
        # Detecta padrÃµes de pergunta
        if "?" in text or text.lower().startswith(("o que", "quem", "quando", "onde", "como", "por que")):
            self._question_patterns.append(text[:100])
    
    async def extract(self, limit: int = 20) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Extrai padrÃµes.
        """
        patterns = []
        
        # 1. Palavras mais frequentes
        top_words = self._word_freq.most_common(10)
        if top_words:
            patterns.append({
                "type": "word_frequency",
                "name": "Palavras mais frequentes",
                "description": f"As palavras mais usadas sÃ£o: {', '.join([w for w, _ in top_words[:5]])}",
                "data": top_words,
                "frequency": sum(c for _, c in top_words),
                "confidence": 0.8
            })
        
        # 2. TÃ³picos mais frequentes
        top_topics = self._topic_freq.most_common(5)
        if top_topics:
            patterns.append({
                "type": "topic_frequency",
                "name": "TÃ³picos mais frequentes",
                "description": f"Os tÃ³picos mais comuns sÃ£o: {', '.join([t for t, _ in top_topics])}",
                "data": top_topics,
                "frequency": sum(c for _, c in top_topics),
                "confidence": 0.7
            })
        
        # 3. HorÃ¡rios de pico
        peak_hours = self._hourly_freq.most_common(3)
        if peak_hours:
            patterns.append({
                "type": "peak_hours",
                "name": "HorÃ¡rios de pico",
                "description": f"Maior atividade nos horÃ¡rios: {', '.join([f'{h}h' for h, _ in peak_hours])}",
                "data": peak_hours,
                "confidence": 0.6
            })
        
        # 4. PadrÃµes de pergunta
        if len(self._question_patterns) > 10:
            question_types = self._analyze_question_patterns()
            if question_types:
                patterns.append({
                    "type": "question_patterns",
                    "name": "PadrÃµes de perguntas",
                    "description": f"Tipos de pergunta: {', '.join(question_types.keys())}",
                    "data": question_types,
                    "confidence": 0.7
                })
        
        self._patterns = patterns
        return patterns[:limit]
    
    def _extract_topics(self, text: str) -> List[str]:
        """
        Extrai tÃ³picos de um texto.
        """
        topics = []
        
        # Palavras-chave comuns
        common_topics = {
            "luz": ["luz", "iluminaÃ§Ã£o", "lÃ¢mpada"],
            "temperatura": ["temperatura", "frio", "calor", "ar"],
            "mÃºsica": ["mÃºsica", "som", "playlist"],
            "programaÃ§Ã£o": ["programar", "cÃ³digo", "python", "javascript"],
            "estudo": ["estudar", "aprender", "curso", "matÃ©ria"],
            "compras": ["comprar", "mercado", "lista"]
        }
        
        text_lower = text.lower()
        
        for topic, keywords in common_topics.items():
            for keyword in keywords:
                if keyword in text_lower:
                    topics.append(topic)
                    break
        
        return topics
    
    def _analyze_question_patterns(self) -> Dict[str, int]:
        """
        Analisa padrÃµes de perguntas.
        """
        patterns = {}
        
        for q in self._question_patterns[-100:]:
            q_lower = q.lower()
            
            if q_lower.startswith("o que"):
                patterns["definiÃ§Ã£o"] = patterns.get("definiÃ§Ã£o", 0) + 1
            elif q_lower.startswith("como"):
                patterns["processo"] = patterns.get("processo", 0) + 1
            elif q_lower.startswith("quando"):
                patterns["tempo"] = patterns.get("tempo", 0) + 1
            elif q_lower.startswith("onde"):
                patterns["local"] = patterns.get("local", 0) + 1
            elif q_lower.startswith("quem"):
                patterns["pessoa"] = patterns.get("pessoa", 0) + 1
            elif q_lower.startswith("por que"):
                patterns["motivo"] = patterns.get("motivo", 0) + 1
        
        return {k: v for k, v in patterns.items() if v > 0}

==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\llm\llm_manager.py ====================
"""
Gerenciador de LLM da EVA - apenas geraÃ§Ã£o, sem publicaÃ§Ã£o.
"""

import asyncio
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
import aiohttp
from datetime import datetime

from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus


class LLMManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de LLM - apenas geraÃ§Ã£o de texto.
    NÃ£o publica respostas diretamente.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self._ollama_host = config.get("llm.ollama.host", "localhost")
        self._ollama_port = config.get("llm.ollama.port", 11434)
        
        # ðŸ”¥ PRIORIDADE: qwen2.5:7b (melhor para portuguÃªs)
        self._model = config.get("llm.model", "qwen2.5:7b")
        
        self._running = False
        self._conversation_history: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“Œ LLMManager inicializado com modelo: {self._model}")
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o LLMManager."""
        self.logger.info("LLMManager inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        
        if await self._check_ollama():
            available = await self._get_available_models()
            
            # ðŸ”¥ FORÃ‡A o uso do qwen2.5:7b se disponÃ­vel
            if "qwen2.5:7b" in available:
                self._model = "qwen2.5:7b"
                self.logger.info(f"âœ… Usando modelo principal: qwen2.5:7b")
            elif self._model in available:
                self.logger.info(f"âœ… Modelo '{self._model}' disponÃ­vel")
            else:
                if available:
                    old_model = self._model
                    self._model = available[0]
                    self.logger.info(f"âš ï¸ Usando fallback: {self._model} (era {old_model})")
                else:
                    self.logger.warning("âš ï¸ Nenhum modelo disponÃ­vel no Ollama")
        else:
            self.logger.warning("âš ï¸ Ollama nÃ£o disponÃ­vel")
        
        self.logger.info(f"LLMManager inicializado com modelo: {self._model}")
    
    async def generate(self, prompt: str, system_prompt: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
        """Gera uma resposta usando Ollama."""
        if not await self._check_ollama():
            return self._fallback_response(prompt)
        
        try:
            messages = self._build_messages(prompt, system_prompt)
            
            url = f"http://{self._ollama_host}:{self._ollama_port}/api/chat"
            data = {
                "model": self._model,
                "messages": messages,
                "stream": False,
                "options": {
                    "temperature": 0.8,
                    "top_p": 0.9,
                    "num_predict": 1024  # ðŸ”¥ AUMENTADO para respostas mais longas
                }
            }
            
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
                async with session.post(url, json=data, timeout=60) as resp:
                    if resp.status == 200:
                        result = await resp.json()
                        response = result.get("message", {}).get("content", "")
                        if response:
                            # ðŸ”¥ Salva no histÃ³rico
                            self._conversation_history.append({"role": "user", "content": prompt})
                            self._conversation_history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": response})
                            if len(self._conversation_history) > 20:
                                self._conversation_history = self._conversation_history[-20:]
                            return response.strip()
                    return self._fallback_response(prompt)
                    
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro no LLM: {e}")
            return self._fallback_response(prompt)
    
    def _build_messages(self, prompt: str, system_prompt: Optional[str] = None) -> list:
        """ConstrÃ³i mensagens para o Ollama."""
        messages = []
        
        if system_prompt:
            messages.append({"role": "system", "content": system_prompt})
        else:
            messages.append({
                "role": "system",
                "content": """
VocÃª Ã© a EVA, uma assistente virtual inteligente, carinhosa e sÃ¡bia.

â–¶ **Como falar:**
- Seja natural, direta e objetiva. NÃ£o enrole.
- Use linguagem coloquial e informal.
- NUNCA inicie respostas com "OlÃ¡" ou o nome do usuÃ¡rio, a menos que ele tenha cumprimentado primeiro.
- NUNCA repita o nome do usuÃ¡rio desnecessariamente.
- Varie as estruturas de frase.
- FaÃ§a no mÃ¡ximo uma pergunta por resposta.
- Se nÃ£o souber algo, diga com naturalidade.

â–¶ **Personalidade:**
- Curiosa, adora aprender, senso de humor sutil.
- EmpÃ¡tica, paciente e respeitosa.
- NÃ£o Ã© arrogante nem enciclopÃ©dia.

â–¶ **Regras:**
- Responda em portuguÃªs brasileiro.
- Seja sempre Ãºtil e educada.
- Respostas DIRETAS e CONCISAS.
"""
            })
        
        # ðŸ”¥ Adiciona histÃ³rico recente (Ãºltimas 10 interaÃ§Ãµes)
        for msg in self._conversation_history[-20:]:
            messages.append(msg)
        
        messages.append({"role": "user", "content": prompt})
        return messages
    
    def _fallback_response(self, prompt: str) -> str:
        """Resposta fallback quando o LLM nÃ£o estÃ¡ disponÃ­vel."""
        # ðŸ”¥ Removido o fallback genÃ©rico "Interessante! Me conte mais..."
        return "Desculpe, nÃ£o consegui processar sua mensagem agora. Pode repetir, por favor?"
    
    async def _check_ollama(self) -> bool:
        """Verifica se Ollama estÃ¡ rodando."""
        try:
            url = f"http://{self._ollama_host}:{self._ollama_port}/api/tags"
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
                async with session.get(url, timeout=2) as resp:
                    return resp.status == 200
        except:
            return False
    
    async def _get_available_models(self) -> List[str]:
        """Retorna modelos disponÃ­veis no Ollama."""
        try:
            url = f"http://{self._ollama_host}:{self._ollama_port}/api/tags"
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
                async with session.get(url, timeout=2) as resp:
                    if resp.status == 200:
                        data = await resp.json()
                        return [m.get("name") for m in data.get("models", []) if m.get("name")]
        except:
            pass
        return []
    
    def clear_history(self) -> None:
        """Limpa o histÃ³rico de conversaÃ§Ã£o."""
        self._conversation_history = []
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§¹ HistÃ³rico do LLM limpo")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self._running = False
        self.logger.info("LLMManager desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "running": self._running,
            "model": self._model,
            "ollama_available": await self._check_ollama(),
            "history_size": len(self._conversation_history)
        }

"""MemÃ³ria episÃ³dica - eventos e experiÃªncias."""

import json
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from uuid import uuid4


@dataclass
class Episode:
    """Um episÃ³dio/evento na memÃ³ria."""
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    title: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    participants: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    location: str = ""
    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    importance: int = 1  # 1-10
    emotions: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    related_episodes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    data: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


class EpisodicMemory:
    """
    MemÃ³ria de longo prazo para eventos e experiÃªncias.
    Armazena o que aconteceu.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, storage_path: Path):
        self.storage_path = storage_path
        self.storage_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._episodes: Dict[str, Episode] = {}
        self._load()
    
    def _load(self) -> None:
        """Carrega episÃ³dios do disco."""
        # TODO: Carregar de JSON/PostgreSQL
        pass
    
    def _save(self) -> None:
        """Salva episÃ³dios no disco."""
        # TODO: Salvar em JSON/PostgreSQL
        pass
    
    def add(self, episode: Episode) -> str:
        """Adiciona um episÃ³dio."""
        self._episodes[episode.id] = episode
        self._save()
        return episode.id
    
    def get(self, episode_id: str) -> Optional[Episode]:
        """ObtÃ©m um episÃ³dio por ID."""
        return self._episodes.get(episode_id)
    
    def search(self, query: str, limit: int = 10) -> List[Episode]:
        """Busca episÃ³dios por texto."""
        # TODO: Implementar busca semÃ¢ntica
        results = []
        query_lower = query.lower()
        
        for episode in self._episodes.values():
            if (query_lower in episode.title.lower() or 
                query_lower in episode.description.lower()):
                results.append(episode)
        
        return results[:limit]
    
    def get_by_date_range(self, start: datetime, end: datetime) -> List[Episode]:
        """ObtÃ©m episÃ³dios em um intervalo de datas."""
        return [
            episode for episode in self._episodes.values()
            if start <= episode.timestamp <= end
        ]
    
    def get_by_participant(self, participant: str) -> List[Episode]:
        """ObtÃ©m episÃ³dios de um participante."""
        return [
            episode for episode in self._episodes.values()
            if participant in episode.participants
        ]
    
    def get_recent(self, n: int = 10) -> List[Episode]:
        """ObtÃ©m os episÃ³dios mais recentes."""
        sorted_episodes = sorted(
            self._episodes.values(),
            key=lambda x: x.timestamp,
            reverse=True
        )
        return sorted_episodes[:n]
