"""MemÃ³ria Familiar da EVA - Ãrvore GenealÃ³gica."""

import json
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from src.kernel.events import EventBus


class FamilyMemory:
    """
    MemÃ³ria Familiar da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Armazenar Ã¡rvore genealÃ³gica
    - Relacionar membros da famÃ­lia
    - Responder perguntas sobre famÃ­lia
    - Manter histÃ³rico familiar
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self._family_tree: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "members": {},
            "relationships": [],
            "last_updated": None
        }
        
        data_path = Path(config.get("paths.data", "./data"))
        self._family_path = data_path / "family" / "tree.json"
        self._family_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ  [FamilyMemory] Inicializado")
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ  [FamilyMemory] Inicializando...")
        await self._load_tree()
        self.logger.info("ðŸ  [FamilyMemory] Inicializado")
    
    def add_member(self, name: str, relation: str, parent: str = None, spouse: str = None) -> None:
        """Adiciona um membro da famÃ­lia."""
        member_id = name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
        
        self._family_tree["members"][member_id] = {
            "name": name,
            "relation": relation,
            "parent": parent,
            "spouse": spouse,
            "children": [],
            "added_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        # Se tem parente, atualiza
        if parent and parent in self._family_tree["members"]:
            parent_id = parent.lower().replace(" ", "_")
            if "children" not in self._family_tree["members"][parent_id]:
                self._family_tree["members"][parent_id]["children"] = []
            if member_id not in self._family_tree["members"][parent_id]["children"]:
                self._family_tree["members"][parent_id]["children"].append(member_id)
        
        self._save_tree()
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ  Membro adicionado: {name} ({relation})")
    
    def get_member(self, name: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m um membro da famÃ­lia pelo nome."""
        member_id = name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
        return self._family_tree["members"].get(member_id)
    
    def get_family_tree(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Retorna a Ã¡rvore genealÃ³gica completa."""
        return self._family_tree
    
    def get_children(self, parent_name: str) -> List[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m os filhos de um pai/mÃ£e."""
        parent_id = parent_name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
        parent = self._family_tree["members"].get(parent_id)
        if parent:
            return parent.get("children", [])
        return []
    
    def get_parents(self, child_name: str) -> List[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m os pais de uma pessoa."""
        child_id = child_name.lower().replace(" ", "_")
        child = self._family_tree["members"].get(child_id)
        if child and child.get("parent"):
            return [child["parent"]]
        return []
    
    def get_relation(self, name1: str, name2: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m a relaÃ§Ã£o entre duas pessoas."""
        # TODO: Implementar lÃ³gica de relaÃ§Ã£o
        return None
    
    def search(self, query: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Busca membros da famÃ­lia por nome."""
        results = []
        query_lower = query.lower()
        
        for member_id, member in self._family_tree["members"].items():
            if query_lower in member["name"].lower():
                results.append(member)
        
        return results
    
    def format_tree(self) -> str:
        """Formata a Ã¡rvore genealÃ³gica para exibiÃ§Ã£o."""
        lines = []
        lines.append("ðŸ“Š ÃRVORE GENEALÃ“GICA")
        lines.append("=" * 30)
        
        # Pega todos os membros
        members = self._family_tree["members"]
        
        for member_id, member in members.items():
            name = member["name"]
            relation = member["relation"]
            parent = member.get("parent", "")
            children = member.get("children", [])
            
            lines.append(f"\nðŸ‘¤ {name} ({relation})")
            if parent:
                parent_name = members.get(parent, {}).get("name", "Desconhecido")
                lines.append(f"  ðŸ‘¨â€ðŸ‘§ Filho(a) de: {parent_name}")
            if children:
                children_names = [members.get(c, {}).get("name", c) for c in children]
                lines.append(f"  ðŸ‘¨â€ðŸ‘©â€ðŸ‘§â€ðŸ‘¦ Filhos: {', '.join(children_names)}")
        
        return "\n".join(lines)
    
    async def _load_tree(self) -> None:
        """Carrega a Ã¡rvore do disco."""
        try:
            if self._family_path.exists():
                with open(self._family_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    self._family_tree = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(f"Erro ao carregar Ã¡rvore: {e}")
    
    async def _save_tree(self) -> None:
        """Salva a Ã¡rvore no disco."""
        try:
            self._family_tree["last_updated"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
            with open(self._family_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(self._family_tree, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao salvar Ã¡rvore: {e}")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ  [FamilyMemory] Desligando...")
        await self._save_tree()
        self.logger.info("ðŸ  [FamilyMemory] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "members": len(self._family_tree["members"])
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\memory\long_term_memory.py ====================
"""MemÃ³ria de Longo Prazo - Persistente."""

import json
import sqlite3
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from src.kernel.events import EventBus


class LongTermMemory:
    """
    MemÃ³ria de Longo Prazo da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Armazenar conversas permanentemente
    - Recuperar histÃ³rico por usuÃ¡rio
    - Buscar por palavras-chave
    - Manter contexto de longo prazo
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        data_path = Path(config.get("paths.data", "./data"))
        self.db_path = data_path / "memory" / "eva_memory.db"
        self.db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        self._init_db()
        self._running = False
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ’¾ [LongTermMemory] Inicializado")
    
    def _init_db(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o banco de dados SQLite."""
        conn = sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        # Tabela de conversas
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS conversations (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                user_name TEXT,
                message TEXT NOT NULL,
                response TEXT,
                mode TEXT DEFAULT 'normal',
                timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
                metadata TEXT
            )
        """)
        
        # Tabela de conhecimentos extraÃ­dos
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS knowledge (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                user_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                key TEXT NOT NULL,
                value TEXT NOT NULL,
                source TEXT,
                confidence REAL DEFAULT 0.5,
                timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
                UNIQUE(user_id, key)
            )
        """)
        
        # Ãndices
        cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_id ON conversations(user_id)")
        cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_timestamp ON conversations(timestamp)")
        cursor.execute("CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_user_key ON knowledge(user_id, key)")
        
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
        self.logger.info("ðŸ’¾ [LongTermMemory] Banco de dados inicializado")
    
    async def store_conversation(self, user_id: str, user_name: str, 
                                  message: str, response: str, mode: str = "normal") -> None:
        """Armazena uma conversa."""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            cursor.execute("""
                INSERT INTO conversations (user_id, user_name, message, response, mode, timestamp)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (user_id, user_name, message, response, mode, datetime.now().isoformat()))
            
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao armazenar conversa: {e}")
    
    async def get_history(self, user_id: str, limit: int = 20) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m histÃ³rico de conversas de um usuÃ¡rio."""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM conversations
                WHERE user_id = ?
                ORDER BY timestamp DESC
                LIMIT ?
            """, (user_id, limit))
            
            results = [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
            conn.close()
            
            return results
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao buscar histÃ³rico: {e}")
            return []
    
    async def search_conversations(self, query: str, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Busca conversas por palavra-chave."""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM conversations
                WHERE message LIKE ? OR response LIKE ?
                ORDER BY timestamp DESC
                LIMIT ?
            """, (f"%{query}%", f"%{query}%", limit))
            
            results = [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
            conn.close()
            
            return results
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao buscar conversas: {e}")
            return []
    
    async def store_knowledge(self, user_id: str, key: str, value: str, 
                               source: str = "conversation", confidence: float = 0.5) -> None:
        """Armazena conhecimento extraÃ­do."""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            cursor.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO knowledge (user_id, key, value, source, confidence, timestamp)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (user_id, key, value, source, confidence, datetime.now().isoformat()))
            
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao armazenar conhecimento: {e}")
    
    async def get_knowledge(self, user_id: str, key: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m conhecimento de um usuÃ¡rio."""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT value FROM knowledge
                WHERE user_id = ? AND key = ?
            """, (user_id, key))
            
            result = cursor.fetchone()
            conn.close()
            
            return result[0] if result else None
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao buscar conhecimento: {e}")
            return None
    
    async def get_user_knowledge(self, user_id: str) -> Dict[str, str]:
        """ObtÃ©m todo o conhecimento de um usuÃ¡rio."""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            cursor.execute("""
                SELECT key, value FROM knowledge
                WHERE user_id = ?
            """, (user_id,))
            
            results = {row[0]: row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()}
            conn.close()
            
            return results
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao buscar conhecimento: {e}")
            return {}
    
    async def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m estatÃ­sticas da memÃ³ria."""
        try:
            conn = sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            
            cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM conversations")
            total_conversations = cursor.fetchone()[0]
            
            cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT user_id) FROM conversations")
            total_users = cursor.fetchone()[0]
            
            cursor.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM knowledge")
            total_knowledge = cursor.fetchone()[0]
            
            conn.close()
            
            return {
                "total_conversations": total_conversations,
                "total_users": total_users,
                "total_knowledge": total_knowledge
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao obter estatÃ­sticas: {e}")
            return {}
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self._running = True
        self.logger.info("ðŸ’¾ [LongTermMemory] Inicializado")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self._running = False
        self.logger.info("ðŸ’¾ [LongTermMemory] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        stats = await self.get_stats()
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "conversations": stats.get("total_conversations", 0),
            "users": stats.get("total_users", 0),
            "knowledge": stats.get("total_knowledge", 0)
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\memory\memory_consolidator.py ====================
"""Consolidador de memÃ³rias."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List
from datetime import datetime, timedelta


class MemoryConsolidator:
    """
    Consolida memÃ³rias de curto para longo prazo.
    
    CritÃ©rios de consolidaÃ§Ã£o:
    - ImportÃ¢ncia alta (>= 5)
    - Mensagens com muita informaÃ§Ã£o
    - PadrÃµes repetidos
    """
    
    def __init__(self, memory_manager):
        self.memory_manager = memory_manager
        self.logger = memory_manager.logger
    
    async def consolidate(self) -> int:
        """
        Consolida working memory para episodic memory.
        Retorna nÃºmero de itens consolidados.
        """
        working = self.memory_manager.working
        episodic = self.memory_manager.episodic
        
        # Pega itens importantes
        important_items = working.get_important(min_importance=5)
        
        # Pega itens com informaÃ§Ãµes longas
        long_items = [
            item for item in working._items
            if len(str(item.content)) > 200
        ]
        
        # Combina e remove duplicatas
        to_consolidate = []
        seen = set()
        for item in important_items + long_items:
            if id(item) not in seen:
                seen.add(id(item))
                to_consolidate.append(item)
        
        if not to_consolidate:
            return 0
        
        self.logger.info(f"Consolidando {len(to_consolidate)} itens...")
        
        consolidated = 0
        for item in to_consolidate:
            try:
                # Cria episÃ³dio
                episode_id = episodic.add(
                    title=f"Consolidado: {str(item.content)[:50]}...",
                    description=str(item.content),
                    participants=[item.source] if item.source != "system" else [],
                    tags=item.tags + ["consolidated"],
                    importance=min(10, item.importance + 1),
                    source=item.source
                )
                
                # Remove da working memory
                # (Nota: nÃ£o podemos remover facilmente de deque)
                # A working memory vai naturalmente descartar itens antigos
                
                consolidated += 1
                
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro ao consolidar item: {e}")
        
        self.logger.info(f"Consolidados {consolidated} itens")
        return consolidated


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\memory\memory_importance.py ====================
"""Calculadora de importÃ¢ncia de memÃ³rias."""

from typing import Dict, Any, List
from datetime import datetime, timedelta


class MemoryImportance:
    """
    Calcula a importÃ¢ncia de uma memÃ³ria.
    
    Fatores considerados:
    - FrequÃªncia de acesso
    - RecÃªncia
    - EmoÃ§Ã£o associada
    - Contexto (quem, onde, quando)
    - RelevÃ¢ncia para objetivos atuais
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._access_count: Dict[str, int] = {}
        self._last_access: Dict[str, datetime] = {}
    
    def calculate(self, memory: Dict[str, Any], context: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> int:
        """
        Calcula importÃ¢ncia (1-10).
        """
        importance = 5  # Valor base
        
        # 1. FrequÃªncia de acesso
        memory_id = memory.get("id", "")
        if memory_id in self._access_count:
            count = self._access_count[memory_id]
            if count > 10:
                importance += 2
            elif count > 5:
                importance += 1
        
        # 2. RecÃªncia
        if memory_id in self._last_access:
            days_ago = (datetime.now() - self._last_access[memory_id]).days
            if days_ago < 1:
                importance += 2
            elif days_ago < 7:
                importance += 1
            elif days_ago > 30:
                importance -= 1
        
        # 3. Tags (algumas tags indicam importÃ¢ncia)
        tags = memory.get("tags", [])
        importance_tags = ["importante", "urgente", "memorÃ¡vel", "especial"]
        for tag in tags:
            if tag.lower() in importance_tags:
                importance += 1
        
        # 4. Contexto (quem perguntou)
        if context:
            user = context.get("user", {}).get("current")
            if user == "alberto":  # Admin
                importance += 1
        
        # 5. Valor explÃ­cito
        explicit = memory.get("importance", 0)
        if explicit > 0:
            importance = max(importance, explicit)
        
        # Limita entre 1 e 10
        return max(1, min(10, importance))
    
    def record_access(self, memory_id: str) -> None:
        """Registra acesso a uma memÃ³ria."""
        if memory_id in self._access_count:
            self._access_count[memory_id] += 1
        else:
            self._access_count[memory_id] = 1
        self._last_access[memory_id] = datetime.now()


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\memory\memory_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de MemÃ³ria - FASE 2 do pipeline cognitivo."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
import json
from pathlib import Path

from src.core.event_types import EventType
from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus


class MemoryManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de MemÃ³ria - FASE 2 do pipeline cognitivo.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Armazenar histÃ³rico de conversas
    - Recuperar memÃ³rias relevantes
    - Manter preferÃªncias do usuÃ¡rio
    - Contexto de longo prazo
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        self._running = False
        
        # MemÃ³rias em memÃ³ria (futuramente: banco de dados)
        self._conversations: Dict[str, List[Dict]] = {}  # user_id -> [messages]
        self._preferences: Dict[str, Dict] = {}  # user_id -> preferences
        self._memory_limit = 100  # mensagens por usuÃ¡rio
        
        # Caminho para persistÃªncia
        data_path = Path(config.get("paths.data", "./data"))
        self._memory_path = data_path / "memory" / "conversations.json"
        self._preferences_path = data_path / "memory" / "preferences.json"
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [MemoryManager] Inicializado")
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa o MemoryManager."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [MemoryManager] Inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        
        # Carrega dados persistidos
        await self._load_memories()
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [MemoryManager] Inicializado")
    
    async def process(self, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Processa o contexto e recupera memÃ³rias relevantes.
        
        Args:
            context: Contexto da mensagem (do ContextManager)
            
        Returns:
            Dados de memÃ³ria recuperados
        """
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§  [MemoryManager] Processando...")
        
        user_id = context.get("user_id", "unknown")
        message = context.get("message", "")
        
        # 1. Busca histÃ³rico do usuÃ¡rio
        history = self._get_user_history(user_id, limit=10)
        
        # 2. Busca preferÃªncias
        preferences = self._get_user_preferences(user_id)
        
        # 3. Busca memÃ³rias relevantes Ã  mensagem
        relevant = self._search_relevant_memories(user_id, message)
        
        # 4. Registra nova mensagem no histÃ³rico
        self._add_to_history(user_id, message, role="user")
        
        memory_data = {
            "user_id": user_id,
            "history": history,
            "preferences": preferences,
            "relevant_memories": relevant,
            "history_count": len(history),
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§  [MemoryManager] {len(history)} histÃ³ricos, {len(relevant)} relevantes")
        
        # Publica evento
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            EventType.MEMORY_RETRIEVED,
            {
                "context": context,
                "memory": memory_data,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="memory_manager"
        )
        
        return memory_data
    
    def _get_user_history(self, user_id: str, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict]:
        """ObtÃ©m histÃ³rico do usuÃ¡rio."""
        history = self._conversations.get(user_id, [])
        return history[-limit:] if history else []
    
    def _get_user_preferences(self, user_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m preferÃªncias do usuÃ¡rio."""
        return self._preferences.get(user_id, {})
    
    def _search_relevant_memories(self, user_id: str, message: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """Busca memÃ³rias relevantes Ã  mensagem."""
        # Por enquanto, busca palavras-chave no histÃ³rico
        relevant = []
        history = self._conversations.get(user_id, [])
        
        if not history:
            return relevant
        
        # Palavras-chave simples
        keywords = message.lower().split()
        keywords = [k for k in keywords if len(k) > 3]
        
        for entry in history[-50:]:  # Ãšltimas 50 mensagens
            text = entry.get("content", "").lower()
            for keyword in keywords:
                if keyword in text:
                    relevant.append(entry)
                    break
        
        return relevant[-5:]  # Limita a 5 relevantes
    
    def _add_to_history(self, user_id: str, message: str, role: str = "user") -> None:
        """Adiciona mensagem ao histÃ³rico."""
        if user_id not in self._conversations:
            self._conversations[user_id] = []
        
        self._conversations[user_id].append({
            "role": role,
            "content": message,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
        
        # Limita histÃ³rico
        if len(self._conversations[user_id]) > self._memory_limit:
            self._conversations[user_id] = self._conversations[user_id][-self._memory_limit:]
    
    async def store_response(self, user_id: str, response: str) -> None:
        """Armazena resposta da EVA no histÃ³rico."""
        self._add_to_history(user_id, response, role="assistant")
        await self._save_memories()
    
    async def _load_memories(self) -> None:
        """Carrega memÃ³rias do disco."""
        try:
            if self._memory_path.exists():
                with open(self._memory_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    self._conversations = json.load(f)
                self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§  [MemoryManager] Carregadas {len(self._conversations)} conversas")
            
            if self._preferences_path.exists():
                with open(self._preferences_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    self._preferences = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(f"Erro ao carregar memÃ³rias: {e}")
    
    async def _save_memories(self) -> None:
        """Salva memÃ³rias no disco."""
        try:
            self._memory_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            
            with open(self._memory_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(self._conversations, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            
            with open(self._preferences_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(self._preferences, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
                
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao salvar memÃ³rias: {e}")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        """Desliga o MemoryManager."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [MemoryManager] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        await self._save_memories()
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [MemoryManager] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "conversations": len(self._conversations),
            "total_messages": sum(len(h) for h in self._conversations.values())
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\memory\memory_retriever.py ====================
"""Buscador de memÃ³rias."""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime


class MemoryRetriever:
    """
    Busca inteligente em todas as memÃ³rias.
    
    EstratÃ©gias de busca:
    - Busca por palavras-chave
    - Busca por relevÃ¢ncia (importÃ¢ncia)
    - Busca temporal (recentes primeiro)
    - Busca combinada
    """
    
    def __init__(self, memory_manager):
        self.memory_manager = memory_manager
        self.logger = memory_manager.logger
    
    async def search(self, query: str, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Busca em todas as camadas de memÃ³ria.
        Retorna resultados combinados.
        """
        results = []
        
        # 1. Busca na working memory
        working_results = self.memory_manager.working.search_by_content(query)
        for item in working_results[:5]:
            results.append({
                "source": "working",
                "content": item.content,
                "timestamp": item.timestamp.isoformat(),
                "importance": item.importance,
                "tags": item.tags
            })
        
        # 2. Busca na episodic memory
        episodic_results = self.memory_manager.episodic.search(query, limit=5)
        for episode in episodic_results:
            results.append({
                "source": "episodic",
                "id": episode.id,
                "title": episode.title,
                "description": episode.description,
                "timestamp": episode.timestamp.isoformat(),
                "importance": episode.importance,
                "tags": episode.tags
            })
        
        # 3. Busca na semantic memory
        semantic_results = self.memory_manager.semantic.search(query, limit=5)
        for node in semantic_results:
            results.append({
                "source": "semantic",
                "id": node.id,
                "concept": node.concept,
                "description": node.description,
                "confidence": node.confidence,
                "category": node.category
            })
        
        # 4. Busca na procedural memory
        procedural_results = self.memory_manager.procedural.search(query, limit=5)
        for procedure in procedural_results:
            results.append({
                "source": "procedural",
                "id": procedure.id,
                "name": procedure.name,
                "description": procedure.description,
                "steps": procedure.steps,
                "confidence": procedure.confidence
            })
        
        # Ordena por importÃ¢ncia
        results.sort(key=lambda x: x.get("importance", 0), reverse=True)
        
        return results[:limit]


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\memory\procedural_memory.py ====================
"""MemÃ³ria procedural - como fazer as coisas."""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from uuid import uuid4


@dataclass
class Procedure:
    """Um procedimento na memÃ³ria."""
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    name: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    steps: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    category: str = "general"
    confidence: float = 0.5
    source: str = "unknown"
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    success_count: int = 0
    failure_count: int = 0
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


class ProceduralMemory:
    """
    MemÃ³ria procedural - como fazer as coisas.
    
    CaracterÃ­sticas:
    - Armazena procedimentos e receitas
    - Passos sequenciais
    - AvaliaÃ§Ã£o de sucesso
    - Aprendizado contÃ­nuo
    """
    
    def __init__(self, storage_path: Path):
        self.storage_path = storage_path
        self.storage_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._procedures: Dict[str, Procedure] = {}
        self._load()
    
    def _load(self) -> None:
        """Carrega do disco."""
        pass
    
    def _save(self) -> None:
        """Salva no disco."""
        pass
    
    def add(self, name: str, description: str,
            steps: List[str],
            category: str = "general",
            confidence: float = 0.5,
            source: str = "unknown",
            tags: List[str] = None,
            metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> str:
        """Adiciona um procedimento."""
        procedure = Procedure(
            name=name,
            description=description,
            steps=steps,
            category=category,
            confidence=confidence,
            source=source,
            tags=tags or [],
            metadata=metadata or {}
        )
        self._procedures[procedure.id] = procedure
        self._save()
        return procedure.id
    
    def get(self, procedure_id: str) -> Optional[Procedure]:
        """ObtÃ©m um procedimento pelo ID."""
        return self._procedures.get(procedure_id)
    
    def get_by_name(self, name: str) -> Optional[Procedure]:
        """ObtÃ©m um procedimento pelo nome."""
        for procedure in self._procedures.values():
            if procedure.name.lower() == name.lower():
                return procedure
        return None
    
    def search(self, query: str, limit: int = 10) -> List[Procedure]:
        """Busca procedimentos."""
        results = []
        query_lower = query.lower()
        
        for procedure in self._procedures.values():
            if (query_lower in procedure.name.lower() or 
                query_lower in procedure.description.lower() or
                any(query_lower in step.lower() for step in procedure.steps)):
                results.append(procedure)
        
        return results[:limit]
    
    def get_by_category(self, category: str) -> List[Procedure]:
        """ObtÃ©m procedimentos por categoria."""
        return [
            procedure for procedure in self._procedures.values()
            if procedure.category == category
        ]
    
    def get_high_confidence(self, min_confidence: float = 0.7) -> List[Procedure]:
        """ObtÃ©m procedimentos com alta confianÃ§a."""
        return [
            procedure for procedure in self._procedures.values()
            if procedure.confidence >= min_confidence
        ]
    
    def record_success(self, procedure_id: str) -> bool:
        """Registra sucesso na execuÃ§Ã£o."""
        procedure = self._procedures.get(procedure_id)
        if procedure:
            procedure.success_count += 1
            # Aumenta confianÃ§a
            procedure.confidence = min(1.0, procedure.confidence + 0.05)
            procedure.updated_at = datetime.now()
            self._save()
            return True
        return False
    
    def record_failure(self, procedure_id: str) -> bool:
        """Registra falha na execuÃ§Ã£o."""
        procedure = self._procedures.get(procedure_id)
        if procedure:
            procedure.failure_count += 1
            # Diminui confianÃ§a
            procedure.confidence = max(0.1, procedure.confidence - 0.05)
            procedure.updated_at = datetime.now()
            self._save()
            return True
        return False
    
    def delete(self, procedure_id: str) -> bool:
        """Remove um procedimento."""
        if procedure_id in self._procedures:
            del self._procedures[procedure_id]
            self._save()
            return True
        return False
    
    def get_recent(self, n: int = 10) -> List[Procedure]:
        """ObtÃ©m procedimentos recentes."""
        sorted_procedures = sorted(
            self._procedures.values(),
            key=lambda x: x.created_at,
            reverse=True
        )
        return sorted_procedures[:n]


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\memory\semantic_memory.py ====================
"""MemÃ³ria semÃ¢ntica - conhecimento factual."""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from uuid import uuid4


@dataclass
class SemanticNode:
    """NÃ³ de conhecimento semÃ¢ntico."""
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    concept: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    category: str = "general"
    confidence: float = 0.5  # 0.0 a 1.0
    source: str = "unknown"
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    updated_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    related_concepts: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


class SemanticMemory:
    """
    MemÃ³ria semÃ¢ntica - conhecimento factual.
    
    CaracterÃ­sticas:
    - Armazena fatos e conceitos
    - Conhecimento estruturado
    - Relacionamentos entre conceitos
    - Grau de confianÃ§a
    """
    
    def __init__(self, storage_path: Path):
        self.storage_path = storage_path
        self.storage_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._nodes: Dict[str, SemanticNode] = {}
        self._index_by_concept: Dict[str, str] = {}  # conceito -> id
        self._load()
    
    def _load(self) -> None:
        """Carrega do disco."""
        pass
    
    def _save(self) -> None:
        """Salva no disco."""
        pass
    
    def add(self, concept: str, description: str, 
            category: str = "general",
            confidence: float = 0.5,
            source: str = "unknown",
            metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None,
            related_concepts: List[str] = None) -> str:
        """Adiciona um conceito."""
        node = SemanticNode(
            concept=concept,
            description=description,
            category=category,
            confidence=confidence,
            source=source,
            metadata=metadata or {},
            related_concepts=related_concepts or []
        )
        self._nodes[node.id] = node
        self._index_by_concept[concept.lower()] = node.id
        self._save()
        return node.id
    
    def get(self, node_id: str) -> Optional[SemanticNode]:
        """ObtÃ©m um nÃ³ pelo ID."""
        return self._nodes.get(node_id)
    
    def get_by_concept(self, concept: str) -> Optional[SemanticNode]:
        """ObtÃ©m um nÃ³ pelo conceito."""
        node_id = self._index_by_concept.get(concept.lower())
        if node_id:
            return self._nodes.get(node_id)
        return None
    
    def search(self, query: str, limit: int = 10) -> List[SemanticNode]:
        """Busca conceitos por texto."""
        results = []
        query_lower = query.lower()
        
        for node in self._nodes.values():
            if (query_lower in node.concept.lower() or 
                query_lower in node.description.lower()):
                results.append(node)
        
        return results[:limit]
    
    def get_by_category(self, category: str) -> List[SemanticNode]:
        """ObtÃ©m nÃ³s por categoria."""
        return [node for node in self._nodes.values() if node.category == category]
    
    def get_high_confidence(self, min_confidence: float = 0.7) -> List[SemanticNode]:
        """ObtÃ©m nÃ³s com alta confianÃ§a."""
        return [
            node for node in self._nodes.values()
            if node.confidence >= min_confidence
        ]
    
    def add_relation(self, concept1: str, concept2: str) -> bool:
        """Adiciona uma relaÃ§Ã£o entre conceitos."""
        node1 = self.get_by_concept(concept1)
        node2 = self.get_by_concept(concept2)
        
        if node1 and node2:
            if concept2 not in node1.related_concepts:
                node1.related_concepts.append(concept2)
            if concept1 not in node2.related_concepts:
                node2.related_concepts.append(concept1)
            self._save()
            return True
        return False
    
    def update_confidence(self, concept: str, new_confidence: float) -> bool:
        """Atualiza confianÃ§a de um conceito."""
        node = self.get_by_concept(concept)
        if node:
            node.confidence = max(0.0, min(1.0, new_confidence))
            node.updated_at = datetime.now()
            self._save()
            return True
        return False
    
    def get_recent(self, n: int = 10) -> List[SemanticNode]:
        """ObtÃ©m nÃ³s recentes."""
        sorted_nodes = sorted(
            self._nodes.values(),
            key=lambda x: x.created_at,
            reverse=True
        )
        return sorted_nodes[:n]


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\memory\working_memory.py ====================
"""MemÃ³ria de curto prazo - conversa atual."""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from collections import deque


@dataclass
class MemoryItem:
    """Item na memÃ³ria de trabalho."""
    content: Any
    timestamp: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    source: str = "system"
    importance: int = 1  # 1-10
    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


class WorkingMemory:
    """
    MemÃ³ria de curto prazo (Working Memory).
    
    CaracterÃ­sticas:
    - MantÃ©m contexto imediato
    - Capacidade limitada (100 itens por padrÃ£o)
    - Itens mais antigos sÃ£o descartados
    - Alta velocidade de acesso
    """
    
    def __init__(self, max_items: int = 100):
        self.max_items = max_items
        self._items: deque = deque(maxlen=max_items)
        self._current_context: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    
    def add(self, content: Any, source: str = "system", 
            importance: int = 1, tags: List[str] = None,
            metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> None:
        """Adiciona um item Ã  memÃ³ria de trabalho."""
        item = MemoryItem(
            content=content,
            source=source,
            importance=importance,
            tags=tags or [],
            metadata=metadata or {}
        )
        self._items.append(item)
    
    def get_recent(self, n: int = 10) -> List[MemoryItem]:
        """ObtÃ©m os N itens mais recentes."""
        return list(self._items)[-n:]
    
    def get_by_source(self, source: str) -> List[MemoryItem]:
        """ObtÃ©m itens de uma fonte especÃ­fica."""
        return [item for item in self._items if item.source == source]
    
    def search_by_tag(self, tag: str) -> List[MemoryItem]:
        """Busca itens por tag."""
        return [item for item in self._items if tag in item.tags]
    
    def search_by_content(self, query: str) -> List[MemoryItem]:
        """Busca itens por conteÃºdo."""
        query_lower = query.lower()
        return [
            item for item in self._items
            if query_lower in str(item.content).lower()
        ]
    
    def get_important(self, min_importance: int = 5) -> List[MemoryItem]:
        """ObtÃ©m itens com importÃ¢ncia acima do limite."""
        return [item for item in self._items if item.importance >= min_importance]
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Limpa a memÃ³ria de trabalho."""
        self._items.clear()
        self._current_context.clear()
    
    def set_context(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
        """Define um valor no contexto atual."""
        self._current_context[key] = value
    
    def get_context(self, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        """ObtÃ©m um valor do contexto atual."""
        return self._current_context.get(key, default)
    
    def get_all_context(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m todo o contexto atual."""
        return self._current_context.copy()
    
    def get_conversation_chain(self, n: int = 20) -> str:
        """Gera a cadeia de conversaÃ§Ã£o para contexto."""
        recent = self.get_recent(n)
        chain = []
        for item in recent:
            chain.append(f"[{item.source}] {item.content}")
        return "\n".join(chain)
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m estatÃ­sticas da working memory."""
        return {
            "total_items": len(self._items),
            "max_items": self.max_items,
            "sources": list(set(item.source for item in self._items)),
            "tags": list(set(tag for item in self._items for tag in item.tags))
        }

"""Gerenciador de NotÃ­cias da EVA."""

import asyncio
import json
import re  # <-- ADICIONADO
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
import aiohttp

from src.kernel.events import EventBus
from src.core.event_types import EventType

try:
    import feedparser
    FEEDPARSER_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    FEEDPARSER_AVAILABLE = False
    feedparser = None


class NewsManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de NotÃ­cias da EVA.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self._running = False
        self._llm_manager = None
        
        self._categories = {
            "mundo": ["world", "global", "international", "onu", "guerra"],
            "brasil": ["brasil", "polÃ­tica", "congresso", "governo"],
            "campinas": ["campinas", "regiÃ£o", "metropolitana", "sumarÃ©"],
            "tecnologia": ["tech", "technology", "ia", "programaÃ§Ã£o", "software"],
            "economia": ["economy", "finance", "negÃ³cios", "mercado", "dÃ³lar"],
            "esportes": ["sports", "futebol", "olÃ­mpico", "campeonato"],
            "ciencia": ["science", "research", "descoberta", "nasa", "espacial"],
            "cultura": ["culture", "arts", "entretenimento", "cinema", "mÃºsica"],
            "trabalhista": ["trabalhista", "trabalho", "lei", "direitos", "clt"]
        }
        
        # Fontes RSS em portuguÃªs
        self._rss_feeds = {
            "mundo": "https://www.bbc.com/portuguese/index.xml",
            "brasil": "https://g1.globo.com/rss/g1/",
            "tecnologia": "https://canaltech.com.br/feed/",
            "ciencia": "https://revistagalileu.globo.com/rss/",
            "economia": "https://www.infomoney.com.br/feed/",
            "esportes": "https://globoesporte.globo.com/rss/",
            "cultura": "https://www1.folha.uol.com.br/rss/ilustrada.xml",
            "campinas": "https://correio.rac.com.br/feed/"
        }
        
        self._user_preferences: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
        self._last_briefing: Dict[str, str] = {}
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“° [NewsManager] Inicializado")
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“° [NewsManager] Inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        await self._load_preferences()
        
        if not FEEDPARSER_AVAILABLE:
            self.logger.warning("ðŸ“° [NewsManager] feedparser nÃ£o disponÃ­vel. Instale: pip install feedparser")
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“° [NewsManager] Inicializado")
    
    async def get_news(self, user_id: str, categories: List[str] = None, limit: int = 3) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“° [NewsManager] Buscando notÃ­cias para {user_id}")
        
        if not categories:
            categories = self._user_preferences.get(user_id, ["mundo", "brasil", "tecnologia"])
        
        result = {}
        
        for category in categories:
            if category in self._categories:
                news = await self._fetch_category(category, limit)
                if news:
                    result[category] = news
        
        return result
    
    async def _fetch_category(self, category: str, limit: int = 3) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        news = await self._fetch_from_api(category, limit)
        
        if not news and FEEDPARSER_AVAILABLE:
            news = await self._fetch_from_rss(category, limit)
        
        if not news:
            news = self._get_fallback_news(category, limit)
        
        return news
    
    async def _fetch_from_api(self, category: str, limit: int = 3) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        try:
            api_key = self.config.get("news.api_key", "")
            if not api_key:
                return []
            
            keywords = self._categories.get(category, [category])
            query = " OR ".join(keywords[:3])
            
            url = "https://newsapi.org/v2/everything"
            params = {
                "q": query,
                "language": "pt",
                "pageSize": limit,
                "apiKey": api_key
            }
            
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
                async with session.get(url, params=params, timeout=10) as response:
                    if response.status == 200:
                        data = await response.json()
                        articles = data.get("articles", [])
                        
                        return [{
                            "title": self._clean_text(a.get("title", "Sem tÃ­tulo")),
                            "description": self._clean_text(a.get("description", "")),
                            "content": self._clean_text(a.get("content", "")),
                            "source": a.get("source", {}).get("name", "Desconhecido"),
                            "url": a.get("url", ""),
                            "published": a.get("publishedAt", ""),
                            "category": category
                        } for a in articles if a.get("title")]
        
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro na API: {e}")
        
        return []
    
    async def _fetch_from_rss(self, category: str, limit: int = 3) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        if not FEEDPARSER_AVAILABLE:
            return []
        
        feed_url = self._rss_feeds.get(category)
        if not feed_url:
            return []
        
        try:
            feed = feedparser.parse(feed_url)
            articles = feed.entries[:limit]
            
            return [{
                "title": self._clean_text(a.get("title", "Sem tÃ­tulo")),
                "description": self._clean_text(a.get("description", "")),
                "content": self._clean_text(a.get("summary", "")),
                "source": a.get("source", {}).get("title", "RSS"),
                "url": a.get("link", ""),
                "published": a.get("published", ""),
                "category": category
            } for a in articles if a.get("title")]
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro no RSS: {e}")
            return []
    
    def _clean_text(self, text: str) -> str:
        """Limpa o texto removendo caracteres especiais e quebras."""
        if not text:
            return ""
        
        # Remove HTML tags
        text = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '', text)
        
        # Remove mÃºltiplos espaÃ§os e quebras
        text = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', text)
        
        # Remove caracteres especiais que atrapalham a fala
        text = re.sub(r'[=]{3,}', '', text)  # Remove =====
        text = re.sub(r'[_-]{3,}', '', text)  # Remove ___ ou ---
        text = re.sub(r'[""``\']', '', text)  # Remove aspas
        
        # Remove URLs
        text = re.sub(r'http[s]?://\S+', '', text)
        
        return text.strip()
    
    def _get_fallback_news(self, category: str, limit: int = 3) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        import random
        
        fallback_titles = {
            "mundo": ["ONU discute novas metas climÃ¡ticas", "Conflito no Oriente MÃ©dio se intensifica", "G20 anuncia pacote econÃ´mico"],
            "brasil": ["Congresso aprova nova lei", "InflaÃ§Ã£o tem leve queda", "Governo anuncia programa social"],
            "tecnologia": ["Nova IA revoluciona mercado", "Apple lanÃ§a novo dispositivo", "CiberseguranÃ§a em alta"],
            "campinas": ["Nova obra na regiÃ£o", "Campinas recebe evento internacional", "Prefeitura anuncia projeto"],
            "economia": ["DÃ³lar fecha em baixa", "Bolsa de Valores sobe", "Juros mantidos"],
            "esportes": ["Brasil vence amistoso", "Campeonato tem novo lÃ­der", "Atleta brasileiro quebra recorde"],
            "ciencia": ["Cientistas descobrem novo planeta", "Vacina contra cÃ¢ncer avanÃ§a", "Buraco negro Ã© fotografado"],
            "cultura": ["Novo filme bate recorde", "Museu recebe exposiÃ§Ã£o", "Livro ganha prÃªmio internacional"],
            "trabalhista": ["Nova regra trabalhista Ã© aprovada", "SalÃ¡rio mÃ­nimo tem reajuste", "Empresas adotam home office"]
        }
        
        titles = fallback_titles.get(category, ["NotÃ­cia importante", "AtualizaÃ§Ã£o do dia"])
        
        news = []
        for i in range(min(limit, len(titles))):
            news.append({
                "title": self._clean_text(titles[i] if i < len(titles) else f"NotÃ­cia {i+1}"),
                "description": self._clean_text(f"Resumo da notÃ­cia sobre {category} - atualizaÃ§Ã£o importante."),
                "content": self._clean_text(f"ConteÃºdo completo da notÃ­cia sobre {category}."),
                "source": "Fonte simulada",
                "url": "#",
                "published": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "category": category,
                "fallback": True
            })
        
        return news
    
    def _format_briefing(self, news: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """Formata o briefing sem caracteres especiais."""
        parts = []
        parts.append(f"ðŸ“° BRIEFING DO DIA {datetime.now().strftime('%d/%m/%Y')}")
        parts.append("")
        
        total_news = 0
        for category, articles in news.items():
            if articles:
                parts.append(f"ðŸ“Œ {category.upper()}:")
                for i, article in enumerate(articles[:3], 1):
                    title = article.get('title', 'Sem tÃ­tulo')
                    desc = article.get('description', '')[:150]
                    parts.append(f"  {i}. {title}")
                    if desc:
                        parts.append(f"     {desc}")
                    parts.append("")
                    total_news += 1
        
        if total_news == 0:
            parts.append("âš ï¸ NÃ£o encontrei notÃ­cias no momento.")
        
        parts.append("ðŸ’¡ Diga 'mais detalhes' para aprofundar.")
        
        return "\n".join(parts)
    
    async def daily_briefing(self, user_id: str, categories: List[str] = None) -> str:
        if not categories:
            categories = self._user_preferences.get(user_id, ["mundo", "brasil", "tecnologia"])
        
        news = await self.get_news(user_id, categories)
        
        # Formata sem caracteres especiais
        result = self._format_briefing(news)
        self._last_briefing[user_id] = result
        
        return result
    
    async def set_user_preferences(self, user_id: str, categories: List[str]) -> None:
        valid_categories = list(self._categories.keys())
        valid = [c for c in categories if c in valid_categories]
        
        if not valid:
            valid = ["mundo", "brasil", "tecnologia"]
        
        self._user_preferences[user_id] = valid
        await self._save_preferences()
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“° PreferÃªncias de {user_id}: {valid}")
    
    async def _load_preferences(self) -> None:
        try:
            data_path = Path(self.config.get("paths.data", "./data"))
            prefs_path = data_path / "news" / "preferences.json"
            
            if prefs_path.exists():
                with open(prefs_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    self._user_preferences = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(f"Erro ao carregar preferÃªncias: {e}")
    
    async def _save_preferences(self) -> None:
        try:
            data_path = Path(self.config.get("paths.data", "./data"))
            prefs_path = data_path / "news" / "preferences.json"
            prefs_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            
            with open(prefs_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(self._user_preferences, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao salvar preferÃªncias: {e}")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“° [NewsManager] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        await self._save_preferences()
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“° [NewsManager] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "categories": len(self._categories),
            "users_preferences": len(self._user_preferences),
            "feedparser_available": FEEDPARSER_AVAILABLE
        }

==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\planner\decomposer.py ====================
"""Decompositor de objetivos em tarefas."""

from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime

from src.planner.goal import Goal, GoalType
from src.planner.task import Task, TaskPriority


class GoalDecomposer:
    """
    DecompÃµe objetivos em tarefas atÃ´micas.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, planner_manager):
        self.planner_manager = planner_manager
        self.logger = planner_manager.logger
    
    async def decompose(self, goal: Goal) -> List[Task]:
        """
        DecompÃµe um objetivo em tarefas.
        
        EstratÃ©gias por tipo de objetivo:
        - ONESHOT: Tarefa Ãºnica
        - RECURRING: Tarefa com agendamento
        - EMERGENCY: Tarefa de alta prioridade
        - LEARNING: Tarefas de estudo/pesquisa
        """
        if goal.goal_type == GoalType.ONESHOT:
            return await self._decompose_oneshot(goal)
        elif goal.goal_type == GoalType.RECURRING:
            return await self._decompose_recurring(goal)
        elif goal.goal_type == GoalType.EMERGENCY:
            return await self._decompose_emergency(goal)
        elif goal.goal_type == GoalType.LEARNING:
            return await self._decompose_learning(goal)
        elif goal.goal_type == GoalType.CONTINUOUS:
            return await self._decompose_continuous(goal)
        elif goal.goal_type == GoalType.MAINTENANCE:
            return await self._decompose_maintenance(goal)
        elif goal.goal_type == GoalType.EVOLUTION:
            return await self._decompose_evolution(goal)
        else:
            return await self._decompose_generic(goal)
    
    async def _decompose_oneshot(self, goal: Goal) -> List[Task]:
        """DecompÃµe objetivo Ãºnico."""
        # Tenta identificar se hÃ¡ subtarefas no contexto
        subtasks = goal.context.get("subtasks", [])
        
        if subtasks:
            tasks = []
            for i, subtask in enumerate(subtasks):
                task = Task(
                    goal_id=goal.id,
                    name=subtask.get("name", f"Subtarefa {i+1}"),
                    description=subtask.get("description", ""),
                    priority=TaskPriority.NORMAL,
                    agent_name=subtask.get("agent"),
                    tool_name=subtask.get("tool"),
                    tool_params=subtask.get("params", {}),
                    dependencies=subtask.get("dependencies", []),
                    context=subtask.get("context", {})
                )
                tasks.append(task)
            return tasks
        
        # Tarefa Ãºnica
        task = Task(
            goal_id=goal.id,
            name=goal.title,
            description=goal.description,
            priority=TaskPriority.NORMAL,
            context=goal.context.copy()
        )
        return [task]
    
    async def _decompose_recurring(self, goal: Goal) -> List[Task]:
        """DecompÃµe objetivo recorrente."""
        schedule = goal.context.get("schedule", {})
        
        task = Task(
            goal_id=goal.id,
            name=goal.title,
            description=goal.description,
            priority=TaskPriority.NORMAL,
            context={
                **goal.context,
                "schedule": schedule
            }
        )
        return [task]
    
    async def _decompose_emergency(self, goal: Goal) -> List[Task]:
        """DecompÃµe objetivo de emergÃªncia."""
        task = Task(
            goal_id=goal.id,
            name=f"URGENTE: {goal.title}",
            description=goal.description,
            priority=TaskPriority.CRITICAL,
            context=goal.context.copy()
        )
        return [task]
    
    async def _decompose_learning(self, goal: Goal) -> List[Task]:
        """DecompÃµe objetivo de aprendizado."""
        topics = goal.context.get("topics", [])
        tasks = []
        
        for topic in topics:
            task = Task(
                goal_id=goal.id,
                name=f"Aprender: {topic}",
                description=f"Estudar e aprender sobre {topic}",
                priority=TaskPriority.LOW,
                context={"topic": topic}
            )
            tasks.append(task)
        
        return tasks
    
    async def _decompose_continuous(self, goal: Goal) -> List[Task]:
        """DecompÃµe objetivo contÃ­nuo."""
        task = Task(
            goal_id=goal.id,
            name=f"Monitorar: {goal.title}",
            description=goal.description,
            priority=TaskPriority.BACKGROUND,
            context=goal.context.copy()
        )
        return [task]
    
    async def _decompose_maintenance(self, goal: Goal) -> List[Task]:
        """DecompÃµe objetivo de manutenÃ§Ã£o."""
        task = Task(
            goal_id=goal.id,
            name=f"ManutenÃ§Ã£o: {goal.title}",
            description=goal.description,
            priority=TaskPriority.LOW,
            context=goal.context.copy()
        )
        return [task]
    
    async def _decompose_evolution(self, goal: Goal) -> List[Task]:
        """DecompÃµe objetivo de evoluÃ§Ã£o."""
        components = goal.context.get("components", [])
        
        if components:
            tasks = []
            for component in components:
                task = Task(
                    goal_id=goal.id,
                    name=f"Evoluir: {component}",
                    description=f"Atualizar e melhorar {component}",
                    priority=TaskPriority.LOW,
                    context={"component": component}
                )
                tasks.append(task)
            return tasks
        
        task = Task(
            goal_id=goal.id,
            name=f"Evoluir: {goal.title}",
            description=goal.description,
            priority=TaskPriority.LOW,
            context=goal.context.copy()
        )
        return [task]
    
    async def _decompose_generic(self, goal: Goal) -> List[Task]:
        """DecompÃµe objetivo genÃ©rico."""
        task = Task(
            goal_id=goal.id,
            name=goal.title,
            description=goal.description,
            priority=TaskPriority.NORMAL,
            context=goal.context.copy()
        )
        return [task]


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\planner\dependency_resolver.py ====================
"""Resolvedor de dependÃªncias entre tarefas."""

from typing import List, Dict, Set, Optional
from collections import defaultdict

from src.planner.task import Task


class DependencyResolver:
    """
    Resolve dependÃªncias entre tarefas.
    Implementa ordenaÃ§Ã£o topolÃ³gica.
    """
    
    def resolve(self, tasks: List[Task]) -> List[Task]:
        """
        Resolve dependÃªncias entre tarefas.
        Retorna tarefas com dependÃªncias resolvidas (ordenadas).
        """
        if not tasks:
            return tasks
        
        # Cria grafo de dependÃªncias
        graph = defaultdict(set)
        in_degree = defaultdict(int)
        
        for task in tasks:
            graph[task.id] = set()
            in_degree[task.id] = 0
        
        for task in tasks:
            for dep_id in task.dependencies:
                if dep_id in graph:
                    graph[dep_id].add(task.id)
                    in_degree[task.id] += 1
        
        # OrdenaÃ§Ã£o topolÃ³gica (Kahn)
        queue = [tid for tid, deg in in_degree.items() if deg == 0]
        result = []
        
        while queue:
            task_id = queue.pop(0)
            result.append(task_id)
            
            for neighbor in graph[task_id]:
                in_degree[neighbor] -= 1
                if in_degree[neighbor] == 0:
                    queue.append(neighbor)
        
        # Verifica ciclos
        if len(result) != len(tasks):
            # HÃ¡ ciclo - tenta resolver
            unresolved = set(t.id for t in tasks) - set(result)
            self.logger.warning(f"Ciclo de dependÃªncia detectado: {unresolved}")
            
            # Adiciona os nÃ£o resolvidos no final
            result.extend(unresolved)
        
        # Reordena tarefas
        task_map = {t.id: t for t in tasks}
        return [task_map[tid] for tid in result if tid in task_map]
    
    def detect_cycles(self, tasks: List[Task]) -> List[List[str]]:
        """
        Detecta ciclos no grafo de dependÃªncias.
        Retorna lista de ciclos encontrados.
        """
        graph = defaultdict(set)
        for task in tasks:
            for dep_id in task.dependencies:
                if dep_id in {t.id for t in tasks}:
                    graph[task.id].add(dep_id)
        
        visited = set()
        rec_stack = set()
        cycles = []
        
        def dfs(node_id: str, path: List[str]) -> None:
            visited.add(node_id)
            rec_stack.add(node_id)
            path.append(node_id)
            
            for neighbor in graph[node_id]:
                if neighbor not in visited:
                    dfs(neighbor, path.copy())
                elif neighbor in rec_stack:
                    # Ciclo encontrado
                    cycle_start = path.index(neighbor)
                    cycles.append(path[cycle_start:] + [neighbor])
            
            rec_stack.remove(node_id)
        
        for task in tasks:
            if task.id not in visited:
                dfs(task.id, [])
        
        return cycles


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\planner\execution_plan.py ====================
"""Plano de execuÃ§Ã£o do Planner."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any
from uuid import uuid4

from src.planner.goal import Goal
from src.planner.task import Task, TaskStatus


class PlanStatus(Enum):
    """Status de um plano."""
    DRAFT = "draft"
    APPROVED = "approved"
    EXECUTING = "executing"
    PAUSED = "paused"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    FAILED = "failed"
    CANCELLED = "cancelled"


@dataclass
class ExecutionPlan:
    """Plano completo de execuÃ§Ã£o."""
    
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    name: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    goal_id: str = ""
    
    status: PlanStatus = PlanStatus.DRAFT
    
    # Hierarquia
    tasks: List[Task] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    # Ordem de execuÃ§Ã£o (topolÃ³gica)
    execution_order: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    # Timeline
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    approved_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    started_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    completed_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    
    # Prioridade geral do plano
    priority: int = 5
    
    # Contexto do plano
    context: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    
    # Resultado
    result: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    
    # Progresso (0.0 a 1.0)
    progress: float = 0.0
    
    def get_pending_tasks(self) -> List[Task]:
        """ObtÃ©m todas as tarefas pendentes."""
        return [t for t in self.tasks if t.status == TaskStatus.PENDING]
    
    def get_ready_tasks(self) -> List[Task]:
        """ObtÃ©m tarefas prontas para execuÃ§Ã£o."""
        ready = []
        for task in self.tasks:
            if not task.can_execute():
                continue
            
            # Verifica dependÃªncias
            deps_ok = True
            for dep_id in task.dependencies:
                dep = self._find_task(dep_id)
                if dep and not dep.is_done():
                    deps_ok = False
                    break
            
            if deps_ok:
                ready.append(task)
        
        return ready
    
    def _find_task(self, task_id: str) -> Optional[Task]:
        """Encontra uma task pelo ID."""
        for task in self.tasks:
            if task.id == task_id:
                return task
        return None
    
    def update_progress(self) -> float:
        """Atualiza e retorna o progresso do plano."""
        if not self.tasks:
            return 0.0
        
        done = sum(1 for t in self.tasks if t.is_done())
        in_progress = sum(1 for t in self.tasks if t.status == TaskStatus.IN_PROGRESS)
        
        # Peso: completas = 1, em progresso = 0.5
        self.progress = (done + (in_progress * 0.5)) / len(self.tasks)
        return self.progress
    
    def is_complete(self) -> bool:
        """Verifica se todas as tarefas foram concluÃ­das."""
        return all(t.is_done() for t in self.tasks)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Converte para dicionÃ¡rio."""
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "name": self.name,
            "description": self.description,
            "goal_id": self.goal_id,
            "status": self.status.value,
            "task_count": len(self.tasks),
            "execution_order": self.execution_order,
            "started_at": self.started_at.isoformat() if self.started_at else None,
            "completed_at": self.completed_at.isoformat() if self.completed_at else None,
            "priority": self.priority,
            "progress": self.progress,
            "result": self.result
        }

"""DefiniÃ§Ã£o de objetivos do Planner."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any
from uuid import uuid4


class GoalStatus(Enum):
    """Status de um objetivo."""
    DRAFT = "draft"
    APPROVED = "approved"
    PLANNING = "planning"
    IN_PROGRESS = "in_progress"
    BLOCKED = "blocked"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    CANCELLED = "cancelled"
    FAILED = "failed"


class GoalType(Enum):
    """Tipo de objetivo."""
    ONESHOT = "oneshot"          # Ãšnica execuÃ§Ã£o
    RECURRING = "recurring"      # Recorrente
    CONTINUOUS = "continuous"    # ContÃ­nuo (monitoramento)
    EMERGENCY = "emergency"      # EmergÃªncia
    MAINTENANCE = "maintenance"  # ManutenÃ§Ã£o
    LEARNING = "learning"        # Aprendizado
    EVOLUTION = "evolution"      # Auto-evoluÃ§Ã£o


@dataclass
class Goal:
    """Um objetivo que o Planner deve realizar."""
    
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    title: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    goal_type: GoalType = GoalType.ONESHOT
    status: GoalStatus = GoalStatus.DRAFT
    
    # Prioridade: 0 (crÃ­tico) a 10 (baixo)
    priority: int = 5
    
    # Quem criou
    created_by: str = "system"
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    
    # Metadados
    deadline: Optional[datetime] = None
    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    dependencies: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # IDs de outros goals
    parent_goal: Optional[str] = None
    
    # Contexto
    context: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    
    # Resultado
    result: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None
    completed_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    
    # Progresso (0.0 a 1.0)
    progress: float = 0.0
    
    def is_complete(self) -> bool:
        return self.status == GoalStatus.COMPLETED
    
    def is_active(self) -> bool:
        return self.status in [GoalStatus.APPROVED, GoalStatus.PLANNING, GoalStatus.IN_PROGRESS]
    
    def can_start(self) -> bool:
        return self.status == GoalStatus.APPROVED and self.progress == 0.0
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Converte para dicionÃ¡rio."""
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "title": self.title,
            "description": self.description,
            "goal_type": self.goal_type.value,
            "status": self.status.value,
            "priority": self.priority,
            "created_by": self.created_by,
            "created_at": self.created_at.isoformat(),
            "deadline": self.deadline.isoformat() if self.deadline else None,
            "tags": self.tags,
            "dependencies": self.dependencies,
            "parent_goal": self.parent_goal,
            "progress": self.progress,
            "result": self.result
        }

"""Gerenciador de Planejamento - FASE 4 com Sistema de ConfianÃ§a."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
from src.core.event_types import EventType
from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus


class PlannerManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de Planejamento - com Sistema de ConfianÃ§a.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Decidir o que fazer com a mensagem
    - Criar um plano de aÃ§Ã£o
    - Avaliar confianÃ§a do conhecimento
    - Definir estratÃ©gia baseada em confianÃ§a
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        self._running = False
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“‹ [PlannerManager] Inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“‹ [PlannerManager] Inicializado")
    
    async def process(self, context: Dict, memory: Dict, knowledge: Dict) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Cria um plano baseado no contexto, memÃ³ria e conhecimento."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“‹ [PlannerManager] Processando...")
        
        intent = context.get("intent", "unknown")
        query_type = context.get("query_type", "general")
        
        # ===== SISTEMA DE CONFIANÃ‡A =====
        confidence = self._calculate_confidence(knowledge, memory, context)
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“‹ [PlannerManager] ConfianÃ§a: {confidence}%")
        
        # ===== ESTRATÃ‰GIA BASEADA EM CONFIANÃ‡A =====
        if confidence >= 80:
            strategy = "answer_with_confidence"
        elif confidence >= 50:
            strategy = "answer_with_context"
        elif confidence >= 20:
            strategy = "ask_clarification"
        else:
            strategy = "ask_question"
        
        # ===== PLANO =====
        plan = await self._create_plan(intent, query_type, confidence, strategy)
        
        plan_data = {
            "plan": plan,
            "strategy": strategy,
            "confidence": confidence,
            "requires_llm": plan.get("requires_llm", True),
            "requires_action": plan.get("requires_action", False),
            "priority": plan.get("priority", "normal"),
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“‹ [PlannerManager] Plano: {plan.get('name')} (confianÃ§a: {confidence}%)")
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            EventType.PLAN_READY,
            {
                "context": context,
                "memory": memory,
                "knowledge": knowledge,
                "plan": plan_data,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="planner_manager"
        )
        
        return plan_data
    
    def _calculate_confidence(self, knowledge: Dict, memory: Dict, context: Dict) -> int:
        """Calcula confianÃ§a baseada no conhecimento disponÃ­vel."""
        confidence = 0
        
        # 1. Conhecimento direto (peso 50%)
        facts = knowledge.get("facts", [])
        if facts:
            confidence += min(len(facts) * 15, 50)
        
        # 2. MemÃ³ria contextual (peso 30%)
        history_count = memory.get("history_count", 0)
        if history_count > 0:
            confidence += min(history_count * 5, 30)
        
        # 3. Entidades identificadas (peso 20%)
        entities = context.get("entities", {})
        if entities:
            confidence += min(len(entities) * 10, 20)
        
        # Garante que fique entre 0 e 100
        return min(max(confidence, 0), 100)
    
    async def _create_plan(self, intent: str, query_type: str, confidence: int, strategy: str) -> Dict:
        """Cria o plano baseado na estratÃ©gia."""
        
        # Planos base por tipo de pergunta
        plans = {
            "direct_question": {
                "name": "answer_question",
                "requires_llm": True,
                "requires_action": False,
                "priority": "high",
                "steps": [
                    "Buscar conhecimento especÃ­fico",
                    "Verificar memÃ³ria de contexto",
                    "Gerar resposta direta"
                ],
                "expected_output": "resposta informativa"
            },
            "direct_command": {
                "name": "execute_command",
                "requires_llm": True,
                "requires_action": True,
                "priority": "high",
                "steps": [
                    "Validar comando",
                    "Executar aÃ§Ã£o",
                    "Confirmar execuÃ§Ã£o"
                ],
                "expected_output": "confirmaÃ§Ã£o de execuÃ§Ã£o"
            },
            "planning_request": {
                "name": "create_plan",
                "requires_llm": True,
                "requires_action": False,
                "priority": "medium",
                "steps": [
                    "Analisar objetivo",
                    "Criar plano de aÃ§Ã£o",
                    "Apresentar plano"
                ],
                "expected_output": "plano detalhado"
            },
            "learning_request": {
                "name": "teach_topic",
                "requires_llm": True,
                "requires_action": False,
                "priority": "medium",
                "steps": [
                    "Identificar tÃ³pico",
                    "Buscar conhecimento",
                    "Estruturar explicaÃ§Ã£o"
                ],
                "expected_output": "explicaÃ§Ã£o educativa"
            },
            "general": {
                "name": "general_conversation",
                "requires_llm": True,
                "requires_action": False,
                "priority": "low",
                "steps": [
                    "Analisar mensagem",
                    "Manter conversa natural"
                ],
                "expected_output": "resposta conversacional"
            }
        }
        
        # Seleciona plano base
        base_plan = plans.get(query_type, plans["general"])
        
        # Ajusta com base na confianÃ§a
        if confidence >= 80:
            base_plan["steps"].append("âœ… Alta confianÃ§a - responder diretamente")
        elif confidence >= 50:
            base_plan["steps"].append("âš ï¸ ConfianÃ§a mÃ©dia - incluir contexto")
        elif confidence >= 20:
            base_plan["steps"].append("â“ Baixa confianÃ§a - pedir clarificaÃ§Ã£o")
        else:
            base_plan["steps"].append("ðŸ”„ Sem conhecimento - fazer pergunta")
        
        return base_plan
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“‹ [PlannerManager] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“‹ [PlannerManager] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {"status": "running" if self._running else "stopped"}
