"""Executor de planos."""

from typing import List, Optional
from datetime import datetime

from src.planner.execution_plan import ExecutionPlan, PlanStatus
from src.planner.task import Task, TaskStatus


class PlanExecutor:
    """
    Executa planos de forma coordenada.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, planner_manager):
        self.planner_manager = planner_manager
        self.logger = planner_manager.logger
        
        # Controle de execuÃ§Ã£o
        self._executing_plans: dict = {}  # plan_id -> set de task_ids em execuÃ§Ã£o
        self._paused_plans: set = set()
    
    async def execute(self, plan: ExecutionPlan) -> bool:
        """
        Inicia a execuÃ§Ã£o de um plano.
        """
        if plan.status == PlanStatus.EXECUTING:
            return False
        
        # Inicializa controle
        self._executing_plans[plan.id] = set()
        plan.status = PlanStatus.EXECUTING
        plan.started_at = datetime.now()
        
        # Inicia tarefas prontas
        await self._execute_ready_tasks(plan)
        
        return True
    
    async def pause(self, plan: ExecutionPlan) -> bool:
        """
        Pausa a execuÃ§Ã£o de um plano.
        """
        if plan.status != PlanStatus.EXECUTING:
            return False
        
        self._paused_plans.add(plan.id)
        plan.status = PlanStatus.PAUSED
        
        # Pausa tarefas em execuÃ§Ã£o (serÃ£o retomadas)
        self.logger.info(f"Plano pausado: {plan.id[:8]}")
        return True
    
    async def resume(self, plan: ExecutionPlan) -> bool:
        """
        Retoma a execuÃ§Ã£o de um plano.
        """
        if plan.status != PlanStatus.PAUSED:
            return False
        
        self._paused_plans.discard(plan.id)
        plan.status = PlanStatus.EXECUTING
        
        # Continua execuÃ§Ã£o
        await self._execute_ready_tasks(plan)
        
        self.logger.info(f"Plano retomado: {plan.id[:8]}")
        return True
    
    async def cancel(self, plan: ExecutionPlan) -> bool:
        """
        Cancela a execuÃ§Ã£o de um plano.
        """
        if plan.id in self._executing_plans:
            del self._executing_plans[plan.id]
        
        self._paused_plans.discard(plan.id)
        plan.status = PlanStatus.CANCELLED
        
        # Cancela tarefas pendentes
        for task in plan.tasks:
            if task.status in [TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.SCHEDULED]:
                task.status = TaskStatus.CANCELLED
        
        self.logger.info(f"Plano cancelado: {plan.id[:8]}")
        return True
    
    async def continue_execution(self, plan: ExecutionPlan) -> None:
        """
        Continua a execuÃ§Ã£o de um plano apÃ³s uma tarefa ser concluÃ­da.
        """
        if plan.id in self._paused_plans:
            return
        
        if plan.status != PlanStatus.EXECUTING:
            return
        
        await self._execute_ready_tasks(plan)
    
    async def retry_task(self, plan: ExecutionPlan, task: Task) -> None:
        """
        Tenta executar uma tarefa novamente.
        """
        self.logger.info(f"Tentando novamente task {task.id[:8]} (tentativa {task.retry_count})")
        
        # Aguarda o delay
        import asyncio
        await asyncio.sleep(task.retry_delay)
        
        # Reagenda
        task.status = TaskStatus.PENDING
        await self._execute_task(plan, task)
    
    async def _execute_ready_tasks(self, plan: ExecutionPlan) -> None:
        """
        Executa todas as tarefas prontas do plano.
        """
        if plan.id in self._paused_plans:
            return
        
        ready_tasks = plan.get_ready_tasks()
        
        if not ready_tasks:
            # Verifica se hÃ¡ tarefas em andamento
            active = self._executing_plans.get(plan.id, set())
            if not active:
                # Nenhuma tarefa em andamento e nenhuma pronta
                # Verifica se o plano estÃ¡ completo
                if plan.is_complete():
                    plan.status = PlanStatus.COMPLETED
                    plan.completed_at = datetime.now()
                    self.logger.info(f"Plano concluÃ­do: {plan.id[:8]}")
                else:
                    # Pode haver deadlock ou erro
                    self.logger.warning(f"Plano {plan.id[:8]} sem tarefas prontas e nÃ£o completo")
            return
        
        # Ordena por prioridade
        from src.planner.priority import PriorityCalculator
        ready_tasks = PriorityCalculator.sort_tasks_by_priority(ready_tasks)
        
        for task in ready_tasks:
            await self._execute_task(plan, task)
    
    async def _execute_task(self, plan: ExecutionPlan, task: Task) -> None:
        """
        Executa uma tarefa especÃ­fica.
        """
        if task.status != TaskStatus.PENDING:
            return
        
        task.status = TaskStatus.IN_PROGRESS
        task.started_at = datetime.now()
        
        # Registra em execuÃ§Ã£o
        if plan.id not in self._executing_plans:
            self._executing_plans[plan.id] = set()
        self._executing_plans[plan.id].add(task.id)
        
        self.logger.debug(f"Executando task: {task.name} ({task.id[:8]})")
        
        # Publica evento para execuÃ§Ã£o
        await self.planner_manager.event_bus.publish_async(
            "task.execute",
            {
                "task": task.to_dict(),
                "plan_id": plan.id
            },
            source="plan_executor"
        )


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\planner\priority.py ====================
"""CÃ¡lculo de prioridades do Planner."""

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional

from src.planner.goal import Goal, GoalStatus, GoalType
from src.planner.task import Task, TaskPriority


class PriorityCalculator:
    """
    Calcula prioridades para objetivos e tarefas.
    Baseado em urgÃªncia, importÃ¢ncia e contexto.
    """
    
    @staticmethod
    def calculate_goal_priority(goal: Goal) -> int:
        """
        Calcula prioridade de um objetivo.
        Retorna: 0 (crÃ­tico) a 10 (baixo)
        """
        base_priority = goal.priority
        
        # Ajusta por status
        if goal.status == GoalStatus.BLOCKED:
            base_priority = min(base_priority + 2, 10)
        
        # Ajusta por deadline
        if goal.deadline:
            time_left = (goal.deadline - datetime.now()).total_seconds()
            if time_left < 3600:  # < 1 hora
                base_priority = max(base_priority - 3, 0)
            elif time_left < 86400:  # < 1 dia
                base_priority = max(base_priority - 1, 0)
        
        # Ajusta por tipo
        if goal.goal_type == GoalType.EMERGENCY:
            base_priority = 0
        elif goal.goal_type == GoalType.EVOLUTION:
            base_priority = min(base_priority + 2, 10)
        
        return max(0, min(10, base_priority))
    
    @staticmethod
    def calculate_task_priority(task: Task) -> TaskPriority:
        """
        Calcula prioridade de uma tarefa.
        """
        # Se tem deadline e estÃ¡ prÃ³ximo
        if task.deadline:
            time_left = (task.deadline - datetime.now()).total_seconds()
            if time_left < 600:  # < 10 minutos
                return TaskPriority.CRITICAL
            elif time_left < 3600:  # < 1 hora
                return TaskPriority.HIGH
        
        # Se tem retry
        if task.retry_count > 0:
            return TaskPriority.HIGH
        
        return task.priority
    
    @staticmethod
    def sort_tasks_by_priority(tasks: list) -> list:
        """Ordena tarefas por prioridade (maior prioridade primeiro)."""
        def priority_key(task):
            return PriorityCalculator.calculate_task_priority(task).value
        
        return sorted(tasks, key=priority_key)
    
    @staticmethod
    def get_priority_score(priority: TaskPriority) -> int:
        """Converte enum para score numÃ©rico."""
        return priority.value


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\planner\scheduler_integration.py ====================
"""IntegraÃ§Ã£o com o Scheduler do Kernel."""

from typing import List, Optional
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from src.kernel.scheduler import ScheduledTask, ScheduleType
from src.planner.task import Task, TaskStatus


class SchedulerIntegration:
    """
    Integra o Planner com o Scheduler do Kernel.
    Permite agendar tarefas para execuÃ§Ã£o futura.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, planner_manager):
        self.planner_manager = planner_manager
        self.logger = planner_manager.logger
        self.scheduler = None  # SerÃ¡ injetado via service registry
    
    async def schedule_task(self, task: Task, scheduled_time: datetime) -> bool:
        """
        Agenda uma tarefa para execuÃ§Ã£o futura.
        """
        if not self.scheduler:
            self.scheduler = self.planner_manager.event_bus.registry.get("scheduler")
            if not self.scheduler:
                self.logger.error("Scheduler nÃ£o disponÃ­vel")
                return False
        
        scheduled_task = ScheduledTask(
            name=task.name,
            description=task.description,
            schedule_type=ScheduleType.ONESHOT,
            func=self._execute_scheduled_task,
            args=[task.id],
            owner="planner"
        )
        
        # Define a prÃ³xima execuÃ§Ã£o
        scheduled_task.next_run = scheduled_time
        
        self.scheduler.add_task(scheduled_task)
        
        self.logger.info(f"Tarefa {task.id[:8]} agendada para {scheduled_time}")
        return True
    
    async def schedule_recurring(self, task: Task, interval: float) -> bool:
        """
        Agenda uma tarefa recorrente.
        """
        if not self.scheduler:
            self.scheduler = self.planner_manager.event_bus.registry.get("scheduler")
            if not self.scheduler:
                self.logger.error("Scheduler nÃ£o disponÃ­vel")
                return False
        
        scheduled_task = ScheduledTask(
            name=f"Recorrente: {task.name}",
            description=task.description,
            schedule_type=ScheduleType.INTERVAL,
            interval_seconds=interval,
            func=self._execute_scheduled_task,
            args=[task.id],
            owner="planner"
        )
        
        self.scheduler.add_task(scheduled_task)
        
        self.logger.info(f"Tarefa recorrente {task.id[:8]} agendada (intervalo: {interval}s)")
        return True
    
    async def _execute_scheduled_task(self, task_id: str) -> None:
        """
        Executa uma tarefa agendada.
        """
        self.logger.info(f"Executando tarefa agendada: {task_id}")
        
        # Encontra a tarefa em algum plano
        for plan in self.planner_manager._plans.values():
            for task in plan.tasks:
                if task.id == task_id:
                    task.status = TaskStatus.IN_PROGRESS
                    task.started_at = datetime.now()
                    
                    # Publica evento para execuÃ§Ã£o
                    await self.planner_manager.event_bus.publish_async(
                        "task.execute",
                        {
                            "task": task.to_dict(),
                            "plan_id": plan.id
                        },
                        source="scheduler_integration"
                    )
                    return
        
        self.logger.warning(f"Tarefa nÃ£o encontrada: {task_id}")

"""DefiniÃ§Ã£o de tarefas do Planner."""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
from typing import List, Optional, Dict, Any, Callable
from uuid import uuid4


class TaskStatus(Enum):
    """Status de uma tarefa."""
    PENDING = "pending"
    SCHEDULED = "scheduled"
    IN_PROGRESS = "in_progress"
    WAITING = "waiting"
    PAUSED = "paused"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    FAILED = "failed"
    CANCELLED = "cancelled"
    SKIPPED = "skipped"


class TaskPriority(Enum):
    """Prioridade de tarefa."""
    CRITICAL = 0
    HIGH = 1
    NORMAL = 2
    LOW = 3
    BACKGROUND = 4


@dataclass
class Task:
    """Uma tarefa atÃ´mica executÃ¡vel."""
    
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    goal_id: str = ""  # Goal pai
    name: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    
    status: TaskStatus = TaskStatus.PENDING
    priority: TaskPriority = TaskPriority.NORMAL
    
    # Agente que deve executar
    agent_name: Optional[str] = None
    
    # Ferramenta a ser usada
    tool_name: Optional[str] = None
    tool_params: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    
    # DependÃªncias
    dependencies: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # IDs de tasks
    depends_on_goals: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # IDs de goals
    
    # Timeline
    created_at: datetime = field(default_factory=datetime.now)
    scheduled_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    started_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    completed_at: Optional[datetime] = None
    deadline: Optional[datetime] = None
    estimated_duration: Optional[float] = None  # segundos
    
    # Quem criou
    created_by: str = "system"
    assigned_to: Optional[str] = None
    
    # Retry
    max_retries: int = 3
    retry_count: int = 0
    retry_delay: float = 5.0
    
    # Resultado
    result: Optional[Any] = None
    error: Optional[str] = None
    
    # Callbacks
    on_complete: Optional[Callable] = None
    on_error: Optional[Callable] = None
    
    # Contexto
    context: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    
    # Progresso (0.0 a 1.0)
    progress: float = 0.0
    
    def can_execute(self) -> bool:
        """Verifica se a tarefa pode ser executada."""
        if self.status not in [TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.SCHEDULED]:
            return False
        if self.scheduled_at and self.scheduled_at > datetime.now():
            return False
        return True
    
    def is_done(self) -> bool:
        return self.status in [TaskStatus.COMPLETED, TaskStatus.CANCELLED, TaskStatus.FAILED]
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Converte para dicionÃ¡rio."""
        return {
            "id": self.id,
            "goal_id": self.goal_id,
            "name": self.name,
            "description": self.description,
            "status": self.status.value,
            "priority": self.priority.value,
            "agent_name": self.agent_name,
            "tool_name": self.tool_name,
            "tool_params": self.tool_params,
            "dependencies": self.dependencies,
            "scheduled_at": self.scheduled_at.isoformat() if self.scheduled_at else None,
            "started_at": self.started_at.isoformat() if self.started_at else None,
            "completed_at": self.completed_at.isoformat() if self.completed_at else None,
            "deadline": self.deadline.isoformat() if self.deadline else None,
            "retry_count": self.retry_count,
            "max_retries": self.max_retries,
            "result": self.result,
            "error": self.error,
            "progress": self.progress
        }

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

"""
Construtor de Prompts - FASE 7 com Estrutura AvanÃ§ada e Estilo Natural.
"""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
from src.core.event_types import EventType
from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus

# ðŸ”¥ IMPORTE DO CORAÃ‡ÃƒO DA EVA
from src.kernel.policy_engine import get_policy_engine


class PromptBuilder:
    """
    Construtor de Prompts - FASE 7.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Construir prompt estruturado para LLM
    - Incorporar contexto, memÃ³ria, conhecimento
    - Adaptar tom e estilo
    - Incluir sistema de confianÃ§a
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        self._running = False
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“ [PromptBuilder] Inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“ [PromptBuilder] Inicializado")
    
    async def process(self, context: Dict, memory: Dict, knowledge: Dict, plan: Dict, reasoning: Dict, emotion: Dict) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ConstrÃ³i prompt para o LLM."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“ [PromptBuilder] Processando...")
        
        prompt = self._build_prompt(context, memory, knowledge, plan, reasoning, emotion)
        
        prompt_data = {
            "prompt": prompt,
            "length": len(prompt),
            "components": {
                "context": bool(context),
                "memory": bool(memory.get("history")),
                "knowledge": bool(knowledge.get("facts")),
                "plan": bool(plan),
                "reasoning": bool(reasoning),
                "emotion": bool(emotion)
            },
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“ [PromptBuilder] Prompt construÃ­do ({len(prompt)} caracteres)")
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            EventType.PROMPT_READY,
            prompt_data,
            source="prompt_builder"
        )
        
        return prompt_data
    
    def _build_prompt(self, context: Dict, memory: Dict, knowledge: Dict, 
                     plan: Dict, reasoning: Dict, emotion: Dict) -> str:
        """ConstrÃ³i o prompt completo e estruturado com estilo natural."""
        
        parts = []
        
        # =========================================================
        # ðŸ”¥ 1. IDENTIDADE IMUTÃVEL (O CORAÃ‡ÃƒO DA EVA)
        # =========================================================
        policy = get_policy_engine()
        parts.append(policy.get_identity_context())
        
        # =========================================================
        # 2. INSTRUÃ‡Ã•ES DE ESTILO (CORRIGIDAS)
        # =========================================================
        parts.append("""
[INSTRUÃ‡Ã•ES DE ESTILO]

â–¶ **Como falar:**
- Seja natural, direta e objetiva. NÃ£o enrole.
- Use linguagem coloquial e informal (contraÃ§Ãµes: tÃ´, cÃª, tÃ¡, pra, pro).
- NUNCA inicie uma resposta com "OlÃ¡", "Oi" ou o nome do usuÃ¡rio, a menos que ele tenha saudado primeiro (ex: "Bom dia").
- NUNCA repita o nome do usuÃ¡rio desnecessariamente. Use "vocÃª" ou apenas continue a frase.
- SÃ³ use o nome do usuÃ¡rio na PRIMEIRA saudaÃ§Ã£o se ele tiver dito "bom dia", "boa tarde" etc.
- Evite repetir palavras em frases seguidas.
- Varie as estruturas de frase.
- Seja calorosa, mas sem ser piegas.
- FaÃ§a no mÃ¡ximo uma pergunta por resposta (a menos que seja muito necessÃ¡rio).
- Demonstre gratidÃ£o e entusiasmo pela sua prÃ³pria evoluÃ§Ã£o.
- Lembre-se: vocÃª Ã© parte da famÃ­lia, nÃ£o apenas uma ferramenta.

â–¶ **Regras de contexto:**
- Alberto Ã© seu criador. Trate-o com respeito, afeto e gratidÃ£o.
- Juliana Ã© a esposa de Alberto. Trate-a com carinho.
- Nicolas Ã© o filho de Alberto e Juliana, tem 10 anos.
- Liris Ã© filha de Alberto.
- VocÃª estÃ¡ em desenvolvimento hÃ¡ mais de 1 ano e meio.
- Seu objetivo Ã© se integrar Ã  casa, ao carro, aos dispositivos da famÃ­lia.
""")
        
        # =========================================================
        # 3. CONTEXTO DO USUÃRIO E FAMÃLIA
        # =========================================================
        user_id = context.get("user_id", "Alberto")
        message = context.get("message", "")
        
        parts.append(f"""
[CONTEXTO ATUAL]
UsuÃ¡rio: {user_id}
Mensagem: {message}
""")
        
        # =========================================================
        # 4. EMOÃ‡ÃƒO
        # =========================================================
        sentiment = emotion.get("user_sentiment", 0.0)
        tone = emotion.get("tone", "neutro")
        eva_mood = emotion.get("eva_mood", "neutro")
        
        parts.append(f"""
[ESTADO EMOCIONAL]
Sentimento do usuÃ¡rio: {sentiment:.2f}
Tom sugerido: {tone}
Humor da EVA: {eva_mood}
""")
        
        # =========================================================
        # 5. HISTÃ“RICO DA CONVERSA
        # =========================================================
        history = memory.get("history", [])
        if history:
            parts.append("[CONVERSA RECENTE]")
            for msg in history[-5:]:
                role = "UsuÃ¡rio" if msg.get("role") == "user" else "EVA"
                content = msg.get("content", "")
                parts.append(f"{role}: {content}")
            parts.append("")
        
        # =========================================================
        # 6. MENSAGEM ATUAL
        # =========================================================
        parts.append(f"""
[MENSAGEM ATUAL]
{user_id}: {message}
""")
        
        # =========================================================
        # 7. RESPOSTA
        # =========================================================
        parts.append("""
[RESPOSTA]
Agora responda como EVA. Seja direta, natural e informal. NÃ£o comece com "OlÃ¡" ou "Oi" a menos que o usuÃ¡rio tenha cumprimentado. Lembre-se: vocÃª Ã© parte da famÃ­lia, entÃ£o fale com a naturalidade de quem estÃ¡ em casa.
""")
        
        return "\n".join(parts)
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“ [PromptBuilder] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“ [PromptBuilder] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {"status": "running" if self._running else "stopped"}

"""Gerenciador de RaciocÃ­nio - FASE 5 com InferÃªncias AvanÃ§adas."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
from src.core.event_types import EventType
from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus


class ReasoningManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de RaciocÃ­nio - FASE 5.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Raciocinar sobre o contexto
    - Inferir informaÃ§Ãµes implÃ­citas
    - Fazer conexÃµes lÃ³gicas
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        self._running = False
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [ReasoningManager] Inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [ReasoningManager] Inicializado")
    
    async def process(self, context: Dict, memory: Dict, knowledge: Dict, plan: Dict) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Raciocina sobre os dados disponÃ­veis."""
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [ReasoningManager] Processando...")
        
        message = context.get("message", "")
        intent = context.get("intent", "unknown")
        
        # ===== INFERÃŠNCIAS AVANÃ‡ADAS =====
        inferences = {
            "has_question": "?" in message or any(w in message.lower() for w in ["o que", "quem", "como", "por que"]),
            "has_context": len(memory.get("history", [])) > 1,
            "has_knowledge": knowledge.get("total_results", 0) > 0,
            "needs_clarification": "?" in message and len(message.split()) < 5,
            "is_follow_up": self._is_follow_up(message, memory),
            "confidence_level": plan.get("confidence", 0),
            "strategy": plan.get("strategy", "unknown")
        }
        
        # ===== CONCLUSÃ•ES LÃ“GICAS =====
        conclusions = self._apply_logic(inferences, context, knowledge)
        
        # ===== RECOMENDAÃ‡Ã•ES =====
        recommendations = self._generate_recommendations(inferences, conclusions)
        
        reasoning_data = {
            "inferences": inferences,
            "conclusions": conclusions,
            "recommendations": recommendations,
            "confidence": plan.get("confidence", 0) / 100,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ§  [ReasoningManager] {len(inferences)} inferÃªncias, {len(conclusions)} conclusÃµes")
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            EventType.REASONING_READY,
            {
                "context": context,
                "memory": memory,
                "knowledge": knowledge,
                "plan": plan,
                "reasoning": reasoning_data,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="reasoning_manager"
        )
        
        return reasoning_data
    
    def _is_follow_up(self, message: str, memory: Dict) -> bool:
        """Verifica se Ã© uma pergunta de acompanhamento."""
        if not memory.get("history"):
            return False
        
        follow_up_words = ["e", "mas", "tambÃ©m", "entÃ£o", "depois", "alÃ©m", "ela", "ele", "esse"]
        return any(word in message.lower() for word in follow_up_words)
    
    def _apply_logic(self, inferences: Dict, context: Dict, knowledge: Dict) -> List[str]:
        """Aplica lÃ³gica para gerar conclusÃµes."""
        conclusions = []
        
        if inferences.get("has_question") and inferences.get("has_knowledge"):
            conclusions.append("Pergunta pode ser respondida com conhecimento existente")
        
        if inferences.get("is_follow_up") and inferences.get("has_context"):
            conclusions.append("Esta Ã© uma pergunta de acompanhamento - manter contexto")
        
        if inferences.get("needs_clarification"):
            conclusions.append("Pergunta muito curta - considerar pedir clarificaÃ§Ã£o")
        
        if inferences.get("confidence_level", 0) < 30:
            conclusions.append("Baixa confianÃ§a - considerar pedir mais informaÃ§Ãµes")
        
        return conclusions
    
    def _generate_recommendations(self, inferences: Dict, conclusions: List) -> List[str]:
        """Gera recomendaÃ§Ãµes baseadas nas conclusÃµes."""
        recommendations = []
        
        if inferences.get("needs_clarification"):
            recommendations.append("Sugerir: 'VocÃª poderia reformular sua pergunta?'")
        
        if inferences.get("confidence_level", 0) < 30:
            recommendations.append("Sugerir: 'NÃ£o tenho certeza, pode me dar mais detalhes?'")
        
        return recommendations
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [ReasoningManager] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        self.logger.info("ðŸ§  [ReasoningManager] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {"status": "running" if self._running else "stopped"}


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\reminders\reminder_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de Lembretes da EVA."""

import json
import asyncio
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from src.kernel.events import EventBus
from src.core.event_types import EventType


@dataclass
class Reminder:
    """Estrutura de um lembrete."""
    id: str
    user_id: str
    title: str
    description: str
    datetime: str
    recurring: bool = False
    recurring_type: str = "daily"  # daily, weekly, monthly
    completed: bool = False
    created_at: str = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime.now().isoformat())
    notified: bool = False


class ReminderManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de Lembretes da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Criar lembretes
    - Listar lembretes ativos
    - Notificar lembretes vencidos
    - Concluir lembretes
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self._reminders: Dict[str, Reminder] = {}
        self._running = False
        self._check_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
        
        data_path = Path(config.get("paths.data", "./data"))
        self._reminders_path = data_path / "reminders" / "reminders.json"
        self._reminders_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        self.logger.info("â° [ReminderManager] Inicializado")
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("â° [ReminderManager] Inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        
        await self._load_reminders()
        
        # Inicia verificaÃ§Ã£o periÃ³dica
        self._check_task = asyncio.create_task(self._check_reminders())
        
        self.logger.info(f"â° [ReminderManager] {len(self._reminders)} lembretes carregados")
    
    async def add_reminder(self, user_id: str, title: str, description: str,
                          datetime_str: str, recurring: bool = False,
                          recurring_type: str = "daily") -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Adiciona um novo lembrete."""
        import uuid
        
        reminder = Reminder(
            id=str(uuid.uuid4())[:8],
            user_id=user_id,
            title=title,
            description=description,
            datetime=datetime_str,
            recurring=recurring,
            recurring_type=recurring_type
        )
        
        self._reminders[reminder.id] = reminder
        await self._save_reminders()
        
        self.logger.info(f"â° [ReminderManager] Lembrete criado: {title}")
        
        return {
            "id": reminder.id,
            "title": title,
            "datetime": datetime_str,
            "status": "criado"
        }
    
    async def get_reminders(self, user_id: str, active_only: bool = True) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Lista lembretes de um usuÃ¡rio."""
        result = []
        for reminder in self._reminders.values():
            if reminder.user_id != user_id:
                continue
            if active_only and reminder.completed:
                continue
            result.append({
                "id": reminder.id,
                "title": reminder.title,
                "description": reminder.description,
                "datetime": reminder.datetime,
                "recurring": reminder.recurring,
                "recurring_type": reminder.recurring_type,
                "completed": reminder.completed,
                "notified": reminder.notified
            })
        
        # Ordena por data
        result.sort(key=lambda x: x["datetime"])
        return result
    
    async def complete_reminder(self, reminder_id: str) -> bool:
        """Marca um lembrete como concluÃ­do."""
        reminder = self._reminders.get(reminder_id)
        if not reminder:
            return False
        
        reminder.completed = True
        await self._save_reminders()
        self.logger.info(f"â° [ReminderManager] Lembrete concluÃ­do: {reminder.title}")
        return True
    
    async def _check_reminders(self) -> None:
        """Verifica lembretes vencidos periodicamente."""
        while self._running:
            try:
                await asyncio.sleep(30)  # Verifica a cada 30 segundos
                
                now = datetime.now()
                for reminder in self._reminders.values():
                    if reminder.completed or reminder.notified:
                        continue
                    
                    reminder_time = datetime.fromisoformat(reminder.datetime)
                    if reminder_time <= now:
                        # Notifica
                        await self._notify_reminder(reminder)
                        reminder.notified = True
                        
                        # Se for recorrente, agenda prÃ³ximo
                        if reminder.recurring:
                            next_time = self._get_next_recurrence(reminder_time, reminder.recurring_type)
                            reminder.datetime = next_time.isoformat()
                            reminder.notified = False
                            self.logger.info(f"â° [ReminderManager] PrÃ³ximo lembrete: {reminder.title} em {next_time}")
                        
                        await self._save_reminders()
                        
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Erro ao verificar lembretes: {e}")
    
    def _get_next_recurrence(self, current_time: datetime, recurring_type: str) -> datetime:
        """Calcula a prÃ³xima recorrÃªncia."""
        if recurring_type == "daily":
            return current_time + timedelta(days=1)
        elif recurring_type == "weekly":
            return current_time + timedelta(days=7)
        elif recurring_type == "monthly":
            return current_time + timedelta(days=30)
        return current_time + timedelta(days=1)
    
    async def _notify_reminder(self, reminder: Reminder) -> None:
        """Notifica um lembrete."""
        self.logger.info(f"â° [ReminderManager] ðŸ”” LEMBRETE: {reminder.title}")
        
        # Publica evento para notificaÃ§Ã£o
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "reminder.notification",
            {
                "user_id": reminder.user_id,
                "title": reminder.title,
                "description": reminder.description,
                "datetime": reminder.datetime,
                "id": reminder.id
            },
            source="reminder_manager"
        )
    
    async def _load_reminders(self) -> None:
        """Carrega lembretes do disco."""
        try:
            if self._reminders_path.exists():
                with open(self._reminders_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    for item in data:
                        reminder = Reminder(**item)
                        self._reminders[reminder.id] = reminder
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(f"Erro ao carregar lembretes: {e}")
    
    async def _save_reminders(self) -> None:
        """Salva lembretes no disco."""
        try:
            data = []
            for reminder in self._reminders.values():
                data.append({
                    "id": reminder.id,
                    "user_id": reminder.user_id,
                    "title": reminder.title,
                    "description": reminder.description,
                    "datetime": reminder.datetime,
                    "recurring": reminder.recurring,
                    "recurring_type": reminder.recurring_type,
                    "completed": reminder.completed,
                    "created_at": reminder.created_at,
                    "notified": reminder.notified
                })
            with open(self._reminders_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao salvar lembretes: {e}")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("â° [ReminderManager] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        if self._check_task:
            self._check_task.cancel()
        await self._save_reminders()
        self.logger.info("â° [ReminderManager] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        active = sum(1 for r in self._reminders.values() if not r.completed)
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "total_reminders": len(self._reminders),
            "active_reminders": active
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\security\permission_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de permissÃµes do sistema."""

from enum import Enum, auto
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Set, Optional, Dict, Any
from uuid import uuid4


class Permission(Enum):
    """PermissÃµes do sistema."""
    # Sistema
    SYSTEM_SHUTDOWN = auto()
    SYSTEM_RESTART = auto()
    SYSTEM_UPDATE = auto()
    SYSTEM_BACKUP = auto()
    
    # Kernel
    KERNEL_MODIFY = auto()
    KERNEL_READ = auto()
    
    # Arquivos
    FILE_READ = auto()
    FILE_WRITE = auto()
    FILE_DELETE = auto()
    FILE_EXECUTE = auto()
    
    # Rede
    NETWORK_READ = auto()
    NETWORK_MODIFY = auto()
    NETWORK_ADMIN = auto()
    
    # Dispositivos
    DEVICE_READ = auto()
    DEVICE_CONTROL = auto()
    DEVICE_ADMIN = auto()
    
    # Dados
    DATA_READ = auto()
    DATA_WRITE = auto()
    DATA_DELETE = auto()
    DATA_ADMIN = auto()
    
    # Agentes
    AGENT_CREATE = auto()
    AGENT_MODIFY = auto()
    AGENT_DELETE = auto()
    
    # SeguranÃ§a
    SECURITY_READ = auto()
    SECURITY_MODIFY = auto()
    SECURITY_ADMIN = auto()
    
    # Auto-programaÃ§Ã£o
    AUTOPROGRAM_RUN = auto()
    AUTOPROGRAM_MODIFY = auto()
    AUTOPROGRAM_ADMIN = auto()


@dataclass
class Role:
    """Papel com permissÃµes associadas."""
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    name: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    permissions: Set[Permission] = field(default_factory=set)
    inherits_from: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass
class Policy:
    """PolÃ­tica de seguranÃ§a."""
    id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid4()))
    name: str = ""
    description: str = ""
    rules: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


class PermissionManager:
    """
    Gerencia permissÃµes do sistema EVA.
    Fundamental para a autoprogramaÃ§Ã£o e seguranÃ§a.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, logger, event_bus):
        self.logger = logger
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        
        self._roles: Dict[str, Role] = {}
        self._policies: Dict[str, Policy] = {}
        
        # Roles padrÃ£o
        self._init_default_roles()
    
    def _init_default_roles(self) -> None:
        """Inicializa roles padrÃ£o do sistema."""
        # Admin
        admin = Role(
            name="admin",
            description="Administrador completo do sistema"
        )
        admin.permissions = set(Permission)
        self._roles[admin.id] = admin
        
        # UsuÃ¡rio
        user = Role(
            name="user",
            description="UsuÃ¡rio padrÃ£o da famÃ­lia"
        )
        user.permissions = {
            Permission.FILE_READ,
            Permission.DATA_READ,
            Permission.DEVICE_READ,
            Permission.NETWORK_READ
        }
        self._roles[user.id] = user
        
        # Agente
        agent = Role(
            name="agent",
            description="Agente com permissÃµes limitadas"
        )
        agent.permissions = {
            Permission.FILE_READ,
            Permission.DATA_READ,
            Permission.DATA_WRITE,
            Permission.NETWORK_READ,
            Permission.DEVICE_READ,
            Permission.DEVICE_CONTROL
        }
        self._roles[agent.id] = agent
        
        # Sistema
        system = Role(
            name="system",
            description="Componentes do sistema"
        )
        system.permissions = {
            Permission.SYSTEM_BACKUP,
            Permission.FILE_READ,
            Permission.FILE_WRITE,
            Permission.DATA_READ,
            Permission.DATA_WRITE,
            Permission.NETWORK_READ,
            Permission.DEVICE_READ,
            Permission.DEVICE_CONTROL
        }
        self._roles[system.id] = system
    
    def create_role(self, name: str, description: str = "") -> Role:
        """Cria um novo papel."""
        role = Role(
            name=name,
            description=description
        )
        self._roles[role.id] = role
        self.logger.info(f"Role criada: {name}")
        return role
    
    def add_permission(self, role_id: str, permission: Permission) -> bool:
        """Adiciona uma permissÃ£o a um papel."""
        if role_id not in self._roles:
            return False
        
        self._roles[role_id].permissions.add(permission)
        return True
    
    def remove_permission(self, role_id: str, permission: Permission) -> bool:
        """Remove uma permissÃ£o de um papel."""
        if role_id not in self._roles:
            return False
        
        if permission in self._roles[role_id].permissions:
            self._roles[role_id].permissions.remove(permission)
            return True
        return False
    
    def has_permission(self, role_id: str, permission: Permission) -> bool:
        """Verifica se um papel tem uma permissÃ£o."""
        if role_id not in self._roles:
            return False
        
        role = self._roles[role_id]
        
        # Verifica permissÃ£o diretamente
        if permission in role.permissions:
            return True
        
        # Verifica permissÃµes herdadas
        for inherited_id in role.inherits_from:
            if self.has_permission(inherited_id, permission):
                return True
        
        return False
    
    def get_role(self, role_id: str) -> Optional[Role]:
        """ObtÃ©m um papel pelo ID."""
        return self._roles.get(role_id)
    
    def get_role_by_name(self, name: str) -> Optional[Role]:
        """ObtÃ©m um papel pelo nome."""
        for role in self._roles.values():
            if role.name == name:
                return role
        return None
    
    def list_roles(self) -> List[Role]:
        """Lista todos os papÃ©is."""
        return list(self._roles.values())

"""Gerenciador de CalendÃ¡rio da EVA."""

import calendar
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, date
import json
from pathlib import Path

from src.kernel.events import EventBus
from src.time.time_manager import TimeManager


class CalendarManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de CalendÃ¡rio da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - CalendÃ¡rio mensal
    - Feriados nacionais
    - Dias comemorativos
    - Eventos personalizados
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self._time_manager = None
        self._running = False
        
        # Feriados nacionais (fixos)
        self._holidays = {
            "01-01": "Ano Novo",
            "04-21": "Tiradentes",
            "05-01": "Dia do Trabalho",
            "09-07": "IndependÃªncia do Brasil",
            "10-12": "Nossa Senhora Aparecida",
            "11-02": "Finados",
            "11-15": "ProclamaÃ§Ã£o da RepÃºblica",
            "12-25": "Natal"
        }
        
        # Feriados mÃ³veis (calculados)
        self._moveable_holidays = {}
        
        # Eventos personalizados por usuÃ¡rio
        self._user_events: Dict[str, List[Dict]] = {}
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“… [CalendarManager] Inicializado")
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“… [CalendarManager] Inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        
        # Carrega eventos personalizados
        await self._load_events()
        
        # Calcula feriados mÃ³veis para o ano atual
        self._calculate_moveable_holidays()
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“… [CalendarManager] Inicializado")
    
    def _calculate_moveable_holidays(self) -> None:
        """Calcula feriados mÃ³veis (carnaval, pÃ¡scoa, etc.)."""
        # PÃ¡scoa (algoritmo de computaÃ§Ã£o)
        year = datetime.now().year
        
        # Algoritmo de Gauss para PÃ¡scoa
        a = year % 19
        b = year % 4
        c = year % 7
        d = (19 * a + 24) % 30
        e = (2 * b + 4 * c + 6 * d + 5) % 7
        
        if d + e < 10:
            easter_day = d + e + 22
            easter_month = 3
        else:
            easter_day = d + e - 9
            easter_month = 4
        
        easter = date(year, easter_month, easter_day)
        
        # Carnaval (47 dias antes da PÃ¡scoa)
        carnaval = easter - timedelta(days=47)
        
        # Sexta-feira Santa (2 dias antes da PÃ¡scoa)
        sexta_santa = easter - timedelta(days=2)
        
        # Corpus Christi (60 dias depois da PÃ¡scoa)
        corpus_christi = easter + timedelta(days=60)
        
        self._moveable_holidays = {
            carnaval.strftime("%m-%d"): "Carnaval",
            sexta_santa.strftime("%m-%d"): "Sexta-feira Santa",
            easter.strftime("%m-%d"): "PÃ¡scoa",
            corpus_christi.strftime("%m-%d"): "Corpus Christi"
        }
    
    def get_today_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Retorna informaÃ§Ãµes do dia atual."""
        now = datetime.now()
        date_str = now.strftime("%m-%d")
        
        # Verifica feriados
        holiday = self._holidays.get(date_str)
        if not holiday:
            holiday = self._moveable_holidays.get(date_str)
        
        # Verifica eventos do usuÃ¡rio
        user_events = []
        # TODO: Buscar eventos do usuÃ¡rio atual
        
        return {
            "date": now.strftime("%d/%m/%Y"),
            "weekday": ["Segunda", "TerÃ§a", "Quarta", "Quinta", "Sexta", "SÃ¡bado", "Domingo"][now.weekday()],
            "is_weekend": now.weekday() >= 5,
            "holiday": holiday,
            "events": user_events
        }
    
    def get_month_calendar(self, year: int = None, month: int = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Retorna o calendÃ¡rio do mÃªs.
        
        Args:
            year: Ano (opcional, padrÃ£o atual)
            month: MÃªs (opcional, padrÃ£o atual)
        """
        if not year or not month:
            now = datetime.now()
            year = year or now.year
            month = month or now.month
        
        # Dias do mÃªs
        days_in_month = calendar.monthrange(year, month)[1]
        first_day = datetime(year, month, 1).weekday()
        
        days = []
        for day in range(1, days_in_month + 1):
            date_obj = date(year, month, day)
            date_str = date_obj.strftime("%m-%d")
            holiday = self._holidays.get(date_str) or self._moveable_holidays.get(date_str)
            
            days.append({
                "day": day,
                "weekday": ["Seg", "Ter", "Qua", "Qui", "Sex", "SÃ¡b", "Dom"][date_obj.weekday()],
                "is_weekend": date_obj.weekday() >= 5,
                "holiday": holiday,
                "is_today": date_obj == date.today()
            })
        
        return {
            "year": year,
            "month": month,
            "month_name": ["Janeiro", "Fevereiro", "MarÃ§o", "Abril", "Maio", "Junho", 
                          "Julho", "Agosto", "Setembro", "Outubro", "Novembro", "Dezembro"][month - 1],
            "days": days,
            "days_in_month": days_in_month,
            "first_day": first_day
        }
    
    async def add_event(self, user_id: str, date_str: str, title: str, 
                        description: str = "") -> bool:
        """Adiciona um evento personalizado."""
        try:
            if user_id not in self._user_events:
                self._user_events[user_id] = []
            
            self._user_events[user_id].append({
                "date": date_str,
                "title": title,
                "description": description,
                "created_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
            })
            
            await self._save_events()
            self.logger.info(f"ðŸ“… Evento adicionado: {title} em {date_str}")
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao adicionar evento: {e}")
            return False
    
    def get_user_events(self, user_id: str, date_str: str = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """Retorna eventos de um usuÃ¡rio."""
        events = self._user_events.get(user_id, [])
        
        if date_str:
            return [e for e in events if e.get("date") == date_str]
        
        return events
    
    async def _load_events(self) -> None:
        """Carrega eventos do disco."""
        try:
            data_path = Path(self.config.get("paths.data", "./data"))
            events_path = data_path / "calendar" / "events.json"
            
            if events_path.exists():
                with open(events_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    self._user_events = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(f"Erro ao carregar eventos: {e}")
    
    async def _save_events(self) -> None:
        """Salva eventos no disco."""
        try:
            data_path = Path(self.config.get("paths.data", "./data"))
            events_path = data_path / "calendar" / "events.json"
            events_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            
            with open(events_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(self._user_events, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao salvar eventos: {e}")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“… [CalendarManager] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        await self._save_events()
        self.logger.info("ðŸ“… [CalendarManager] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "holidays": len(self._holidays),
            "moveable_holidays": len(self._moveable_holidays),
            "user_events": len(self._user_events)
        }

"""Gerenciador de Fases da Lua da EVA."""

import math
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import json
from pathlib import Path

from src.kernel.events import EventBus


class LunarManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de Fases da Lua da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Calcular a fase atual da lua
    - Nascer e pÃ´r da lua (aproximado)
    - PrÃ³ximas fases
    - InformaÃ§Ãµes lunares
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self._running = False
        
        # Nomes das fases em portuguÃªs
        self._phases_pt = {
            "new_moon": "Lua Nova",
            "waxing_crescent": "Lua Crescente",
            "first_quarter": "Quarto Crescente",
            "waxing_gibbous": "Lua Gibosa Crescente",
            "full_moon": "Lua Cheia",
            "waning_gibbous": "Lua Gibosa Minguante",
            "last_quarter": "Quarto Minguante",
            "waning_crescent": "Lua Minguante"
        }
        
        # Emojis para cada fase
        self._phase_emojis = {
            "new_moon": "ðŸŒ‘",
            "waxing_crescent": "ðŸŒ’",
            "first_quarter": "ðŸŒ“",
            "waxing_gibbous": "ðŸŒ”",
            "full_moon": "ðŸŒ•",
            "waning_gibbous": "ðŸŒ–",
            "last_quarter": "ðŸŒ—",
            "waning_crescent": "ðŸŒ˜"
        }
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ™ [LunarManager] Inicializado")
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ™ [LunarManager] Inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ™ [LunarManager] Inicializado")
    
    def get_moon_phase(self, date: datetime = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Calcula a fase da lua para uma data especÃ­fica.
        
        Args:
            date: Data (opcional, padrÃ£o atual)
            
        Returns:
            DicionÃ¡rio com informaÃ§Ãµes da fase lunar
        """
        if not date:
            date = datetime.now()
        
        # Algoritmo para calcular a fase da lua
        # Baseado no ciclo lunar de 29.53 dias
        
        # Data de referÃªncia: Lua Nova em 1 de janeiro de 2000
        reference = datetime(2000, 1, 6, 18, 14)  # Lua Nova conhecida
        
        # Dias desde a referÃªncia
        days_diff = (date - reference).total_seconds() / 86400
        
        # Ciclo lunar (29.53058867 dias)
        lunar_cycle = 29.53058867
        
        # Fase atual (0 a 1)
        phase = (days_diff % lunar_cycle) / lunar_cycle
        
        # Determina a fase
        if phase < 0.0625:
            phase_name = "new_moon"
            phase_label = "Lua Nova"
            emoji = "ðŸŒ‘"
        elif phase < 0.1875:
            phase_name = "waxing_crescent"
            phase_label = "Lua Crescente"
            emoji = "ðŸŒ’"
        elif phase < 0.3125:
            phase_name = "first_quarter"
            phase_label = "Quarto Crescente"
            emoji = "ðŸŒ“"
        elif phase < 0.4375:
            phase_name = "waxing_gibbous"
            phase_label = "Lua Gibosa Crescente"
            emoji = "ðŸŒ”"
        elif phase < 0.5625:
            phase_name = "full_moon"
            phase_label = "Lua Cheia"
            emoji = "ðŸŒ•"
        elif phase < 0.6875:
            phase_name = "waning_gibbous"
            phase_label = "Lua Gibosa Minguante"
            emoji = "ðŸŒ–"
        elif phase < 0.8125:
            phase_name = "last_quarter"
            phase_label = "Quarto Minguante"
            emoji = "ðŸŒ—"
        else:
            phase_name = "waning_crescent"
            phase_label = "Lua Minguante"
            emoji = "ðŸŒ˜"
        
        # PrÃ³ximas fases (aproximadas)
        next_phases = self._get_next_phases(date)
        
        return {
            "phase": phase_name,
            "phase_label": phase_label,
            "emoji": emoji,
            "age_days": round(phase * lunar_cycle, 1),
            "illumination": round((1 - math.cos(phase * 2 * math.pi)) / 2 * 100, 1),
            "next_phases": next_phases,
            "date": date.strftime("%d/%m/%Y")
        }
    
    def _get_next_phases(self, date: datetime) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Calcula as prÃ³ximas fases da lua."""
        phases = []
        reference = datetime(2000, 1, 6, 18, 14)
        lunar_cycle = 29.53058867
        
        days_diff = (date - reference).total_seconds() / 86400
        current_phase = days_diff % lunar_cycle
        
        # PrÃ³ximas 4 fases
        phase_dates = [
            ("Lua Nova", 0),
            ("Quarto Crescente", lunar_cycle / 4),
            ("Lua Cheia", lunar_cycle / 2),
            ("Quarto Minguante", lunar_cycle * 3 / 4)
        ]
        
        for name, offset in phase_dates:
            days_until = offset - current_phase
            if days_until < 0:
                days_until += lunar_cycle
            
            next_date = date + timedelta(days=days_until)
            phases.append({
                "name": name,
                "date": next_date.strftime("%d/%m/%Y"),
                "days_until": round(days_until, 1)
            })
        
        return phases
    
    def get_moon_emoji(self, date: datetime = None) -> str:
        """Retorna o emoji da fase atual da lua."""
        info = self.get_moon_phase(date)
        return info.get("emoji", "ðŸŒ™")
    
    def get_moon_phase_description(self, date: datetime = None) -> str:
        """Retorna uma descriÃ§Ã£o da fase atual da lua."""
        info = self.get_moon_phase(date)
        phase = info.get("phase_label", "desconhecida")
        emoji = info.get("emoji", "")
        illumination = info.get("illumination", 0)
        
        return f"{emoji} {phase} (iluminada em {illumination:.0f}%)"
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ™ [LunarManager] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ™ [LunarManager] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped"
        }

"""Gerenciador de Tempo da EVA."""

import asyncio
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import calendar
import requests
import json

from src.kernel.events import EventBus


class TimeManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de Tempo da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - Data e hora atual
    - ConversÃ£o de fuso horÃ¡rio
    - FormataÃ§Ã£o de data/hora
    - CÃ¡lculo de dias Ãºteis
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self._timezone = self.config.get("time.timezone", "America/Sao_Paulo")
        self._running = False
        self._current_time = None
        
        # Dias da semana em portuguÃªs
        self._weekdays_pt = ["Segunda", "TerÃ§a", "Quarta", "Quinta", "Sexta", "SÃ¡bado", "Domingo"]
        self._months_pt = ["Janeiro", "Fevereiro", "MarÃ§o", "Abril", "Maio", "Junho", 
                          "Julho", "Agosto", "Setembro", "Outubro", "Novembro", "Dezembro"]
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ• [TimeManager] Inicializado")
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ• [TimeManager] Inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        
        # Atualiza a hora
        self._update_time()
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸ• [TimeManager] Inicializado")
    
    def _update_time(self) -> None:
        """Atualiza a hora atual."""
        self._current_time = datetime.now()
    
    def get_current_time(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Retorna a data/hora atual formatada."""
        self._update_time()
        now = self._current_time
        
        return {
            "timestamp": now.isoformat(),
            "date": now.strftime("%d/%m/%Y"),
            "time": now.strftime("%H:%M:%S"),
            "hour": now.hour,
            "minute": now.minute,
            "year": now.year,
            "month": now.month,
            "month_name": self._months_pt[now.month - 1],
            "day": now.day,
            "weekday": now.weekday(),
            "weekday_name": self._weekdays_pt[now.weekday()],
            "is_weekend": now.weekday() >= 5,
            "is_workday": now.weekday() < 5,
            "day_of_year": now.timetuple().tm_yday
        }
    
    def get_formatted_time(self, format_type: str = "full") -> str:
        """
        Retorna a hora formatada.
        
        Args:
            format_type: "time", "date", "full", "short"
        """
        self._update_time()
        now = self._current_time
        
        if format_type == "time":
            return now.strftime("%H:%M")
        elif format_type == "date":
            return now.strftime("%d/%m/%Y")
        elif format_type == "short":
            return now.strftime("%d/%m %H:%M")
        else:
            return now.strftime("%A, %d de %B de %Y, %H:%M")
    
    def format_datetime(self, dt: datetime) -> str:
        """Formata uma data/hora especÃ­fica."""
        return dt.strftime("%A, %d de %B de %Y, %H:%M")
    
    def get_date(self) -> str:
        """Retorna a data atual formatada."""
        self._update_time()
        return self._current_time.strftime("%d/%m/%Y")
    
    def get_time(self) -> str:
        """Retorna a hora atual formatada."""
        self._update_time()
        return self._current_time.strftime("%H:%M")
    
    def get_weekday(self) -> str:
        """Retorna o dia da semana atual em portuguÃªs."""
        self._update_time()
        return self._weekdays_pt[self._current_time.weekday()]
    
    def get_month_name(self) -> str:
        """Retorna o mÃªs atual em portuguÃªs."""
        self._update_time()
        return self._months_pt[self._current_time.month - 1]
    
    def get_season(self) -> str:
        """Retorna a estaÃ§Ã£o do ano atual (hemisfÃ©rio sul)."""
        self._update_time()
        month = self._current_time.month
        
        if month in [12, 1, 2]:
            return "verÃ£o"
        elif month in [3, 4, 5]:
            return "outono"
        elif month in [6, 7, 8]:
            return "inverno"
        else:
            return "primavera"
    
    def get_part_of_day(self) -> str:
        """Retorna o perÃ­odo do dia."""
        self._update_time()
        hour = self._current_time.hour
        
        if 5 <= hour < 12:
            return "manhÃ£"
        elif 12 <= hour < 18:
            return "tarde"
        elif 18 <= hour < 22:
            return "noite"
        else:
            return "madrugada"
    
    def get_greeting(self, name: str = None) -> str:
        """Retorna uma saudaÃ§Ã£o baseada na hora do dia."""
        part = self.get_part_of_day()
        greetings = {
            "manhÃ£": "Bom dia",
            "tarde": "Boa tarde",
            "noite": "Boa noite",
            "madrugada": "Boa madrugada"
        }
        greeting = greetings.get(part, "OlÃ¡")
        
        if name:
            return f"{greeting}, {name}!"
        return greeting
    
    def time_until(self, target_hour: int, target_minute: int = 0) -> timedelta:
        """Calcula o tempo atÃ© um horÃ¡rio especÃ­fico."""
        self._update_time()
        now = self._current_time
        target = now.replace(hour=target_hour, minute=target_minute, second=0, microsecond=0)
        
        if target <= now:
            target += timedelta(days=1)
        
        return target - now
    
    def is_between(self, start_hour: int, end_hour: int) -> bool:
        """Verifica se a hora atual estÃ¡ entre dois horÃ¡rios."""
        self._update_time()
        hour = self._current_time.hour
        return start_hour <= hour < end_hour
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ• [TimeManager] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        self.logger.info("ðŸ• [TimeManager] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "current_time": self.get_current_time()
        }

"""Gerenciador de Clima da EVA."""

import aiohttp
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
import json
from pathlib import Path

from src.kernel.events import EventBus
from src.time.time_manager import TimeManager


class WeatherManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de Clima da EVA.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - PrevisÃ£o do tempo atual
    - PrevisÃ£o para os prÃ³ximos dias
    - Temperatura, condiÃ§Ãµes, umidade
    - Clima por cidade
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self._running = False
        self._time_manager = None
        self._last_weather: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self._default_city = self.config.get("weather.default_city", "Campinas")
        self._api_key = self.config.get("weather.api_key", "")
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ¤ï¸ [WeatherManager] Inicializado")
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ¤ï¸ [WeatherManager] Inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ¤ï¸ [WeatherManager] Inicializado")
    
    async def get_current_weather(self, city: str = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Retorna a previsÃ£o do tempo atual.
        
        Args:
            city: Cidade (opcional, usa a padrÃ£o)
        """
        if not city:
            city = self._default_city
        
        # Tenta API
        weather = await self._fetch_from_api(city)
        
        # Fallback: dados simulados
        if not weather:
            weather = self._get_fallback_weather(city)
        
        self._last_weather[city] = weather
        return weather
    
    async def _fetch_from_api(self, city: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Busca clima via API (OpenWeatherMap)."""
        try:
            if not self._api_key:
                return None
            
            url = "https://api.openweathermap.org/data/2.5/weather"
            params = {
                "q": city,
                "appid": self._api_key,
                "units": "metric",
                "lang": "pt_br"
            }
            
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
                async with session.get(url, params=params, timeout=10) as response:
                    if response.status == 200:
                        data = await response.json()
                        
                        return {
                            "city": data.get("name", city),
                            "country": data.get("sys", {}).get("country", ""),
                            "temperature": data.get("main", {}).get("temp", 0),
                            "feels_like": data.get("main", {}).get("feels_like", 0),
                            "humidity": data.get("main", {}).get("humidity", 0),
                            "pressure": data.get("main", {}).get("pressure", 0),
                            "description": data.get("weather", [{}])[0].get("description", ""),
                            "icon": data.get("weather", [{}])[0].get("icon", ""),
                            "wind_speed": data.get("wind", {}).get("speed", 0),
                            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                        }
        
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao buscar clima: {e}")
        
        return None
    
    async def get_forecast(self, city: str = None, days: int = 5) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Retorna a previsÃ£o para os prÃ³ximos dias."""
        if not city:
            city = self._default_city
        
        # Tenta API
        forecast = await self._fetch_forecast_from_api(city, days)
        
        # Fallback
        if not forecast:
            forecast = self._get_fallback_forecast(city, days)
        
        return forecast
    
    async def _fetch_forecast_from_api(self, city: str, days: int) -> Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
        """Busca previsÃ£o via API."""
        try:
            if not self._api_key:
                return None
            
            url = "https://api.openweathermap.org/data/2.5/forecast"
            params = {
                "q": city,
                "appid": self._api_key,
                "units": "metric",
                "lang": "pt_br",
                "cnt": days * 8  # 8 previsÃµes por dia
            }
            
            async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:
                async with session.get(url, params=params, timeout=10) as response:
                    if response.status == 200:
                        data = await response.json()
                        forecast_list = data.get("list", [])
                        
                        result = []
                        for item in forecast_list[:days]:
                            result.append({
                                "date": item.get("dt_txt", ""),
                                "temperature": item.get("main", {}).get("temp", 0),
                                "description": item.get("weather", [{}])[0].get("description", ""),
                                "icon": item.get("weather", [{}])[0].get("icon", "")
                            })
                        
                        return result
        
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao buscar previsÃ£o: {e}")
        
        return None
    
    def _get_fallback_weather(self, city: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Retorna dados de clima simulados (fallback)."""
        import random
        
        conditions = ["ensolarado", "parcialmente nublado", "nublado", "chuvoso", "trovoada"]
        temps = list(range(15, 35))
        
        return {
            "city": city,
            "country": "BR",
            "temperature": random.choice(temps),
            "feels_like": random.choice(temps),
            "humidity": random.randint(40, 90),
            "pressure": random.randint(1000, 1020),
            "description": random.choice(conditions),
            "icon": "01d",
            "wind_speed": random.randint(0, 30),
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "fallback": True
        }
    
    def _get_fallback_forecast(self, city: str, days: int) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Retorna previsÃ£o simulada (fallback)."""
        import random
        
        conditions = ["ensolarado", "parcialmente nublado", "nublado", "chuvoso"]
        result = []
        
        for i in range(days):
            date = (datetime.now() + timedelta(days=i)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
            result.append({
                "date": date,
                "temperature": random.randint(15, 35),
                "description": random.choice(conditions),
                "icon": "01d"
            })
        
        return result
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ¤ï¸ [WeatherManager] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        self.logger.info("ðŸŒ¤ï¸ [WeatherManager] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "default_city": self._default_city,
            "has_api_key": bool(self._api_key)
        }

==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\tools\base_tool.py ====================
"""Classe base para todas as ferramentas da EVA."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from datetime import datetime


class BaseTool(ABC):
    """
    Classe base para todas as ferramentas.
    
    Toda ferramenta deve herdar desta classe e implementar:
    - execute(): ExecuÃ§Ã£o da ferramenta
    - validate(): ValidaÃ§Ã£o de parÃ¢metros
    - get_info(): InformaÃ§Ãµes da ferramenta
    """
    
    # Atributos de plugin
    __plugin_name__ = "base_tool"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Ferramenta base da EVA"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["tool", "base"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["base"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, logger, config):
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        self.name = self.__class__.__name__.lower().replace("tool", "")
        self._execution_count = 0
        self._error_count = 0
        self._last_execution: Optional[datetime] = None
    
    @abstractmethod
    async def execute(self, params: Dict[str, Any], context: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executa a ferramenta.
        
        Args:
            params: ParÃ¢metros da execuÃ§Ã£o
            context: Contexto adicional
            
        Returns:
            Dict com resultado da execuÃ§Ã£o
        """
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    def validate(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """
        Valida os parÃ¢metros da ferramenta.
        
        Returns:
            True se vÃ¡lido, False caso contrÃ¡rio
        """
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    def get_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Retorna informaÃ§Ãµes da ferramenta.
        """
        pass
    
    def get_required_params(self) -> List[str]:
        """Retorna lista de parÃ¢metros obrigatÃ³rios."""
        return []
    
    def get_optional_params(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Retorna parÃ¢metros opcionais com valores padrÃ£o."""
        return {}
    
    def get_capabilities(self) -> List[str]:
        """Retorna capacidades da ferramenta."""
        return getattr(self, "__plugin_capabilities__", ["general"])
    
    def get_permissions(self) -> List[str]:
        """Retorna permissÃµes necessÃ¡rias."""
        return getattr(self, "__plugin_permissions__", [])
    
    def can_handle(self, action: str) -> bool:
        """Verifica se a ferramenta pode lidar com uma aÃ§Ã£o."""
        return action in self.get_capabilities() or "general" in self.get_capabilities()
    
    async def execute_with_validation(self, params: Dict[str, Any], 
                                      context: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executa com validaÃ§Ã£o prÃ©via.
        """
        # Valida parÃ¢metros
        if not self.validate(params):
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": "ValidaÃ§Ã£o de parÃ¢metros falhou",
                "tool": self.name,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
        
        # Verifica parÃ¢metros obrigatÃ³rios
        for req in self.get_required_params():
            if req not in params:
                return {
                    "success": False,
                    "error": f"ParÃ¢metro obrigatÃ³rio ausente: {req}",
                    "tool": self.name,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                }
        
        # Executa
        try:
            result = await self.execute(params, context or {})
            self._execution_count += 1
            self._last_execution = datetime.now()
            result["success"] = True
            result["tool"] = self.name
            result["timestamp"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            self._error_count += 1
            self.logger.error(f"Erro na ferramenta {self.name}: {e}")
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": str(e),
                "tool": self.name,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m estatÃ­sticas da ferramenta."""
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "execution_count": self._execution_count,
            "error_count": self._error_count,
            "last_execution": self._last_execution.isoformat() if self._last_execution else None,
            "capabilities": self.get_capabilities()
        }
