"""Contexto para ferramentas."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
from datetime import datetime


class ToolContext:
    """
    Gerencia o contexto para execuÃ§Ã£o de ferramentas.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, tool_manager):
        self.tool_manager = tool_manager
        self.logger = tool_manager.logger
    
    def get_context(self, tool_name: str, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        ObtÃ©m contexto para uma ferramenta.
        """
        return {
            "tool": tool_name,
            "params": params,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    def enrich_context(self, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Enriquece o contexto com informaÃ§Ãµes adicionais.
        """
        enriched = context.copy()
        
        if "user" not in enriched:
            enriched["user"] = {"role": "guest"}
        
        if "timestamp" not in enriched:
            enriched["timestamp"] = datetime.now().isoformat()
        
        return enriched


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\tools\tool_executor.py ====================
"""Executor de ferramentas."""

import asyncio
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime


class ToolExecutor:
    """
    Executa ferramentas com gerenciamento de recursos.
    
    Responsabilidades:
    - ExecuÃ§Ã£o assÃ­ncrona
    - Timeout
    - Retry automÃ¡tico
    - Log de execuÃ§Ã£o
    """
    
    def __init__(self, tool_manager):
        self.tool_manager = tool_manager
        self.logger = tool_manager.logger
        
        # ConfiguraÃ§Ãµes
        self._default_timeout = 30  # segundos
        self._max_retries = 3
        self._retry_delay = 2  # segundos
        
        # ExecuÃ§Ãµes em andamento
        self._executions: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
    async def execute(self, tool_name: str, params: Dict[str, Any],
                     context: Dict[str, Any] = None,
                     timeout: Optional[float] = None,
                     retries: Optional[int] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executa uma ferramenta com timeout e retry.
        """
        execution_id = f"{tool_name}_{datetime.now().timestamp()}"
        
        self._executions[execution_id] = {
            "tool": tool_name,
            "params": params,
            "started_at": datetime.now(),
            "status": "running"
        }
        
        try:
            result = await self._execute_with_retry(
                tool_name, params, context,
                timeout or self._default_timeout,
                retries or self._max_retries
            )
            
            self._executions[execution_id]["status"] = "completed"
            self._executions[execution_id]["completed_at"] = datetime.now()
            
            return result
            
        except asyncio.TimeoutError:
            self._executions[execution_id]["status"] = "timeout"
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": f"Timeout apÃ³s {timeout or self._default_timeout}s",
                "execution_id": execution_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
        except Exception as e:
            self._executions[execution_id]["status"] = "error"
            self._executions[execution_id]["error"] = str(e)
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": str(e),
                "execution_id": execution_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
        finally:
            # Limpa execuÃ§Ãµes antigas
            self._cleanup_executions()
    
    async def _execute_with_retry(self, tool_name: str, params: Dict[str, Any],
                                  context: Dict[str, Any], timeout: float,
                                  max_retries: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Executa com retry automÃ¡tico."""
        last_error = None
        
        for attempt in range(max_retries):
            try:
                result = await asyncio.wait_for(
                    self._execute_once(tool_name, params, context),
                    timeout=timeout
                )
                
                if result.get("success", False):
                    return result
                
                # Se falhou mas nÃ£o Ã© erro crÃ­tico, tenta novamente
                if attempt < max_retries - 1:
                    await asyncio.sleep(self._retry_delay * (attempt + 1))
                    continue
                
                return result
                
            except asyncio.TimeoutError as e:
                last_error = e
                if attempt < max_retries - 1:
                    await asyncio.sleep(self._retry_delay * (attempt + 1))
                    continue
                raise
            
            except Exception as e:
                last_error = e
                if attempt < max_retries - 1:
                    await asyncio.sleep(self._retry_delay * (attempt + 1))
                    continue
                raise
        
        raise last_error or Exception("Falha apÃ³s mÃºltiplas tentativas")
    
    async def _execute_once(self, tool_name: str, params: Dict[str, Any],
                           context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Executa uma ferramenta uma vez."""
        tool = self.tool_manager.get_tool(tool_name)
        
        if not tool:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": f"Ferramenta nÃ£o encontrada: {tool_name}"
            }
        
        return await tool.execute_with_validation(params, context)
    
    def _cleanup_executions(self) -> None:
        """Limpa execuÃ§Ãµes antigas."""
        now = datetime.now()
        to_remove = []
        
        for exec_id, exec_data in self._executions.items():
            started = exec_data.get("started_at")
            if started and (now - started).total_seconds() > 3600:  # 1 hora
                to_remove.append(exec_id)
        
        for exec_id in to_remove:
            del self._executions[exec_id]
    
    def get_execution_status(self, execution_id: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m status de uma execuÃ§Ã£o."""
        return self._executions.get(execution_id)
    
    def get_executions(self, limit: int = 10) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m execuÃ§Ãµes recentes."""
        sorted_execs = sorted(
            self._executions.items(),
            key=lambda x: x[1].get("started_at", datetime.min),
            reverse=True
        )
        return [
            {
                "id": exec_id,
                **data
            }
            for exec_id, data in sorted_execs[:limit]
        ]


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\tools\tool_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de ferramentas da EVA."""

import asyncio
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List, Type
from datetime import datetime

from src.kernel.events import Event, EventBus
from src.tools.base_tool import BaseTool
from src.tools.tool_registry import ToolRegistry
from src.tools.tool_executor import ToolExecutor
from src.tools.tool_permission import ToolPermission
from src.tools.tool_context import ToolContext


class ToolManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de ferramentas da EVA.
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "tool_manager"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Gerenciador de ferramentas da EVA"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["core", "tools", "execution"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["tool_management"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["event_bus", "logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self.registry = ToolRegistry()
        self.executor = ToolExecutor(self)
        self.permission = ToolPermission(self)
        self.context = ToolContext(self)
        
        self._tools: Dict[str, BaseTool] = {}
        self._cache: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        self._cache_ttl = 300
        self._running = False
        
        self._register_listeners()
        self.logger.info("ToolManager inicializado")
    
    def _register_listeners(self) -> None:
        self.event_bus.subscribe("tool.register", self._on_register)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("tool.execute", self._on_execute)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("tool.list", self._on_list)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("tool.get_info", self._on_get_info)
        self.event_bus.subscribe("tool.clear_cache", self._on_clear_cache)
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ToolManager inicializando...")
        self._register_builtin_tools()
        self._running = True
        self.logger.info(f"ToolManager inicializado com {len(self._tools)} ferramentas")
    
    async def start(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ToolManager iniciando...")
        self.logger.info("ToolManager pronto")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ToolManager desligando...")
        self._running = False
        self._cache.clear()
        self.logger.info("ToolManager desligado")
    
    def _register_builtin_tools(self) -> None:
        try:
            from src.tools.builtin.python_tool import PythonTool
            from src.tools.builtin.shell_tool import ShellTool
            from src.tools.builtin.file_tool import FileTool
            from src.tools.builtin.network_tool import NetworkTool
            from src.tools.builtin.system_tool import SystemTool
            from src.tools.builtin.date_tool import DateTool
            
            self.register_tool(PythonTool)
            self.register_tool(ShellTool)
            self.register_tool(FileTool)
            self.register_tool(NetworkTool)
            self.register_tool(SystemTool)
            self.register_tool(DateTool)
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao registrar ferramentas built-in: {e}")
    
    def register_tool(self, tool_class: Type[BaseTool]) -> bool:
        try:
            tool = tool_class(logger=self.logger, config=self.config)
            self._tools[tool.name] = tool
            self.registry.register(tool.name, tool_class, {
                "name": tool.name,
                "capabilities": tool.get_capabilities(),
                "permissions": tool.get_permissions(),
                "required_params": tool.get_required_params(),
                "optional_params": tool.get_optional_params()
            })
            self.logger.info(f"Ferramenta registrada: {tool.name}")
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao registrar ferramenta: {e}")
            return False
    
    async def _on_register(self, event: Event) -> None:
        payload = event.payload
        tool_class_path = payload.get("tool_class")
        
        if not tool_class_path:
            await self._send_error("no_tool_class", "Nenhuma classe de ferramenta fornecida")
            return
        
        try:
            module_path, class_name = tool_class_path.rsplit(".", 1)
            module = __import__(module_path, fromlist=[class_name])
            tool_class = getattr(module, class_name)
            success = self.register_tool(tool_class)
            
            await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                "tool.registered",
                {
                    "success": success,
                    "tool_class": tool_class_path,
                    "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                },
                source="tool_manager"
            )
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Erro ao registrar ferramenta via evento: {e}")
    
    async def _on_execute(self, event: Event) -> None:
        payload = event.payload
        tool_name = payload.get("tool")
        params = payload.get("params", {})
        context = payload.get("context", {})
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        use_cache = payload.get("use_cache", True)
        
        if not tool_name:
            await self._send_error("no_tool", "Nenhuma ferramenta especificada", request_id)
            return
        
        cache_key = f"{tool_name}:{str(params)}"
        if use_cache and cache_key in self._cache:
            cached = self._cache[cache_key]
            if (datetime.now() - datetime.fromisoformat(cached["timestamp"])).total_seconds() < self._cache_ttl:
                await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                    "tool.execution_result",
                    {
                        "result": cached["result"],
                        "cached": True,
                        "request_id": request_id,
                        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                    },
                    source="tool_manager"
                )
                return
        
        result = await self.execute_tool(tool_name, params, context)
        
        if result.get("success") and use_cache:
            self._cache[cache_key] = {
                "result": result,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "tool.execution_result",
            {
                "result": result,
                "cached": False,
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="tool_manager"
        )
    
    async def execute_tool(self, tool_name: str, params: Dict[str, Any],
                          context: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        tool = self._tools.get(tool_name)
        if not tool:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": f"Ferramenta nÃ£o encontrada: {tool_name}",
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
        
        if not self.permission.check(tool_name, context or {}):
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": f"PermissÃ£o negada para ferramenta: {tool_name}",
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
        
        return await tool.execute_with_validation(params, context)
    
    async def _on_list(self, event: Event) -> None:
        request_id = event.payload.get("request_id")
        tools = self.list_tools()
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "tool.list_result",
            {
                "tools": tools,
                "count": len(tools),
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="tool_manager"
        )
    
    async def _on_get_info(self, event: Event) -> None:
        payload = event.payload
        tool_name = payload.get("tool")
        request_id = payload.get("request_id")
        info = self.get_tool_info(tool_name)
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "tool.info",
            {
                "tool": tool_name,
                "info": info,
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="tool_manager"
        )
    
    async def _on_clear_cache(self, event: Event) -> None:
        self._cache.clear()
        self.logger.info("Cache de ferramentas limpo")
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "tool.cache_cleared",
            {"timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()},
            source="tool_manager"
        )
    
    async def _send_error(self, code: str, message: str, request_id: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "tool.error",
            {
                "code": code,
                "message": message,
                "request_id": request_id,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="tool_manager"
        )
    
    def get_tool(self, name: str) -> Optional[BaseTool]:
        return self._tools.get(name)
    
    def get_tool_info(self, name: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        tool = self._tools.get(name)
        if tool:
            return {
                **tool.get_info(),
                "stats": tool.get_stats(),
                "permissions": tool.get_permissions()
            }
        return None
    
    def list_tools(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        return [
            {
                "name": name,
                "capabilities": tool.get_capabilities(),
                "stats": tool.get_stats(),
                "info": tool.get_info()
            }
            for name, tool in self._tools.items()
        ]
    
    def get_cache_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "cache_size": len(self._cache),
            "cache_ttl": self._cache_ttl,
            "keys": list(self._cache.keys())[:10]
        }
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "total_tools": len(self._tools),
            "cache_size": len(self._cache),
            "tools": {
                name: tool.get_stats()
                for name, tool in self._tools.items()
            }
        }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\tools\tool_permission.py ====================
"""Gerenciamento de permissÃµes de ferramentas."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List


class ToolPermission:
    """
    Gerencia permissÃµes para execuÃ§Ã£o de ferramentas.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, tool_manager):
        self.tool_manager = tool_manager
        self.logger = tool_manager.logger
        
        # Mapeamento de roles para permissÃµes
        self._role_permissions = {
            "admin": ["*"],
            "user": ["read", "execute", "basic"],
            "child": ["read", "basic"],
            "guest": ["read"]
        }
    
    def check(self, tool_name: str, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """
        Verifica se a ferramenta pode ser executada no contexto atual.
        """
        # ObtÃ©m permissÃµes da ferramenta
        tool = self.tool_manager.get_tool(tool_name)
        if not tool:
            return False
        
        required_permissions = tool.get_permissions()
        
        # Se nÃ£o requer permissÃµes, permite
        if not required_permissions:
            return True
        
        # ObtÃ©m role do usuÃ¡rio
        user = context.get("user", {}).get("role", "guest")
        user_permissions = self._role_permissions.get(user, [])
        
        # Admin tem todas as permissÃµes
        if "admin" in user_permissions or "*" in user_permissions:
            return True
        
        # Verifica cada permissÃ£o requerida
        for perm in required_permissions:
            if perm not in user_permissions:
                self.logger.warning(f"PermissÃ£o negada: {perm} para {tool_name}")
                return False
        
        return True
    
    def add_role_permission(self, role: str, permission: str) -> None:
        """Adiciona uma permissÃ£o a um role."""
        if role not in self._role_permissions:
            self._role_permissions[role] = []
        if permission not in self._role_permissions[role]:
            self._role_permissions[role].append(permission)
    
    def remove_role_permission(self, role: str, permission: str) -> None:
        """Remove uma permissÃ£o de um role."""
        if role in self._role_permissions and permission in self._role_permissions[role]:
            self._role_permissions[role].remove(permission)
    
    def get_role_permissions(self, role: str) -> List[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m permissÃµes de um role."""
        return self._role_permissions.get(role, [])


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\tools\tool_registry.py ====================
"""Registro central de ferramentas."""

from typing import Dict, Type, Optional, List, Any

from src.tools.base_tool import BaseTool


class ToolRegistry:
    """
    Registro central de todas as ferramentas disponÃ­veis.
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._tools: Dict[str, Type[BaseTool]] = {}
        self._tool_metadata: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
    def register(self, name: str, tool_class: Type[BaseTool],
                 metadata: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> None:
        """
        Registra uma ferramenta.
        
        Args:
            name: Nome da ferramenta
            tool_class: Classe da ferramenta
            metadata: Metadados adicionais
        """
        self._tools[name] = tool_class
        self._tool_metadata[name] = {
            "name": name,
            "class": tool_class.__name__,
            "module": tool_class.__module__,
            "capabilities": getattr(tool_class, "__plugin_capabilities__", ["general"]),
            "permissions": getattr(tool_class, "__plugin_permissions__", []),
            "version": getattr(tool_class, "__plugin_version__", "1.0.0"),
            "description": getattr(tool_class, "__plugin_description__", ""),
            **(metadata or {})
        }
    
    def get(self, name: str) -> Optional[Type[BaseTool]]:
        """ObtÃ©m uma classe de ferramenta pelo nome."""
        return self._tools.get(name)
    
    def get_metadata(self, name: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m metadados de uma ferramenta."""
        return self._tool_metadata.get(name)
    
    def list(self) -> List[str]:
        """Lista todas as ferramentas registradas."""
        return list(self._tools.keys())
    
    def list_with_metadata(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Lista todas as ferramentas com metadados."""
        return list(self._tool_metadata.values())
    
    def get_by_capability(self, capability: str) -> List[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m ferramentas com uma capacidade especÃ­fica."""
        result = []
        for name, meta in self._tool_metadata.items():
            if capability in meta.get("capabilities", []):
                result.append(name)
        return result
    
    def get_by_permission(self, permission: str) -> List[str]:
        """ObtÃ©m ferramentas que requerem uma permissÃ£o especÃ­fica."""
        result = []
        for name, meta in self._tool_metadata.items():
            if permission in meta.get("permissions", []):
                result.append(name)
        return result
    
    def unregister(self, name: str) -> bool:
        """Remove uma ferramenta do registro."""
        if name in self._tools:
            del self._tools[name]
            if name in self._tool_metadata:
                del self._tool_metadata[name]
            return True
        return False
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Limpa o registro."""
        self._tools.clear()
        self._tool_metadata.clear()

==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\tools\builtin\date_tool.py ====================
"""Ferramenta de operaÃ§Ãµes com data/hora."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import time

from src.tools.base_tool import BaseTool


class DateTool(BaseTool):
    """
    OperaÃ§Ãµes com data e hora.
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "date_tool"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "OperaÃ§Ãµes com data e hora"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["tool", "date", "time"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["date", "time"]
    __plugin_permissions__ = ["read"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["logger", "config"]
    
    def get_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "description": "OperaÃ§Ãµes com data e hora",
            "capabilities": ["date", "time"]
        }
    
    def get_required_params(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["action"]
    
    def validate(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        if "action" not in params:
            return False
        return params["action"] in ["now", "format", "add", "diff", "parse"]
    
    async def execute(self, params: Dict[str, Any], context: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executa operaÃ§Ã£o de data/hora.
        """
        action = params["action"]
        
        result = {
            "type": "date",
            "action": action,
            "success": True
        }
        
        try:
            if action == "now":
                result["data"] = self._get_now()
            elif action == "format":
                result["data"] = self._format_date(params)
            elif action == "add":
                result["data"] = self._add_time(params)
            elif action == "diff":
                result["data"] = self._get_diff(params)
            elif action == "parse":
                result["data"] = self._parse_date(params)
                
        except Exception as e:
            result["error"] = str(e)
            result["success"] = False
        
        return result
    
    def _get_now(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m data/hora atual."""
        now = datetime.now()
        return {
            "timestamp": now.isoformat(),
            "iso": now.isoformat(),
            "date": now.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
            "time": now.strftime("%H:%M:%S"),
            "weekday": now.weekday(),
            "weekday_name": ["Segunda", "TerÃ§a", "Quarta", "Quinta", "Sexta", "SÃ¡bado", "Domingo"][now.weekday()],
            "unix": int(time.time())
        }
    
    def _format_date(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Formata uma data."""
        date_str = params.get("date")
        format_str = params.get("format", "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        
        if date_str:
            dt = datetime.fromisoformat(date_str)
        else:
            dt = datetime.now()
        
        return {
            "formatted": dt.strftime(format_str),
            "original": dt.isoformat()
        }
    
    def _add_time(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Adiciona tempo a uma data."""
        date_str = params.get("date")
        days = params.get("days", 0)
        hours = params.get("hours", 0)
        minutes = params.get("minutes", 0)
        
        if date_str:
            dt = datetime.fromisoformat(date_str)
        else:
            dt = datetime.now()
        
        result = dt + timedelta(days=days, hours=hours, minutes=minutes)
        
        return {
            "result": result.isoformat(),
            "original": dt.isoformat(),
            "added": {
                "days": days,
                "hours": hours,
                "minutes": minutes
            }
        }
    
    def _get_diff(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Calcula diferenÃ§a entre datas."""
        date1 = datetime.fromisoformat(params.get("date1", datetime.now().isoformat()))
        date2 = datetime.fromisoformat(params.get("date2", datetime.now().isoformat()))
        
        diff = date2 - date1
        
        return {
            "seconds": diff.total_seconds(),
            "minutes": diff.total_seconds() / 60,
            "hours": diff.total_seconds() / 3600,
            "days": diff.total_seconds() / 86400
        }
    
    def _parse_date(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Parseia uma string de data."""
        date_str = params.get("date")
        if not date_str:
            return {"error": "Data nÃ£o fornecida"}
        
        # Tenta vÃ¡rios formatos
        formats = [
            "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S",
            "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M",
            "%Y-%m-%d",
            "%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S",
            "%d/%m/%Y %H:%M",
            "%d/%m/%Y"
        ]
        
        for fmt in formats:
            try:
                dt = datetime.strptime(date_str, fmt)
                return {
                    "parsed": dt.isoformat(),
                    "format": fmt
                }
            except ValueError:
                continue
        
        return {"error": f"NÃ£o foi possÃ­vel parsear: {date_str}"}


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\tools\builtin\file_tool.py ====================
"""Ferramenta de operaÃ§Ãµes com arquivos."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from pathlib import Path
import json
import os
import shutil

from src.tools.base_tool import BaseTool


class FileTool(BaseTool):
    """
    OperaÃ§Ãµs com arquivos (ler, escrever, listar, etc).
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "file_tool"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "OperaÃ§Ãµes com arquivos"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["tool", "file", "filesystem"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["file", "filesystem"]
    __plugin_permissions__ = ["read", "write", "file"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, logger, config):
        super().__init__(logger, config)
        self._base_path = Path(config.get("paths.data", "./data"))
    
    def get_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "description": "OperaÃ§Ãµes com arquivos",
            "capabilities": ["file", "filesystem"],
            "base_path": str(self._base_path)
        }
    
    def get_required_params(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["action", "path"]
    
    def validate(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        if "action" not in params or "path" not in params:
            return False
        return params["action"] in ["read", "write", "delete", "list", "exists", "mkdir"]
    
    async def execute(self, params: Dict[str, Any], context: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executa operaÃ§Ã£o com arquivo.
        """
        action = params["action"]
        path = params["path"]
        
        # SeguranÃ§a: previne path traversal
        full_path = self._safe_path(path)
        
        result = {
            "type": "file",
            "action": action,
            "path": str(full_path),
            "success": False
        }
        
        try:
            if action == "read":
                result["content"] = await self._read_file(full_path)
                result["success"] = True
                
            elif action == "write":
                content = params.get("content", "")
                await self._write_file(full_path, content)
                result["success"] = True
                
            elif action == "delete":
                await self._delete_file(full_path)
                result["success"] = True
                
            elif action == "list":
                result["files"] = await self._list_files(full_path)
                result["success"] = True
                
            elif action == "exists":
                result["exists"] = full_path.exists()
                result["success"] = True
                
            elif action == "mkdir":
                full_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                result["success"] = True
                
        except Exception as e:
            result["error"] = str(e)
            result["success"] = False
        
        return result
    
    def _safe_path(self, path: str) -> Path:
        """Garante que o path Ã© seguro."""
        p = Path(path)
        if p.is_absolute():
            # Verifica se estÃ¡ dentro do base_path
            try:
                p.relative_to(self._base_path)
                return p
            except ValueError:
                # Se nÃ£o estiver, trata como relativo
                pass
        return self._base_path / p
    
    async def _read_file(self, path: Path) -> str:
        """LÃª um arquivo."""
        with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            return f.read()
    
    async def _write_file(self, path: Path, content: str) -> None:
        """Escreve um arquivo."""
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            f.write(content)
    
    async def _delete_file(self, path: Path) -> None:
        """Deleta um arquivo ou diretÃ³rio."""
        if path.is_file():
            path.unlink()
        elif path.is_dir():
            shutil.rmtree(path)
    
    async def _list_files(self, path: Path) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Lista arquivos em um diretÃ³rio."""
        if not path.exists():
            return []
        
        files = []
        for item in path.iterdir():
            files.append({
                "name": item.name,
                "is_dir": item.is_dir(),
                "size": item.stat().st_size if item.is_file() else 0,
                "modified": item.stat().st_mtime
            })
        return files

"""Ferramenta de operaÃ§Ãµes de rede."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
import socket
import asyncio
import subprocess
import platform

from src.tools.base_tool import BaseTool


class NetworkTool(BaseTool):
    """
    OperaÃ§Ãµes de rede (ping, DNS, etc).
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "network_tool"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "OperaÃ§Ãµes de rede"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["tool", "network", "ping"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["network", "ping", "dns"]
    __plugin_permissions__ = ["read", "network"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["logger", "config"]
    
    def get_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "description": "OperaÃ§Ãµes de rede",
            "capabilities": ["network", "ping", "dns"]
        }
    
    def get_required_params(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["action", "host"]
    
    def validate(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        if "action" not in params or "host" not in params:
            return False
        return params["action"] in ["ping", "dns", "port"]
    
    async def execute(self, params: Dict[str, Any], context: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executa operaÃ§Ã£o de rede.
        """
        action = params["action"]
        host = params["host"]
        timeout = params.get("timeout", 5)
        
        result = {
            "type": "network",
            "action": action,
            "host": host,
            "success": False
        }
        
        try:
            if action == "ping":
                result["data"] = await self._ping(host, timeout)
                result["success"] = True
            elif action == "dns":
                result["data"] = await self._dns(host)
                result["success"] = True
            elif action == "port":
                port = params.get("port", 80)
                result["data"] = await self._port_scan(host, port, timeout)
                result["success"] = True
                
        except Exception as e:
            result["error"] = str(e)
            result["success"] = False
        
        return result
    
    async def _ping(self, host: str, timeout: float) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Ping em um host."""
        try:
            ip = socket.gethostbyname(host)
            
            param = "-n" if platform.system().lower() == "windows" else "-c"
            cmd = ["ping", param, "1", "-w", str(int(timeout * 1000)), host]
            
            proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_exec(
                *cmd,
                stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
                stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE
            )
            
            await proc.wait()
            
            return {
                "host": host,
                "ip": ip,
                "reachable": proc.returncode == 0
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {
                "host": host,
                "reachable": False,
                "error": str(e)
            }
    
    async def _dns(self, host: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Resolve DNS."""
        try:
            ip = socket.gethostbyname(host)
            return {
                "host": host,
                "ip": ip,
                "resolved": True
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {
                "host": host,
                "resolved": False,
                "error": str(e)
            }
    
    async def _port_scan(self, host: str, port: int, timeout: float) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Escaneia uma porta."""
        try:
            reader, writer = await asyncio.wait_for(
                asyncio.open_connection(host, port),
                timeout=timeout
            )
            writer.close()
            await writer.wait_closed()
            
            return {
                "host": host,
                "port": port,
                "open": True
            }
        except Exception:
            return {
                "host": host,
                "port": port,
                "open": False
            }


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\tools\builtin\python_tool.py ====================
"""Ferramenta de execuÃ§Ã£o de cÃ³digo Python."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
import sys
import io
import contextlib
import traceback

from src.tools.base_tool import BaseTool


class PythonTool(BaseTool):
    """
    Executa cÃ³digo Python em um ambiente seguro.
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "python_tool"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Executa cÃ³digo Python"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["tool", "python", "code"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["python", "code_execution"]
    __plugin_permissions__ = ["execute", "python"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["logger", "config"]
    
    def __init__(self, logger, config):
        super().__init__(logger, config)
        self._safe_modules = ['math', 'json', 'datetime', 're', 'random']
    
    def get_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "description": "Executa cÃ³digo Python",
            "capabilities": ["python", "code_execution"],
            "safe_modules": self._safe_modules
        }
    
    def get_required_params(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["code"]
    
    def get_optional_params(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "timeout": 5,
            "safe_mode": True
        }
    
    def validate(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        if "code" not in params:
            return False
        if not isinstance(params["code"], str):
            return False
        return True
    
    async def execute(self, params: Dict[str, Any], context: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executa cÃ³digo Python.
        """
        code = params["code"]
        
        stdout = io.StringIO()
        stderr = io.StringIO()
        
        result = {
            "type": "python",
            "code": code,
            "output": "",
            "error": None
        }
        
        try:
            safe_globals = {
                "__builtins__": {
                    name: __builtins__[name] 
                    for name in ['print', 'len', 'str', 'int', 'float', 'list', 
                                 'dict', 'set', 'tuple', 'range', 'sum', 'min', 
                                 'max', 'abs', 'bool', 'type', 'isinstance']
                }
            }
            
            for mod in self._safe_modules:
                try:
                    safe_globals[mod] = __import__(mod)
                except:
                    pass
            
            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(stdout), contextlib.redirect_stderr(stderr):
                exec(code, safe_globals, {})
            
            result["output"] = stdout.getvalue()
            result["error"] = stderr.getvalue() if stderr.getvalue() else None
            result["success"] = True
            
        except Exception as e:
            result["error"] = str(e)
            result["traceback"] = traceback.format_exc()
            result["success"] = False
        
        return result


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\tools\builtin\shell_tool.py ====================
"""Ferramenta de execuÃ§Ã£o de comandos shell."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
import asyncio
import subprocess
import shlex

from src.tools.base_tool import BaseTool


class ShellTool(BaseTool):
    """
    Executa comandos shell.
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "shell_tool"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "Executa comandos shell"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["tool", "shell", "command"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["shell", "command_execution"]
    __plugin_permissions__ = ["execute", "shell"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["logger", "config"]
    
    def get_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "description": "Executa comandos shell",
            "capabilities": ["shell", "command_execution"]
        }
    
    def get_required_params(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["command"]
    
    def get_optional_params(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "timeout": 10,
            "shell": True
        }
    
    def validate(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        if "command" not in params:
            return False
        if not isinstance(params["command"], str):
            return False
        return True
    
    async def execute(self, params: Dict[str, Any], context: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executa um comando shell.
        """
        command = params["command"]
        timeout = params.get("timeout", 10)
        use_shell = params.get("shell", True)
        
        result = {
            "type": "shell",
            "command": command,
            "stdout": "",
            "stderr": "",
            "returncode": None
        }
        
        try:
            # Executa
            proc = await asyncio.create_subprocess_shell(
                command if use_shell else command,
                stdout=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE,
                stderr=asyncio.subprocess.PIPE
            )
            
            try:
                stdout, stderr = await asyncio.wait_for(
                    proc.communicate(),
                    timeout=timeout
                )
                
                result["stdout"] = stdout.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
                result["stderr"] = stderr.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
                result["returncode"] = proc.returncode
                result["success"] = proc.returncode == 0
                
            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                proc.kill()
                await proc.wait()
                result["error"] = f"Timeout apÃ³s {timeout}s"
                result["success"] = False
            
        except Exception as e:
            result["error"] = str(e)
            result["success"] = False
        
        return result

"""Ferramenta de operaÃ§Ãµes do sistema."""

from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
import psutil
import platform
import os

from src.tools.base_tool import BaseTool


class SystemTool(BaseTool):
    """
    OperaÃ§Ãµes do sistema (info, processo, etc).
    """
    
    __plugin_name__ = "system_tool"
    __plugin_version__ = "1.0.0"
    __plugin_description__ = "OperaÃ§Ãµes do sistema"
    __plugin_author__ = "EVA OS Team"
    __plugin_tags__ = ["tool", "system", "info"]
    __plugin_capabilities__ = ["system", "info"]
    __plugin_permissions__ = ["read", "system"]
    __plugin_requires__ = ["logger", "config"]
    
    def get_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "name": self.name,
            "description": "OperaÃ§Ãµes do sistema",
            "capabilities": ["system", "info"]
        }
    
    def get_required_params(self) -> List[str]:
        return ["action"]
    
    def validate(self, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        if "action" not in params:
            return False
        return params["action"] in ["info", "cpu", "memory", "disk", "processes"]
    
    async def execute(self, params: Dict[str, Any], context: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Executa operaÃ§Ã£o do sistema.
        """
        action = params["action"]
        
        result = {
            "type": "system",
            "action": action,
            "success": True
        }
        
        try:
            if action == "info":
                result["data"] = self._get_system_info()
            elif action == "cpu":
                result["data"] = self._get_cpu_info()
            elif action == "memory":
                result["data"] = self._get_memory_info()
            elif action == "disk":
                result["data"] = self._get_disk_info()
            elif action == "processes":
                result["data"] = self._get_process_info(params.get("limit", 10))
                
        except Exception as e:
            result["error"] = str(e)
            result["success"] = False
        
        return result
    
    def _get_system_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m informaÃ§Ãµes do sistema."""
        return {
            "platform": platform.platform(),
            "python_version": platform.python_version(),
            "hostname": platform.node(),
            "processor": platform.processor(),
            "cpus": psutil.cpu_count(),
            "uptime": (datetime.now() - datetime.fromtimestamp(psutil.boot_time())).total_seconds()
        }
    
    def _get_cpu_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m informaÃ§Ãµes de CPU."""
        return {
            "percent": psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.5),
            "per_cpu": psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.5, percpu=True),
            "count": psutil.cpu_count(),
            "freq": psutil.cpu_freq()._asdict() if psutil.cpu_freq() else None
        }
    
    def _get_memory_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m informaÃ§Ãµes de memÃ³ria."""
        mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
        return {
            "total": mem.total,
            "available": mem.available,
            "percent": mem.percent,
            "used": mem.used,
            "free": mem.free
        }
    
    def _get_disk_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """ObtÃ©m informaÃ§Ãµes de disco."""
        disk = psutil.disk_usage('/')
        return {
            "total": disk.total,
            "used": disk.used,
            "free": disk.free,
            "percent": disk.percent
        }
    
    def _get_process_info(self, limit: int) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """ObtÃ©m lista de processos."""
        processes = []
        for proc in psutil.process_iter(['pid', 'name', 'cpu_percent', 'memory_percent']):
            try:
                processes.append(proc.info)
            except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied):
                pass
        
        # Ordena por uso de CPU
        processes.sort(key=lambda x: x.get('cpu_percent', 0), reverse=True)
        return processes[:limit]

from datetime import datetime

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

"""
User Manager - Gerencia perfis e preferÃªncias dos usuÃ¡rios.
"""

import json
import os
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, List
from datetime import datetime
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

# ðŸ”¥ CORRIGIDO: Import absoluto (em vez de relativo)
from src.utils.logger import Logger


@dataclass
class UserProfile:
    user_id: str
    name: str = "UsuÃ¡rio"
    preferred_name: Optional[str] = None
    preferred_tone: str = "neutro"
    preferred_response_length: str = "medio"
    preferences: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    first_seen: Optional[str] = None
    last_seen: Optional[str] = None
    interaction_count: int = 0
    facts: List[Dict[str, str]] = field(default_factory=list)


class UserManager:
    def __init__(self, event_bus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = Logger.get_logger() if logger is None else logger
        self.config = config or {}
        self._users: Dict[str, UserProfile] = {}
        self._current_user_id: Optional[str] = None
        self.persist_file = config.get("users_persist_file", "data/users.json") if config else "data/users.json"
        self._load_users()
        self.logger.info("ðŸ‘¤ [UserManager] Inicializado")
        self.logger.info(f"   {len(self._users)} usuÃ¡rios carregados")
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸ‘¤ [UserManager] Pronto")
    
    async def process(self, context: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        user_id = context.get("user_id", "unknown")
        user_message = context.get("message", "")
        user = self.get_or_create_user(user_id)
        self._current_user_id = user_id
        user.last_seen = datetime.now().isoformat()
        user.interaction_count += 1
        self._save_users()
        return {
            "user_id": user.user_id,
            "name": user.preferred_name or user.name,
            "preferred_tone": user.preferred_tone,
            "preferred_response_length": user.preferred_response_length,
            "interaction_count": user.interaction_count,
            "preferences": user.preferences,
            "facts": user.facts
        }
    
    def get_or_create_user(self, user_id: str, name: str = None) -> UserProfile:
        if user_id in self._users:
            return self._users[user_id]
        user = UserProfile(
            user_id=user_id,
            name=name or "UsuÃ¡rio",
            first_seen=datetime.now().isoformat(),
            last_seen=datetime.now().isoformat(),
            interaction_count=0
        )
        self._users[user_id] = user
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ‘¤ [UserManager] Novo usuÃ¡rio criado: {user_id}")
        return user
    
    def get_current_user(self) -> Optional[UserProfile]:
        if self._current_user_id and self._current_user_id in self._users:
            return self._users[self._current_user_id]
        return None
    
    def update_user_preference(self, user_id: str, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
        user = self.get_or_create_user(user_id)
        user.preferences[key] = value
        self._save_users()
    
    def add_fact_about_user(self, user_id: str, fact: str, confidence: float = 0.7) -> None:
        user = self.get_or_create_user(user_id)
        existing = [f for f in user.facts if f["fact"].lower() == fact.lower()]
        if existing:
            return
        user.facts.append({
            "fact": fact,
            "confidence": confidence,
            "learned_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
        })
        self._save_users()
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸ‘¤ [UserManager] Fato aprendido sobre {user_id}: {fact}")
    
    def get_user_facts(self, user_id: str) -> List[str]:
        user = self.get_or_create_user(user_id)
        return [f["fact"] for f in user.facts]
    
    def get_user_profile_prompt(self, user_id: str) -> str:
        user = self.get_or_create_user(user_id)
        parts = []
        if user.preferred_name:
            parts.append(f"O usuÃ¡rio prefere ser chamado de {user.preferred_name}.")
        if user.preferred_tone != "neutro":
            parts.append(f"O usuÃ¡rio prefere um tom {user.preferred_tone}.")
        if user.preferred_response_length != "medio":
            parts.append(f"O usuÃ¡rio prefere respostas {user.preferred_response_length}.")
        if user.facts:
            facts_str = ", ".join([f["fact"] for f in user.facts[-5:]])
            parts.append(f"Fatos conhecidos sobre o usuÃ¡rio: {facts_str}.")
        return " ".join(parts) if parts else "UsuÃ¡rio desconhecido. Trate com respeito e educaÃ§Ã£o."
    
    def _load_users(self) -> None:
        try:
            if os.path.exists(self.persist_file):
                with open(self.persist_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    for user_id, user_data in data.items():
                        profile = UserProfile(
                            user_id=user_id,
                            name=user_data.get("name", "UsuÃ¡rio"),
                            preferred_name=user_data.get("preferred_name"),
                            preferred_tone=user_data.get("preferred_tone", "neutro"),
                            preferred_response_length=user_data.get("preferred_response_length", "medio"),
                            preferences=user_data.get("preferences", {}),
                            first_seen=user_data.get("first_seen"),
                            last_seen=user_data.get("last_seen"),
                            interaction_count=user_data.get("interaction_count", 0),
                            facts=user_data.get("facts", [])
                        )
                        self._users[user_id] = profile
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(f"âš ï¸ Erro ao carregar usuÃ¡rios: {e}")
    
    def _save_users(self) -> None:
        try:
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.persist_file), exist_ok=True)
            data = {}
            for user_id, profile in self._users.items():
                data[user_id] = {
                    "name": profile.name,
                    "preferred_name": profile.preferred_name,
                    "preferred_tone": profile.preferred_tone,
                    "preferred_response_length": profile.preferred_response_length,
                    "preferences": profile.preferences,
                    "first_seen": profile.first_seen,
                    "last_seen": profile.last_seen,
                    "interaction_count": profile.interaction_count,
                    "facts": profile.facts
                }
            with open(self.persist_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(f"âš ï¸ Erro ao salvar usuÃ¡rios: {e}")
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self._save_users()
        self.logger.info("ðŸ‘¤ [UserManager] Desligado")
    
    def get_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "total_users": len(self._users),
            "current_user": self._current_user_id,
            "active": self._current_user_id is not None
        }

==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\utils\logger.py ====================
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

"""
Logger - Sistema de logging centralizado da EVA.
"""

import logging
import sys
from datetime import datetime


class Logger:
    """
    Logger singleton para a EVA.
    """
    
    _instance = None
    _loggers = {}
    
    @classmethod
    def get_logger(cls, name: str = "EVA") -> logging.Logger:
        """
        Retorna uma instÃ¢ncia do logger.
        
        Args:
            name: Nome do logger (padrÃ£o: "EVA")
        
        Returns:
            Logger configurado
        """
        if name not in cls._loggers:
            logger = logging.getLogger(name)
            logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
            
            # Remove handlers duplicados
            if logger.hasHandlers():
                logger.handlers.clear()
            
            # Handler para console
            console_handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
            console_handler.setLevel(logging.INFO)
            
            # Formato: HORA | NIVEL | NOME | MENSAGEM
            formatter = logging.Formatter(
                '%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-8s | %(name)s | %(message)s',
                datefmt='%H:%M:%S'
            )
            console_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
            logger.addHandler(console_handler)
            
            cls._loggers[name] = logger
        
        return cls._loggers[name]
    
    @classmethod
    def set_level(cls, name: str, level: str) -> None:
        """
        Altera o nÃ­vel de log de um logger.
        
        Args:
            name: Nome do logger
            level: NÃ­vel (DEBUG, INFO, WARNING, ERROR, CRITICAL)
        """
        if name in cls._loggers:
            level_map = {
                "DEBUG": logging.DEBUG,
                "INFO": logging.INFO,
                "WARNING": logging.WARNING,
                "ERROR": logging.ERROR,
                "CRITICAL": logging.CRITICAL
            }
            cls._loggers[name].setLevel(level_map.get(level.upper(), logging.INFO))


==================== C:\Users\Alberto_Fernandes\Desktop\EVA-OS\src\voice\voice_manager.py ====================
"""Gerenciador de Voz da EVA - STT (Whisper) + TTS (gTTS)."""

import asyncio
import os
import tempfile
import wave
import numpy as np
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional, Callable
from datetime import datetime
import subprocess

from src.kernel.events import EventBus


class VoiceManager:
    """
    Gerenciador de Voz da EVA.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, event_bus: EventBus, logger, config):
        self.event_bus = event_bus
        self.logger = logger
        self.config = config
        
        self._stt_model = None
        self._running = False
        self._listening = False
        self._listen_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
        self._continuous_mode = False
        
        self._wake_word = "eva"
        self._sample_rate = 16000
        self._silence_threshold = 300
        self._silence_duration = 1.5
        self._min_speech_duration = 0.5
        
        self.logger.info("ðŸŽ¤ [VoiceManager] Inicializado")
    
    async def initialize(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸŽ¤ [VoiceManager] Inicializando...")
        self._running = True
        await self._load_stt()
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸŽ¤ [VoiceManager] Inicializado (STT: {self._stt_model is not None})")
    
    async def _load_stt(self) -> None:
        try:
            import whisper
            model_name = self.config.get("voice.stt_model", "base")
            self._stt_model = whisper.load_model(model_name)
            self.logger.info(f"ðŸŽ¤ [STT] Whisper carregado: {model_name}")
        except ImportError:
            self.logger.warning("ðŸŽ¤ [STT] Whisper nÃ£o disponÃ­vel")
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"ðŸŽ¤ [STT] Erro: {e}")
    
    async def speech_to_text(self, audio_path: Path) -> Optional[str]:
        if not self._stt_model:
            return None
        try:
            result = self._stt_model.transcribe(str(audio_path), language="pt")
            text = result.get("text", "").strip()
            if text:
                self.logger.info(f"ðŸŽ¤ [STT] Transcrito: '{text}'")
                return text
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"ðŸŽ¤ [STT] Erro: {e}")
            return None
    
    def _remove_emojis(self, text: str) -> str:
        emoji_pattern = re.compile(
            "[\U0001F600-\U0001F64F"
            "\U0001F300-\U0001F5FF"
            "\U0001F680-\U0001F6FF"
            "\U0001F700-\U0001F77F"
            "\U0001F780-\U0001F7FF"
            "\U0001F800-\U0001F8FF"
            "\U0001F900-\U0001F9FF"
            "\U0001FA00-\U0001FA6F"
            "\U0001FA70-\U0001FAFF"
            "\u2600-\u26FF"
            "\u2700-\u27BF"
            "\uFE00-\uFE0F"
            "]+",
            flags=re.UNICODE
        )
        clean = emoji_pattern.sub('', text)
        clean = re.sub(r'[âœ¦â–¶ï¸â¸ï¸ðŸ§µðŸ””ðŸ“°ðŸ’¡]', '', clean)
        clean = re.sub(r'[""``\']', '', clean)
        clean = re.sub(r'[=]{3,}', ' ', clean)
        clean = re.sub(r'[_-]{3,}', ' ', clean)
        clean = re.sub(r'http[s]?://\S+', '', clean)
        clean = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', clean).strip()
        return clean
    
    async def text_to_speech(self, text: str, output_path: Optional[Path] = None) -> Optional[Path]:
        try:
            from gtts import gTTS
            clean_text = self._remove_emojis(text)
            if output_path is None:
                output_path = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / f"eva_tts_{datetime.now().timestamp()}.mp3"
            tts = gTTS(text=clean_text, lang='pt-br', slow=False)
            tts.save(str(output_path))
            self.logger.info(f"ðŸŽ¤ [TTS] Ãudio gerado: {output_path}")
            return output_path
        except ImportError:
            self.logger.warning("ðŸŽ¤ [TTS] gTTS nÃ£o disponÃ­vel")
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"ðŸŽ¤ [TTS] Erro: {e}")
            return None
    
    async def start_listening(self, callback: Optional[Callable] = None) -> None:
        if self._listening:
            return
        self._listening = True
        self._continuous_mode = False
        self._listen_task = asyncio.create_task(self._continuous_listen(callback))
        self.logger.info("ðŸŽ¤ [ESCUTA] Escuta ativa iniciada")
    
    async def stop_listening(self) -> None:
        self._listening = False
        self._continuous_mode = False
        if self._listen_task:
            self._listen_task.cancel()
            try:
                await self._listen_task
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                pass
        self.logger.info("ðŸŽ¤ [ESCUTA] Escuta ativa parada")
    
    async def _continuous_listen(self, callback: Optional[Callable]) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸŽ¤ [ESCUTA] Aguardando 'EVA...'")
        
        try:
            import sounddevice as sd
            import numpy as np
            
            chunk = 1024
            silent_chunks = 0
            max_silent_chunks = int(self._sample_rate / chunk * self._silence_duration)
            recording = False
            frames = []
            
            stream = sd.InputStream(
                samplerate=self._sample_rate,
                channels=1,
                dtype=np.int16,
                blocksize=chunk
            )
            stream.start()
            
            while self._listening:
                data, _ = stream.read(chunk)
                audio_data = np.frombuffer(data, dtype=np.int16)
                volume = np.abs(audio_data).mean()
                
                if volume > self._silence_threshold:
                    if not recording:
                        recording = True
                        frames = []
                        silent_chunks = 0
                        self.logger.debug("ðŸŽ¤ [ESCUTA] Fala detectada")
                    frames.append(data)
                    silent_chunks = 0
                elif recording:
                    frames.append(data)
                    silent_chunks += 1
                    
                    if silent_chunks > max_silent_chunks and len(frames) > int(self._sample_rate / chunk * self._min_speech_duration):
                        recording = False
                        self.logger.debug("ðŸŽ¤ [ESCUTA] Fala finalizada")
                        
                        temp_file = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / f"eva_speech_{datetime.now().timestamp()}.wav"
                        
                        with wave.open(str(temp_file), 'wb') as wf:
                            wf.setnchannels(1)
                            wf.setsampwidth(2)
                            wf.setframerate(self._sample_rate)
                            wf.writeframes(b''.join(frames))
                        
                        asyncio.create_task(self._process_audio(temp_file, callback))
                        frames = []
                
                await asyncio.sleep(0.01)
            
            stream.stop()
            stream.close()
            
        except ImportError:
            self.logger.warning("ðŸŽ¤ [ESCUTA] sounddevice nÃ£o disponÃ­vel")
            self._listening = False
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"ðŸŽ¤ [ESCUTA] Erro: {e}")
            self._listening = False
    
    async def _process_audio(self, audio_path: Path, callback: Optional[Callable]) -> None:
        try:
            text = await self.speech_to_text(audio_path)
            
            if text:
                text_lower = text.lower()
                
                if self._wake_word in text_lower or self._continuous_mode:
                    clean_text = text_lower
                    if self._wake_word in clean_text:
                        clean_text = clean_text.replace(self._wake_word, "").strip()
                        self._continuous_mode = True
                        self.logger.info("ðŸŽ¤ [ESCUTA] Modo contÃ­nuo ativado")
                    
                    if clean_text in ["stop", "eva stop", "parar"]:
                        self._continuous_mode = False
                        self.logger.info("ðŸŽ¤ [ESCUTA] Modo contÃ­nuo desativado")
                        await self.say("Ok, parando.")
                        return
                    
                    if clean_text:
                        self.logger.info(f"ðŸŽ¤ [COMANDO] '{clean_text}'")
                        
                        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
                            "voice.command",
                            {
                                "text": clean_text,
                                "source": "voice",
                                "continuous": self._continuous_mode,
                                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
                            },
                            source="voice_manager"
                        )
                        
                        if callback:
                            if asyncio.iscoroutinefunction(callback):
                                await callback(clean_text)
                            else:
                                callback(clean_text)
                else:
                    self.logger.debug(f"ðŸŽ¤ [ESCUTA] Ignorado: '{text}'")
            
            try:
                audio_path.unlink()
            except:
                pass
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"ðŸŽ¤ [PROCESSAMENTO] Erro: {e}")
    
    # ===== FALAR (CORRIGIDO - COM FFPLAY) =====
    
    async def say(self, text: str) -> bool:
        """Fala o texto usando ffplay (sem janela)."""
        clean_text = self._remove_emojis(text)
        self.logger.info(f"ðŸŽ¤ [FALAR] '{clean_text[:50]}...'")
        
        await self.event_bus.publish_async(
            "voice.speaking",
            {
                "text": text,
                "clean_text": clean_text,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            },
            source="voice_manager"
        )
        
        audio_path = await self.text_to_speech(text)
        
        if audio_path:
            try:
                # ðŸ”¥ SOLUÃ‡ÃƒO DEFINITIVA: ffplay com -nodisp (SEM JANELA)
                subprocess.Popen(
                    ['ffplay', '-nodisp', '-autoexit', str(audio_path)],
                    stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                    stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                    creationflags=subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW
                )
                
                self.logger.info(f"ðŸŽ¤ [FALAR] Ãudio tocando em segundo plano (ffplay): {audio_path}")
                
                async def delete_audio():
                    await asyncio.sleep(5)
                    try:
                        if audio_path.exists():
                            audio_path.unlink()
                    except:
                        pass
                
                asyncio.create_task(delete_audio())
                return True
                
            except FileNotFoundError:
                self.logger.warning("âš ï¸ ffplay nÃ£o encontrado. Instale o FFmpeg.")
                # Fallback: tenta o player padrÃ£o (vai abrir janela)
                try:
                    os.startfile(str(audio_path))
                    return True
                except:
                    return False
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"ðŸŽ¤ [FALAR] Erro ao tocar: {e}")
                return False
        return False
    
    async def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.logger.info("ðŸŽ¤ [VoiceManager] Desligando...")
        self._running = False
        await self.stop_listening()
        self.logger.info("ðŸŽ¤ [VoiceManager] Desligado")
    
    async def health_check(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "status": "running" if self._running else "stopped",
            "stt_available": self._stt_model is not None,
            "listening": self._listening,
            "continuous_mode": self._continuous_mode
        }

"""Testes do Core EVA OS."""

import asyncio
import pytest
from unittest.mock import Mock, AsyncMock

from src.core.event_bus import EventBus, Event, EventPriority
from src.core.service_registry import ServiceRegistry, ServiceStatus
from src.core.dependency_container import DependencyContainer
from src.core.lifecycle_manager import LifecycleManager, ModuleState
from src.core.module_loader import ModuleLoader
from src.core.state_manager import StateManager, SystemState


class TestEventBus:
    """Testes do EventBus."""
    
    def setup_method(self):
        self.logger = Mock()
        self.bus = EventBus(self.logger)
        self.bus._running = True  # Para testes sÃ­ncronos
    
    def test_publish_sync(self):
        """Testa publicaÃ§Ã£o sÃ­ncrona."""
        called = False
        
        def callback(event):
            nonlocal called
            called = True
            assert event.type == "TEST_EVENT"
            assert event.data == {"foo": "bar"}
        
        self.bus.subscribe("TEST_EVENT", callback)
        self.bus.publish(Event("TEST_EVENT", {"foo": "bar"}))
        
        assert called
    
    @pytest.mark.asyncio
    async def test_publish_async(self):
        """Testa publicaÃ§Ã£o assÃ­ncrona."""
        called = False
        
        async def callback(event):
            nonlocal called
            called = True
            assert event.type == "TEST_EVENT"
        
        self.bus.subscribe_async("TEST_EVENT", callback)
        await self.bus.publish_async(Event("TEST_EVENT"))
        
        # Aguarda processamento
        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)
        assert called
    
    def test_wildcard(self):
        """Testa listener wildcard."""
        called = []
        
        def callback(event):
            called.append(event.type)
        
        self.bus.subscribe("*", callback)
        self.bus.publish(Event("EVENT_1"))
        self.bus.publish(Event("EVENT_2"))
        
        assert "EVENT_1" in called
        assert "EVENT_2" in called
    
    def test_has_subscribers(self):
        """Testa verificaÃ§Ã£o de inscritos."""
        def callback(event): pass
        
        assert not self.bus.has_subscribers("TEST_EVENT")
        self.bus.subscribe("TEST_EVENT", callback)
        assert self.bus.has_subscribers("TEST_EVENT")


class TestServiceRegistry:
    """Testes do ServiceRegistry."""
    
    def setup_method(self):
        self.logger = Mock()
        self.registry = ServiceRegistry(self.logger)
    
    def test_register_and_get(self):
        """Testa registro e obtenÃ§Ã£o."""
        class TestService:
            pass
        
        service = TestService()
        self.registry.register("test", service)
        
        assert self.registry.get("test") is service
        assert self.registry.exists("test")
    
    def test_alias(self):
        """Testa alias."""
        class TestService:
            pass
        
        service = TestService()
        self.registry.register("test", service)
        self.registry.register_alias("alias", "test")
        
        assert self.registry.get("alias") is service
    
    def test_status(self):
        """Testa status."""
        class TestService:
            pass
        
        service = TestService()
        self.registry.register("test", service)
        
        assert self.registry.get_status("test") == ServiceStatus.REGISTERED
        
        self.registry.set_status("test", ServiceStatus.RUNNING)
        assert self.registry.get_status("test") == ServiceStatus.RUNNING


class TestDependencyContainer:
    """Testes do DependencyContainer."""
    
    def setup_method(self):
        self.logger = Mock()
        self.registry = ServiceRegistry(self.logger)
        self.container = DependencyContainer(self.logger, self.registry)
    
    def test_resolve_singleton(self):
        """Testa resoluÃ§Ã£o de singleton."""
        class TestService:
            pass
        
        service = TestService()
        self.container.register_singleton("test", service)
        
        resolved = self.container.resolve("test")
        assert resolved is service
    
    def test_resolve_factory(self):
        """Testa resoluÃ§Ã£o de fÃ¡brica."""
        class TestService:
            pass
        
        self.container.register_factory("test", lambda: TestService())
        
        resolved = self.container.resolve("test")
        assert isinstance(resolved, TestService)


class TestStateManager:
    """Testes do StateManager."""
    
    def setup_method(self):
        self.logger = Mock()
        self.event_bus = Mock()
        self.state_manager = StateManager(self.logger, self.event_bus)
    
    def test_initial_state(self):
        """Testa estado inicial."""
        assert self.state_manager.get_state() == SystemState.OFFLINE
    
    def test_set_state(self):
        """Testa mudanÃ§a de estado."""
        result = self.state_manager.set_state(SystemState.BOOTING, "Teste")
        assert result
        assert self.state_manager.get_state() == SystemState.BOOTING
    
    def test_invalid_transition(self):
        """Testa transiÃ§Ã£o invÃ¡lida."""
        result = self.state_manager.set_state(SystemState.EXECUTING, "Teste")
        assert not result
        assert self.state_manager.get_state() == SystemState.OFFLINE
    
    def test_busy_state(self):
        """Testa verificaÃ§Ã£o de ocupado."""
        assert not self.state_manager.is_busy()
        
        self.state_manager.set_state(SystemState.THINKING, "Teste")
        assert self.state_manager.is_busy()
    
    def test_idle_state(self):
        """Testa verificaÃ§Ã£o de ocioso."""
        assert not self.state_manager.is_idle()
        
        self.state_manager.set_state(SystemState.IDLE, "Teste")
        assert self.state_manager.is_idle()
